### [1] [Singlet-triplet superconductivity from repulsive interactions in a $p$-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14609)
*Hanbit Oh,Laura Classen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在p波磁体的Van Hove奇点附近，纯排斥相互作用下超导始终是最强不稳定性，且配对态为单重态-三重态混合，双层模型下还存在有限动量等自旋配对密度波竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期提出的具有奇宇称自旋劈裂的p波磁体启发，作者希望厘清p波自旋劈裂的对称性如何决定Van Hove填充附近电子系统各不稳定性之间的竞争关系。

Method: 采用parquet重整化群（pRG）方法，先分析简单的单子格p波磁体模型，再推广到更真实的双子格模型，考察局域排斥相互作用下各散射通道的发散行为。

Result: 单子格模型中，在整个相图上超导都是领先不稳定性，配对态一般混合自旋单重态与三重态；当Tc远小于每个Van Hove电子与其反演配对体之间的能量偏移时，趋于等权重混合。双子格模型中超导仍占主导，但子格自由度导致多个单-三重态配对通道竞争，并出现有限动量的等自旋配对密度波。

Conclusion: 该机制普遍适用于奇宇称磁体，并为实现节点拓扑超导提供了一条自然途径。

Abstract: Motivated by recent proposals of $p$-wave magnets, which host odd-parity spin splitting, we study electrons near Van Hove singularities with local repulsive interactions. We demonstrate how the symmetry of the $p$-wave spin splitting governs the competition among instabilities. Using a parquet renormalization group (pRG) analysis of a simple one-sublattice model of a $p$-wave magnet, we find that superconductivity is the leading instability over the entire phase diagram for purely repulsive interactions. The resulting superconducting state is generically a mixture of spin-singlet and spin-triplet pairing and approaches an equal-weight mixture when $T_c$ is small compared with the energy offset between each Van Hove electron and its inversion partner. Extending the pRG analysis to a more realistic two-sublattice model, we find that superconductivity remains the leading instability, while the sublattice degree of freedom leads to a competition among various singlet-triplet pairing channels and even a finite-momentum equal-spin pair-density wave. Our mechanism applies broadly to odd-parity magnets and opens a natural route to nodal topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [2] [Superlattice Stabilization of the Topological Quantum Hall Ferromagnet in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14679)
*Tiago Cecchi,Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过可调超晶格势重构石墨烯中自旋与谷序的竞争，在无需强面内磁场或强介电屏蔽的条件下稳定了电荷中性点的铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体，并证实其与量子霍尔铁磁体绝热连通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯量子霍尔区中自旋序与谷序的竞争可产生关联拓扑相，但铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体通常需要大面内磁场或对库仑相互作用的强介电抑制才能实现，缺乏更简便的调控手段。

Method: 先用计入超晶格势的广义重整化群方法计算决定竞争态选择的相互作用各向异性；再用含重整化各向异性的自洽动量空间Hartree-Fock计算确定绝缘自旋极化铁磁体的稳定区域；最后检验该铁磁体与量子霍尔铁磁体的绝热连通性。

Result: 超晶格势抑制了与自旋极化相竞争的各向异性；但同时引入的色散倾向于部分自旋极化的补偿半金属。综合两者，自洽Hartree-Fock计算给出了绝缘自旋极化铁磁体的稳定区间，并验证其与量子霍尔铁磁体绝热连通，确认其拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 超晶格势可作为调控相互作用石墨烯相图的手段，并提供了进入其螺旋量子霍尔区间的途径。

Abstract: Competition between spin and valley orders makes graphene a versatile platform for engineering correlated topological phases in the quantum Hall regime. We show that a tunable superlattice potential reorganizes this competition, and stabilizes the ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator at charge neutrality without requiring a large in-plane magnetic field or strong dielectric suppression of Coulomb interactions. The interaction anisotropies responsible for choosing between different competing states are first computed by a generalized renormalization group procedure that accounts for the superlattice potential. We find that the potential suppresses anisotropies that compete with spin-polarization. At the same time, the potential introduces dispersion which prefers a partly spin-polarized compensated semi-metal. Therefore, we carry out a self-consistent momentum-space Hartree-Fock computation with renormalized aniostropies to establish the regime of stability of the insulating spin-polarized ferromagnet. Finally, we verify that this ferromagnet is adiabatically connected to the quantum Hall ferromagnet, thereby confirming its topological character. Our results establish superlattice potentials as a means of controlling the phase diagram of interacting graphene and accessing its helical quantum Hall regime.

</details>


### [3] [Termination-selective Kondo hybridization of topological surface states in CeCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15022)
*Bo Chen,Chen Zhang,Hao Liu,Qi-Yi Wu,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Ying Zhou,Chun-Hui Lyu,Yang Luo,Zi-Jie Xu,Hai-Long Deng,Zheng-Tai Liu,Mao Ye,Zhen-Hua Chen,Yao-Bo Huang,Sheng-Tao Cui,Zhe Sun,Da-Wei Shen,Jun He,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合解理面分辨ARPES、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱与表面电子结构计算，在Kondo晶格化合物CeCuAs₂中区分出As1与CeAs2两种表面终端，并在CeAs2终端上观测到与非平庸体拓扑对应的Dirac表面态与近E_F弱色散Ce 4f能带共存，给出终端选择性f-Dirac杂化的谱学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联Kondo晶格中的拓扑态是凝聚态前沿问题：边界电子与局域f态的耦合会重构低能电子结构，但缺乏终端分辨的直接谱学证据，且难以区分不同表面终端各自的电子结构。

Method: 以Kondo晶格化合物CeCuAs₂为对象，联合使用终端分辨的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱以及表面电子结构计算；依据费米面几何特征与终端特异性计算，将两套表面电子结构分别归属为As1终端与CeAs2终端。

Result: 在CeAs2终端上，与计算所得非平庸体拓扑相对应的Dirac表面态与近E_F处弱色散的Ce 4f能带共存；共振光电子能谱确认了低能4f特征，并在Dirac支所经过的能量区间揭示出与晶体场相关的精细结构；降温时4f与Dirac区域的谱权重均增加，而作为参照的导带区域变化明显较小。

Conclusion: 观测结果提供了终端选择性f-Dirac杂化以及4f特征进入拓扑表面态的光谱证据，表明表面终端可作为调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子相互作用的微观手段，为强关联拓扑态的终端工程提供依据。

Abstract: Strongly correlated topological states in Kondo lattices represent a frontier in condensed matter physics, where the coupling between boundary electrons and localized f states can reshape the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we combine termination-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), Ce 4$d \rightarrow 4f$ resonant photoemission, and surface electronic structure calculations to investigate the Kondo lattice compound CeCuAs$_2$. Two distinct surface electronic structures are assigned to the As1 and CeAs2 terminations through their characteristic Fermi-surface geometries and termination-specific calculations. On CeAs2, a Dirac surface state associated with the calculated nontrivial bulk topology coexists with a weakly dispersive Ce 4$f$ band near $E_F$. Resonant photoemission identifies the low-energy 4$f$ character and reveals crystal-field-related fine structure in the energy range traversed by the Dirac branch. On cooling, the 4$f$ and Dirac regions both gain spectral weight, whereas a reference conduction-band region changes much less. Together, these observations provide spectroscopic evidence for termination-selective $f$-Dirac hybridization and the development of 4$f$ character in a topological surface state. Our results identify surface termination as a microscopic means of controlling the interplay between Kondo correlations and topological boundary electrons.

</details>


### [4] [Maximally Chiral Eigenstate from Inverse Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15081)
*Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 作者为一维自旋-1/2海森堡链的特定本征态“定制”色散：单向有限速度传播、反向指数小能量，并反演出非局域哈密顿量，使该有序、携带电流的态成为热力学基态，从而呈现发散磁化率斜率与仅按1/ln T衰减的熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过设计相互作用（而非仅描述给定模型）来人为规定量子态的激发谱，从而把“态的设计”与“定向动力学、反常热力学”联系起来。动机在于寻找既有序又携带电流、且在低温下表现出非常规熵与磁响应的可控量子物质方案。

Method: 以自旋-1/2海森堡本征态为目标态，规定其单向有限速度、反向指数小能量的色散谱，逆向重构非局域哈密顿量；通过热力学构造使该有序电流态成为基态；计算磁矩、弱场响应斜率、每自旋熵（含黄金比例系数）；分析自旋响应中的束缚对及其可调能量；再加入交换项与更光滑的模式选择以恢复最低能区的二次激发。

Result: 该态可成为基态且磁矩有限，但弱场响应斜率发散；每自旋熵仅在T→0时按1/ln T消失，系数精确包含黄金比例；自旋响应中出现强度不变、能量可调的束缚对；引入交换与更光滑模式选择后，最低能激发恢复为二次型色散。

Conclusion: 结果表明相互作用设计可同时决定定向动力学与反常热力学，为“谱工程”提供范例；提出动量分辨自旋谱学、弱场磁强计与低温熵测量作为检验手段。

Abstract: We show that an ordered quantum state can support waves with a finite speed in one direction and exponentially small energies in the other. We prescribe this spectrum for a spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg eigenstate and reconstruct a nonlocal Hamiltonian. The thermodynamic construction selects the ordered, current-carrying state as a ground state. Its magnetic moment remains finite while its response to a weak field has a divergent slope. The entropy per spin vanishes only inversely with a logarithm of temperature, with an exact coefficient containing the golden ratio. Our spin-response calculations reveal a bound pair whose energy can be tuned without changing its intensity. Added exchange and smoother mode selection restore quadratic excitations at the lowest energies. These results connect the design of interactions to directional dynamics and unusual thermodynamics. We propose momentum-resolved spin spectroscopy, weak-field magnetometry, and low-temperature entropy measurements to probe these counterintuitive signatures.

</details>


### [5] [Higher-order topology induced by altermagnetism in quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15139)
*Xingmin Huo,Xingchuan Zhu,Xingyu Ma,Shiping Feng,Huaiming Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了Thue-Morse与Ammann-Beenker准晶中d波交替磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合所诱导的高阶拓扑相，发现只有d_{x²-y²}波交替磁能在无平移对称性的体系中打开高阶拓扑能隙并产生零能角态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁（AM）兼具零净磁化与动量依赖自旋劈裂，是探索新奇量子物性的理想平台；近期概念已被推广至准晶，但在缺乏平移对称性的体系中，动量依赖自旋劈裂的物理以及交替磁序如何驱动拓扑物态仍缺乏理解。

Method: 将d波准晶交替磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合，在Thue-Morse与Ammann-Beenker两种准晶格上构建模型，系统比较d_{x²-y²}波交替磁、常规反铁磁与d_{xy}波交替磁三种构型对边缘能隙与高阶拓扑角态的调控差异。

Result: d_{x²-y²}波交替磁在打开边缘能隙、诱导零能高阶拓扑角态方面表现出类似铁磁的行为；而常规反铁磁与d_{xy}波交替磁构型均无法打开高阶拓扑能隙。

Conclusion: 交替磁对称性及其与晶格角方向的匹配在高阶拓扑相的实现中起关键作用；高阶拓扑相的出现可作为在非周期体系中识别交替磁序的拓扑判据。

Abstract: Altermagnetism (AM), a novel magnetic phase featuring zero net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provides a compelling platform for exploring exotic quantum phenomena. While recent studies have extended the concept of AM to quasicrystals, the momentum-dependent spin splitting in the absence of translational symmetry remains inadequately understood. Here, we investigate the emergence of higher-order topological phases (HOTPs) in Thue-Morse and Ammann-Beenker quasicrystals, driven by the coupling between $d$-wave quasicrystal AM orders and a two-dimensional topological insulator. We reveal that the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave AM exhibits a ferromagnetism-like aspect in opening edge gaps and inducing zero-energy higher-order topological corner states. In contrast, conventional antiferromagnetic and $d_{xy}$-wave AM configurations are unable to induce the higher-order topological gaps. These findings highlight the pivotal role of altermagnetic symmetry and its alignment with lattice corners in the realization of HOTPs and establish that the emergence of HOTPs can serve as a signature for identifying altermagnetic orders in aperiodic systems.

</details>


### [6] [Magnetism on emergent molecular-orbital lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15141)
*Zhan Liu,Chenrui Wang,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在ruby晶格紧束缚Hubbard模型中，改变跃迁参数可使涌现的分子轨道晶格由三角格子变为蜂窝格子，从而在同一原子晶格、同一填充下实现阻挫与非阻挫反铁磁性；分子轨道磁序可在低能动态结构因子中通过分子形状因子的零点被实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中邻近原子轨道强杂化形成覆盖原子团簇的分子轨道，其中心构成不同于原子晶格的涌现晶格；部分填充分子轨道上的电子相互作用可能产生局域磁矩来自分子轨道而非单个原子的新型磁性，需明确该磁性的晶格依赖性及其可观测特征。

Method: 采用ruby晶格紧束缚Hubbard模型，调节跃迁参数使涌现的分子轨道晶格由三角格子转为蜂窝格子；并以随机相近似（RPA）计算低能动态结构因子，分析分子形状因子（分子自旋密度的傅里叶变换）对不同布里渊区谱权重及等效动量处谱重比的影响。

Result: 同一原子晶格与同一填充下，随跃迁参数变化可分别出现阻挫与非阻挫反铁磁序；分子形状因子的零点无论分子磁矩呈何种磁序均压低低能谱权重。RPA计算显示，低能积分结构因子在形状因子零点附近出现极小值，且不同布里渊区中选定等效动量间的谱重比与形状因子平方比一致。

Conclusion: 分子轨道磁性是一类由涌现晶格几何决定的可调磁序，其标志性实验特征为低能动态结构因子的形状因子调制与零点压制，可据此在散射实验中区分并验证分子轨道磁性。

Abstract: In solids, strong hybridization between nearby atomic orbitals forms molecular orbitals (MOs), the range of which covers a cluster of atoms. The centers of molecular orbitals form an emergent lattice generally different from the atomic lattice. For partially filled MOs, electron interaction may induce a new type of magnetism on this emergent lattice where the local moments come from the molecular orbitals instead of single atoms. In this work, we show in a ruby-lattice tight-binding Hubbard model that frustrated and unfrustrated antiferromagnetism may appear on the same atomic lattice and at the same filling, as the emergent molecular-orbital lattice changes from triangular to honeycomb upon changing the hopping parameters. Moreover, we show that general molecular orbital magnetism may be experimentally verified in the low-energy dynamic structure factor: the molecular form factors, as the Fourier transforms of the molecular spin-density, modulate the low-energy dynamic structure factor across different Brillouin zones. Zeros of these form factors suppress low energy spectral weight irrespective of the magnetic order formed by the molecular moments. This is exemplified by random phase approximation calculations for the ruby-lattice model: the structure factor integrated over low energies exhibits minima around molecular form-factor zeros, while its ratios between selected equivalent momenta in different Brillouin zones agree with ratios of squared form factors.

</details>


### [7] [Kitaev Exchange and Altermagnetism in the Double Perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$: An Ab Initio Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15152)
*Alexey V. Ushakov,Sergey V. Strelsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，Sr₂CoTeO₆中最近邻存在显著的铁磁Kitaev交换（|K|~J/3），在面心立方晶格上导致强交换阻挫，使其跻身中等Kitaev强度材料之列；同时该化合物是具有可观非相对论性自旋劈裂的交变磁体，可产生横向压磁效应与非常规磁光响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sr₂CoTeO₆因Co²⁺基双钙钛矿的非平庸磁性质受到关注，需澄清其磁交换性质（是否存在Kitaev相互作用）、阻挫程度以及在Kitaev材料谱系中的定位，并探究其磁有序类型与可观测响应。

Method: 采用从头算（ab initio）密度泛函理论计算，提取最近邻交换参数，比较Kitaev项K与海森堡项J的相对强度，并结合面心立方晶格的几何构型分析交换阻挫；进一步计算非相对论性自旋劈裂以判断交变磁（altermagnetism）特征。

Result: 得到最近邻铁磁Kitaev交换|K|~J/3；面心立方晶格与各向异性交换共同造成显著交换阻挫，使该材料处于中等Kitaev相互作用强度区间；同时确认其交变磁基态并具有较大非相对论性自旋劈裂，预言了横向压磁效应和非常规磁光响应。

Conclusion: Sr₂CoTeO₆是一类兼具中等强度铁磁Kitaev交换与交变磁性的双钙钛矿候选材料，阻挫与非相对论性自旋劈裂为其带来压磁与磁光等多重可测响应，为在fcc晶格上探索Kitaev物理与交变磁性提供了新平台。

Abstract: The Co$^{2+}$-based double perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ attracts attention due to its nontrivial magnetic properties. \textit{Ab initio} density functional theory calculations reveal a substantial ferromagnetic Kitaev exchange $|K|\sim J/3$ between nearest neighbors that, combined with the face-centered cubic lattice, leads to pronounced exchange frustration and places Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ among Kitaev materials with moderate Kitaev interaction strength. In addition, Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ is found to be altermagnetic with a sizable non-relativistic spin splitting, which can give rise to a transverse piezomagnetic effect and a nontrivial magneto-optical response.

</details>


### [8] [Multifractal collective order : Application to multifractal superconductivity in dodecagonal quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15446)
*SeHyun Park,Junmo Jeon,SungBin Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出：确定性非周期几何可稳定"多重分形集体序"，并给出一个可同时用于超导、磁性与电荷通道的通用判据；在十二边形氧化物准晶的真实Ti网络上，超导序参量满足全部三项判据，形成具多重分形标度的凝聚体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集体序（超导、磁性、电荷序）的描述通常默认序参量在空间上均匀，但实际电子态可呈强空间非均匀性甚至多重分形性。作者希望厘清：空间非均匀的集体序是否只是无序的附属效应，还是可由确定的非周期几何本身"稳定"出来，并需要一个可操作的判定框架来区分二者。

Method: 建立通用诊断方法：对序参量做直接的标度（scaling）分析，并配合三项判据——(1) 序参量的空间层级结构必须由底层几何决定；(2) 该结构必须出现在主导的不稳定通道（leading ordering instability）中；(3) 在单一平均场序起始点之下、自洽有序态中仍持续存在。随后将该框架应用于实验解析的十二边形氧化物准晶（dodecagonal oxide quasicrystal）的Ti网络，检验其超导通道。

Result: 该Ti网络上的超导性同时满足三项判据：凝聚体在已解析的空间尺度范围内呈现多重分形标度，其层级结构在整个有序相中得以保持，且磁通诱导的平衡电流沿几何锁定的环（geometry-locked rings）被导引。

We establish this by resolving the ground state constraints using a height mapping, which we further relate to a 3-state Potts antiferromagnet. This identifies the pyrochlore's weak AIAO order as permutationally symmetric sublattice (PSS) order, albeit with an exceptionally weak locking strength. We extend the SVM to lattices of corner-sharing octahedra and cubes, where we identify the alternative broken-sublattice-symmetry (BSS) order, a hidden order without magnetic Bragg peaks coexisting with collective paramagnetism. All three models undergo a 3D Ising transition consistent with a symmetry-allowed locking potential in the coarse-grained height theory. The spin liquid persists over intermediate length and temperature scales, but a critical Coulomb ground state ensemble fails to materialize, its correlations cut off by the finite locking length. Using this framework, we obtain two distinct forms for the fermionic self-energy: a conformal-limit solution, $Σ\sim\sqrtω$, and a marginal Fermi-liquid solution, $Σ\simω\log(1/ω)$, corresponding to regimes above and below the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, respectively. At the same time, theoretical literature often lacks consensus about the ground states of quantum spins on even the simplest GF magnetic lattices. We summarize experimental evidence that in the impurity-free limit, available GF magnetic materials that do not develop magnetic order at low temperatures would form {\it a quantum singlet liquid}, i.e. a disordered state of non-magnetic excitations. Abstract: The triangular lattice compound Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ has been recently established as a strong candidate for realizing the spin-nematic physics. Abstract: We investigate an unconstrained $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory coupled to fermions on the triangular lattice at half filling, using auxiliary field quantum Monte Carlo simulations. The triangular geometry gives rise to a global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry that acts as an Ising symmetry on the plaquette fluxes. At weak transverse field $h$, this symmetry is spontaneously broken, stabilizing a vison crystal. We argue that this phase is deconfined: fermions, charged under an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge field, propagate in an ordered $π$-flux background and form a Dirac semimetal. Increasing the transverse field triggers a first-order quantum phase transition to a confined $s$-wave superfluid, at which confinement and global symmetry breaking occur simultaneously. This contrasts with the square lattice, where the corresponding transition is continuous. We extend our analysis to finite temperature and determine the thermal phase boundaries of both ordered phases. The superfluid melts through a Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, whereas the vison crystal undergoes a thermal Ising transition. Together, our results establish the $T \times h$ phase diagram and demonstrate how the triangular geometry leads to qualitative changes in both the phases and the criticality of the model.

Abstract: $O(N)$ models provide a versatile framework for describing continuous phase transitions in a wide variety of physical systems. We investigate an extension of these models arising from order parameters that transform under interlocked internal and spatial symmetries which describe transitions towards non-polar structural order. Such order occurs, for instance, in dipolar antiferromagnets, spin-orbit-coupled magnets, non-polar ferroics, and certain nematic systems. The resulting effective theory is an $O(d)$ model with an additional symmetry-allowed kinetic term $h(\partial_iφ_i)^2$, which modifies the relative stiffness of longitudinal and transverse fluctuations. We study both thermal and quantum phase transitions within this $O(d)$ model using the functional renormalization group (FRG). In contrast to conventional $O(N)$ models, the interlocking of internal and spatial symmetries distinguishes spatial and imaginary-time derivatives, such that the quantum model is not simply obtained from the thermal one by a dimensional extension $d\rightarrow d+1$. We derive and analyze the corresponding FRG flow equations, paying particular attention to the tensor structure of the two-point function, which is essential for consistently extracting the flow of the kinetic terms. Our analysis shows that the additional kinetic coupling is irrelevant at the $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher fixed point in both thermal and quantum cases, where quantum-to-classical correspondence thus re-emerges. However, its small but sizable correction-to-scaling exponent gives rise to substantial corrections to scaling in the vicinity of the critical point.

</details>

We find that discrete translation symmetry, encoded in a nonuniform quantum geometry, affects fractionalized states at filling fractions $ν=1/3$ and $ν=2/3$ differently, and that band mixing can stabilize incommensurate crystalline ground states. The resulting nematic state also leaves distinctive signatures in the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations. At fixed chemical potential, the field-dependent deformation of the Fermi surface can produce substantial changes in the oscillation frequency, even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading oscillation frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. Conversely, the evolving nematic order modifies the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations in ways that depend on the thermodynamic conditions. At fixed chemical potential, the oscillation frequency can change significantly even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. We also study SSPT phases beyond intersection pairings, including those described by subsystem analogues of type-II group cocycles involving the Bockstein homomorphism. On its boundary, the 0-form symmetry acts by stacking with a 2+1d linear SSPT phase, signaling a mixed 't Hooft anomaly.

Abstract: Light can manipulate magnetic order and its excitations on ultrafast timescales, including through resonantly driven phonons. In antiferromagnets, light can also create correlated magnon pairs, or bimagnons. Here we pump the phonon-assisted two-magnon absorption of NiO with mid-infrared pulses, far below its bandgap, and probe the resulting magnetic dynamics at finite momentum with time-resolved resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). Upon photoexcitation, the one-magnon linewidth and energy are essentially unchanged, indicating that the long-range magnetic order is preserved without the photocarrier-induced demagnetization characteristic of above-bandgap excitation. A pump-induced two-magnon signal appears on the energy-gain (anti-Stokes) side of the spectrum. Its spectral weight peaks 4 ps after the pump, well beyond our 0.5 ps time resolution, indicative of a delayed growth likely due to momentum redistribution of the light-induced magnon pairs across the Brillouin zone. This two-magnon signal decays with a time constant of $28\pm11$ ps, exceeding the previously reported two-magnon dephasing time by more than two orders of magnitude. These results establish long-lived light-driven bimagnon correlations beyond the one-magnon state, akin to the higher-order correlations measured in ultracold gases.

Abstract: Electronic Fabry-Pérot interferometers provide a powerful probe of coherent quantum Hall edge transport, yet the presence of multiple spin and valley degrees of freedom in existing van der Waals material platforms can complicate their interpretation. Monolayer WSe$_2$ offers a distinct opportunity because strong spin-orbit coupling and spin-valley locking lift these degeneracies, producing intrinsically single-component Landau levels. Here we realize the first gate-defined quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot interferometer in monolayer WSe$_2$. By continuously tuning the cavity size in a single device, we observe robust Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interference of quantum Hall edge channels across the interferometer; the observed predominance in the AB regime is consistent with a weak bulk-edge coupling ($Δ\approx 0.034$) estimated by electrostatics. Moreover, temperature and bias dependence measurements consistently yield an edge velocity $v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. Our results establish monolayer WSe$_2$ as a clean, flavor-resolved platform for quantum Hall interferometry and provide a foundation for future studies of single-component fractional quantum Hall states and interferometric probes of anyonic statistics in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

Abstract: The phase diagram of the boundary sine-Gordon model (bsG), or equivalently of the resistively shunted Josephson junction, has been the subject of recent debate. We introduce a discrete model that reproduces exactly the properties of the bsG model and can be simulated extremely efficiently using cluster Monte Carlo algorithms. This enables us to explore a broad range of parameters and compute the complete universal crossover between the weak- and strong-tunneling regimes. We establish the self-duality of the model and unambiguously identify the vertical line of critical fixed points characteristic of the Schmid transition, recovering the conformal field theory predictions along this line with high accuracy. We further clarify the essential distinctions between the Schmid and Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. Our results definitively establish the existence and nature of the Schmid transition and demonstrate the validity of the Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario.

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of weakly nonlinear electric and thermoelectric transport through a T-shaped double quantum dot coupled to normal and superconducting leads. Nonlinear screening is treated within the characteristic-potential formalism, in which an internal electrostatic potential accounts for nonequilibrium charge redistribution on the lead-coupled central dot. The side-coupled dot induces the Fano-Andreev effect, which, together with nonlinear screening, significantly modifies the transport properties. Under voltage bias, the screening-induced level renormalization produces a pronounced bias asymmetry in the Andreev differential conductance. In response to a pure thermal bias, increasing the interdot coupling weakens the level renormalization and suppresses the quasiparticle thermoelectric response, leading to reduced thermovoltage and nonlinear Seebeck coefficient. When voltage and thermal biases are applied simultaneously, a finite subgap cross thermoelectric response emerges beyond the linear regime. The sign of this response is governed by the competition between the energy-dependent transport contribution, modified by the Fano-Andreev effect, and the nonlinear screening contribution, thereby determining whether a thermal bias enhances or suppresses the low-bias Andreev current.

Abstract: Valley-selective transport is central to valleytronics, but existing valley filters are largely tailored to specific materials or lattice geometries, motivating the search for a common microscopic ingredient. Here we identify the momentum-space mismatch between the band-touching nodes of a system and its dissipative reservoir as a route to node-selective non-Hermitian skin effects (NHSE). When the two are coupled, the mismatch is encoded in the retarded self-energy, producing node-dependent dissipation and contrasting quasiparticle lifetimes. The resulting effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian exhibits opposite point-gap spectral winding at the two nodes, giving rise to a node-resolved NHSE with strongly asymmetric dissipation rates: one node decays rapidly into the reservoir while the other survives over the device, yielding a nearly valley-polarized outgoing current. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal one-dimensional dimer chain and in two-dimensional systems hosting genuine Dirac valleys, including $π$-flux rectangular and honeycomb lattices. In all cases, transport calculations yield nearly 100\% valley polarization, while remaining gate-tunable and robust against disorder and edge imperfections. Our results show that momentum-space mismatch provides a route to non-Hermitian valley filtering.

Abstract: Frustrated lattices with exactly flat bands and compact localized states provide an ideal setting to uncover the topology of flat bands, leading to intriguing physics in two dimensions.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构手性在磁体中普遍存在，但其对绝缘体自旋输运的影响几乎未被研究，仅有少量近期实验给出初步线索。作者希望厘清螺旋手性在自旋注入与探测过程中究竟作用于何处，并给出可与实验对照的判据。

Method: 理论计算与模型分析：分别针对铁磁与反铁磁螺旋，分析螺旋的螺旋对称性（screw symmetry）对磁振子自旋矩阵对角元的约束，研究本征模的偏振与自旋携带性质，并将自旋注入与探测过程分解为注入界面与探测界面两部分，考察手性反转对磁振子自旋、注入量子相干、注入自旋流及电子-磁振子散射振幅的影响；最后与多铁性BiFeO$_3$中电场切换下的二次谐波电压实验进行对照（原文为first-harmonic，即一阶谐波）。

Result: 螺旋对称性禁止磁振子自旋矩阵出现任何非零对角元，故每个本征模均为线偏振且不携带自旋，自旋输运完全由不同模式之间的叠加中介。反转手性会同时翻转沿螺旋轴的磁振子自旋与注入的量子相干，二者同步反号，从而使相应的注入自旋流保持不变；手性真正起作用的是探测界面：电子-磁振子散射振幅随之反号，使磁振子积累被读出为符号相反的电压。该界面读出机制与多铁性BiFeO$_3$中电场切换下反向的一阶谐波电压一致。

Conclusion: 结构手性可作为磁振子相干性及其电学读出的内禀控制参量；手性对自旋输运的效应主要体现在探测界面的读出符号上，而非注入端，这为绝缘体磁振子学与多铁材料中的手性调控提供了可检验的理论框架。

Abstract: Structural chirality is ubiquitous in magnets, yet its impact on spin transport in insulators remains largely unexplored, with only a few recent experiments offering the first glimpses. Here we predict the chiral response in the electric spin injection-detection configuration in both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic spirals. The screw symmetry of the spiral forbids any nonzero diagonal element of the magnon spin matrix: every eigenmode is linearly polarized and carries no spin, so spin transport is mediated entirely by superposition between distinct modes. Reversing chirality flips the magnon spin along the spiral axis and the injected quantum coherence in lockstep, leaving the associated injected spin current invariant. Spiral chirality acts instead at the detection interface, where electron--magnon scattering amplitude is reversed and causes the magnon accumulation to be read out as a voltage of opposite sign. This interfacial readout is consistent with reversed first-harmonic voltage under electric-field switching in multiferroic BiFeO$_3$, establishing structural chirality as an intrinsic control parameter for magnon coherence and its electrical readout.

Within this model a skyrmion responds only to spatial variations of the coupling, so a uniform layer leaves its position unaffected, whereas boundaries and coverage gradients exert forces on it. The net effect on the mean diffusion is set by the balance between the gyrotropic and dissipative responses of the skyrmion, as we show exactly for one-dimensional landscapes. Stochastic micromagnetic simulations and collective-coordinate calculations confirm these predictions and show how molecular orientation, spatial patterning, and skyrmion properties together determine the motion.

Abstract: Magnetic domain walls (DWs) are central to emerging spintronic technologies for memory, logic and neuromorphic computing, where their controlled displacement is a key functionality. Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases remain a leading cause of mortality worldwide, highlighting the need for rapid, sensitive and reliable detection of cardiac injury at the point of care. This dual-sensor architecture combines molecular recognition, high analytical sensitivity and matrix-aware electrical signal correction, providing a potential pathway towards more reliable FET-based biomarker detection in complex biological fluids. The approach may further enable multiplexed detection of cardiac biomarkers through incorporation of target-specific aptamers, offering potential for broader assessment of cardiac stress and myocardial injury risk.

Abstract: Ferroelectric polarization couples to the layer degree of freedom in a bilayer, but a valley-selective energy response requires the valleys to carry different interlayer character. The resulting contrast converts the band-effective ferroelectric layer asymmetry into a shift of the $K$ edge, and so displaces the $K$--$Γ$ landscape: a finite-field fit that does not impose the rule leaves the $K$ coupling consistent with zero, and the shift reaches \polshiftdft~meV, the most that asymmetry can give a single valley. Reversing the polarization mirrors the valley landscape in displacement field, so the two registries have equal and opposite differential transconductance $g_d$ at zero field. In the ideal symmetric limit a second parity holds about each registry's own compensation field and places a zero of $g_d$ there. The valley-dependent screening calculated here displaces that zero by only \gdnulllow\% at the read point, so device asymmetry limits the accuracy. The result links a known stacking selection rule to ferroelectric valley transduction, with a quantitative valley shift and a directly testable transport signature.

Abstract: Carbon nanotubes (CNTs) are prime nanofluidic conduits and nanopores for studying matter under extreme confinement. Since most synthesis routes yield CNTs with closed ends, opening and filling strategies are required to access their interior for fluid and ion transport, whereas studies of the encapsulated species themselves may additionally require sealing to prevent their escape in dry or vacuum environments. Capacitively coupled local anodic oxidation (LAO) in an atomic force microscope (AFM) has been shown to cut individual CNTs at predefined positions, but whether cutting induces opening, filling, or sealing has remained unclear. Using spatially resolved micro-Raman spectroscopy, we find that LAO cutting surprisingly produces filled CNT segments that remain sealed under dry Ar atmosphere for weeks. The magnitudes of the blueshift and broadening of the innermost-wall radial breathing-like mode are consistent with water filling, pointing to the water meniscus condensed between the AFM tip and the substrate as the source of CNT filling during cutting. High-vacuum annealing at 140 °C releases the encapsulated species from some, but not all, segments, indicating a thermally surmountable barrier rather than re-formed end caps. This one-step cut-fill-seal capability offers a route to diameter-resolved studies of nanoconfined water and aqueous solutions, and to zeptoliter-scale sample delivery for vacuum-based analytical techniques.

Abstract: Phonon hydrodynamics enables collective heat flow beyond the classical diffusive limit and offers a promising route to controlling thermal transport in low-dimensional materials and nanoscale devices. Starting from the Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation, we derive an effective Schrodinger equation for the coupled dynamics of temperature and phonon drift fields, allowing the hydrodynamic regime to be identified directly from material transport properties. Within this framework, a pair of exceptional points quantitatively define the lower and upper boundaries of the hydrodynamic window, providing a critical design criterion beyond the qualitative scattering-rate inequalities proposed by Guyer and Krumhansl six decades ago. Furthermore, we derive an analytical criterion for the existence of hydrodynamic transport, showing that a finite hydrodynamic window can be supported only when the effective thermal diffusivity exceeds the phononic momentum diffusivity. Using ab initio phonon properties as inputs, our theory accurately captures the experimentally observed temperature- and length-dependent ultrafast second-sound dynamics. These results establish a symmetry-based framework for understanding collective energy transport and provide quantitative design principles for identifying the materials, temperatures, and characteristic length scales that support wavelike heat transport in low-dimensional materials and functional devices.

Abstract: Semiconductor InSb nanowires are a major material candidate for topological quantum computation. Moreover, the observation of quasi-ballistic transport, quantum dots, and Kondo correlations demonstrates the coherent quantum transport capability of the MBE-grown InSb nanowires. Our work provides a substrate-independent, self-catalyzed route for the controllable synthesis of high-quality InSb nanowires, establishing a robust material platform for high-performance InSb-based electronic and quantum devices.

We then review the linear dynamics of inertial spin waves, highlighting the coexistence of GHz precessional and THz nutational branches, the inertial redshift of precessional modes, spectral linewidths, and the effects of exchange, magnetostatic interactions, confinement, curvature and torsion. Finally, we address frequency- and time-domain micromagnetic simulation techniques for spatially nonuniform inertial dynamics, including geometric midpoint integrators preserving the fundamental constraints and energy properties of the continuous dynamics. Abstract: Hybrid superconducting-semiconducting circuits provide a versatile platform for investigating coherent transport in mesoscopic weak links. Motivation: 有机半导体因弱自旋轨道耦合、小超精细相互作用、机械柔性与可调电子性质而适于自旋电子学应用，有机自旋阀（OSV）是研究自旋相关输运的代表性平台。但磁阻（MR）响应对铁磁电极/有机半导体界面（spinterface）高度敏感，界面形貌、化学相互作用、能级排布与自旋极化的差异强烈影响自旋注入、输运与探测，导致OSV性能难以复现。因此需要厘清退火等工艺对完整器件层面自旋输运的影响机制。

Method: 构建完整的NDI基有机自旋阀，对比两种顶电极构型：直接蒸发的Co/Au与转移石墨烯（Gr）/Co/Au。对两类器件进行原位热退火处理，比较退火前后的电学与磁输运特性；同时用伴随薄膜表征结构有序度，并用电子-only器件提取有效纵向电导，以区分体相效应与界面效应。

Result: 两类铸态器件均表现出清晰的MR响应，其中转移电极器件的绝对响应更强。原位退火后，两类器件电阻均显著增大，且无法分辨出可复现的MR响应；与此同时，伴随薄膜的结构有序度提高，电子-only器件提取的有效纵向电导提升约36倍。这种体相改善与器件自旋阀性能恶化之间的反差表明：仅靠体相有序度提升不足以保证自旋阀性能增强，退火引起的界面接触变化、接触均匀性与局域电流分布成为决定完整器件响应的主导因素。

Conclusion: 退火诱导的体相有序度与纵向电导提升并不能转化为自旋阀性能改善，界面接触状态与接触均匀性会主导完整器件响应。结果强调了spinterface稳定性与界面工程对于实现可靠、热稳定的OSV工作的决定性作用。

Abstract: Organic semiconductors are attractive for spintronic applications owing to their weak spin-orbit coupling, small hyperfine interactions, mechanical flexibility, and tunable electronic properties. Organic spin valves (OSVs) provide a representative platform for investigating spin-dependent transport. However, the magnetoresistance (MR) response is highly sensitive to the ferromagnetic electrode/organic semiconductor interface (spinterface). Variations in interfacial morphology, chemical interaction, energy-level alignment, and spin polarization can strongly affect spin injection, transport, and detection, making reproducible OSV performance difficult to achieve. Here, we investigate how in situ thermal annealing affects the electrical and magnetotransport properties of complete NDI-based OSVs. Two top-electrode configurations are compared: directly evaporated Co/Au and transferred Gr (graphene)/Co/Au. Both as-cast devices exhibit clear MR responses, with a stronger absolute response observed for the transferred-electrode device. After in situ annealing, the resistance of both device types increases substantially, and no reproducible MR response is resolved, despite the enhanced structural order observed in companion films and an approximately 36-fold increase in the effective vertical conductivity extracted from electron-only devices. This contrasting behavior indicates that improved bulk order alone is insufficient to ensure enhanced spin-valve performance. Instead, annealing-induced changes in interfacial contact, contact homogeneity, and local current distribution can become dominant in determining the complete-device response. These results emphasize the critical role of spinterface stability and interface engineering in achieving reliable and thermally robust OSV operation.

</details>

Result: 现有GaAs样品、弱受限腔（品质因子约10²）下预测输出压缩1.8–2.4 dB；迁移率或腔频适度改善后可达约9 dB；载流子加热通过材料品质因子限制压缩，导出最优腔品质因子与超强耦合强度成反比的设计规则。

Abstract: Two-dimensional electron gases in terahertz cavities are among the most mature platforms for ultrastrong light--matter coupling, yet their collective response is captured by the linear Hopfield model, which has an exact classical correspondence. We show that the intrinsic band nonparabolicity of the electrons, recently revealed through nonlinear diamagnetism, turns this system into a quantum Kerr oscillator that squeezes terahertz light. Using a non-rotating-wave theory with ultrastrong-coupling-consistent input--output, an ab initio cavity bath, and a self-consistent model of electron heating, we predict output squeezing of 1.8--2.4\,dB with present GaAs samples in weakly confined cavities with quality factors of order $10^2$, and up to $\approx9$\,dB with modest improvements in mobility or cavity frequency. Carrier heating sets limits through a material figure of merit, which yields a simple design rule that the optimal cavity quality factor scales inversely with the ultrastrong-coupling strength.

</details>

TL;DR: 将spinterface模型的CISS输运理论推广到自旋分辨耗散，揭示自旋流分解为"无手性/手性"两部分并随铅自旋不对称而解耦，发现精确结构零点及其在非共线铁磁电极下的线性-二次形变层级。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前量子非平衡格林函数形式的spinterface模型（Alwan2026）仅处理自旋简并的电极展宽，未考虑实际电极的自旋分辨耗散（自旋不对称展宽）。本文旨在把该框架推广到自旋分辨铅展宽，以刻画自旋分辨耗散对CISS自旋流各分量的影响及其可调控性。

Result: 随铅自旋不对称增大，无手性分量线性增长，手性分量按几何平均方式受抑制，两部门解耦，且二者峰值出现在不同的耦合尺度上；耦合与偏压最优值是参考构型的工作点而非普适标度律。共线构型下在λ_R sin(φ/2)=t_mol处出现结构零点，手性信号坍缩至机器精度，且跨链长、频率分辨率、扭转角普适成立，其来源是秩一奇异键矩阵的零本征矢将电子困于不跃迁的自旋叠加暗态。非共线铁磁电极下零点被受控破坏：残余量一般线性于横向极化（源于边界处单次相干自旋翻转），在调谐的相消角处两边界贡献破坏性干涉而变为二次，指数从1突跳到2。

Conclusion: spinterface模型可作为一个具有"双轴解耦结构"、非标度性工作最优点以及精确结构零点（其非共线形变为清晰的线性加二次层级）的输运平台；为自旋分辨耗散下CISS效应的定量建模与实验判别提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study the transport sector of the spinterface model of chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) under spin-resolved dissipation, extending the quantum non-equilibrium Green's function formulation of Ref.~\cite{Alwan2026QSpinterface} to spin-resolved lead broadenings. The spin current decomposes into an achiral part, driven by the interfacial moment, and a chiral part, driven by the Rashba spin flip. Under increasing lead spin asymmetry, the achiral part grows linearly while the chiral part follows the geometric-mean suppression, so the two sectors dissociate; they also peak at different coupling scales along the lead broadening. The coupling and bias optima are operating points of the reference configuration, not universal scaling laws. A structural zero appears at $λ_R\sin(φ/2)=t_{\mathrm{mol}}$ in the collinear-lead configuration, where the chiral signal collapses to machine precision universally across chain lengths, frequency resolutions, and twist angles; the zero originates from the rank-one singular bond matrix, whose null eigenvector traps an electron in a spin-superposition dark state that does not hop. With non-collinear ferromagnetic leads, the zero is broken in a controlled way: the residual is generically linear in the transverse polarization, arising from a single coherent spin flip at a boundary, and becomes quadratic at a tuned cancellation angle where two boundary contributions interfere destructively, with the scaling exponent jumping sharply from one to two. These results establish the spinterface model as a transport platform with a two-axis dissociation structure, non-scaling operating optima, and an exact structural zero whose non-collinear deformation is a clean linear-plus-quadratic hierarchy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana 模被视为受拓扑保护的量子信息载体，但其输运特征——零偏压电导峰——可被普通束缚态模仿，导致"是否真正观测到 Majorana 模"长期存在争议。拓扑相变由长导线零能电子反射系数的符号 $Q$ 标记，但 $Q$ 在有限长度器件中究竟"证明"了什么此前并不清楚。本文动机即为厘清 $Q$ 的物理内涵，并为输运实验提供一个可在有限器件中读取的判据。

Method: 从散射理论出发，将零能电子反射系数符号 $Q$ 与复能量平面中"无反射态"（完全透射模式）的数目相联系；在有限长超导量子线中分析 Majorana 模劈裂与其与电极耦合强度的相对大小；针对等耦合接触情形，考察 $Q$ 符号变化点处透射谱的奇异性（奇异点），并给出零温下零偏压电导与 $Q$ 的严格对应关系。

Result: 证明了 $Q$ 精确计数量子线中完全无反射的态数：当复能量平面下半部存在奇数个此类态时 $Q=-1$；有限线中该条件等价于两个 Majorana 模的劈裂小于其与接触的耦合；等耦合时 $Q$ 每次变号（含体拓扑相变）均为两个无反射态合并的奇异点，此时透射最平坦；零温下 $Q=-1$ 当且仅当在接触处测得的零偏压电导超过 $e^2/h$。

Conclusion: 本研究明确了输运测量能在 Majorana 器件中"证明"什么：反射符号 $Q$ 并不单指数个零能峰的形态，而是关联无反射态计数与 Majorana 劈裂-耦合比；零偏压电导是否超过 $e^2/h$ 给出了零温下读取拓扑不变量的直接方法。该结果既澄清了零偏压电导峰判据的模糊性，也为从电导数据提取拓扑不变量提供了可操作的实验方案。

Abstract: Majorana modes, zero-energy excitations of superconducting wires that are their own antiparticles, could store quantum information protected from local noise. In transport experiments, however, ordinary bound states mimic their signature, a conductance peak at zero voltage, and whether Majorana modes have been observed remains debated. A sign, $Q$, of the electron reflection at zero energy marks the topological phase of a long wire, but what $Q$ certifies in a finite device has been unclear. Here we show for the first time that $Q$ counts reflectionless states, waves that enter through one contact and leave entirely through the other: $Q=-1$ when an odd number of them lie in the lower half of the complex energy plane. In a finite wire this means that the two Majorana modes are split by less than their coupling to the contacts. With equal contacts, every sign change of $Q$, including the bulk topological transition, is an exceptional point at which two such states merge and transmission is maximally flat. At zero temperature, $Q=-1$ exactly when the zero-bias conductance measured at a contact exceeds $e^2/h$. These results show what transport can certify about a Majorana device and how to read the invariant from conductance.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出声表面波(SAW)–六方氮化硼(hBN)集成平台，实现hBN表面上DNA的无接触、可调控定向输运。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建SAW-hBN集成平台：hBN提供原子级平整表面使DNA被限域同时保持面内迁移性；SAW作为无接触驱动，不依赖分子自身电学、介电或磁性性质；结合轨迹分析与跳跃分析定量表征输运行为。

Abstract: Developments in single-molecule imaging and sensing techniques have enabled direct observation of biomolecular dynamics at interfaces, which has important implications for the fields of membrane biophysics, nanofluidic sensing and transport. However, integrating active surface transport, localization and control over molecule motion remains challenging, limiting further progress in the realization of single-molecule devices. Here, we introduce an integrated surface acoustic wave (SAW)-hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) platform as a contactless actuation platform for directional transport of biomolecules over hBN surfaces. hBN provides an atomically flat surface on which DNA can be confined and still retain in-plane mobility, whereas SAW actuation enables active and guided transport without requiring specific electrical, dielectric or magnetic properties of the biomolecules. Trajectory- and jump-based analyses show that increasing the SAW power leads to higher displacement bias in the streaming direction, resulting in transport velocities along increasingly more linearized trajectories. We thus demonstrate that SAWs can induce tunable directional transport of DNA molecules confined on hBN surfaces, providing a new approach for the on-chip manipulation of biomolecules in nanofluidic systems. Our findings have important implications for the development of advanced single-molecule sequencing platforms and other technologies for biomolecular manipulation and analysis.

</details>

Result: 获得三端情形下非相互作用费米子统计的精确界，并给出两费米子三端口情形下由六个测量概率组成的四个不等式；将其应用于近期冷原子散射实验，可识别出相互作用对出射粒子有显著贡献的参数区间。

Abstract: Quantum scattering is a fundamental probe of the scattering region and the quantum dynamics of outgoing particles. In two terminals, the statistics of non-interacting fermions is described by a generalized binomial distribution, that arises from the Pauli exclusion principle, while deviations are a signature of interactions. In this work, we extend this result to a scattering region coupled to three outgoing terminals (or channels), obtaining exact bounds for the statistics of the outgoing fermions. In particular, for two fermions and three terminals, four inequalities involving six measured probabilities determine whether a non-interacting scatterer can explain the observed statistics. Applying these inequalities to recent scattering experiments in cold atoms, we are able to identify a regime where interactions have a significant contribution on the outgoing particles. Therefore, our work opens up the possibility of identifying fermion interactions at the nanoscale in systems that range from mesoscopic conductors to optical traps in cold atoms.

</details>

### [66] [Quantitative Gaussian limits of periodic O(N) models at and below the critical temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14570)
*Weiqi Jiang,Pengcheng Hou,Zhiyi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在 d≥3 的零场最近邻 O(N) 模型周期盒上，证明了局域自旋坐标的体积一致高斯逼近：给定空间窗口内前 p≤N 个坐标的非归一化 1-Wasserstein 误差上界为 C√p(N^{-1/2}+s_M)，并给出误差趋于零的充要条件与尖锐阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画有限体积 O(N) 自旋模型中局域自旋分布在临界点及以下的高斯涨落，并量化误差随体积 M、分量数 N 与观测坐标数 p 的依赖关系。

Method: 在周期盒上构造极限球面模型的谱移 s_M，对局域自旋坐标与目标高斯场（独立坐标场，每个为缩放各向异性高斯自由场，临界温度以下附加空间常数高斯项）计算非归一化 1-Wasserstein 距离，并在正方向耦合与逆温度的紧区间上做一致性估计；借助含最近邻星形结构的窗口得到必要条件。

Result: 固定窗口、充分大 M,N 时误差 ≤ C√p(N^{-1/2}+s_M)。含最近邻星的窗口上误差趋零当且仅当 p=o(N) 且 √p·s_M→0；任意周期无穷体积累积态下，任意非空固定窗口误差趋零当且仅当 p=o(N)。d≥5 且逆温度超过临界值固定量时界改进为 C(p/N+√p·M^{-d})。固定 p 时尖锐阶为星形窗口上 N^{-1}+M^{-d}，周期累积态任意非空固定窗口上 N^{-1}。

Conclusion: 局域自旋坐标在高维 O(N) 模型中呈体积一致的高斯行为，误差由分量数比例 p/N 与有限体积谱移共同控制，且所给上界在星形窗口与累积态情形下阶数最优。

Abstract: We prove a volume-uniform Gaussian approximation for local spin coordinates in the zero-field nearest-neighbour $O(N)$ model on periodic boxes of side $M$ in $d\ge3$, at and below the critical temperature of the limiting spherical model. On a fixed spatial window, for sufficiently large $M,N$, the unnormalized $1$-Wasserstein error for the first $p\le N$ coordinates is at most $C\sqrt p (N^{-1/2}+s_M)$, where $s_M\to0$ is the spectral shift of the finite-volume mean spherical model. The estimate is uniform on compact ranges of positive directional couplings and inverse temperatures. The target has independent coordinate fields, each a scaled anisotropic Gaussian free field with an independent spatially constant Gaussian term below criticality.
  On a window containing a nearest-neighbour star (a site and its $d$ positive-direction neighbours), the error tends to zero as $M,N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$ and $\sqrt p s_M\to0$. For any choice of periodic infinite-volume accumulation states, the error on any nonempty fixed window tends to zero as $N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$. In $d\ge5$, when the inverse temperature exceeds its critical value by at least a fixed positive amount, the bound improves to $C(p/N+\sqrt p M^{-d})$. For fixed $p$, the sharp order is $N^{-1}+M^{-d}$ on star-containing windows and $N^{-1}$ on every nonempty fixed window in periodic accumulation states.

Abstract: A low-temperature expansion orders Ising droplets by wall area, but droplets with different transverse spans can occur at the same order. We instead organize the zero-field simple-cubic model by the excess above the minimum wall area compatible with two prescribed spans. We prove that every fixed excess layer, including all logarithmic cover corrections and Euler inversion, has a rational bivariate generating function with denominator factors restricted to 1 - x, 1 - y and 1 - x - y. The proof compresses repeated coordinate cuts into bounded decorations and assigns each unbounded interval to one monotone chain. A finite controller then enforces induced contacts and arbitrary cover-color reuse. We explicitly assemble the complete layers of excess zero through three over all widths, attributing their known geometric constituents to earlier polyomino enumeration. Exact lattice checks corroborate the formulas. A certified low-temperature comparison shows a modest hybrid accuracy gain at one sampling point and higher evaluation cost. The result establishes an exact layer structure; summation over unbounded excess remains a separate problem.

Abstract: The central limit theorem is among the most fundamental principles in the natural sciences, governing the fluctuations of sums of weakly correlated random variables through Gaussian statistics. At critical points, however, long-range correlations invalidate this paradigm, raising a fundamental question: does any general limit theorem survive at criticality? Although scale invariance has long been recognised as the defining feature of typical critical fluctuations, the behaviour of rare fluctuations has remained largely unexplored, as their extraordinarily small probabilities place them beyond the reach of conventional numerical methods. Recent theoretical advances suggest that universal asymptotic laws {may govern large deviations in systems at criticality}, yet direct numerical evidence has been lacking. Here we provide the first such evidence in the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Using large-scale Tethered Monte Carlo simulations, we probe order-parameter fluctuations with probabilities as small as $10^{-200}$ and show that, once normalised by their characteristic width, the far tails collapse onto a single asymptotic law shared by distinct microscopic models in the same universality class. This collapse is governed by a universal amplitude, whose numerical extraction is compatible with the conjectured exact result we derive from conformal field theory, and points towards a generalised central limit theorem for critical fluctuations.

</details>

Conclusion: 拉力可作为调控柔性聚合物结构相图的有效参数，微观正则拐点分析能够灵敏区分转变阶数与中间相，为理解分子力对高分子构象与相行为的影响提供依据。

Abstract: Manipulating polymer chains by means of experimental instruments such as optical tweezers has helped understand better the effects of molecular forces in macromolecular structures. In this study, we simulate the influence of a pulling force on structural phases of a coarse-grained model for flexible polymers using generalized-ensemble Monte Carlo methodologies. The recently introduced microcanonical inflection-point analysis method is employed to identify and classify structural phases of the system. Specific order parameters help characterize the transition behavior in response to the present pulling force. We show that, upon increasing the pulling strength, the coil-globule transition turns from second to first order, then merges with the liquid-solid transition. An intermediate phase, separating the coil and globule phases, is identified for certain force strengths. Clear evidence of the Gough-Joule effect is found as well. Transitions do not occur at all if the pulling force exceeds a certain threshold value.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究非平衡稳态间驱动的总熵产生（总量EP）优化：在给定领头阶豪斯 Keeping EP 预算下，同时优化控制协议与运行时长以最小化过剩EP，将问题映射为被稳态EP速率加权的摩擦度量下的测地线优化，并给出近平衡下总EP的几何下界，在三态ATP驱动钙泵模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动系统在两个非平衡稳态之间切换会同时付出两类热力学代价：改变控制参数引起的过剩熵产生，以及维持稳态流所需的豪斯 Keeping 熵产生。传统的固定时长优化只最小化前者、忽略后者，因而在持续耗散的非平衡稳态系统中给出不完整的优化方案。作者希望把两者纳入统一的资源约束框架。

Method: 在慢速驱动（准静态）区间内，把累积的豪斯 Keeping EP 视为"熵时间"，将原问题转化为在由稳态EP速率加权的摩擦度量下的测地线优化；对协议与其时长联合变分，约束为固定领头阶的豪斯 Keeping EP 预算；并在近平衡极限下推导总EP的几何下界，其由修正的热力学长度给出，最优预算处由能量均分实现。最后对ATP驱动钙泵的三态模型做数值模拟。

Result: 基于资源的几何结构确定了最优路径与调度（schedule）；当稳态仍持续耗散时，豪斯 Keeping EP 与过剩EP之间存在内禀权衡关系。近平衡情形下得到总EP的几何下界，并在最优预算处通过能量均分达到。三态钙泵模型的数值结果显示，与固定时长驱动相比最优协议明显不同，且验证了总EP下界。

Conclusion: 把豪斯 Keeping EP 作为资源预算而非被忽略的常数，可以把非平衡稳态间的驱动优化统一为熵时间加权的测地线问题，给出总EP的几何下界与内禀权衡，为持续耗散的分子机器等系统的有限时间热力学设计提供新准则。

Abstract: Driving between nonequilibrium steady states incurs two thermodynamic costs: excess entropy production (EP) resulting from changing control parameters, and housekeeping EP required to sustain steady currents. Conventional fixed-duration optimization minimizes the former while neglecting the latter. Here, we instead optimize excess EP over both the protocol and its duration subject to a fixed leading order of housekeeping-EP budget, thereby minimizing total EP for that budget constraint. In the slow-driving regime, accumulated housekeeping EP acts as an entropic time, mapping the problem to a geodesic optimization under a friction metric weighted by the steady-state EP rate. This resource-based geometry determines optimal paths and schedules for systems that continue to dissipate even in steady states, establishing an intrinsic trade-off between housekeeping and excess EPs. Near equilibrium, this framework further yields geometric lower bounds on total EP, attained with equipartition at the optimal budget given by a modified thermodynamic length. Numerical simulations of a three-state model for an ATP-driven calcium pump reveal distinct optimal protocols compared to fixed-duration driving, confirming the total-EP bounds.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准粒子图像描述可积系统淬火后的纠缠传播时，依赖"淬火产生准粒子纠缠对"的假设，而该假设仅对所谓可积初态成立。作者希望突破这一限制，考察非可积初态（n-rainbow态）下的纠缠动力学。

Method: 选取由逐渐增长的长程Bell对构成的n-rainbow态（n>1时非可积）作为初态。解析计算局域稳态趋近无限温度态的行为（修正为1/n阶）。在非相互作用XX链中分析多粒子（2n种准粒子）纠缠多重态；在相互作用XXZ链中采用tDMRG数值模拟，考察均质淬火及与无限温度态耦合的情形。

Result: 局域稳态随n→∞趋近无限温度态，修正为O(1/n)。XX链中淬火产生2n种准粒子的纠缠多重态，种间关联以1/n衰减但强烈抑制纠缠熵增长，其增长率随n→∞趋于零，可用推广的准粒子图像刻画。XXZ链的tDMRG显示纠缠熵按切割处热力学熵交换速率增长，在可及时间尺度内未见多重态抑制迹象。

Conclusion: 非相互作用情形下多重态关联显著抑制纠缠增长，需推广准粒子图像；而相互作用XXZ链中该抑制未显现，纠缠熵增长遵循热力学熵交换速率。作者并对n-rainbow态与无限温度态耦合情形提出von Neumann熵的猜想公式。

Abstract: The quasiparticle picture provides a quantitative description of entanglement spreading after quantum quenches in integrable systems. In interacting models, however, it relies on the assumption that the quench creates entangled pairs of quasiparticles, which is justified only for the so-called integrable initial states. Here we go beyond this setting by studying quenches in the XXZ spin chain from the $n$-rainbow states, a family of macroscopically homogeneous states formed by increasingly long-range Bell pairs, which are not integrable for $n>1$. The local stationary state after the quench approaches the infinite-temperature state as $n\to\infty$, with corrections of order $1/n$, as we show analytically. Nevertheless, in the noninteracting XX chain the quench produces entangled multiplets of $2n$ species of quasiparticles. Although interspecies correlations vanish as $1/n$, they strongly suppress the growth of the entanglement entropy, whose rate vanishes as $n\to\infty$. We capture this behaviour within a generalized quasiparticle picture. In the interacting XXZ chain, instead, time-dependent density matrix renormalization group (tDMRG) simulations show that the entanglement entropy grows at the rate at which thermodynamic entropy is exchanged across the cut. Within the time scales accessible to tDMRG, the data show no trace of the multiplet suppression. This holds both after homogeneous quenches and after joining the $n$-rainbow state with an infinite-temperature state, for which we conjecture a formula for the von Neumann entropy.

</details>


### [72] [Cumulant dynamics of the ferromagnetic dipolar XY model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15485)

TL;DR: 本文用二阶截断累积量动力学方法，研究方晶格偶极铁磁XY模型中动力学态的制备，系统尺寸达 L = 60；发现形成具有有限尺度与有限磁化的相干区域，且该区域即使在非绝热但足够缓慢的扫描下也能出现。相干尺度与磁化尺度是扫描常数、初始熵和时间的函数，并对未来大尺寸偶极XY平衡态实验提出严格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极铁磁XY模型是冷原子/极性分子量子模拟的重要平衡态物理平台，但实际制备动力学态受限于有限扫描速率与初始熵；需要理解在非绝热条件下能否、以及以何种空间尺度形成磁化相干区域，从而评估未来大尺寸实验的可行性。

Method: 采用二阶截断累积量（second-order truncated cumulant）动力学近似，在方晶格偶极铁磁XY模型上模拟态制备过程；系统线性尺寸最大至 L = 60；通过扫描常数（ramp constant）、初始熵与时间等参数系统变化，分析相干区域范围与磁化强度两个特征尺度的演化。

Result: 得到一个清晰的物理图像：体系中形成空间有限、磁化有限的相干区域；该结果即使在非绝热（diabatic）但足够缓慢的扫描条件下仍然成立；相干区域尺度与磁化尺度均随扫描常数、初始熵及时间变化，二者为独立但相关的两个标度。

Conclusion: 动力学态制备可在非绝热但缓慢扫描下产生有限尺度的磁化相干区域；由于该双尺度行为对参数敏感，对未来在更大系统尺寸上探测偶极XY平衡态物理的实验提出了较强的实验要求（需控制扫描速率与初始熵）。

Abstract: We study the second-order truncated cumulant dynamics for the preparation of dynamical states in the dipolar ferromagnetic XY model on the square lattice for system sizes up to linear size L = 60. The observations lead to a transparent picture in which a coherent region of finite extent and finite magnetization forms, which is possible even for diabatic but sufficiently slow ramps. These two scales are functions of the ramp constant, the initial entropy of the system, and time. Our findings put strong requirements on future experiments addressing dipolar XY equilibrium physics in bigger system sizes.

</details>


### [73] [Probe Dynamics in Active Baths via Trajectory-Response Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15518)

Abstract: Passive probes in active baths can undergo spontaneous motion driven by nonequilibrium forces that depend on probe velocity and interaction geometry. Using trajectory-response relations that express the bath response to a probe in terms of free-particle displacement statistics, we investigate the dynamics of freely moving probes weakly coupled to ideal active baths. The mean force exerted by the bath defines a velocity-space potential whose minima describe the stable states. By analyzing how the mean force depends on the probe velocity, we identify stable resting and moving states and determine the parameter regime in which they coexist. For elliptical interactions, the resting state becomes unstable first along the narrow axis, resulting in direction selection already at the linear-response level. For fourfold interactions, linear friction remains isotropic, while the nonlinear dependence of the mean force on probe velocity selects axial or diagonal motion and produces directional multistability. We further show that the trajectory-response relations extend to higher orders in the probe strength, where multi-interval free-particle trajectory statistics become relevant. For a Gaussian probe, these higher-order terms shift the speed of the stable moving state.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We present the spin structure factor of the Ising model on the centered pyrochlore lattice. Generalizing Henley's method [Phys. Rev. B 71, 014424] to the case of inequivalent center and vertex spins, we obtain spin correlation functions that are in excellent agreement with Monte Carlo results and can capture sharp and broadened pinch points, also at finite temperature. Our approach can be extended to other centered lattice structures in classical frustrated magnetism.

</details>

Abstract: We study the long-time statistics of spatial moments of the time-dependent energy distribution in a one-dimensional Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti model following an instantaneous release of energy on a single lattice site. The moments are $M_n(T)=\sum_i x_i^n u_i(T)$, where $u_i(T)$ is the energy at site $i$ at the observation time $T$. Employing macroscopic fluctuation theory (MFT), we calculate the variance of $M_n$ for every positive integer $n$ and determine the non-Gaussian distribution tails for the representative cases $n=1$ and $2$. For the first moment, we establish an exact duality, within MFT, to integrated-current statistics for a deterministic flat initial density. For both $n=1$ and $n=2$, the large-moment tails are dominated by a single adiabatically evolving doublon: a rapidly propagating pair of narrow solitary pulses of the energy density and of the gradient of the conjugate field. The small-second-moment tail is associated with concentration of the energy near the origin at time $T$. Comparing single- and two-doublon histories, we predict a dynamical phase transition at a critical value of $M_2$ at which the optimal path breaks reflection symmetry $x\to -x$. Numerical solutions obtained by direct minimization of the MFT action establish the second-order character of this transition and support our analytical predictions. Finally, we extend calculations of the large- and small-moment tails to arbitrary $n$ and numerically find a transition of the same type for $n=4$.

Abstract: Ice formation, a crucial phase transition for life on Earth, mainly occurs through heterogeneous nucleation on a solid substrate. Despite the importance of this phenomenon, the correlation between the structure of the substrate and its ability to induce ice nucleation remains poorly understood. In this work, we use molecular dynamics simulations with an atomistic model of water to investigate how the lattice mismatch affects the ability of the substrate to nucleate ice. To unambiguously quantify the mismatch, we use substrates with a stretched ice structure. We put the substrate in contact with supercooled water and determine the temperature at which ice spontaneously nucleates during the course of the simulations. We find that the nucleation temperature decreases by $\sim$ 5-6 K for each percent by which the substrate lattice is stretched relative to that of ice. This trend is similar to that previously obtained with a coarse-grained water model [Camarillo et al., J. Chem. Phys. 160, 134505 (2024)]. \textcolor{black}{The substrate induces a fluctuating network of subcritical ice-like clusters, from which a spherical-cap-shaped supercritical nucleus eventually emerges stochastically.} Using Classical Nucleation Theory, combine previously reported homogeneous nucleation rates with heterogeneous nucleation rates computed in this work to estimate key heterogeneous nucleation parameters, including the nucleation barrier, the kinetic pre-factor, and the contact angle. The latter is consistent with a microscopic visualization of nucleation trajectories, supporting the applicability of Classical Nucleation Theory to describe a microscopic phenomenon such as heterogeneous ice nucleation.

Abstract: The Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model provides an analytically controlled setting for strongly interacting quantum dynamics and holography, but its fermionic formulation leads to nonlocal encoding overheads on qubit-based quantum processors. We study a U(1)-symmetric spin analogue of the complex SYK model, constructed from random charge-conserving $q$-spin interactions that are native to a qubit Hilbert space. In the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, the model reproduces the leading saddle-point structure of complex fermionic SYK under the identification of chemical potential with magnetic field and charge density with magnetization. We investigate the finite-$N$, finite-$q$ regime using exact diagonalization. A U(1)-invariant ordering susceptibility increases with system size for $q = 2, 4$ but decreases for $q = 6$, while bulk-spectrum level statistics are consistent with the Gaussian orthogonal ensemble at half filling and the Gaussian unitary ensemble in the adjacent magnetization sector. We further construct coupled-cluster chains and obtain domain-wall dynamics consistent with diffusive magnetization transport in a small-cluster regime accessible to matrix-product-state simulations. These results establish analytical benchmarks and finite-size signatures of ordering, quantum chaos, and magnetization transport in U(1)-symmetric spin-SYK models, and they motivate quantum simulation of the low-temperature, large-system regime, which remains difficult for classical methods.

</details>

*Pasquale Casaburi,Pierpaolo Vivo*

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入对称欧氏随机矩阵中的秩一信号回收问题：稠密且几何相关的噪声条目缺乏旋转不变性，信号回收由噪声与信号主方向之间的竞争决定，可呈现不连续的BBP型相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典尖峰矩阵（spiked matrix）理论多假设噪声具有旋转不变性，此时主特征向量回收信号的阈值由BBP相变刻画。但欧氏随机矩阵的条目稠密且随空间位置几何相关，失去旋转不变性，信号可能被编码在次主特征方向上而非主方向上，经典理论失效，需重新刻画回收机制与阈值。

Method: 采用复制法（replica method），将信号回收问题约化为Fredholm型本征值方程；以未扰动能谱边缘、尖峰均值以及主本征函数的空间平均为基本量，推导正回收阈值存在的一般判据；解析求出极限最大本征值、特征向量分量分布与信号重叠度，并对二次距离核与秩二核给出显式解，与数值对角化对照验证。

Result: 证明信号回收由噪声与信号主方向之间的竞争支配；随信号强度增大，竞争本征值发生交叉并改变排序，主特征向量由噪声主导方向突变为承载信号的方向，产生不连续的BBP型相变，重叠度由零跳跃至有限值；给出正阈值存在的一般判据及最大本征值、分量分布、重叠度的解析表达式，二次距离核与秩二核的显式解与数值对角化高度吻合。

Conclusion: 在缺乏旋转不变性的欧氏随机矩阵中，尖峰回收不再遵循连续BBP图景，而由本征值交叉驱动的不连续相变所支配；所建立的Fredholm判据与解析结果将尖峰矩阵理论推广至几何相关噪声情形，并可由未扰动谱边缘与核的空间统计量预测回收阈值。

Abstract: We study the recovery of a rank-one signal embedded in a symmetric Euclidean random matrix, whose dense and geometrically correlated entries generally lack rotational invariance. We show that signal recovery through the leading eigenvector is governed by a competition between the principal directions of the noise and the signal. In particular, the signal may already be encoded in a subleading eigendirection, while remaining undetected by the leading principal component. As the signal strength increases, a crossing between competing eigenvalues can change their ordering, causing the leading eigenvector to switch abruptly from a noise-dominated to a signal-bearing direction. This mechanism gives rise to a discontinuous BBP-type transition, in which the overlap with the signal jumps from zero to a finite value. Using the replica method, we reduce the problem to a Fredholm eigenvalue equation and derive general criteria for the existence of a positive recovery threshold, expressed in terms of the unperturbed spectral edge, the spike mean and the spatial averages of the principal eigenfunctions. We obtain analytically the limiting largest eigenvalue, eigenvector-component distribution and signal overlap, and illustrate the results with explicit solutions for quadratic distance and rank-two kernels, finding excellent agreement with numerical diagonalisation.

</details>


### [79] [Entropy production decomposition and fluctuation theorems under continuous feedback: From prescribed to stochastic protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16070)
*Long Him Cheung*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究反馈控制下熵产生的分解问题：将低通滤波的测量信号D视为随机协议，为粒子位置x与信号D的动力学分别建立涨落定理，并借助信息流解释其含义；在可解谐振模型中把系统映射为自治系统，把测量-滤波过程当作有效热浴，从而将反馈冷却解释为从物理热浴经粒子到较冷有效热浴的热传导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果中，系统受既定协议驱动时熵产生可分解为housekeeping项与非自治项，且各自满足涨落定理；但反馈控制下反馈参数依赖测量结果而非既定协议，原有分解是否成立、如何改变尚不清楚，故需厘清其涨落定理形式与物理意义。

Method: 考虑过阻尼布朗粒子（位置x），以低通滤波的测量信号D作为反馈参数；将每一次D的实现视为一个随机协议，推导与x动力学及D动力学相关的熵产生的涨落定理，并用信息流加以解释；进一步在可解谐振模型中把动力学映射为自治系统，其中测量与滤波过程充当有效热浴；最后用数值模拟验证。

Result: 得到了分别对应x与D动力学的熵产生的涨落定理，并给出其信息流解释；在谐振模型中证明反馈冷却等价于从物理热浴经粒子向更冷的有效热浴的导热，信息流可解释为x与D之间热传导所对应的熵；数值模拟结果与理论一致。

Conclusion: 反馈控制下熵产生分解需以随机协议视角重构，借助测量信号D的动力学与信息流可获得各自的涨落定理；反馈冷却可在自治映射下被理解为向有效冷热浴的导热过程，信息流对应其熵学代价，这为反馈控制热力学提供了统一框架。

Abstract: When a system is driven by a prescribed protocol, the entropy production can be decomposed into the housekeeping and non-autonomous contributions, each satisfying a fluctuation theorem. Under feedback control, however, the feedback parameter depends on measurement outcomes rather than following a prescribed protocol. To study how the decomposition changes, we consider an overdamped Brownian particle at position $x$ with a low-pass-filtered measurement signal $D$ as the feedback parameter. Regarding each realization of $D$ as a stochastic protocol, we derive fluctuation theorems for the entropy production associated with the dynamics of $x$ and of $D$, and interpret them in terms of the information flow. For a solvable harmonic model, we map the dynamics onto an autonomous system in which the measurement and filtering process acts as an effective thermal bath. Feedback cooling thus corresponds to heat conduction from the physical bath through the particle to this colder bath, and the information flow can be interpreted as the entropy associated with the heat conduction between $x$ and $D$. We verify these results by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [80] [Anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior driven by dichotomous Markov noise in a quantum Ising chain: Generalized stochastic resonance in the defect density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16237)
*Mingwei Yang,Peng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在量子Ising链中研究二态马尔可夫噪声(DMN，非高斯色噪声)对反Kibble-Zurek(AKZ)缺陷密度的影响，发现噪声诱导缺陷密度随关联时间呈非单调变化，即广义随机共振，其根源在于慢噪声区DMN实现的稀疏性，是功率谱无法刻画的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有AKZ研究多限于高斯噪声；对高斯色噪声(GCN)，功率谱决定噪声诱导缺陷密度随关联时间单调变化，非高斯色噪声下的行为尚不清楚，需揭示高斯与非高斯噪声在本征非平衡临界淬火动力学中的本质差异。

Method: 以量子Ising链为平台，引入DMN作为典型非高斯色噪声，将噪声诱导缺陷密度视为响应函数，系统考察其随噪声关联时间（快噪声/慢噪声区间）的变化规律，并与GCN的单调预测对比，从DMN实现（realization）的稀疏性角度分析机制。

Result: 噪声诱导缺陷密度对关联时间呈非单调依赖：快噪声区随关联时间增长，慢噪声区随关联时间减小，表现广义随机共振；该共振源于慢噪声区DMN诱导趋势的非单调（反常AKZ）行为，与GCN单调情形相反；机制在于慢噪声区DMN实现稀疏，功率谱无法捕捉此特征。

Conclusion: 工作揭示了高斯与非高斯噪声在量子Ising链非平衡临界淬火动力学中的根本区别：噪声的完整统计结构（而非仅功率谱）决定AKZ行为，DMN可实现广义随机共振。

Abstract: The anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior may arise under noise, adding a noise-induced contribution to the defect density beyond the conventional Kibble-Zurek term. Most investigations of such behavior have so far been restricted to Gaussian noise. For Gaussian colored noise (GCN), the power spectrum dictates a monotonic variation of this term with the correlation time. Here, we focus on dichotomous Markov noise (DMN), a prototypical non-Gaussian colored noise, in the quantum Ising chain. We show that the noise-induced defect density, taken as the response function, exhibits generalized stochastic resonance: it is nonmonotonic in the correlation time, growing in the fast-noise regime and decreasing in the slow-noise regime. This resonance stems from the anomalous AKZ behavior in the slow-noise regime, where the DMN-induced trend is nonmonotonic, in contrast to the monotonic GCN case. The underlying mechanism lies in the sparse realizations of DMN in the slow-noise regime, a feature not captured by the power spectrum. Our work thereby reveals a fundamental distinction between Gaussian and non-Gaussian noises in the nonequilibrium critical quench dynamics of the quantum Ising chain.

</details>


### [81] [Optimal staircase structure for bacterial adaptation in antibiotic landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16325)
*Kento Matsukawa,Shin-ichi Sasa,Namiko Mitarai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在二维格点（物理空间×表型空间）上的随机模型中，固定总空间与浓度范围而改变台阶数，并按端到端首达时间缩放迁移与突变率，发现台阶数增加既加快搜索下一个适应态又增大反复饱和的累积代价，二者竞争可产生中间最优台阶数，Gillespie模拟验证之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗生素环境的空间异质性驱动细菌耐药性演化，但这种环境的"离散结构"（台阶/梯度分段数）如何控制适应所需时间尚不清楚，缺乏定量理论刻画。
