Abstract: Spatial heterogeneity in antibiotic environments drives the evolution of bacterial resistance. However, how the discrete structure of these environments controls the time required for adaptation of bacteria remains unclear. We study this question using a stochastic model on a two-dimensional lattice whose axes represent physical space and phenotype space, comparing different numbers of steps while holding the overall spatial and concentration ranges fixed and scaling the migration and mutation rates by their end-to-end first-passage times. We find that increasing the number of steps accelerates the search for the next adapted state but increases the cumulative cost of repeated population saturation; this competition can produce an optimum at an intermediate number of steps. Direct Gillespie simulations reproduce the predicted dependence of the adaptation time on the number of steps, providing testable predictions for how discrete environmental structure shapes the timescale of evolutionary adaptation.

</details>


### [82] [Information Thermodynamics in Closed Quantum Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16329)
*Yusuke O. Nakai,Shoki Sugimoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为初始处于微观热平衡（MITE）的封闭量子多体系统建立了信息热力学框架，证明在局域测量、局域哈密顿量有限时间控制、且反馈仅依赖邻近测量结果的限制下，可提取功在热力学极限下与正则系综情形一致；并用量子-经典互信息、Holevo信息等量子信息量加以表达；同时指出基于远距离测量结果的反馈可利用MITE中隐藏的长程关联，带来相对于正则系综的可提取功的广延性增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息热力学虽已阐明麦克斯韦妖获取信息如何修正第二定律，但其在多体系统中的表述仍基本空白，因此需要构建适用于封闭量子多体系统的信息热力学框架。

Method: 针对初始处于微观热平衡（MITE）的封闭量子多体系统，在物理上合理的限制下（局域量子测量、由局域哈密顿量生成的有限时间控制）建立信息热力学框架；并额外假设每个局域反馈项仅依赖邻近测量结果，从而给出严格证明。

Result: 严格证明在热力学极限下，可提取功与从相应正则系综可提取的功一致；该可提取功可用量子信息量（量子-经典互信息、正则系综对应的Holevo信息）表达；并证明基于远距离测量结果的反馈可利用MITE中隐藏的长程关联，使可提取功相对于正则系综出现广延性增加。

Conclusion: 局域反馈下MITE多体系统的可提取功在热力学极限下等价于正则系综结果，而利用长程关联的非局域反馈可带来广延性收益，说明多体系统中的信息热力学需同时考虑测量局域性与关联结构。

Abstract: Although information thermodynamics explains how the second law is modified due to the information acquired by Maxwell's demon, its formulation in many-body systems remains largely open. Here, we develop an information-thermodynamic framework for closed quantum many-body systems initially in microscopic thermal equilibrium (MITE), under physically motivated restrictions: local quantum measurements and finite-time controls generated by local Hamiltonians. By additionally assuming that each local feedback term depends only on nearby measurement outcomes, we rigorously prove that the extractable work coincides in the thermodynamic limit with that extractable from the corresponding canonical ensemble. We further express this extractable work in terms of quantum-information quantities, including the quantum-classical mutual information and the Holevo information for the corresponding canonical ensemble. We also show that feedback based on distant measurement outcomes can exploit long-range correlations hidden in MITE, yielding an extensive increase in extracted work relative to the canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [83] [Continuous-Time Random Walks with Internal Stochastic Renewal Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16383)
*Boris Z. Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"结构化密度"的连续时间随机游走（CTRW）框架：粒子每次跳跃时其内部状态或加一、或归零，内部状态按更新过程演化，由此可导出多种积分-微分方程，并统一刻画反常扩散等行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统（时间-空间分数阶）Fokker-Planck方程常被用于同时刻画长等待与大步长的耦合反常扩散，但缺乏一个统一的微观机制来源；作者希望在单一结构化密度表述下把记忆核、重置、等待时间与跳跃分布纳入同一框架，从而系统揭示微观机制与宏观非局部方程之间的联系。

Method: 构造带内部状态的CTRW：跟踪粒子状态，跳跃时内部状态以一定概率加一或重置为零（更新过程）。由结构化密度推导一族积分-微分主方程；对特定重置概率、等待时间与跳跃分布显式求取"模式依赖"的记忆核及其连续极限；并给出若干特例：可还原时间-空间分数阶Fokker-Planck方程；设计等待时间按几何级数递减、在有限时刻趋于平衡分布的过程；以及在每个状态的等待时间中引入自激发与自抑制，得到均方位移∝t^ν（任意ν>0）。

Result: 该框架可恢复时间-空间分数阶Fokker-Planck方程；给出有限时刻收敛到平衡分布的过程（几何递减等待时间序列）；给出对任意ν>0具有MSD∝t^ν的自激发/自抑制过程；并显式得到多种模式依赖记忆核及其连续极限。

Conclusion: 结构化密度CTRW框架统一了多种非局部宏观方程，清晰建立了微观机制（重置、等待、跳跃、自激发/自抑制）与宏观非局部（分数阶、记忆核）方程之间的对应关系，为构造具有定制反常扩散指数的模型提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-time random walk process via a structured density by tracking the state of the particle as the system evolves. Whenever the particle makes a jump, its internal state either increases by one or resets to zero, so that the internal state evolves according to a renewal process. A wide range of governing integro-differential equations may be obtained from this formulation, some of which exhibit various types of anomalous behaviour. For certain choices of reset probabilities, waiting times and jump distributions, we explicitly derive the mode-dependent memory kernels and investigate their continuum limits. The framework can recover the time-and-space-fractional Fokker-Planck equation, which traditionally models the interplay between long wait times and large jumps and exhibits anomalous diffusion. Furthermore, we obtain a process which converges toward the equilibrium distribution as time approaches some finite value through a sequence of waiting times which decrease geometrically. Finally, we investigate a process that incorporates self-excitation and self-inhibition into the waiting times for each state to produce a mean square displacement proportional to $t^ν$ for any $ν> 0$. Through these examples, we illustrate the link between the microscopic mechanisms in these processes and their non-local macroscopic governing equations.

</details>


### [84] [Freezing transition and shock-like transients in ballistic random walks under threshold resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16733)
*Rahul Das,Satya N Majumdar,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维弹道随机游走者在阈值重置下的瞬态动力学，揭示速度分布对弛豫过程的空间依赖性与异常慢弛豫等非平衡特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈值重置下的稳态已知为均匀分布且与速度分布φ(v)无关，但系统趋于稳态的瞬态弛豫行为及其对φ(v)的依赖尚未被系统研究。

Method: 解析求解一维弹道游走者在阈值重置下的瞬态传播子与弛豫率，针对不同φ(v)类别（含幂律分布与带尖锐截断的分布）进行分析，并用蒙特卡洛模拟验证。

Result: 弛豫率显式依赖空间坐标（不同于常规位置无关的弛豫）；超过临界位置后弛豫率冻结为常数（"冻结转变"）；幂律速度分布导致代数型异常慢弛豫；φ(v)的尖锐截断在传播子中产生向阈值行进的激波型不连续，跳变高度随时间衰减。模拟与解析高度吻合。

Conclusion: 速度涨落虽不改变稳态分布，却深刻影响瞬态空间结构与弛豫速率，展现丰富的非平衡动力学行为。

Abstract: We investigate the transient dynamics of a one-dimensional ballistic random walker under threshold resetting (TR). The walker starts at $x_0>0$, moves towards a threshold at the origin with a velocity drawn from a distribution $φ(v)$, and on reaching the threshold is reset to $x_0$ with a new sampled velocity. Whenever the mean first-passage time to the threshold is finite, the walker reaches a steady state that is uniform on $[0,x_0]$ and independent of $φ(v)$. The relaxation towards it, in contrast, is highly sensitive to the behavior of $φ(v)$. For a large class of $φ(v)$-s, we find, rather remarkably, that the relaxation rate depends explicitly on the spatial coordinate, in contrast to conventional relaxation phenomena, where the rate is typically position-independent. Furthermore, this space-dependent relaxation rate eventually freezes to a constant, position-independent value beyond a critical position, a phenomenon we identify as \textit{freezing transition}. Additionally, for a power-law velocity distribution, the system exhibits an anomalously slow, algebraic relaxation towards the steady state. We also showed that a sharp cutoff in $φ(v)$ generates a shock-like discontinuity in the particle propagator that travels towards the threshold, with a jump height that decays with time. These results demonstrate how velocity fluctuations, despite leaving the steady state unchanged, can profoundly influence the transient spatial structures and showcase rich non-equilibrium behavior. Our analytical results are corroborated by Monte Carlo simulations, showing an excellent agreement.

</details>


### [85] [Work extraction from a single negative-temperature bath: a temperature gradient-driven heat flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16743)
*Wolfgang Rudolf Bauer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文通过引入"大量弱耦合工作系统构成的虚拟集合"并以其香农/信息熵定义正则温度，调和了从负温浴中"唯一功提取"与热力学第二定律(开尔文-普朗克表述)的矛盾：负温浴其实是"高温热源"，而工作集合温度更低，故过程等同于常规热流，完全合法；该框架还自然解释了负温浴中"负载越大力提取越多"的"驴子行为"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从热浴中单纯提取功而不引起净状态变化，违反开尔文-普朗克表述的第二定律；但对负绝对温度热浴，这一禁令可被绕过，从而引发关于负温系统热力学稳定性的长期争论。作者希望在不牺牲第二定律的前提下，重新诠释负温浴中的"唯一功提取"现象，并给出可推广到分子尺度机器的解释框架。

Method: 不将提取装置视为孤立单机，而设想一个由大量弱耦合工作系统构成的"虚拟集合"(assembly)；以该集合的香农/信息熵定义其正则温度(canonical temperature)；再比较该集合温度与负温热浴温度，从信息与统计力学角度分析热流方向。

Result: 给出了若干典型示例，其中工作集合的正则温度低于负温热浴温度。因此"唯一功提取"等价于从高温系统(负温浴)向低温系统(工作集合)的常规热流，与第二定律完全一致；该框架还自然解释了"驴子行为"，即增大负载反而增强功提取的反直觉现象。

Conclusion: 对负温热浴的"唯一功提取"并非违反第二定律，而是被信息/统计力学框架下的温度定义所容纳的常规热流；将工作系统视为大集合、并用信息熵定义温度，可将热力学论证自然延伸至分子尺度机器，并统一解释负温环境中负载与功提取的反常关系。

Abstract: Work dissipation into a thermal bath is standard, whereas the reverse process -- sole work extraction from a thermal bath without a net state change -- contradicts the Kelvin-Planck formulation of the second law of thermodynamics. However, this prohibition is bypassed for baths at negative absolute temperature, sparking ongoing debate regarding their thermodynamic stability. Here, we reconcile the second law with sole work extraction from a negative-temperature bath by considering a large virtual assembly of weakly coupled working systems rather than an isolated machine. By assigning this assembly a canonical temperature derived from its Shannon/information entropy, the framework naturally extends to molecular-scale machines. We demonstrate paradigmatic examples where the assembly's canonical temperature is cooler than the negative-temperature bath. Consequently, sole work extraction represents standard heat flow from a hot system (the negative-temperature bath) to a cooler system (the working assembly), rendering the process fully consistent with the second law. This informational and statistical-mechanical framework also naturally explains the counter-intuitive phenomenon of "donkey behavior", where increasing the workload in a negative-temperature bath leads to enhanced work extraction.

</details>


### [86] [Detecting non-Hermitian topology from long-time dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16745)
*Antonio D'Abbruzzo,Rosario Fazio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种从协方差矩阵弛豫动力学测量手征对称开放高斯系统Lindbladian谱的非厄米拓扑不变量的协议，该方法在长时间区间有效且不依赖孤立边缘模，适用于Liouvillian趋肤效应遮蔽边缘模的情形，并在含储库诱导非互易性的Kitaev链上验证可区分所有相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放高斯系统的非厄米拓扑不变量通常由Lindbladian谱定义，但实验/数值上依赖识别边缘模；当Liouvillian趋肤效应使边缘模被体态遮蔽或非局域化时，基于边缘模的探测方案失效，需要一种不依赖边缘模、可在长时间弛豫过程中稳定读取拓扑信息的方法。

Method: 构造基于协方差矩阵长时间弛豫行为的测量协议：利用手征对称性将协方差矩阵弛豫模式与Lindbladian谱的拓扑不变量关联，从弛豫过程的（长时）动力学量提取拓扑不变量，无需隔离边缘模。以含储库诱导非互易跃迁的Kitaev链为具体模型，先解析求解其拓扑相图（此前未有完整解析描述），再用该协议做数值检验。

Result: 给出该非互易Kitaev链完整解析的拓扑相图（此前未知），并证明所提协议能够在存在Liouvillian趋肤效应的情况下可靠区分体系中所有不同的拓扑相，且工作于长时间区间。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵弛豫可作为开放高斯系统非厄米拓扑不变量的稳健探针，摆脱对边缘模隔离的依赖，对趋肤效应鲁棒；为开放量子系统中拓扑相的动力学诊断提供了可操作方案。

Abstract: We propose a protocol to measure the non-Hermitian topological invariant associated with the Lindbladian spectrum of chiral-symmetric open Gaussian systems from the relaxation of the covariance matrix. Importantly, the method works in the long-time regime and does not rely on isolating edge modes, so it remains applicable even when the Liouvillian skin effect may obscure them. We illustrate the technique on a Kitaev chain with reservoir-induced nonreciprocity, for which we first obtain a fully analytical and previously unknown description of its topological phase diagram. We then show that our protocol reliably distinguishes all the different phases.

</details>


### [87] [A deterministic theory of kinetic fluctuations in long-range interacting homogeneous systems without collective effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16893)
*Anwar El Rhirhayi,Jean-Baptiste Fouvry,Julien Barré*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文指出在无集体效应的均匀系统中，有限 $N$ 涨落的完整统计可由三条确定性动理学方程封闭求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限 $N$ 涨落支配长程相互作用系统的慢弛豫，但完整统计需要大量昂贵的 $N$ 体模拟，计算代价过高。作者希望用少量确定性方程替代大规模系综模拟。

Method: 从 Landau-Dean-Kawasaki 方程（Landau 方程大偏差原理的随机对应）出发，在 $1/N$ 领头阶导出经验分布函数 $n$ 点矩的无穷层级，并证明存在准高斯闭合使其精确可解，得到"极小 Landau 涨落层级"：Landau 方程、两点矩的 Lyapunov 方程、三点矩的动理学方程。

Result: 该层级精确保持碰撞不变量及其涨落，并给出中心涨落区可观测量完整概率分布；所有高阶矩可由三式代数推出。与 $N$ 体实现对比，均值、方差及高阶矩的精度随 $N$ 系统性提升。

Conclusion: 对任意大 $N$，三条耦合偏微分方程的单次确定性时间积分即可替代大规模系综模拟。

Abstract: Finite-$N$ fluctuations govern the slow relaxation of long-range interacting systems, yet accessing their full statistics demands huge ensembles of costly simulations. We show that, in homogeneous systems without collective effects, this statistical problem collapses onto just three deterministic kinetic equations. Starting from the Landau-Dean-Kawasaki equation, i.e. the stochastic counterpart of the large deviation principle for the Landau equation, we derive the infinite hierarchy of $n$-point moments of the empirical distribution function at leading order in $1/N$. We prove that it is solved exactly by a quasi-Gaussian closure. The resulting minimal Landau fluctuation hierarchy is formed by the Landau equation, a Lyapunov equation for the two-point moment, and a kinetic equation for the three-point moment. From these three equations, all higher-order moments follow algebraically. The hierarchy exactly preserves the collisional invariants and their fluctuations, and predicts the full probability distribution of observables in the central fluctuation regime. Benchmarked against $N$-body realizations, it reproduces the mean, variance and higher-order moments with an accuracy that improves systematically with $N$. A single deterministic time-integration of three coupled partial differential equations thus replaces large ensembles of simulations, for any large $N$.

</details>


### [88] [Discrete Time Crystals from Anti-Unitary Floquet Driving and Dynamical Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17333)
*Anirban Das,Asmi Haldar,Arnab Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出用反幺正周期驱动经“动力学冻结”直接稳定离散时间晶体，磁化为涌现守恒量，二周期准能级精确配对锁定次谐响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间晶体（DTC）的次谐响应通常易受加热与退相干破坏，需寻求更直接、鲁棒的稳定化机制。

Method: 采用反幺正（anti-unitary）周期驱动构造动力学冻结（DF）机制：由DF产生的有效哈密顿量保持准局域性，并分析二周期准能级配对结构与磁化算符的涌现守恒性；辅以无限链数值模拟检验通用态的长时演化。

Result: 磁化表现为涌现守恒算符；两周期准能级的精确配对将次谐响应锁定；任意有限磁化的通用态在无限链模拟中无可探测的衰减；并给出该反幺正驱动的实验实现方案。

Conclusion: 反幺正周期驱动结合动力学冻结为DTC提供了一条无需复杂调控即可稳定化的直接路径，且准局域有效描述与无衰减动力学具备实验可行性。

Abstract: We show anti-unitary periodic drive paves a direct path for stabilizing a Discrete Time-Crystal by Dynamical Freezing (DF). The magnetization appears as an emergent conserved operator, and an exact pairing of two-cycle quasi-energies pins the subharmonic response. DF keeps the effective Hamiltonian $\textit{quasi-local}$; infinite-chain simulations show no detectable decay for a generic state with any finite magnetization. We outline an experimental realization of the anti-unitary drive.

</details>

### [89] [Susceptibility Bottlenecks in Equilibrium Propagation Using Oscillator Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14727)
*Malihe Farasat,Oshada Rathnayake,Nikhil Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究振荡器伊辛机(OIM)中平衡传播(EP)学习信号如何由振荡器动力学决定，指出隐藏层响应可分解为输出端微扰力与跨层磁化率两因子，并表明仅有目标能量地貌不足以实现有效物理学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统可利用自由态与弱扰动平衡态之间的位移实现学习（EP），但振荡器动力学如何调控学习信号的传递尚不清楚，需明确其物理机制。

Method: 将隐藏层响应分解为输出端施加的nudging力与支配其向隐藏层传递的磁化率；构建相互作用可在相位差耦合与相位和耦合之间连续插值的二部OIM，并保证所有耦合形式在二值锁相态下对应同一伊辛哈密顿量；结合解析推导与数值仿真分析二次谐波注入与耦合形式的作用。

Result: 二次谐波注入主要通过相位二值化影响nudging力；耦合形式则通过能量地貌的混合曲率控制跨层磁化率。在等比例混合时，混合曲率在二值流形上消失，隐藏层学习信号被强烈抑制；数值仿真验证了该机制及其对分类性能的影响。

Conclusion: 实现目标能量地貌本身不足以保证有效的物理学习，系统还必须支持学习所需扰动的有效传递。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) enables learning in physical systems by exploiting the displacement between free and weakly perturbed equilibrium states. Here, we investigate how the underlying oscillator dynamics govern the transmission of learning signals in oscillator Ising machines (OIMs). We show that the hidden-layer response can be decomposed into two distinct factors: the nudging force applied at the output and the susceptibility governing its transmission to the hidden layer. Using a bipartite OIM with interactions interpolating between phase-difference and phase-sum coupling, we investigate these factors while ensuring that all coupling forms realize the same Ising Hamiltonian at binary phase-locked states. We demonstrate that second-harmonic injection primarily influences the nudging force through phase binarization, whereas the coupling form controls the cross-layer susceptibility through the mixed curvature of the energy landscape. At equal mixing, the mixed curvature vanishes on the binary manifold, strongly suppressing hidden-layer learning signals. Numerical simulations validate these mechanisms and reveal their consequences for classification performance. Our findings establish that realizing a desired energy landscape alone is insufficient to ensure effective physical learning; the system must also support the transmission of perturbations required for learning.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Ground States: Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians for p-Bit Invertible Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14744)
*Tian Feng,Xutang Fan,Yiyun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 将p-bit可逆逻辑的哈密顿量设计建模为逆统计力学问题：用约束极大似然（含剪枝与重拟合）在最高四阶相互作用池中拟合能量，使其Gibbs分布在每个钳位案例的合法态上趋近均匀，同时以能量间隙下限保证合法态能量均低于非法态；每个能量在全部案例上精确审计，并以九种调优采样器重启下的求解时间计分。实验表明学习型编码在更新次数与工作量上普遍优于手写编码，FPGA实现比特精确且周期更少（但查找表最多增至2.4倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统p-bit可逆逻辑构造只关注基态集合与较宽的最小能量间隙，但有限时间采样器会遍历激发态，导致钳位条件下的采样效率与可靠性不足；因此需要一种直接针对"采样行为"而非仅"基态成员性"的能量设计方法。

Method: 把哈密顿量设计表述为逆统计力学：拟合含至四阶相互作用的能量，使各钳位案例合法态的Gibbs分布趋于均匀，并设能量间隙下限（所有合法态低于所有非法态）。采用固定项池上的约束极大似然，配合项剪枝与重拟合，每个输出能量在所有案例上做精确审计。以重启下达到经校验解的时间为评分，取九个调优采样器中的最优者。另将系数舍入到最窄精确整数表示，在一枚FPGA color-sweep SSA核心上与软件比特精确对齐运行。

Result: 欠定模式下，学习乘数在4×4至7×7分别解释手写编码更新量的0.55、0.44、0.52、0.46 [0.38, 0.56]；7×7以下各规模均更快且无显著工作量惩罚。学习加法器在8比特反向模式用0.71的更新量，24与32比特约四分之一，32比特工作量为0.55。5×5同等条件对照：最大化间隙拟合相对手写编码为0.79 [0.73, 0.88]（更新）与1.26（工作），而似然拟合仅为该间隙拟合更新与工作量的0.55 [0.46, 0.58]与0.58 [0.48, 0.61]。FPGA上学习乘数在4×4用0.41周期、7×7用0.057 [0.045, 0.068]周期，查找表最高为手写的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需电路的合法赋值集合即可自动设计能量，在欠定/反向等模式下显著降低收敛更新与计算工作量，且可硬件落地；但在确定模式下手写编码更省工作量，加法器自12比特起手写编码更新次数更少，说明方法优势依赖于问题欠定程度而非普适替代。

Abstract: Probabilistic-bit (p-bit) invertible logic encodes a Boolean relation as an energy whose ground states are its legal assignments, computing in any direction by sampling. Traditional constructions target ground-state membership and a wide minimum energy gap, yet finite-time samplers traverse excited states. We cast Hamiltonian design as inverse statistical mechanics: fit an energy with interactions up to order four whose Gibbs law approaches uniformity on each clamp case's legal states, under a gap floor keeping every legal state below every illegal one. Constrained maximum likelihood with pruning and refitting fits terms from a fixed pool, and every delivered energy is audited exactly on all cases. Each energy is scored by time to a verified solution under restarts with its best of nine tuned samplers. In underdetermined modes, learned multipliers factor in 0.55, 0.44, 0.52 and 0.46 [0.38, 0.56] of the hand-written encodings' updates from 4x4 to 7x7, faster within every family below 7x7 at no resolved work penalty; learned adders solve the reverse mode in 0.71 of the updates at 8 bits and about a quarter at 24 and 32 bits, at 0.55 of the work at 32 bits. At 5x5 under equal pool, budget and floor, a gap-maximizing fit reaches 0.79 [0.73, 0.88] in updates and 1.26 in work against the hand-written encoding, while likelihood fitting takes 0.55 [0.46, 0.58] and 0.58 [0.48, 0.61] of the gap fit's updates and work. Rounding coefficients to the narrowest exact integer words tested, we run both energies at each pair's common word on one FPGA color-sweep SSA core, bit-exact with software: learned multipliers take 0.41 of the hand-written encoding's cycles at 4x4 and 0.057 [0.045, 0.068] at 7x7, at up to 2.4 times its lookup tables. The method requires only a circuit's legal assignments; in determined modes, hand-written encodings take less work, and from 12 bits on the adder fewer updates.

</details>

*Francisco Belchí,Nahuel Statuto*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文用0维持久同调（条形码/持续景观速度）分析交错磁化关联，考察其相对常规响应的增益：可无阈值定位干净临界点与混合序线，重入区仅能部分恢复，并能从标记合并树中恢复强无序RG团簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在横纵场反铁磁Ising链中，既有连续临界点、混合序转变线，又在淬火无序下出现无穷无序不动点与重入序。作者希望明确0维持久同调对交错基态关联的刻画，能比常规观测量多提供哪些信息（尤其是无阈值判据与多尺度层级结构）。

Method: 采用精确自由费米子解与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）。计算交错基态关联矩阵的0维持久同调：条形码记录单链接（single-linkage）层级的合并尺度；以持续景观速度的最大值定义无阈值伪临界点；并在重入区将过滤限制在有序尺度上；结合标记合并树与强无序重整化群（SDRG）团簇比较。

Result: 持续景观速度极大值定位干净临界点与混合序线的精度与常规响应相当；全局速度在重入区误置进入点，仅在限制过滤尺度后可恢复，而退出点无任何被测估计量能定位。边界条件强制的畴壁抑制了无序平均的重入瓣。对单个无序实现，标记合并树可恢复多数强无序RG团簇（含格点受限层级遗漏的非连续团簇），且无序越强一致性越高。

Conclusion: 相关信息存在于完整关联矩阵中，而0维持久同调提供了一个稳定、无阈值的层级描述，可作为常规响应的补充判据；但重入区的进入/退出定位仍受尺度选择限制。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields exhibits a continuous critical point, a mixed-order transition line and, with quenched disorder, an infinite-disorder fixed point and reentrant order. Using exact free-fermion solutions and the density-matrix renormalization group, we examine what the zeroth persistent homology of staggered ground-state correlations adds to conventional responses. Its barcode records the merge scales of the single-linkage hierarchy, and the maximum of its persistence-landscape velocity defines a threshold-free pseudocritical point. This point locates the clean critical point and the mixed-order line with an accuracy comparable to conventional responses. In the reentrant regime, the global velocity misplaces the entry, which is recovered only by restricting the filtration to the ordering scale, and none of the tested estimators locates the exit. A domain wall forced by the boundary conditions suppresses the disorder-averaged reentrant lobe. For individual disordered realizations, the labelled merge tree recovers most strong-disorder renormalization-group clusters, including noncontiguous ones missed by lattice-restricted hierarchies, with increasing agreement at stronger disorder. This information resides in the full correlation matrix, while zeroth homology provides a stable, threshold-free hierarchy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典力学系统中实现可直接成像的SSH拓扑模型，以便实验研究拓扑简正模及无序（质量扰动）对边界态的影响。

Result: 拓扑截断支持局域于边界的带隙中态，呈现有效偶长SSH链所预期的交替子晶格结构；质量变化使边缘模频率劈裂，并产生相对无无序预测不对称的模态分布。

Conclusion: 该可宏观直接成像的平台为研究经典力学系统中的拓扑简正模与无序效应提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We report a reconfigurable macroscopic mechanical realization of the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model: a suspended chain of alternating masses coupled by identical compression springs. The relevant SSH degree of freedom is the relative longitudinal displacement between neighboring masses. In this bond-coordinate basis, applying a constant shift and rescaling of the mechanical dynamical matrix maps a chain of $N$ blocks onto an (N-1)-site SSH chain. Using video tracking, we reconstruct bond-resolved spectra and mode profiles and compare them with predictions for an ideal finite SSH chain. The topological termination supports mid-gap modes localized near the boundaries, with the alternating-sublattice structure expected for an effective even-length SSH chain. Mass variations split the edge-mode frequencies and produce asymmetric mode profiles relative to the non-disordered predictions. This directly imageable platform enables studies of topological normal modes and disorder effects in a classical mechanical system.

</details>


### [93] [Physical interpretation of spin-glass models with correlated disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16765)
*Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文讨论了具有特定关联键无序的Ising自旋玻璃模型：其键构型由Edwards-Anderson对称±J模型的一个平衡自旋构型经规范变换得到，等价于"反向的Mattis模型"。其铁磁相本质是隐藏的自旋玻璃相，磁化即与参考构型的重叠，从而把温度混沌与磁场混沌问题约化为铁磁序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作用这类关联无序Ising自旋玻璃模型，把自旋玻璃相中的温度混沌、磁场混沌等性质与铁磁序联系起来；作者旨在阐明该关联无序的物理含义、采样构造及其与Mattis模型的对偶关系，从而为自旋玻璃问题提供新视角。

Method: 采用规范变换构造：先在同对称±J分布的Edwards-Anderson模型中抽取一个平衡自旋构型作为参考构型，再取使该构型变为全上态的规范，所得键构型即关联无序模型（对应Oshima–Arai–Hukushima的数值采样方案）。并与Mattis模型作对偶比较；进一步引入附加参数γ的推广，分析规范等价类的概率分布与类内分布。

Result: 关联无序模型的阻挫统计与对称±J模型完全相同；在含参数γ的推广中，规范等价类的概率仅由γ决定，而抽取参考构型所处的逆温度只决定各类内部的分布。模型磁化等于与参考构型的重叠。借助这一对应，其铁磁相研究给出了Edwards-Anderson模型自旋玻璃相中温度混沌与磁场混沌的意外结论。

Conclusion: 关联无序模型与Mattis模型构成"铁磁体伪装／自旋玻璃伪装"的对偶结构，为自旋玻璃相变与混沌现象提供了新的等价描述视角，并已被证明是导出高度非平凡结果的有效途径。

Abstract: Ising spin-glass models with a specific type of correlated bond disorder have recently been used to relate properties of spin-glass phases, such as temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos, to ferromagnetic order. This correlated disorder has a simple physical interpretation, which underlies the sampling procedure introduced by Oshima, Arai, and Hukushima for numerical simulations: the bond configuration is obtained from the Edwards-Anderson model with the symmetric $\pm J$ distribution by drawing one of its equilibrium spin configurations and writing the model in the gauge in which this reference configuration becomes the all-up state. The construction is analogous to the Mattis model, with the roles of the ferromagnet and the spin glass reversed: whereas the Mattis model is a ferromagnet in disguise, the ferromagnetic phase of the correlated model is essentially a spin-glass phase in disguise. The frustration statistics of the correlated-disorder model are exactly those of the symmetric $\pm J$ model, and, in a generalization with an additional parameter $γ$, the probabilities of the gauge equivalence classes are determined by $γ$ alone, whereas the inverse temperature at which the reference configuration is drawn determines only the distribution within each class. The magnetization of this model is the overlap with the reference configuration. Through this correspondence, studies of its ferromagnetic phase have led to unexpected results on temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase of the Edwards-Anderson model. Models with correlated disorder have thus provided a new perspective on spin glasses and have proved fertile ground for highly nontrivial results on the spin-glass problem.

</details>

*V. Sokolenko,A. I. Krivchikov,A. Jeżowski,V. Boiko,O. A. Korolyuk,D. Szewczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色峰（低频振动模式相对德拜预测的过量）通常与结构无序相联系，而有序分子晶体中也出现类似热容异常，但其对分子结构的依赖及与热输运的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 选取六种多环芳烃晶体（芘、并四苯、苝、并五苯、晕苯、碗烯），联合低温热容测量、低频拉曼光谱与热导率测量进行系统对比；对热容过量用无形状参数的上余弦（raised-cosine）标度函数拟合。

Result: 对具有清晰可分辨极大值的化合物，热容过量的上升侧符合无自由形状参数的上余弦标度函数，而衰减侧依赖具体化合物；可分辨晶格模拉曼带的数量与宽度沿系列变化，但指认受谱背景与分辨率限制；热导率从类晶体到类玻璃行为均有分布；五种平面化合物中可分辨出激活型贡献，而碗烯数据在当前测量灵敏度下无需该贡献描述。

Conclusion: 类玻璃的热学与振动特征并非非晶材料独有，而与分子结构和晶体堆积差异相关；但本研究本身不足以将堆积确认为唯一因果变量。

Abstract: The boson peak is an excess of low-frequency vibrational states over the Debye prediction and is commonly associated with structural disorder. Similar heat-capacity anomalies also occur in ordered molecular crystals, but their dependence on molecular structure and their relation to heat transport remain incompletely understood. Here we compare six polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) crystals -- pyrene, tetracene, perylene, pentacene, coronene and corannulene -- using low-temperature heat capacity, low-frequency Raman spectroscopy and thermal conductivity measurements. For compounds with well-resolved maxima, the rising side of the heat-capacity excess is consistent with a raised-cosine scaling function with no adjustable shape parameter, whereas the decay is compound-dependent. The number and widths of resolved lattice-mode Raman bands vary across the series, although their assignment is constrained by spectral background and resolution. Thermal conductivity ranges from crystal-like to glass-like behaviour. An activated contribution is resolved in the five planar compounds but is not required to describe the corannulene data within the sensitivity of the present measurements. These results show that glass-like thermal and vibrational signatures are not unique to amorphous materials and are associated with differences in molecular structure and crystal packing; they do not, by themselves, isolate packing as the sole causal variable.

</details>


### [95] [Cup and cap synchronization: higher-order node dynamics from topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16979)
*Runyue Wang,Yuanzhao Zhang,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文结合代数拓扑与非线性动力学，提出"Cup and Cap Synchronization"统一理论框架，用于研究高阶节点型同步动力学，揭示四面体及更高阶相互作用对同步相变临界性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有高阶节点型同步模型能产生不连续同步相变，但缺乏能统一涵盖现有模型并揭示三角形以外高阶相互作用作用的理论框架。

Method: 将代数拓扑（上积/下积运算）与非线性动力学结合，构建能自然纳入底层高阶网络拓扑组织的高阶节点型动力学框架。

Result: 该框架可重现现有两类高阶Kuramoto型模型的临界普适类（其不同临界行为此前被认为源于不同机制）；同时展现超出既有模型的新现象，尤其是四体相互作用能根本改变同步相变的临界性质，产生仅含边与三角形相互作用的模型无法观测的集体行为。

Conclusion: 高阶相互作用不应止于三体项，需超越三体（三角形）研究；该框架为理解高阶网络集体动力学提供统一理论基础。

Abstract: Higher-order node-based synchronization provides distinct mechanisms for discontinuous synchronization transitions. However, a comprehensive theoretical framework capable of encompassing existing models and revealing the role of higher-order interactions beyond triangles is still missing. Here, combining algebraic topology and nonlinear dynamics, we introduce Cup and Cap Synchronization, a unifying theoretical framework for higher-order node-based dynamics that naturally incorporates the topological organization of the underlying higher-order network. We show that Cup and Cap Synchronization can display critical behavior belonging to the universality classes of two existing higher-order Kuramoto-like models, whose distinct critical behaviors were thought to originate from different mechanisms. Remarkably, we demonstrate that Cup and Cap Synchronization displays additional phenomenology not captured by the previously proposed models of higher-order node-based synchronization. Most relevantly, the inclusion of four-body interactions can fundamentally alter the critical properties of the synchronization transition, giving rise to collective phenomena that cannot be observed in models including only interactions mediated by edges and triangles. These findings reveal the importance of going beyond the commonly studied three-body terms and establish Cup and Cap Synchronization as a unifying theoretical framework for understanding collective dynamics in higher-order networks.

</details>


### [96] [Statistics of Fock-space correlation lengths in the many-body localised phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17306)
*Bibek Saha,David E. Logan,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文针对无序量子系统多体局域相中Fock空间本征态振幅的强非均匀性，建立基于两点Fock空间关联函数的微观定量理论；通过对无序倾斜场Ising链的精确对角化，揭示典型关联指数衰减与平均关联次指数衰减的鲜明对比，并指出其源于关联长度的分布效应与高维超立方Fock空间几何的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域相中存在长程多体共振，表现为Fock空间本征态振幅的强非均匀性，但缺乏定量、微观的理论刻画；同时典型（typical）量与平均（average）量之间行为差异的起源及量子多重分形对称性异常违反的机制尚不清晰。

Method: 以两点Fock空间关联函数为核心构建微观理论；对无序倾斜场Ising链进行精确对角化，分别测量典型关联与平均关联；引入Fock空间关联长度的分布，分析其方差随系统尺寸反比标度的统计性质；并结合超立方Fock空间图的高维几何解释典型与平均行为的定性差异；进而推导多体局域本征态的奇异谱。

Result: 精确对角化显示：典型关联呈完美指数衰减，而平均关联呈次指数衰减，且由稀有共振节点导致系统性尺寸依赖；该现象源于关联长度分布，其方差与系统尺寸成反比；该分布与高维超立方Fock空间几何的结合，导致典型与平均行为的定性分离。理论还给出多体局域本征态的奇异谱，并从理论上确立量子多重分形对称性异常违反是Fock空间几何的内在结果。

Conclusion: Fock空间关联长度的分布统计与高维超立方图几何共同决定了多体局域相中关联的典型—平均二分性；该框架为Fock空间中的多体共振与量子多重分形提供了统一的定量描述，并表明多重分形对称性异常违反是Fock空间几何的固有推论。

Abstract: The presence of long-ranged, many-body resonances in the many-body localised phase of disordered quantum systems is manifest in strong inhomogeneities of eigenstate amplitudes on the Fock space. We develop a quantitative, microscopic theory for this, centred on two-point Fock-space correlation functions. Exact diagonalisation of the disordered tilted-field Ising chain uncovers a stark contrast: typical correlations decay perfectly exponentially, whereas average correlations exhibit subexponential decay with systematic system-size dependence driven by rare resonant nodes. We show that this behaviour stems from an underlying distribution of Fock-space correlation lengths with a variance that scales inversely with system size. Crucially, the interplay of this distribution with the high-dimensional geometry of the hypercubic Fock-space graph, leads to the qualitative difference between the typical and average behaviours. The theory also allows us to derive the singularity spectrum of the many-body localised eigenstates, and to establish theoretically the anomalous symmetry violation of quantum multifractality as an inherent consequence of the Fock-space geometry.

</details>

### [97] [Higher-Order Exceptional Point Sensing with Rydberg Micro-Ensemble Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14893)
*Jun Zhang,Ya-Jun Wang,Zi-Han Li,Jin-Hang Fang,Luan-Yi Wei,Qing Li,Guang-Can Guo,Bang Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Dong-Sheng Ding,Bao-Sen Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在冷里德堡气体中构建空间结构化微系综阵列，利用长程相互作用与空间连通性扩展有效非厄米维度，实现可调控的高阶奇异点，并用于超越标准量子极限的微波传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶奇异点源于多个本征值与本征态的合并，对弱扰动具有增强的谱响应；但在相互作用多体系统中，多个本征态之间耦合复杂，实现与调控高阶简并仍具挑战性。

Method: 构建空间结构化的冷里德堡气体微系综阵列，借长程相互作用与空间连通性提升有效非厄米维度；通过改变格点空间排布实现不同阶数的非厄米奇异点；分析里德堡激发位点间相互作用对量子费舍尔信息的增强作用。

Result: 高阶奇异点呈现随阶数增强的非线性相边界响应，并可通过测量时间与原子数调控；通过追踪相边界位移，获得0.47(10) nV cm⁻¹ Hz⁻¹ᐟ² 的灵敏度，优于标准量子极限。

Conclusion: 里德堡微系综阵列可作为连接高阶非厄米临界性、多体动力学与微波传感的平台。

Abstract: Higher-order exceptional points arise from the coalescence of multiple eigenvalues and eigenstates, leading to enhanced spectral responses to weak perturbations. However, realizing and controlling such higher-order degeneracies in interacting many-body systems remains challenging due to the complex coupling among multiple interacting eigenstates. Here, we construct spatially structured microensemble arrays in cold Rydberg gas, where long-range interaction and spatial connectivity enlarge the effective non-Hermitian dimensionality, enabling higher-order exceptional points. By varying the spatial arrangement of these sites, we realize different orders of non-Hermitian exceptional points. We further show that interactions between Rydberg excitation sites can enhance the quantum Fisher information, thereby improving sensing sensitivity. The resulting higher-order exceptional points exhibit increasingly nonlinear phase-boundary responses, which can be further tuned by varying the measurement time and atom number. By tracking shifts of these phase boundaries, we achieve a sensitivity of 0.47(10) nV cm-1 Hz-1/2, surpassing the standard quantum limit. Our results establish Rydberg microensemble arrays as a platform connecting higher-order non-Hermitian criticality, many-body dynamics, and microwave sensing.

</details>


### [98] [Observing Autler--Townes splitting of an rf-dressed Feshbach resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15016)
*Samyajit Gayen,Matthew Chilcott,Chongwu Xie,Niels Kjærgaard*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用射频缀饰场在磁Feshbach共振附近调控散射长度的方案首次得到实验实现：在偶极阱中^{87}Rb的1007 G共振上观测到Autler–Townes分裂，并演示了射频开关碰撞响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ding、D'Incao和Greene（PRA 95, 022709, 2017）提出用射频缀饰场调控Feshbach共振附近散射长度的理论方案，但其所需强射频磁场的可行性及弹性性质尚未经实验检验。

Method: 在偶极阱中囚禁^{87}Rb，将1007 G Feshbach共振对应的准束缚分子态与自旋投影不同的更深束缚态通过射频场耦合；以原子损失为观测量，扫描射频频率与失谐检验Autler–Townes分裂，并在远离裸共振的固定磁场下切换射频以观测碰撞响应的开关效应。

Result: 观测到1007 G Feshbach共振的Autler–Townes分裂；在固定磁场下实现射频对碰撞响应的开关控制。原子损失增加与散射长度实部幅值增大所导致的三体复合增强一致。

Conclusion: 实验证实了产生足够强射频磁场以实现分子缀饰的可行性，为后续检验射频调控相互作用所预言的弹性本质奠定了基础。

Abstract: We experimentally implement the scheme introduced by Ding, D'Incao, and Greene [Phys. Rev. A {\bf 95}, 022709 (2017)] for controlling the scattering length near a magnetic Feshbach resonance using an rf dressing field. Specifically, we observe Autler--Townes splitting of the 1007 G Feshbach resonance in dipole-trapped $^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$ by coupling the associated quasibound molecular state to a deeper-lying bound state with a different spin projection. At a fixed magnetic field away from the bare resonance, we further demonstrate rf switching of the collisional response. Our experimental observable is atom loss, which is consistent with enhanced three-body recombination arising from an increase in the magnitude of the real part of the scattering length. Our observation of Autler--Townes splitting establishes the feasibility of generating the strong rf magnetic fields required for sufficient molecular dressing and provides an important basis for further experimental tests of the predicted elastic nature of the rf-controlled interactions.

</details>


### [99] [Engineering many-body tunneling dynamics of one-dimensional bosons with finite-core power-law interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15504)
*Abhik Kumar Saha,Cesare Vianello,Andrea Trombettoni,Barnali Chakrabarti*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文用MCTDHB研究一维玻色-约瑟夫森结中有限核幂律相互作用V_α(r)=g/(r^α+d^α)对隧穿动力学的影响，揭示相互作用空间结构是调控相干振荡、退相位、复现与隧穿压制的独立控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多集中于接触相互作用或固定长程形式的双模隧穿，缺乏对相互作用"空间结构"（幂律指数α与短程截断d）这一自由度的系统考察；同时需明确两模描述（扩展Bose-Hubbard模型）在多体、强相互作用下的适用边界。

Method: 研究对象为一维盒形势阱中带中心势垒的超冷玻色子玻色-约瑟夫森结，相互作用为有限核幂律形式V_α(r)=g/(r^α+d^α)。采用玻色子多组态含时Hartree方法（MCTDHB）进行全多体数值模拟，系统扫描相互作用强度g、指数α、短程尺度d、势垒高度及初始布居不平衡；并与两模扩展Bose-Hubbard模型（EBHM）对比，以最低轨道占据情况判断两模近似的有效性。

Result: 弱相互作用下，增大α可使系统从约瑟夫森振荡过渡到退相位并出现复现-重相位；较高势垒抑制复现；较大初始不平衡促进向近零布居差的弛豫。增大相互作用强度逐步压制布居转移涨落，且α越大越显著。EBHM在各阱最低轨道主导时能准确描述动力学，但当更高轨道被占据后精度下降；强相互作用区出现多体空间结构，需多个自然轨道才能收敛，超出两模描述范畴。

Conclusion: 相互作用核的空间剖面与强度、势垒高度及初态构成一组彼此独立的控制参数，可分别实现相干、退相位、复现及被抑制的多体隧穿。通过Rydberg dressing实现软核相互作用，为在超冷原子中开展"相互作用工程化隧穿"研究提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate the many-body dynamics of a one-dimensional Bose-Josephson junction of ultracold bosons in a box-like potential with a central barrier, interacting via finite-core power-law interactions $V_α(r)=g/(r^α+d^α)$, where $d$ sets the short-distance scale and $α$ controls the spatial dependence. Using the multiconfigurational time-dependent Hartree method for bosons (MCTDHB), we explore how the interaction profile and strength, barrier, and initial population imbalance shape tunneling dynamics. At weak interaction, increasing $α$ drives a crossover from Josephson oscillations to dephasing and recurrent rephasing, demonstrating the importance of the interaction kernel's spatial structure. A higher barrier suppresses recurrences, whereas a larger initial imbalance promotes relaxation toward nearly vanishing population imbalance. Increasing interaction strength progressively suppresses population-transfer fluctuations, particularly at larger $α$. The two-mode extended Bose-Hubbard model captures the dynamics when the lowest orbital in each well dominates, but loses accuracy as additional orbitals become populated. In the strongly interacting regime, many-body spatial structures develop and several natural orbitals are needed for converged dynamics, placing the system beyond a two-mode description. Our results establish the interaction profile and strength, barrier, and initial state as distinct control parameters for coherent, dephasing, recurrent, and suppressed many-body tunneling of one-dimensional bosons. The ability to tailor soft-core interactions via Rydberg dressing offers a promising route to exploring interaction-engineered tunneling in ultracold atoms.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum Cost of Emptiness in a One-Dimensional Bose Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16247)
*Bin Huang,Ziyan Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究排斥Lieber-Liniger气体热力学基态中半径为R的空区间概率P0(R)的大R渐近渐近行为，给出完整的普适展开式，并通过静电狭缝映射与辅助散射回返谱解析确定各阶系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空区间概率探测量子多体体系密度涨落的远尾，是刻画强关联一维玻色气体大尺度关联与边界效应的核心可观测标度，此前缺乏对排斥Lieb-Liniger气体（任意γ）远尾的精确解析刻画，尤其缺乏平方根项与对数项系数及常数项的来源理解。

Method: 在固定γ=c/ρ>0下取ℓ=ρR→∞的渐近分析：对辅助场行列式作精确Gaussian完备化，将主导集体响应约化为静电狭缝问题，用椭圆积分求解f(γ)；分析偶极极化尾所驱动的辅助散射回返，其局域密度饱和而活跃rapidity区间按√R扩张；联合完整回返谱与占据rapidity端点定出对数项系数；借助contact恒等式讨论边界稠密愈合尺度与涌现经典场描述。

Result: 得到−log P0 = f(γ)ℓ² + B(γ)√ℓ − (1/4)logℓ + O_γ(1)，无线性项，且B=2ζ(3/2)√(f/(πγ))；f(γ)以椭圆积分闭式给出；√ℓ项源于活跃rapidity区间的平方根扩张所致的边界代价；对数系数精确为−1/4。

Conclusion: 空区间概率的远尾由集体静电型响应主导，具有无线性项的ℓ²+√ℓ特殊标度结构；边界处形成逐渐增宽的稠密愈合区，其特征占据数增长使得即便体相为强相互作用，边界仍可用涌现经典场描述。

Abstract: The probability $P_0(R)$ of an empty interval of radius $R$ probes the far tail of density fluctuations in the thermodynamic ground state of the repulsive Lieb--Liniger gas. Here $ρ$ is the bulk density and $c$ the repulsion. As $\ell = ρR \to \infty$ at fixed $γ= c/ρ> 0$, we find $-\log P_0 = f(γ)\ell^2 + B(γ)\sqrt{\ell} - \frac{1}{4}\log\ell + O_γ(1)$, with no linear term and $B = 2ζ(3/2)\sqrt{f/(πγ)}$. Exact Gaussian completion of the auxiliary-field determinant reduces the leading collective response to an electrostatic slit problem, yielding $f(γ)$ in elliptic integrals. Its dipole polarization tail drives auxiliary scattering returns whose local density saturates while the active rapidity range expands as $\sqrt{R}$, producing the square-root boundary cost. The full return spectrum and the occupied-rapidity endpoints together fix the $-1/4$ logarithmic coefficient. Through the contact identity, widening the void probes a dense healing scale at the boundary whose growing characteristic occupation supports an emergent classical-field description even in a strongly interacting bulk.

</details>

### [101] [Circuit-aware protection against burst errors in lattice surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14583)
*Jaebum Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在模拟的d=9格点手术CNOT中，按"每次merge所测校验类型"放置gap engineering保护，相比深几何带布局可将burst引发的超额风险降低约38%，且以更少受保护ancilla达到浅带参考水平；d=13时该优势在oracle解码下不显著，五轮burst上限会反转浅带排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子计算需抵御同时扰动多个超导qubit的高能冲击（burst error）。gap engineering可抑制此类冲击导致的弛豫，但只能保护部分qubit，因此必须决定保护位置。格点手术中随着patch合并/分裂，测量与ancilla角色、时间边界不断变化，静态布局如何选取缺乏依据。

Method: 构造并比较三种静态保护布局（受保护ancilla数量与X:Z校验类型分配相匹配），在模拟的格点手术CNOT上进行评估：d=9与d=13；在相关背景（correlated background）下匹配，且不给解码器提供impact参数；另以oracle解码检验，并施加五轮burst上限做稳健性检验。

Result: d=9时，保护每次merge期间所测校验类型的"typed"布局相对深几何带，超额风险降低38^{+6}_{-5}%；并估计可少保护49^{+2}_{-14}个ancilla而达到浅带参考风险。机理归因于时间边界处的report error与ancilla故障向data的传播共同抬升浅带布局的超额风险。d=13时优势在oracle解码下不确定，五轮burst上限使浅带排序反转。

Conclusion: 逻辑电路的测量调度可指导gap engineering保护位置的选取，但该收益依赖距离、解码器（是否oracle）与burst时长范围，在测试范围内成立而非普适结论。

Abstract: Large-scale quantum computing will require logical operations protected against high-energy impacts that disturb many superconducting qubits at once. Gap engineering suppresses relaxation induced by these impacts, but protecting only a subset of qubits requires choosing where that protection is placed. During lattice surgery, measurements change as patches merge and split, exposing different ancillas and temporal boundaries to burst errors. Here we compare three static protection layouts for a simulated lattice-surgery CNOT, matching the numbers of protected ancillas and the X:Z check-type splits. At distance 9, protecting the check type measured during each merge reduces burst-induced excess risk by $38^{+6}_{-5}\%$ relative to a deep geometric band under correlated background matching, without giving the decoder the impact parameters. We attribute this difference to report errors at temporal boundaries and ancilla faults propagating to data, which interact to increase the excess risk of a shallow band. We also find that the typed layout reaches the shallow-band reference risk with an estimated $49^{+2}_{-14}$ fewer protected ancillas. At distance 13, the advantage is not resolved under oracle decoding, and a five-round burst cap reverses the shallow-band ordering. These results indicate how the measurements of a logical circuit can guide protection placement within the simulated decoder and burst-duration regimes tested here.

</details>


### [102] [Controlled preparation and unconditional transport on a fluctuating quantum graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14588)
*Xianbin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在给定的方形候选格点"单探针"模型中，将Lieb–Robinson型谱传播控制与无条件读出结合，证明（假定提供相干图基态）可制备并以严格正群速度填充孤立支，使指定接收区无条件下界到达概率≥5/8，计入局域联合态与哈密顿量误差后仍保持≥9/16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb–Robinson界只限制量子信息传播速度，却不能保证给定输入能以可观测概率被制备并传输到读出端；在边涨落的量子图上，还需同时控制图相干性、有限边界与制备误差，并在不依赖全局多体态接近性或图测量后选择的前提下给出可观测读出下界。

Method: 在给定的方形候选格点单探针模型上，组合广义Rokhsar–Kivelson母哈密顿量、谱约化、绝热制备与局域动力学估计；假设已提供相干图基态，构造有限源波包与hopping ramp，使其以严格正群速度填充孤立支；并用显式局域联合态与哈密顿量误差界，配合精确算术证书与独立小系统数值诊断进行定量分析。

Result: 在足够大的有限开放方形中，指定接收区的无条件到达概率可证不低于5/8；引入局域联合态与哈密顿量的显式误差界后，下界仍为9/16，且不需要全局多体态接近性或对图测量做后选择；精确算术证书与小系统数值诊断支持该定量结论。

Conclusion: 该构造为涨落量子网络中的输运与读出提供了模型专属基准，把传播机制与可观测下界直接关联，并给出保持该下界所需的局域误差预算，从而区分"抑制不良影响"与"保证信号送达"。其局限在于资源估计偏保守、需假定图基态可得，且不确立无背景几何或引力动力学。

Abstract: Lieb--Robinson bounds limit the spread of quantum information, but do not establish that a prescribed input can be prepared and transmitted with appreciable probability. For quantum graphs with fluctuating edges, connecting spectral propagation to unconditional readout also requires control of graph coherence, finite boundaries, and preparation errors. We address this problem in a one-probe model on a prescribed square candidate lattice, combining a generalized Rokhsar--Kivelson parent, spectral reduction, adiabatic preparation, and local dynamical estimates. Assuming a supplied coherent graph ground state, we construct a finite-source wave packet and a hopping ramp that populate an isolated branch with strictly positive group velocity. We prove an unconditional arrival probability of at least $5/8$ in a specified receiver region of a sufficiently large finite open square. Explicit bounds on local joint-state and Hamiltonian errors retain a lower probability of $9/16$, without requiring global many-body state closeness or postselection on graph measurements. Exact arithmetic certificates and a separate small-system numerical diagnostic support the quantitative analysis. The construction provides a model-specific benchmark for transport and readout in fluctuating quantum networks, linking a propagation mechanism to an observable lower bound and identifying the local error budgets required to preserve it. It distinguishes suppression of unwanted influence from guaranteed signal delivery, a useful separation for quantum-network and emergent-geometry models. The resource estimates are conservative, graph-state supply is assumed, and the result does not establish background-free geometry or gravitational dynamics.

</details>


### [103] [Probing Krylov Dynamics in Driven Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14611)
*András Grabarits,Adolfo del Campo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了时间依赖的多体Krylov问题，给出标度/旋转不变体系在驱动下扩展复杂度的闭式表达，并连接到可实验测量的可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov复杂度是研究算符增长、扰乱与量子混沌的核心探针，但Krylov构造假定生成元固定、在哈密顿量随时间变化时失效；而实验室量子体系通常被时变驱动，这一矛盾阻碍了Krylov复杂度的实验研究。

Method: 对现有量子模拟与操控平台中实现的标度不变与旋转不变体系，精确且以闭式求解时间依赖的多体Krylov问题：建立将驱动动力学映射到相互正交Krylov子空间的精确关系，导出仅由初始哈密顿量谱性质与驱动时序决定的闭式扩展复杂度；并将构造推广至Liouville空间，求解时间依赖的密度矩阵Krylov问题。

Result: 得到闭式扩展复杂度：在标度不变的量子气体（驱动Lieb-Liniger模型、Tonks-Girardeau气体、Calogero-Sutherland模型）中，复杂度由原子云宽度及其变化率决定，可通过原位与飞行时间成像直接获取；在旋转不变自旋体系（Haldane-Shastry模型、中心自旋模型）中，复杂度由集体自旋进动支配，可在囚禁离子、腔QED与电路QED平台探测读出。

Conclusion: 时变驱动下的多体Krylov复杂度可被精确求解并具有简洁闭式结构，从而把Krylov动力学（含初始热态的密度矩阵情形）转化为现有平台上可直接测量的量，为量子混沌与扰乱实验研究提供可操作途径。

Abstract: Krylov complexity has become a central probe of operator growth, scrambling, and quantum chaos. Yet, a basic fact hinders its experimental study: laboratory quantum systems are routinely driven in time, whereas the Krylov construction assumes a fixed generator and breaks down once the Hamiltonian varies. Here, we solve the time-dependent many-body Krylov problem exactly and in closed form for scale- and rotation-invariant systems realized in current quantum-simulation and control platforms. We find exact relations that map the driven dynamics onto mutually orthogonal Krylov subspaces and yield a closed-form spread complexity, determined solely by the spectral properties of the initial Hamiltonian and the driving schedule. The construction extends to Liouville space and solves the time-dependent density-matrix Krylov problem, which makes the Krylov dynamics of initial thermal states experimentally accessible. In scale-invariant quantum gases, such as the driven Lieb-Liniger model, the Tonks-Girardeau gas, and the Calogero-Sutherland model, the complexity is set by the width of the atomic cloud and its rate of change, directly accessible via in situ and time-of-flight imaging. In rotation-invariant spin systems such as the Haldane-Shastry and central-spin models, it is governed by collective spin precession that can be probed and read out in trapped-ion, cavity-QED, and circuit-QED platforms.

</details>


### [104] [Squeezed Light Generation by Cooper Pair Recombination in a Superconductor-Semiconductor Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14622)
*Sima Buchbinder,Avi Koriat,Alex Hayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论证明：在超导-半导体混合结构中，利用库珀对复合的非线性光-物质相互作用，可在紧凑的电驱动器件中产生压缩光；压缩强度正比于超导能隙平方与器件长度，并随外加电压增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩光产生方法多为光学泵浦、体积较大，难以集成；作者希望探索电驱动、紧凑可集成的压缩光产生机制，因此关注库珀对复合这一电学过程对光子态的正交压缩效应。

Method: 基于二阶微扰理论构建有效哈密顿量，将相互作用的电子部分处理为光子耦合强度系数，从而建立描述库珀对复合对光子态正交压缩作用的模型；并在实际器件长度、材料与偏压条件下计算压缩度。

Result: 压缩强度与超导能隙的平方及器件长度成正比，并随外加电压增大而增强；在临界温度以下、采用实际器件长度、材料与偏压时，计算所得压缩值可与现有压缩光产生方法相当。

Conclusion: 该理论方案表明电泵浦压缩光产生具有可行性，为在紧凑集成结构中实现压缩光、推动集成量子光学器件铺平道路。

Abstract: We theoretically demonstrate squeezed light generation in a compact, electrically driven device utilizing the nonlinear light-matter interaction of Cooper-pair recombination in a hybrid superconductorsemiconductor structure. On the basis of second-order perturbation theory, we developed an effective Hamiltonian which incorporates the electronic component of the interaction as a photon coupling strength coefficient, enabling a model to describe the quadrature squeezing effect of Cooper-pair recombination on the photonic state. We find the squeezing strength to be proportional to the superconducting energy gap squared and device length, as well as increasing with applied voltage. For practical device length, materials and voltage bias, we calculated squeezing values comparable to those of existing squeezed light generation methods when operating below the critical temperature. This theoretically developed concept shows viability for electrically pumped squeezed light generation and can pave the way towards realization of squeezing in compact integrated structures.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Distributed Quantum Computing: Quantum Gate Teleportation via Satellite-Distributed Entanglement under Atmospheric Depolarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14623)
*Artur Czerwinski,Jakub J. Borkowski,Maciej Wiśniewski,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以量子门隐形传态为基本原语，分析星地纠缠分发支持的分布式量子计算中非局域CNOT门的保真度，并将大气信道物理层建模与门级性能联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星可向两个地面站分发纠缠光子对，从而把共享纠缠资源转移至远端量子处理单元，实现跨处理器量子门；但大气传播（引力场中的传播效应）会劣化纠缠，需量化其对门级性能的影响。

Method: 构建最小四量子比特协议：2个网络量子比特提供纠缠资源，2个电路量子比特承载分布式门；大气传播效应等效为参数依赖的兩比特退极化信道，参数由Fried参数、接收孔径等大气与光学量决定；以理想CNOT输出态与含噪门隐形传态输出态的保真度作为性能指标。

Result: 保真度由分布式纠缠的质量决定，并共同依赖于大气相干性（Fried参数）、孔径尺寸与等效退极化强度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了卫星光信道物理层建模与分布式量子计算门级性能之间的桥梁，表明信道条件通过纠缠质量直接约束分布式门的保真度。

Abstract: We investigate quantum gate teleportation as a primitive for satellite-enabled distributed quantum computing. In the considered architecture, a satellite distributes entangled photon pairs to two optical ground stations, which transfer the shared quantum resource to remote quantum processing units. This entanglement is then used to implement a nonlocal controlled-NOT gate between two circuit qubits located in different processors. We formulate the minimal four-qubit protocol, consisting of two network qubits that provide the entangled resource and two circuit qubits on which the distributed gate is performed. Atmospheric propagation effects in the gravitational field are modeled through an effective two-qubit depolarizing channel whose parameters depend on atmospheric and optical quantities, including the Fried parameter and receiver aperture. The performance of the protocol is quantified by the fidelity between the ideal CNOT output state and the state produced by noisy quantum gate teleportation. Our results show that the fidelity is governed by the quality of the distributed entanglement and depends jointly on atmospheric coherence, aperture size, and effective depolarization strength. These findings connect physical-layer modeling of satellite optical channels with gate-level performance in distributed quantum computing.

</details>


### [106] [The memory cost of sequential quantum state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14652)
*Jian Li,Patryk Lipka-Bartosik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文从量子资源理论视角比较"逐次制备"与"集体制备"的渐进内存代价,证明二者每个输出的渐进内存成本相同,但逐次制备可能需要无界更大的内存,并刻画了这一差距依赖于允许相互作用的物理限制的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化量子器件在逐个处理系统流时所需要的内存(存储维度),并厘清逐次(sequential)与集体(collective)制备之间的内存代价差异及其对物理约束的依赖。

Method: 用量子资源理论形式化内存与系统流之间允许相互作用的物理限制;对比逐次与集体制备的渐进内存成本;分别针对能量守恒幺正操作与噪声操作分析最优内存维度;并研究重复固定相互作用的逐次协议。

Result: (1)一般假设下二者每输出渐进内存成本相同;(2)逐次制备可需无界更大内存;(3)能量守恒幺正下给出从能量本征态制备纯态所需最优内存维度,逐次额外内存要么有界、要么随输出数对数增长;(4)噪声操作下存在变换:集体制备任意多独立输出只需一个辅助量子比特,而逐次制备无固定维度内存可行;(5)重复固定相互作用的逐次协议中,d 维内存在 d² 轮成功后即永久成功,且该逐次制备等价于量子催化。

Conclusion: 逐次与集体制备的渐进内存代价相同,但有限轮次/固定维度层面存在本质差距;该差距受能量守恒与噪声等物理约束调控,并揭示重复交互逐次制备与量子催化的等价性。

Abstract: How much memory does a quantum device need to prepare systems one at a time? We address this question through the lens of quantum resource theories, which formalize physical restrictions on how the memory may interact with the stream of systems it processes. By comparing sequential with collective preparation, we show that under general assumptions both have the same asymptotic memory cost per output. Still, sequential preparation can require an unboundedly larger memory than collective one, and we investigate how this gap depends on the physical restrictions on the allowed interactions. For energy-conserving unitaries, we determine both optimal memory dimensions needed for preparing pure states from an energy eigenstate, and find that the extra memory required sequentially either remains bounded or grows logarithmically with the number of outputs. Under noisy operations, we reveal transformations for which one auxiliary qubit suffices for preparing arbitrarily many independent outputs collectively, while no memory of fixed dimension can deliver them sequentially. Finally, for sequential protocols that repeat a fixed interaction, we show that a $d$-dimensional memory that succeeds for $d^2$ rounds will succeed forever, and that such a sequential preparation is equivalent to quantum catalysis.

</details>


### [107] [Defect-induced stabilization of Wigner negativity and parity-protected phase-locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15624)
*Beatrice Donelli,Stefano Gherardini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在量子范德波尔振荡器中引入宇称保持的δ形势阱（模拟局域缺陷），将连续U(1)对称性破缺为离散Z₂对称性，从而获得受宇称保护的偶宇称稳态量子相干性，产生Wigner函数负值化的非经典稳态，并伴随宇称保护的量子相位锁定；最后给出基于光镊中类碱土超冷原子运动态的可行实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子范德波尔振荡器的连续U(1)对称性使稳态不具备确定的宇称结构，难以保护非经典相干性；作者希望找到一种能保留宇称、并稳定产生非经典稳态（Wigner负值）与相位锁定的机制，同时给出可实现的实验平台。

Method: 在量子范德波尔振荡器中引入宇称保持的δ形势阱以模拟局域缺陷，使连续U(1)对称性降为Z₂对称性；分析缺陷与非线性耗散通道的相互作用对稳态宇称、相干性与相位锁定的影响；并设计基于光镊中类碱土超冷原子运动态的实验方案，其中缺陷由态选择性接触相互作用工程化，非线性耗散通道由拉曼边带驱动。

Result: δ形缺陷使系统仅保留Z₂对称性，产生受宇称保护的偶宇称稳态量子相干性；由此得到具有Wigner函数负值性的非经典稳态；缺陷与非线性耗散的协同作用导致宇称保护的量子相位锁定，即能级同时锁定于两个相反相位。

Conclusion: 局域缺陷诱导的对称性降级（U(1)→Z₂）是保护非经典稳态相干性与相位锁定的有效机制，且该效应可在超冷原子光镊平台上通过态选择性相互作用与拉曼边带驱动实现，具备实验可行性。

Abstract: We show that a parity-preserving delta-shaped potential, mimicking the presence of a localized defect, reduces the continuous $U(1)$-symmetry of a quantum van der Pol oscillator to the discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetry. This mechanism guarantees protected even-parity stationary quantum coherences, resulting in the generation of non-classical steady-states characterized by Wigner function negativity. Thanks to a non-trivial interplay between the defect and non-linear dissipative channels, this kind of negativity entails parity-protected quantum phase locking, meaning that the oscillator's energy levels simultaneously lock into two opposite phases. Finally, we present a realistic experimental proposal involving the motional state of ultracold alkaline-earth-like atoms in optical tweezers, where the localized defect is engineered via state-selective contact interactions, while the non-linear dissipative channels are driven by Raman sidebands.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum synchronization in XX chains under relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14657)
*Kübra Sümer,Ege Yiğit Aldırmaz,Steve Campbell,Barış Çakmak,Göktuğ Karpat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了位点依赖频率与交换耦合的量子比特XX链在多局域振幅阻尼噪声下的稳定同步，给出"暗模能量=各无阻尼段共有本征值"的谱匹配判据：边缘量子比特的普适稳定同步当且仅当这些段恰有一个非零共有本征值，并证明单暗模情形下所有参与同步振荡的量子比特对保持非零常数纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B. Çakmak等人在均匀XX链中给出了稳定同步结果，但均匀性假设限制了结论的适用范围；实际器件存在位点依赖频率与耦合的非均匀性，因此需要把同步判据推广到非均匀情形，并厘清非均匀性对同步存在性与准备依赖性的影响。

Method: 以位点依赖频率和交换耦合的XX链为模型，叠加多局域振幅阻尼噪声；通过识别被阻尼位点分隔的各无阻尼段共享的本征值定义暗模能量，据此建立边缘量子比特同步的谱匹配条件，并分析阻尼位点耦合对相对振荡振幅的作用；结合解析实例与渐近两体concurrence计算。

Result: 暗模能量恰为所有无阻尼段共有的本征值；边缘量子比特的普适稳定同步当且仅当这些段仅共享一个本征值且该值非零，此时暗模支撑上的每个量子比特均同步且锁定常数与初态制备无关；谱匹配条件确定支持同步的哈密顿参数，而阻尼位点的耦合控制相对振荡振幅；解析示例表明非均匀性可产生均匀链中不存在的同步，并将准备依赖的锁定转变为普适同步；在单暗模区域，每对参与同步振荡的量子比特保持非零常数concurrence。

Conclusion: 非均匀XX链的稳定同步由无阻尼段的共有谱结构完全刻画，谱匹配判据给出同步的充要条件，表明非均匀性可主动"制造"同步；同步与纠缠在该机制下共存，为耗散驱动量子网络中同步与纠缠的协同设计提供依据。

Abstract: We study stable synchronization in qubit XX chains with site-dependent frequencies and exchange couplings under multi-local amplitude damping noise, extending the uniform-chain results of B. Çakmak et al., [Phys. Rev. A 114, L030202 2026] in several directions. We establish that the dark-mode energies are precisely the eigenvalues shared by all undamped segments separated by the damped sites. Generic stable synchronization of the edge qubits occurs if and only if these segments share exactly one eigenvalue and this eigenvalue is nonzero, in which case every qubit on the support of the corresponding dark mode is synchronized with locking constants independent of the initial preparation. This spectral matching condition identifies the Hamiltonian parameters that support synchronization, while the couplings to the damped sites control the relative oscillation amplitudes. We present analytical examples which show how inhomogeneity can create synchronization absent in uniform chains and turn preparation-dependent locking into generic synchronization. We also calculate the asymptotic pairwise concurrence and prove that, in the single-dark-mode regime, every pair participating in synchronized oscillations remains entangled with a nonzero, constant concurrence.

</details>


### [109] [Faithful filtering of constrained quantum records: Local limits and collective advantage from loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14695)
*Qingtian Miao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了"弱探针"在不揭示共同取向的前提下记录相邻自旋是否对齐的问题，推导了零误报条件下二分纯积记录的最优可分测量成功率，并证明联合读取可借助模糊结果的相干组合超越所有局域策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱探针能够记录相邻自旋是否对齐而不暴露其共同取向，读取探针即可把未知自旋态投影到保持全部相干性的对齐子空间。论文旨在量化这一投影在探针分别读取与联合读取时的成功频率，并厘清联合读取相对局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）的量子优势及其成立条件。

Method: 对二值纯积记录且零误报的情形，在所有可分测量（进而所有LOCC）上求精确最优，证明最优值等于单侧局域见证排除所有替代态的概率，且同时局域测量即可达到该上界；再针对连通简单图上的成对宇称记录与一般二值线性记录，分析联合读取超越局域策略的充分条件（图为含自环、记录重叠严格介于0与1之间；或至少三条记录间存在不可约依赖），并考察三角图情形与三脉冲相干光接收机的实现。

Result: 联合读取通过接受"替代项相互抵消"的模糊结果相干组合获得增益：对连通简单图上的成对宇称记录，当且仅当图含自环且在任意记录重叠∈(0,1)时严格优于一切局域策略；对一般二值线性记录，条件为至少三条记录间的不可约依赖。该优势降低了弱记录下效率的首项幂次：三角图上效率对见证概率由二次变为线性，三脉冲相干光接收机以单次点击即可捕获此增益。

Conclusion: 联合读取可严格超越最优LOCC，其优势由记录结构（自环、线性依赖）刻画，并直接转化为弱记录区效率幂次的提升；三角图给出可实验检验的实例。工作还推广到跨多种模式的目标以及公共输出保真度下的含噪比较。

Abstract: Weak probes can record whether neighboring spins are aligned without revealing their common orientation. Reading the probes can then herald a projection of an unknown spin state onto the aligned subspace that preserves every coherence within it. We ask how often this projection succeeds when the probes are read separately or jointly. For binary pure product records and zero false positives, we derive the exact optimum over all separable measurements, and hence over all local operations and classical communication. It is the probability that one-sided local witnesses exclude every alternative, and simultaneous local measurements attain it. Joint readout can do better by accepting coherent combinations of ambiguous outcomes in which the alternatives cancel. For pairwise parity records on a connected simple graph, it beats every local strategy exactly when the graph has a loop, at all record overlaps strictly between zero and one. For general binary linear records, the condition is an irreducible dependency among at least three records. The advantage lowers the leading power of the efficiency at weak recording. On a triangle the efficiency becomes linear rather than quadratic in the witness probability, and a three-pulse coherent-light receiver captures this gain with a single click. Extensions treat targets spanning several patterns and a noisy comparison at a common output fidelity.

</details>


### [110] [Geometric Theory of Quasiperiodically Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16683)
*Chengzhi Ye,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于绝热反驱动（counterdiabatic driving）构造了处理任意准周期驱动的普适框架，通过有效平均能量算子Æ与规范势分解幺正演化，在频率格算子空间中用变分原理求解动力学不变空间（Floquet态的推广），提出（非）阿贝尔与（非）扭曲的四重分类，并给出非阿贝尔Floquet定理与"非阿贝尔遍历性"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期驱动系统在非平衡动力学、哈密顿量工程与量子控制中应用广泛，但与周期（Floquet）驱动不同，其缺乏用有效时间无关哈密顿量及其本征态描述演化的普适理论，因此无法定义类似Floquet态的动力学不变空间。

Method: 以反驱动（counterdiabatic driving）为基础，用精确的有效平均能量算子Æ及相应规范势分解准周期驱动的幺正演化；在频率格算子空间中通过变分原理计算Æ与规范势；依据动力学不变空间的几何与拓扑性质，将其划分为（非）阿贝尔、（非）扭曲四类。

Result: 得到四类准周期驱动：阿贝尔非扭曲类可直接将Floquet定理推广至不可约（incommensurate）准周期驱动；进一步导出非阿贝尔版的Floquet理论；并提出以动力学不变本征空间合并（coalescing）为标志的遍历性涌现概念，类比混合经典相空间中遍历性的出现。

Conclusion: 所提出的有效数学框架、变分原理与非阿贝尔遍历性概念不仅适用于准周期驱动，还可推广至更一般的驱动系统。

Abstract: Quasiperiodically driven systems give rise to a rich set of physical phenomena in contemporary quantum physics, with applications ranging from nonequilibrium dynamics to Hamiltonian engineering and control. However, unlike periodically driven (Floquet) systems, their study is hindered by the absence of a generic theory to describe their evolution using an effective time-independent Hamiltonian and its eigenstates. Based on counterdiabatic driving, we construct a general framework to identify and determine the dynamically invariant spaces of arbitrary quasiperiodic drives (i.e., the generalization of Floquet states). We decompose their unitary evolution using the exact effective average-energy operator Æ and the corresponding gauge potential, which we compute via a variational principle in frequency-lattice operator space. We then formulate a four-fold taxonomy of quasiperiodic drives based on the geometry and topology of their dynamically invariant spaces into (non)abelian and (un)twisted classes. The abelian untwisted class admits a direct extension of Floquet's theorem to incommensurate quasiperiodic drives; going beyond, we derive a nonabelian generalization of Floquet theory. Finally, we propose a concept of ergodicity whose onset is marked by coalescing dynamically invariant eigenspaces, in direct analogy to the emergence of ergodicity in a mixed classical phase space. The effective mathematical framework, variational principle, and nonabelian ergodicity we put forward apply beyond quasiperiodic drives.

</details>


### [111] [A New Framework for Quantum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14731)
*Alireza Tavakoli,Mohsen Heidari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出全量子强化学习框架：策略与环境均为条件量子信道，并给出带可训练控制基的参数化量子策略梯度算法及新参数移位规则，导出有限/无限时域无偏梯度估计并证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRL仍保留经典强化学习接口：状态与动作编码于计算基、奖励经测量提取。该接口对真正量子的环境有局限——选择测量会丢弃纠缠信息，重复的中间测量会坍缩演化态并需昂贵的重新制备。因此需要一个智能体、环境、奖励与回报累积全部量子的完全QRL框架。

Method: 将策略与环境同时建模为条件量子信道；提出针对带可训练控制基的参数化条件量子信道的量子策略梯度算法；由于策略参数在重复交互块间共享、且无限时域目标不是有限线路，推导新的参数移位规则——不移动每次出现并评估整条轨迹，而只对单个采样的策略块做移位；该块从几何分布采样，从而在奖励有界时得到无偏梯度估计。给出有限时域与无限时域的梯度估计器。

Result: 获得有限时域与无限时域的梯度估计器；所提出的块采样参数移位在奖励有界条件下为无偏估计；在标准光滑性与步长假设下，建立随机梯度上升过程收敛到稳定点的结论。

Conclusion: 本文朝全量子强化学习目标迈进一步，建立了策略与环境均为条件量子信道的框架，并提供了可行的策略梯度算法、无偏梯度估计与收敛性保证，为后续完全量子的智能体—环境—奖励—回报闭环奠定基础。

Abstract: Quantum reinforcement learning (QRL) has emerged as a promising approach for controlling quantum systems and accelerating learning tasks with quantum resources. However, most existing QRL formulations still retain a classical reinforcement learning interface: states and actions are often encoded in computational bases and rewards are extracted through measurements. This interface is restrictive for genuinely quantum environments: choosing a measurement can discard entanglement information, while repeated intermediate measurements collapse the evolving state and require costly re-preparation. This motivates a fully QRL framework in which the agent, environment, rewards, and return accumulation are all quantum. In this paper, we take a step toward this goal and introduce a new QRL framework in which both the policy and the environment are modeled by conditional quantum channels. We then propose a quantum policy-gradient algorithm for parameterized conditional quantum channels with a trainable control basis. We also derive finite- and infinite-horizon gradient estimators using a new parameter-shift rule. This rule is needed because policy parameters are shared across repeated interaction blocks and the infinite-horizon objective is not a finite circuit; rather than shifting every occurrence and evaluating the whole trajectory, our estimator shifts only one sampled policy block. Sampling this block from a geometric distribution yields an unbiased gradient estimator under bounded rewards. Finally, we establish convergence of the resulting stochastic gradient-ascent procedure to stationary points under standard smoothness and step-size assumptions.

</details>


### [112] [Unified master equation for driven multilevel quantum systems: Derivation, dynamics, and spectral signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16952)
*Sagarika Basak,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文用 Lewis-Riesenfeld 动力学不变量导出统一主方程，在 Born-Markov 理论框架内准确刻画任意驱动下的耗散，并以周期驱动三能级 Λ 系统的荧光谱揭示传统主方程遗漏的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主方程无法准确描述多能级量子系统中由驱动修饰的耗散过程，其局限来源未被系统厘清，也缺乏对何时必须采用"统一处理"的参数判据。

Method: 基于 Lewis-Riesenfeld 动力学不变量构造统一主方程，在 Born-Markov 理论内兼容任意驱动；将之应用于恒定 Rabi 耦合（连续波）、周期调制 Rabi 耦合及非周期（ramped、chirped）驱动方案；计算周期驱动三能级 Λ 系统的荧光光谱，并与传统描述对比。

Result: 周期驱动 Λ 系统的荧光谱出现传统描述所缺失的谱特征；作者将传统主方程的局限归因于两方面：浴频率采样不准确，以及忽略非长期（nonsecular）耗散耦合；并给出需要统一处理的参数区域。

Conclusion: 统一主方程适用于多种驱动协议，荧光谱提供了实验可及的谱学判据，可指导量子控制、量子模拟、量子传感与量子信息处理中耗散过程的可靠建模。

Abstract: Drive-modified dissipation in multilevel quantum systems is not, in general, accurately captured by conventional master equations. We derive a unified master equation using Lewis-Riesenfeld dynamical invariants, accommodating arbitrary driving within Born-Markov theory. We demonstrate the applicability of the unified master equation to constant-Rabi-coupling (continuous-wave), periodically-modulated-Rabi-coupling, and nonperiodic (ramped and chirped) driving protocols. Fluorescence spectra of a periodically driven three-level $Λ$ system reveal signatures missed by conventional descriptions. We trace the limitations of the conventional descriptions to inaccurate bath-frequency sampling and the neglect of nonsecular dissipative couplings, and map out the parameter regimes that require a unified treatment. The fluorescence spectra provide experimentally accessible signatures, which serve as a spectroscopic guide to reliable modeling of dissipative processes that can inform quantum control, simulation, sensing, and information processing.

</details>


### [113] [Bounding Freedom of Choice via Causal Network Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14791)
*George Moreno,Emanuele Polino,Giovanni Rodari,Davide Poderini,Askery Canabarro,Elie Wolfe,Robert W. Spekkens,Fabio Sciarrino,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将Bell场景嵌入更大因果网络，得到对测量依赖性的实验可及上下界，其不相容性可在不预设自由选择的前提下直接从数据认证非经典性；并给出系统导出测量依赖一致性(MDC) Bell不等式的线性规划框架，计算简单且对噪声鲁棒，还能认证超过Tsirelson界的真实量子非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bell不等式依赖"测量独立性"假设，即测量设置与关联源完全解耦，而该假设在实验与概念上都难以保证。作者希望摆脱这一先验假设，直接从数据本身认证非经典性。

Method: 把Bell场景嵌入更大的因果网络，使测量依赖性的可检验上下界自然出现；建立一般性线性规划框架，系统推导测量依赖一致性(MDC) Bell不等式，从而绕开标准方法中的非凸优化困难。

Result: 所得MDC不等式上下界的不相容性可仅凭实验数据认证非经典性；该框架在计算复杂度与噪声鲁棒性上显著优于现有技术，并显示即使关联超过Tsirelson界也能认证真正的量子非定域性。

Conclusion: 嵌入因果网络的方法克服了测量独立性限制，提供了可扩展、噪声鲁棒的MDC不等式推导工具，可推广至多输出与多体Bell检验，并拓展了对量子非定域性边界的认识。

Abstract: Standard Bell inequalities rely on measurement independence, the assumption that measurement settings are perfectly decoupled from the source of correlations. We demonstrate that embedding Bell scenarios into larger causal networks naturally overcomes this limitation. This embedding yields experimentally accessible lower and upper bounds on measurement dependence, the incompatibility of which certifies nonclassicality directly from data, without assuming a priori freedom of choice. We formulate a general linear-programming framework to systematically derive these measurement-dependence consistency (MDC) Bell inequalities. By bypassing standard non-convexity hurdles, this approach significantly outperforms existing techniques in computational simplicity and noise robustness. Finally, we reveal that genuine quantum nonlocality can be certified even for correlations exceeding the Tsirelson bound, and demonstrate our method's broad applicability to multi-outcome and multipartite Bell tests.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Telatar formula: fading quantum capacity of Haar-random multimode attenuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14822)
*Eric Culf,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在Haar随机无源玻色网络中，无辅助量子信息传输的量子容量随模式数N线性增长（固定每模光子数），是Telatar经典多模衰落信道线性标度结论的量子类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分析表明发送端对多模信道的统计不确定性并不显著损害通信效率（Telatar：平均容量随模式数线性增长）。作者希望考察这一现象是否有量子对应，即在只知信道分布、不知具体耦合/散射的情况下，多模量子信道的量子容量是否也呈线性标度。

Method: 研究Haar随机无源玻色网络信道：(1) 零温情形，发送端驱动固定比例输入模式、接收端收集过半输出模式，在固定每模光子数下推导量子容量的线性标度；(2) 非零温度情形，以各向同性热输入态在Haar平均下的相干信息作为容量下界，证得其每模取值收敛为对Wachter定律的积分；(3) 对低温微波网络与室温多模光纤两类模型进行数值计算验证。

Result: 零温下量子容量随模式数线性增长，与经典天线容量增长形式相同；非零温度下Haar平均相干信息的每模极限收敛为对Wachter定律的积分；在低温微波网络与室温多模光纤模型中获得数值上为正的可达速率。

Conclusion: 多模玻色信道中发送端缺乏精确信道知识同样不显著妨碍量子通信；量子容量可如经典容量一样随模式数线性标度，且该结论在有限温度与实验相关平台上给出正的可达速率，为多模量子通信的可行性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Cellular communication is done over an imperfectly predictable multimode environment, where the sender only knows about the distribution of possible intermode coupling and scattering a signal faces. It may thus seem surprising that such communication is efficient. Telatar proved that the sender's lack of exact knowledge about the channel, on average, does not significantly hinder multimode communication. As the number $N$ of modes increases at fixed signal-to-noise ratio, the average capacity of a classical multimode fading channel grows linearly in $N$, like that of $N$ decoupled single-mode channels. We study the quantum analogue in a Haar-random passive bosonic network and prove a similar scaling for unassisted quantum information transmission. Suppose that the sender drives a fixed fraction of the input modes and the receiver collects more than half of the output modes. Then the quantum capacity of such channels at zero temperature grows linearly with the number of modes at fixed photon number per mode, just like the classical capacity grows with the number of antennas. At nonzero temperature, the Haar-averaged coherent information of the isotropic thermal input bounds the capacity from below, and its value per mode converges to an integral against Wachter's law. We numerically identify positive achievable rates in models motivated by cryogenic microwave networks and room-temperature multimode fibers.

</details>


### [115] [Evaluating Quantum Circuit Protection Methods After Compilation: An Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14919)
*Ing Muyleang,Leanghok Hour,Youngsun Han*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在统一"先编译到优化级别3再评估"的协议下，比较ObfusQate三种方案、ECQCO与Q-MaskCut，发现多数方案的结构伪装在编译后大幅失效，Q-MaskCut可有效隐藏输出并在噪声下保持保真度，但需承担碎片变体与经典重构开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子云服务商可读取用户提交的线路及其输出，但现有的线路隐藏方法通常只在线路"原始书写形式"上评估，未考虑编译器优化会改变线路结构。因此需要建立以编译后线路为基准、并考察服务商二次编译后伪装是否残留的评估协议，以便公平比较各类保护方案是否真正阻止服务商获知用户算法与答案。

Method: 提出统一评估协议：所有受保护线路先以优化级别3编译，再测量其结构与执行表现。比较对象为ObfusQate的逆门、复合门、延迟门三种方案、ECQCO与Q-MaskCut。Q-MaskCut作为已有技术的组合实例，包含一次性密码本密钥、诱饵线、带随机旋转对跨切口的线路切割、编译后的Pauli twirling与动力学解耦掩蔽。实验包括：无噪声与两种噪声模型下模拟11个3–19量子比特线路；测量23个3–127量子比特线路的编译结构与执行时间；并测试结构伪装在服务商二次编译后能否保持。

Result: 复合门与延迟门方案在编译中丧失大部分结构伪装，输出可读；逆门方案仅靠将执行时间延长7–112倍来维持较高的归一化图编辑距离(normGED)；ECQCO的输出无法用作者提供的实现解码。Q-MaskCut可隐藏输出（无密钥时平均全变差距离0.94），在无噪声且碎片概率精确时可精确解码；其编译后normGED为0.88，主要来自掩蔽部分，二次编译后降至0.42（来自密钥、诱饵与挂锁）。噪声下其保真度与原线路相当；模拟中每线路需7–91个碎片变体（ibm_marrakesh实机运行为7–61）并需经典重构。

Conclusion: 线路保护方案应基于编译后线路评估，并在服务商再次编译后重新评估，否则会高估伪装效果。Q-MaskCut在输出隐藏与噪声保真度上表现较好，但结构伪装部分依赖易被二次编译消除的掩蔽，且带来碎片采样与经典重构开销；逆门方案以巨大时间代价换取结构隐藏；复合门、延迟门及ECQCO在本协议下不具实用性。

Abstract: Quantum cloud providers can read the circuits they receive and their outputs, yet methods for hiding a circuit are usually evaluated on the circuit as written, before compilation optimises it. We compare three ObfusQate schemes (inverse, composite and delayed gates), ECQCO and Q-MaskCut under one protocol that compiles every protected circuit at optimisation level 3 before measuring it; the goal is to keep the provider from learning the user's algorithm and answer. Q-MaskCut is one example combination of existing techniques: a one-time-pad key, decoy wires, circuit cutting with a random rotation pair across each cut, and Pauli twirling with dynamical-decoupling masking after compilation. We simulate 11 circuits of 3 to 19 qubits without noise and on two noise models, measure the compiled structure and execution time of 23 circuits of 3 to 127 qubits, and test whether the structural disguise survives a second compilation by the provider. Composite and delayed gates lose most of their structural disguise in compilation and leave the output readable; inverse gates keep a high normalised graph edit distance (normGED) only by making execution 7 to 112 times longer; ECQCO's output could not be decoded with the implementation provided by its authors. Q-MaskCut hides the output (mean total variation distance without the key 0.94), and without noise, with exact fragment probabilities, it decodes exactly. Its compiled normGED of 0.88 comes mostly from the masking, which a second compilation removes, leaving 0.42 from the key, decoys and padlocks. With noise, its fidelity is comparable to the original's; it needs 7 to 91 fragment variants per circuit in the simulations (7 to 61 in a run on ibm_marrakesh) and a classical reconstruction. Protection should therefore be evaluated on the compiled circuit and again after the provider recompiles it.

</details>


### [116] [Symmetry-Controlled Criticality and Zeno Localization in a Monitored Quasiperiodic Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16912)
*Pinaki Singha,Marcin Szyniszewski,Nilanjan Roy,Auditya Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究连续监测下带配对项的自由费米子模型，发现准周期势既控制局域化又改变监测场论的对称类，在临界相（熵标度$(\ln L)^2$）与面积律相之间存在转变，且转变线呈强多重分形并对准周期性非单调依赖；在Zeno区导出有效非厄米BdG描述与闭式局域化长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量诱导转变强烈依赖对称性，但其与准周期局域化及超导配对的相互作用尚不清楚；同时洁净极限下是否发生转变、对称类如何影响临界纠缠缺乏系统认识。

Method: 构建连续监测且含配对项的自由费米子模型，用准周期势同时调控局域化并改变监测场论的对称类；以拓扑纠缠熵作为量子态扩散模拟中非局域关联的探针定位相变；对照DIII类与D类非线性sigma模型；并在深度Zeno区导出有效非厄米Bogoliubov–de Gennes理论，解析给出局域化长度。

Result: 识别出临界相（冯诺依曼纠缠熵按$(\ln L)^2$增长）与面积律相之间的转变；洁净极限下无转变，与非线性sigma模型的预期一致；转变线呈强多重分形，且对准周期性非单调依赖，归因于对称类交叉；Zeno区解析局域化长度与数值定量吻合。

Conclusion: 对称性、准周期局域化与测量反作用三者共同决定被监测超导体中临界纠缠行为与微观轨迹局域化，为监测量子物质中的对称性—局域化—测量耦合提供了统一图像。

Abstract: Measurement-induced transitions of free fermions depend strongly on symmetry, but their interplay with quasiperiodic localization and superconducting pairing remains largely unexplored. We study a continuously monitored model of free fermions with pairing, where quasiperiodicity both controls localization and changes the symmetry class of the monitored field theory. Using topological entanglement entropy as a probe of nonlocal correlations in quantum state diffusion simulations, we identify a transition between a critical phase whose von Neumann entanglement entropy scales as $(\ln L)^2$ and an area-law phase, whereas no transition occurs in the clean limit, consistent with the corresponding DIII and D nonlinear sigma models. The transition line exhibits strong multifractality and depends nonmonotonically on quasiperiodicity, which we associate with the symmetry crossover. Deep in the Zeno regime, we derive an effective non-Hermitian Bogoliubov-de Gennes description of individual trajectories and obtain a closed-form localization length that quantitatively agrees with numerics. Our results reveal how symmetry, quasiperiodic localization, and measurement backaction combine to determine both critical entanglement and microscopic trajectory localization in monitored superconductors.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum CSS codes: Decoder-aware multiobjective design and bound-certified selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14923)
*Jiapei Zhuang,Yaocheng Chen,Xingyao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将固定编码资源下的CSS量子纠错码设计形式化为解码器感知的多目标优化问题，以扇区式简并最大似然解码失败概率与校验权重为目标，并给出确定性截断界用于满足失败率约束的选码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码存在"解码可靠性"与"测量校验所需资源"之间的权衡，而以往基于距离的码搜索准则未直接反映实际解码器下的失败概率，因此需要在固定编码资源下进行解码器感知的多目标设计。

Method: 将CSS码设计表述为解码器感知的多目标优化：(1) 目标为扇区式简并最大似然解码下的失败概率与校验权重（独立纯型生成基的最小总权重）；(2) 针对独立比特/相位翻转噪声且综合征测量完美的情形，推导确定性截断界以支持在失败率约束下选码；(3) 在共享同一退火核、相同查询预算的配对搜索中，比较解码器感知引导与距离引导、距离-重数引导的表现，并用Holm校正检验显著性。

Result: 在每扇区错误概率0.01时，界证明一个将2个逻辑量子比特编码进40个物理量子比特的码可在校验权重139下满足0.001的失败率目标，而对应(40,2)"主菜单"中所有更低成本候选的下界均超过该目标；配对搜索中，解码器感知引导在全部八种配置下的平均最终超体积显著高于距离引导；相对距离-重数引导，五种配置显著提升、三种不显著（其中一种平均差为负）。所有臂均在统一的截断可靠性与归一化校验权重坐标下评分。

Conclusion: 该框架在受评估的CSS码池内，把可靠性-校验权重权衡与基于界的认证选码联系起来，表明解码器感知引导优于纯距离类引导。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes trade decoding reliability against the resources needed to measure their checks. We formulate Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code design at fixed encoding resources as a decoder-aware multiobjective problem. The objectives are failure probability under sector-wise degenerate maximum-likelihood decoding and check weight, the minimum total weight of an independent pure-type generator basis. For independent bit- and phase-flip noise with perfect syndrome extraction, deterministic truncation bounds support selection under a failure-rate constraint. At a per-sector error probability of 0.01, the bounds certify that a code encoding two logical qubits into 40 physical qubits meets a failure-rate target of 0.001 at check weight 139. The lower bound of every cheaper candidate in the corresponding (40,2) main menu exceeds this target. In paired searches sharing one annealing kernel and equal query budgets, decoder-aware guidance gives significantly higher mean final hypervolume than distance guidance in all eight tested configurations under the reported Holm correction. Against distance-multiplicity guidance, five configurations show significant gains and three do not, including one with a negative mean difference. All arms are scored in common truncated-reliability and normalized-check-weight coordinates. The framework connects reliability-check-weight trade-offs to bound-certified selection within evaluated CSS pools.

</details>


### [118] [Ergodicity breaking for data classification in a dissipative Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17036)
*Simon Kochsiek,Mario Boneberg,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph
