TL;DR: 本文将量子神经网络与耗散量子 Ising 模型（Lindblad 主方程描述）对应起来，指出其涌现非线性和非遍历动力学（不同吸引盆的初态演化到可区分的定态）是量子数据分类的机制。作者研究具有竞争性两体与四体相互作用的 Ising 模型，通过平均场计算与精确小尺度数值模拟表明定态流形可被设计以实现多类分类，并刻画吸引盆结构，从而证明该系统可实现具有非线性决策边界的量子态分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是探究数据处理与相互作用多体系统动力学之间的紧密联系：某些量子神经网络可映射为耗散量子 Ising 模型，其定态吸引盆结构天然对应分类功能。作者希望借助这一对应，从多体物理角度设计可实现多类分类、且决策边界非线性的量子数据处理架构。

Method: 方法上，考虑具有竞争性两体与四体相互作用的 Ising 模型；采用平均场计算结合精确小尺度数值模拟（exact small-scale numerics）求解其动力学与定态，并分析与设计定态流形及相应的吸引盆结构。

Result: 结果表明，定态流形可被设计以支持多类分类；吸引盆结构得到系统刻画，显示不同初态（对应不同输入量子态）被映射到可区分的定态，从而实现具有非线性决策边界的量子态分类。

Conclusion: 结论是：耗散多体 Ising 动力学可作为量子数据分类的物理实现平台，通过设计相互作用与定态流形即可获得多类分类能力与非线性决策边界；该工作为"多体动力学即数据处理"的观点提供了具体模型层面的支持。

Abstract: We explore the close relationship between the processing of data and the dynamics of interacting many-body systems. For example, certain classes of quantum neural networks can be mapped onto dissipative quantum Ising models whose evolution is described by a Lindblad master equation. In these architectures, emergent nonlinearities lead to non-ergodic dynamics under which initial states located in different basins of attraction evolve towards distinguishable stationary states. This mechanism underlies the classification of quantum data. Motivated by this connection, we consider an Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions. Using a combination of mean-field calculations and exact small-scale numerics, we show that the stationary state manifold can be designed to enable multi-class classification. We also investigate the structure of the ensuing basins of attraction, thereby showing that these systems allow for the classification of quantum states with nonlinear decision boundaries.

</details>


### [119] [CSS Circuits: useful quantum circuits that can be simulated with $O(1)$ cost per gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14944)
*Craig Gidney,Cody Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个每门仅需 O(1) 工作量的 CSS 量子电路模拟器，对应局部隐变量模型，并用 Bell 不等式证明其无法扩展到 H、CZ、S 门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSS 电路（仅由 X、Z、M_X、M_Z、CNOT 构成的量子电路）虽极为受限，却能实现隐形传态、纠错、纠缠纯化等重要协议，因此值得研究其可高效模拟性与边界。作者希望给出一个极简高效模拟器，并厘清该模拟框架的能力边界，同时指出其在含原子丢失误差下基准测试量子纠错的价值。

Method: 构造一个 CSS 电路模拟器，每施加一个门仅付出 O(1) 的计算代价；将该模拟器解释为一种局部隐变量模型（经典模型），再借助 Bell 不等式论证：若把 Hadamard 门、CZ 门或 S 门加入电路，该模拟器无法继续支持，即隐变量模型失效。

Result: 模拟器以 O(1)/门 的代价完成 CSS 电路模拟；Bell 不等式证明该局部隐变量模型不能扩展到含 Hadamard、CZ 或 S 门的电路；并观察到 CSS 电路适合作为存在原子丢失误差时量子纠错的基准测试平台。

Conclusion: CSS 电路可由极高效的经典局部隐变量模拟器复现，但其可模拟性在加入 H、CZ、S 门后即被 Bell 不等式排除；CSS 电路因而成为研究纠错协议及原子丢失误差基准测试的理想对象。

Abstract: "CSS circuits" are quantum circuits generated by $X$, $Z$, $M_X$, $M_Z$, and CNOT gates. Although CSS circuits are extremely restrictive, they can implement important quantum protocols like teleportation, error correction, and entanglement purification.
  We present a simple CSS circuit simulator that uses $O(1)$ work per gate. The simulator corresponds to a local hidden variable model, so we use Bell inequalities to prove the simulator can't also support Hadamard gates, CZ gates, or S gates. We note that CSS circuits appear ideal for benchmarking quantum error correction in the presence of atom loss errors.

</details>


### [120] [Local enhancement of finite-strength code preparation: recoverability and Wilson coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14967)
*Tong Xu,Yuanchen Zhao,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 有限测量强度会使编码映射偏离投影极限，局域逻辑泄漏阻碍渐近忠实恢复；仅增强逻辑支撑附近的少量检验即可局部消除该障碍并恢复可恢复性，且三维去禁闭相中增强"管道"可恢复宏观分离逻辑代表的等价性，二维则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子测量是制备量子纠错码与长程纠缠态的直接途径，但实验中有限测量强度会相干地使编码映射偏离投影极限。虽然某些与长程序相关的非局域特征可在形变下存活，但这不足以保证态能保护编码的量子信息，因此需要厘清"长程序存活"与"信息可恢复"之间的差距及其对测量强度的要求。

Method: 作者通过分析逻辑算符支撑上的局域逻辑泄漏随码距的累积，论证固定测量强度下局域泄漏导致渐近忠实恢复失效，并给出测量强度须随码距对数增长的标度要求；随后构造仅在选定逻辑支撑附近增强消失比例检验（其余体区保持有限测量强度）的方案，在固定toric寄存器与足够弱的局域随机误差下证明恢复性可被局部修复；最后比较三维去禁闭相与二维情形下逻辑代表的等价性。

Result: (1) 固定强度下局域逻辑泄漏阻碍渐近忠实恢复，其沿逻辑算符支撑累积迫使相关测量强度随码距对数增长；(2) 该障碍可局部消除：固定toric寄存器在足够弱局域随机错误下，仅增强逻辑支撑附近消失比例的检验即可恢复恢复性，体区仍保持有限测量强度；(3) 严格三维去禁闭相中，适当的增强管道恢复了宏观分离逻辑代表的等价性，任一代表渐近实现对固定编码相同的受控逻辑作用；(4) 二维中仅靠可恢复性不能恢复该等价性，即便两个关联编码均可恢复，代表仍可能不等价。

Conclusion: 论文区分了"非局域长程序特征存活"与"编码信息真正受保护"两个层次，指出有限测量强度下的局域逻辑泄漏是恢复性的根本障碍并给出对数标度判据；同时证明通过非均匀（局部）增强检验可经济地恢复可恢复性，并揭示了逻辑操作等价性在三维与二维之间的维度差异，为有限强度稳定子测量的容错制备与逻辑操作提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Stabilizer measurements provide a direct route to preparing quantum error-correcting codes and long-range entangled states. In realistic implementations, however, finite measurement strength can coherently deform the encoding map away from the projective limit. Certain nonlocal signatures associated with long-range order can survive such a deformation, but this alone does not guarantee that the resulting state protects encoded quantum information. We show that, at fixed strength, local logical leakage obstructs asymptotically faithful recovery; along logical operators' supports, its accumulation forces the relevant measurement strength to grow logarithmically with code distance. We then prove that this obstruction can be removed locally: for fixed toric registers under sufficiently weak local-stochastic errors, enhancing only a vanishing fraction of checks near selected logical supports restores recovery while the bulk remains at finite measurement strength. With recoverability restored, we prove a further dimensional distinction in logical operations. In the strict three-dimensional deconfined phase, suitable enhanced tubes restore the equivalence of macroscopically separated representatives, so either representative asymptotically implements the same controlled logical action on a fixed encoding. In two dimensions, recoverability alone does not restore this equivalence: the representatives can remain inequivalent even when both associated encodings are recoverable.

</details>


### [121] [Ensemble phase transitions in measurement-unitary hybrid quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17322)
*Saptarshi Mandal,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究混合量子电路中辅助量子比特所对应的轨迹系综（TE）与投影系综（PE）的统计性质，发现在测量诱导纯化转变临界点处两类系综均发生非解析的"系综相变"，揭示其为测量诱导纠缠/纯化转变的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学生成的态系综的普适性质可用于刻画希尔伯特空间的遍历性及深度热化（或其缺失）。作者希望厘清混合电路中测量诱导纯化转变如何体现在态系综的统计结构上，并给出该转变的清晰物理机制。

Method: 考虑与经历幺正演化交织投影测量的系统纠缠的辅助量子比特，分析两类态系综：以混合电路测量结果为条件的轨迹系综（TE），以及在对系统施加最终时刻投影测量后生成的投影系综（PE）。分别在Haar随机电路与Clifford电路中考察两系综的高阶矩（design阶数）随测量率的变化。

Result: 两系综在测量诱导纯化转变临界点均出现非解析变化（系综相变），而其一阶矩始终为完全极大混合、对转变"失明"。Haar随机电路中，TE从平凡恒等系综转变为均匀Haar系综，PE则从Haar系综转变为平凡的单点纯态；Clifford电路中，非稳定性的缺失使PE至多为1-design，而TE可形成k≤3的design。相变对应于遍历性"操控"量子子系统的能力从混合相的时间边界测量转移到纯相的体轨迹测量。

Conclusion: 高阶系综统计（design阶数）构成探测测量诱导纯化/纠缠转变的敏感序参量，系综相变为该转变提供了明确的物理机制：混合相中遍历性操控源于时间边界的投影测量，纯相中则源于体轨迹测量。

Abstract: The universal properties of state ensembles generated by quantum dynamics provide a refined lens into Hilbert-space ergodicity and deep thermalisation, or lack thereof. We investigate deep-ergodicity breaking transitions of an ancillary qubit entangled with a system undergoing dynamics via hybrid quantum circuits, where unitary evolution is interspersed with projective measurements. Specifically, we analyse the statistical properties of two distinct state ensembles: the trajectory ensemble (TE) conditioned on the measurement outcomes of the hybrid circuit, and the projected ensemble (PE) generated by further projective measurements of the system at the final temporal boundary. We find that both ensembles undergo non-analytic changes -- ensemble phase transitions -- at the critical point of the measurement-induced purification transition, despite their first moments remaining completely maximally mixed and blind to the transition. For Haar-random circuits, the TE transitions from a trivial identity ensemble to the uniform Haar ensemble, while the PE transitions from the Haar ensemble to a trivial singleton pure state. In Clifford circuits, absence of non-stabiliserness limits the PE to a $1$-design, while the TE forms a $k$-design for $k \leq 3$. We demonstrate that these genuine ensemble transitions offer a clear physical mechanism for the measurement-induced entanglement or purification transitions, illustrating how the capacity to ergodically steer a quantum subsystem shifts from the temporal boundary measurements in the mixed phase to the bulk trajectory measurements in the pure phase.

</details>


### [122] [From optimization to construction: Expanding quantum measurement designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14986)
*Taotao Zhao,Qianjun Yao,Mengdi Sun,He Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出VGMC方法，通过改变哪些哈密顿项共享测量采样来进一步降低能量估计方差，在保持局域测量的前提下减少采样开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机上估计分子能量需重复测量大量Pauli项，采样是主要资源开销；现有测量设计只在给定测量基上做优化，未改变项与项的共享组合方式。

Method: 先纳入各现有基下所有可测哈密顿项，联合优化估计系数与采样配额，以区分测量利用低效与测量组合本身的局限；比较候选组的收益与从现有基撤出采样配额的代价，据此打分贪心构造qubit-wise commuting组，再做联合再优化。

Result: 精确协方差下，在引入新测量基的分子基准上优于最先进测量设计方法，方差降低18.19%–23.38%；近似协方差下增益保持；有限采样模拟显示接近化学精度时所需采样数更低。

Conclusion: 优化测量组合方式可反过来指导哪些项应一起测量，为变分量子模拟在现有及近期设备上降低采样开销提供路径。

Abstract: Estimating molecular energies on quantum computers requires repeated measurements of many Pauli terms, making sampling a major resource cost. Measurement design chooses which compatible terms are measured together and allocates a finite shot budget among bases to minimize the variance of an energy estimator. We introduce variance-guided measurement group construction (VGMC), which reduces variance beyond optimization on existing measurement bases by changing which Hamiltonian terms share measurement shots. After including every Hamiltonian term measurable in each existing basis, we jointly optimize estimator coefficients and shot allocations to separate inefficient use of these measurements from limitations of their measurement combinations. We compare a candidate's benefit with the cost of withdrawing shots from existing bases, and use the resulting scores to guide greedy construction of qubit-wise commuting groups followed by joint re-optimization. With exact covariances, VGMC outperformed the state-of-the-art measurement design methods across the molecular benchmarks where it introduced additional measurement bases, achieving variance reductions of 18.19%-23.38%. The gains persisted with approximate covariances, and simulations with finite shots indicated lower estimated shot requirements near chemical accuracy. These results show that optimizing how measurements are combined can also guide which terms are measured together, offering a route to reducing the sampling overhead of variational quantum simulation while retaining local measurements on current and near-term devices.

</details>


### [123] [QSim-Arena: an evolving developer for quantum simulation advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15000)
*Jue Xu,Yue Wang,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 QSim-Arena：一个以 AI 智能体为核心的研究框架，将"验证—基准测试"闭环与开放式探索相结合，用于持续、可复现地评估量子动力学模拟中的量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学模拟中的"实际量子优势"仍是核心争议：所声称的优势常被更强的经典方法迅速削弱或推翻，而量子与经典双方的进展速度又快于期刊发表周期，导致基于静态论文的评估很快过时。

Method: 为哈密顿量、初态以及演化态/可观测量期望值的精度目标提供显式规格；智能体将已发表技术转化为可执行组件，复现报告结果并在多类哈密顿量族上基准测试；同时实现并独立交叉校验竞争性经典方法，基于严格误差界与经验校准给出端到端量子资源估计的可信基线；并在闭环之外，通过从文献抽取知识来重组经典模拟技术与协同设计容错量子电路。

Result: 初始应用显示，通过整合已有技术即可改进基线资源估计；竞争性经典方法得到独立实现与交叉校验，形成可信基线；框架能够快速响应算法、误差分析与硬件方面的进展。

Conclusion: QSim-Arena 将量子优势评估视为一个演进式、可复现的研究过程，而非一次性结论，从而为应对算法、误差分析与硬件进步提供可持续的评估范式。

Abstract: Where practical advantage emerges in quantum dynamics simulation remains a central debate in quantum computing. Claimed advantages can be rapidly narrowed or overturned by stronger classical methods, while progress on both sides can outpace the journal publication cycle. We present QSim-Arena, a research framework in which AI agents combine a closed loop of verification and benchmarking with open exploration. Hamiltonians, initial states and accuracy targets for evolved states or observable expectation values provide explicit specifications for evaluating simulation methods. Agents translate published techniques into executable components, reproduce reported results and benchmark their performance across diverse Hamiltonian families. Competing classical methods are implemented and independently crosschecked, providing credible baselines for end-to-end quantum resource estimates based on rigorous error bounds and empirical calibration. Alongside this closed loop, agents recombine classical simulation techniques and co-design fault-tolerant quantum circuits using knowledge extracted from the literature. Initial applications improve baseline resource estimates through the integration of established techniques. QSim-Arena thus treats quantum advantage assessment as an evolving, reproducible research process that can respond to advances in algorithms, error analysis and hardware.

</details>


### [124] [Time- and Photon-Optimal Paths for the Preparation and Logical Flip of Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15018)
*Aidan Curtis,Juan S. Salcedo-Gallo,Sergei Leonov,Kevin Limanta,Peter Chin,Salil Bedkihal,Mattias Fitzpatrick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以"最短时路径（brachistochrone）"为基准构造GKP态制备与逻辑门的理想非线性哈密顿量，并与"光子最优路径"比较，提出"切向容量"指标量化门集的路径跟踪能力，发现条件压缩门最优：824 ns内制备保真度0.99的GKP态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP态高度非高斯，在光子损耗下难以控制；需要在有限速度约束下找到态制备与逻辑门操作的最优控制路径，并评估现有实验门集能在多大程度上逼近该理想演化。

Method: 以有界速度下的最快路径（brachistochrone）导出态制备与逻辑门的理想非线性哈密顿量，并与光子最优路径对比；提出切向容量（tangential capacity）指标，量化conditional squeezing、conditional displacement、SNAP三类门集对目标路径的跟踪效率。

Result: 态制备时两条路径几乎重合，但逻辑门显著不同：brachistochrone在位翻转中保持编码量子比特，光子最优路径则使已知态经真空翻转。切向容量上条件压缩最高：824 ns、保真度0.99、路径贴近度最小0.90；条件位移为0.98与0.51，SNAP为0.83与0.19。

Conclusion: 最优控制路径几何为GKP操控提供清晰评价基准；切向容量可区分门集优劣，条件压缩是实现快速高保真GKP态制备与门操作的最优候选方案。

Abstract: Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) states are highly non-Gaussian, making their control challenging, particularly in the presence of photon loss. Here, we use the brachistochrone, the fastest path at bounded speed, to construct ideal nonlinear Hamiltonians for state preparation and logical gates, and compare it with the photon-optimal path. We find that the two paths nearly coincide for state preparation but differ markedly for logical operations, namely the brachistochrone preserves the encoded qubit during a bit flip, whereas the photon-optimal path flips a known state through the vacuum. We then introduce the tangential capacity to quantify how effectively gate sets track these paths. Using this analysis, we find that conditional squeezing achieves the highest capacity, preparing GKP states in 824 ns with a fidelity of 0.99 and a minimum closeness to the path of 0.90, compared with 0.98 and 0.51 for conditional displacements and 0.83 and 0.19 for SNAP gates.

</details>


### [125] [Reducing TLS Losses in Superconducting Circuits Using Solid Neon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15021)
*A. Palmer,K. Matkovic,P. Russell,E. Helgemo,L. Delventhal,Y. Ashlea Alava,K. Zuo,S. N. Coppersmith,M. C. Cassidy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在NbTiN共面波导谐振器上沉积并随后去除固态氖薄膜，可显著改善超导器件性能；温度与功率依赖分析表明该提升源于双能级系统（TLS）耗散降低，与氖处理过程中表面污染物的重新分布和去除有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS是超导器件的主要损耗来源，限制量子比特、探测器和谐振器的性能。其来源包括表面氧化物、晶体缺陷以及难以去除的制备残留，而许多敏感材料无法承受剧烈清洗工艺，因此亟需一种简单、非破坏性的TLS损耗抑制方法。

Method: 采用NbTiN共面波导谐振器作为测试平台，在器件表面沉积固态氖薄膜并随后升华去除；通过测量谐振器的温度依赖与功率依赖特性来表征TLS耗散变化；并利用原子力显微镜（AFM）对受污染六方氮化硼（hBN）薄片在氖暴露前后的表面形貌进行对比，验证残留物的重新分布与去除。

Result: 氖处理显著改善了器件性能；温度与功率依赖分析证实性能提升来自TLS耗散降低；AFM图像显示氖处理能够重新分布并去除hBN表面的残留污染物。

Conclusion: 沉积并去除固态氖薄膜是一种简单、非破坏性的表面清洁策略，可有效缓解超导器件及其他无法承受剧烈清洗工艺的敏感材料中的TLS损耗。

Abstract: Two level systems (TLSs) are a major source of loss in superconducting devices, limiting the performance of qubits, detectors, and resonators. These defects arise from a range of sources, including surface oxides, crystal defects, as well as fabrication residues which are often difficult to remove. Using NbTiN coplanar waveguide resonators, we show that depositing and subsequently removing a solid neon film can significantly improve device performance. Analysis of the temperature and power dependence indicates that this enhancement arises from reduced TLS dissipation, which we attribute to the redistribution and removal of surface contaminants during neon deposition and evaporation. Atomic force microscopy images of contaminated hexagonal boron nitride flakes before and after neon exposure show that neon treatment can redistribute and remove surface residues. These results demonstrate a simple, non-destructive approach for mitigating TLS losses in superconducting devices and other sensitive materials that cannot withstand aggressive cleaning processes.

</details>


### [126] [High-precision measurement of level leakage via intelligent coherent control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15031)
*Ting Wang,Hang Xu,Shi-Qiang Qiao,Xue-Ke Song,Haipeng Cao,Zhao-Feng Su,Liu Ye,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于强化学习（DQN）的量子传感算法，用于厄米三能级与有效耗散二能级系统中能级泄漏的高精度估计；采用脉冲参数调制策略，发现DQN脉冲序列可提升传感精度，联合相位-失谐调制使CFI接近QFI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能级泄漏是量子态控制中的主要误差源，大规模量子计算需对其精确表征。量子传感可实现高精度泄漏估计，但灵敏度受非幺正泄漏动力学及自由哈密顿量与信号发生器之间干涉的限制。

Method: 面向厄米三能级系统和有效耗散二能级系统，提出强化学习量子传感算法，采用实验可行的脉冲参数调制策略（含相位与失谐调制），在给定控制与测量协议下由DQN搜索脉冲序列，并以CFI与QFI评估精度。

Result: 两种模型中，DQN发现的脉冲序列均提升了针对特定测量模型的传感精度；联合相位-失谐调制进一步改善性能，所得CFI接近对应QFI。

Conclusion: 强化学习可用于设计实验可行的脉冲方案，有效缓解非幺正动力学与哈密顿量干涉对泄漏估计的限制，实现高精度、可靠的能级泄漏测量。

Abstract: Level leakage constitutes a dominant error source in quantum state control and necessitates accurate characterization for large-scale quantum computing. Quantum sensing provides a viable route for high-precision estimation of level leakage, but its sensitivity can be limited by non-unitary leakage dynamics and interference between the free Hamiltonian and signal generator. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-based quantum sensing algorithm for Hermitian three-level and effective dissipative two-level systems. The algorithm adopts an experimentally feasible pulse-parameter modulation strategy, enabling high-precision and reliable measurement under the specified control and measurement protocol. In both models, DQN-discovered pulse sequences enhance the measurement-model-specific sensing precision, while joint phase--detuning modulation further improves the performance and yields a CFI close to the corresponding QFI.

</details>


### [127] [Sensing frequencies at the discrete Grover-Heisenberg limit using a single qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15057)
*Boyang Chen,Andrea Coladangelo,Jerry Li,Haocheng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究单量子比特能否达到"Grover-Heisenberg极限"的传感时间标度；作者证明在信号强度已知、频率取自已知有限候选集的离散版本问题中，单量子比特即可实现，且传感时间仅随候选频率数目的平方根增长，并证明该"离散Grover-Heisenberg极限"在最坏情况下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测强度与频率均未知的弱振荡场是量子传感的基础任务。Allen等此前给出了所需传感时间的基本下界（"Grover-Heisenberg极限"）及达到该极限的协议，但该协议依赖辅助量子比特执行Grover算法子程序，因此单传感量子比特能否达到同样的标度仍是一个未解问题。

Method: 转而研究问题的离散版本：信号强度已知，频率属于任意但已知的有限候选频率集合。设计仅使用单个传感量子比特的协议，利用Grover式振幅放大思想在频率候选集中进行搜索，使所需传感时间随候选频率数的平方根增长；同时给出最坏情况下的最优性证明（下界论证）。

Result: 单量子比特即可完成该离散传感任务，传感时间标度为√N（N为候选频率数目），优于线性扫描；并证明在最坏情况下这一平方根依赖不可改进，即所提出的"离散Grover-Heisenberg极限"是紧的。

Conclusion: 辅助量子比特并非实现Grover-Heisenberg标度的必要条件：在离散频率估计设定下，单个传感量子比特已足以达到最优的平方根加速，确立了"离散Grover-Heisenberg极限"这一基本性能界限。

Abstract: We consider a fundamental quantum sensing task: detecting weak oscillating fields with unknown strength and frequency. Recent work by Allen et al. established a fundamental lower bound on the required sensing time, which they named the "Grover-Heisenberg limit", and gave a protocol attaining this limit. The protocol relies on auxiliary qubits, which are used to run Grover's algorithm as a subroutine, leaving open the question of whether a single sensing qubit can achieve the same scaling.
  We show that a single qubit suffices for a discrete version of this problem, in which the signal strength is known and the frequency belongs to an arbitrary, but known, finite set of candidate frequencies. Our protocol's sensing time grows only as the square root of the number of candidate frequencies. We prove that this square-root dependence, which we refer to as the "discrete Grover-Heisenberg limit", is optimal in the worst case.

</details>


### [128] [Scalable Compilation of Resource-Efficient Tapers for High-Confidence Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15076)
*David Raveh,Andrew Sornborger,Yiğit Subaşı,Lukasz Cincio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出可扩展的张量网络方法，为DPSS（Slepian）taper构造高优化、资源高效的态制备电路，将tQPE的辅助比特规模从l+m=24扩展至l≤50、m≤4，并在Quantinuum H2-2上验证失败概率降低3–18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相位估计是众多量子算法的核心组件。近期提出的高置信度tapered QPE（tQPE）可显著减少达到给定失败概率ε所需的辅助比特数，其失败概率随置信比特数m呈双指数衰减，因此少量置信比特即可满足绝大多数问题需求。然而tQPE的关键瓶颈在于辅助寄存器上taper态的电路构造：已有工作仅能处理l+m=24的小规模，缺乏可扩展的态制备方法。

Method: 作者将tQPE的辅助比特分为l个分辨率比特与m个置信比特（p=l+m）。针对最优集中的DPSS（Slepian）taper态，开发可扩展的张量网络方法，对态制备电路进行高度优化（含门数与深度压缩），构建了覆盖l=1,…,50与m=1,…,4的资源高效态制备电路数据库。随后选取小l值的浅层电路，在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱量子计算机上对单比特系统（初态为基态）运行tQPE电路进行实验验证。

Result: 张量网络方法成功给出l=1,…,50、m=1,…,4的大规模优化态制备电路库，突破此前l+m=24的规模限制；所得电路深度足够浅，小l情形可在近期量子设备上演示。在含噪声的H2-2硬件上，对l=1,…,10，tQPE相较基线方案实现失败概率3倍至18倍的降低。

Conclusion: 可扩展张量网络方法使tQPE的taper态制备电路在比特规模上大幅扩展并保持资源高效，配以公开电路数据库可直接复用；硬件实验证明其在真实噪声设备上即能带来显著失败概率优势，为tQPE在近期及容错量子计算中的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation is a key component of many quantum algorithms. Recently, high-confidence, tapered QPE (tQPE) has been introduced, significantly reducing the number of ancilla qubits necessary for a given failure probability, $ε$. We consider tQPE with $p = l+m$ ancillas, where $l$ represents the number of ancillas necessary to estimate a phase to a given resolution and $m$ represents the number of ''confidence'' qubits added to ensure a desired success probability. With tQPE the failure probability decays double exponentially in $m$, implying that only a few confidence qubits suffice for virtually any problem of interest. A key challenge for tQPE is the construction of the quantum circuit for preparing the taper state on the ancilla register. Here, we develop scalable tensor network methods (previous work was limited to ($l + m = 24$)) giving highly optimized state preparation circuits for optimally concentrated DPSS (Slepian) tapers. We provide an accompanying database of resource-efficient state preparation circuits for $l = 1, \dots, 50$ and $m = 1, \dots, 4$. Our circuits are shallow enough for a demonstration on near-term devices for small values of $l$. We run tQPE circuits for a 1-qubit system initialized in the ground state on Quantinuum's H2-2 quantum computer, demonstrating a $3\times$ to $18\times$ reduction in failure probability for $l=1,\ldots,10$ on a noisy device.

</details>


### [129] [One molecule, many outputs; many molecules, one output: exact diagnostics for atom-level compositional quantum encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15092)
*Gautami Sanjay Naik,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将QNLP的张量网络/量子线路建模迁移到分子上：重原子为词、键为"cup"连接线，使线路布线即分子图；精确刻画了这类线路的"不变量"，揭示同分异构体区分能力主要来自测量成功概率而非输出态，并证明无量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QNLP能把句子语法编译为含可训练角度的量子线路，作者希望把这一框架用于分子表示，并严格回答"这类线路究竟保留了分子的什么信息"、能否区分同分异构体，以及是否存在实际优势。

Method: 构造分子版QNLP线路：每个重原子视为带一根键对应"线"的词，每条键作为连接两线的"cup"，线路拓扑即分子图；比较lambeq的类型化角度共享与更简单的按元素/键型共享；在7对Tox21、1988对MOSES及232,191个MOSES分子上实验，并与1-WL图同构测试、朴素原子计数模型对比；同时论证SMILES书写顺序的影响与运行成功概率上界。

Result: 类型化角度共享可分开所有测试的同分异构体对；简单共享下输出态几乎相同，差异主要落在测量成功概率（标准读出忽略该量）；简单共享时输出仅依赖"以键为顶点"的图，可解释除一例外的全部相同输出对，含MOSES中1.19%分子，烷基侧链情形退化为树形、可区分至15个原子而16个起失效；小样本上该线路与1-WL互有胜负；SMILES顺序可改变输出；n条键时成功概率≤2^{-n}，无cup设计可规避该代价；Tox21上无线路优于简单原子计数模型。

Conclusion: 该分子QNLP线路的可区分性主要来自测量成功概率而非量子态本身，其表达能力可用"键图为顶点"的图不变量刻画；作者不主张任何量子优势，并给出无cup的改进设计以避免成功概率指数衰减。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) turns the grammar of a sentence into a tensor network and then into a quantum circuit with trainable angles. We build such a model for molecules: each heavy atom is a word with one wire per bond, and each bond is a ``cup'' joining two wires, so the circuit's wiring is the molecular graph. We work out exactly what these circuits keep. With the typed angle sharing of lambeq, the circuits separate every isomer pair we tested. Yet for isomers that simpler sharing confuses, the output states stay almost the same, before and after training. On 7 Tox21 pairs and 1,988 MOSES pairs, nearly all the difference lies in how often the measurements succeed, which a standard readout ignores. Simpler sharing, by element and bond type, shows why: the output then depends only on a graph whose vertices are the bonds. This explains all but one of the identical-output pairs we certify, including 1.19\% of 232{,}191 MOSES molecules, and for alkyl side chains it reduces to a tree shape that tells chains apart up to 15 atoms but not from 16. On small pairs, this circuit and the 1-WL graph test each separate pairs the other misses. We also show that SMILES writing order can change the output, that a run succeeds with probability at most $2^{-n}$ for $n$ bonds, and that a cup-free design avoids this cost. No circuit beats a simple atom-count model on Tox21, and we claim no quantum advantage.

</details>


### [130] [An Architecture for Real-Time Quantum Error Correction with Neutral Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15104)
*X. Wang,Y. Jing,Z. Yu,S. Wang,D. Niu,Y. Liu,T. Xiao,A. Yang,J. Han,Y. Wu,X. Lv*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出FPGA与GPU异构级联读出-解码架构，在相机行到达时即处理并进行下一逻辑块的解码，实现亚毫秒级后曝光处理延迟的实时综合征解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量与经典解码的延迟限制了自适应容错电路的执行；中性原子处理器中，空间分辨荧光探测与经典推断相互耦合，要求低延迟读出与解码。目标是在全帧读出完成前即完成逻辑块解码，从而支撑实时量子信息处理。

Method: 采用异构级联架构，在两个空间尺度上重叠采集与处理：FPGA在相机行数据到达时逐行处理，GPU在采集下一逻辑块期间解码已获取的逻辑块；异构组件间采用直接通信，保持千字节级数据微秒级传输，并支持可互换的纠错码与解码算法。通过硬件表征与基于电路级模拟综合征的解码基准进行评估。

Result: 千字节载荷传输时间达微秒量级；对选定小码距工作负载（d=3、5、7）实现亚毫秒级后曝光处理延迟；可在全帧读出完成前完成逻辑块解码。

Conclusion: 该异构架构实现了低延迟综合征解码，满足原子阵列容错计算所需的实时量子信息处理要求，并在纠错码与解码算法选择上具备灵活性。

Abstract: The latency of quantum measurement and classical decoding constrains the execution of adaptive fault-tolerant circuits. In neutral-atom processors, this requirement couples spatially resolved fluorescence detection to classical inference. Here we implement cascaded readout and decoding in a heterogeneous architecture that overlaps acquisition and processing at two spatial scales. Field-programmable gate arrays process camera rows as they arrive, while graphics processors decode an acquired logical block during acquisition of the next. This heterogeneous architecture offers flexibility in the choice of error-correcting codes and decoding algorithms. Direct communication preserves microsecond-scale transfer times for kilobyte payloads and supports interchangeable decoding algorithms. Hardware characterization and decoding benchmarks using circuit-level simulated syndromes yield a submillisecond post-exposure processing latency for selected small-code workloads ($d = 3, 5, 7$). By enabling logical blocks to be decoded before full-frame readout is complete, the architecture implements low-latency syndrome decoding, realizing real-time quantum information processing as required for fault-tolerant computation in atom arrays.

</details>


### [131] [Quantization Breaks Classical Mpemba Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15349)
*Yue Liu,Tan Van Vu,Frédéric van Wijland,Hisao Hayakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于Caldeira-Leggett模型建立热弛豫的量子-经典对应，发现经典局域能量扩散禁止单势阱与对称双势阱中的Mpemba效应，而量子非相邻能级跃迁可打破该限制；ℏ→0时非局域跃迁受抑制、经典约束恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应（初始更热的系统反而弛豫更快）在经典与量子体系中均被广泛研究，但要识别"量子化"本身带来的特有作用，必须与对应的经典动力学做一致比较；既有工作缺乏这种严格的量子-经典对应框架。

Method: 以Caldeira-Leggett模型为出发点，构建热弛豫的量子-经典对应关系；在单势阱与对称双势阱中分别分析经典（局域能量扩散）与量子（允许非相邻能级间直接跃迁）的弛豫路径，并考察ℏ→0的半经典极限。

Result: 经典局域能量扩散规则在单势阱和对称双势阱中禁止Mpemba效应；量子动力学因非相邻能级直接跃迁而突破局域扩散约束，在上述势场中可产生Mpemba效应；半经典极限ℏ→0下非局域跃迁逐渐被抑制，经典约束得以恢复。

Conclusion: 量子化的根本作用是开启经典所不具备的反常弛豫通道，而非仅仅改变弛豫速率；这为区分Mpemba效应中的量子特征提供了判据性框架。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, in which an initially hotter system relaxes faster than a colder one, has been widely studied in both classical and quantum systems, yet identifying the specific effects of quantization requires a consistent comparison with the corresponding classical dynamics. Starting from the Caldeira-Leggett model, we establish a quantum-classical correspondence for thermal relaxation. We find that the locality of classical energy diffusion forbids the Mpemba effect in single-well and symmetric double-well potentials. Conversely, quantum dynamics allows for direct transitions between nonadjacent energy levels, overcoming the constraints of classical local energy diffusion and enabling the Mpemba effect in these potentials. In the semiclassical limit $\hbar\to0$, the nonlocal transitions become increasingly suppressed, restoring the classical constraints. Our findings reveal that quantization fundamentally unlocks anomalous relaxation pathways, rather than merely modifying relaxation rates.

</details>


### [132] [Dissipaton-embedded quantum master equation for nonlinear system-bath coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15198)
*Zi-Fan Zhu,Yao Wang,Rui-Xue Xu,YiJing Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在近期建立的严格数学基础之上，为非线性系统-浴耦合情形给出了耗散子嵌入量子主方程（DE-QME），该主方程原则上精确，描述约化系统与所有耗散子自由度的联合动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散子理论提出十余年并已推广至非线性系统-浴耦合，数值结果虽支持其有效性，但一直缺乏严格的数学基础，尤其是非线性耦合情形；严格基础直到最近才建立，本文据此进一步给出相应的主方程表述。

Method: 基于新建立的严格数学基础，构造非线性系统-浴耦合下的耗散子嵌入量子主方程，将约化系统与全部耗散子自由度纳入统一的联合动力学描述，该方程在原则上为精确主方程。

Result: 得到了耗散子嵌入量子主方程（DE-QME），它在原则上精确地刻画约化系统与所有耗散子自由度的联合动力学，适用于非线性系统-浴耦合。

Conclusion: 耗散子理论在非线性系统-浴耦合下获得了严格的数学支撑与主方程形式，为开放量子系统的精确动力学研究提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: The dissipaton theory was proposed more than a decade ago as a statistical quasiparticle theory for open quantum systems and was soon extended to nonlinear system-bath coupling. Although numerical results provided strong evidence for its validity, a rigorous mathematical foundation, particularly for nonlinear system-bath coupling, was established only recently. On this basis, we formulate the dissipaton-embedded quantum master equation for nonlinear system-bath coupling. This in-principle exact master equation describes the joint dynamics of the reduced system and all dissipaton degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [133] [Trading Memory for Depth: Tight Bounds for LCU Block-Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15229)
*Sebastian Issel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为LCU的块编码引入"多寄存器并行"分支(stage-addressing)作为降低选择深度(select depth)的旋钮:将T个项分到g个并行寄存器中,选择深度紧致为Θ(C+log(g·max{n,m})),其中C为分箱makespan,并以经典多处理器调度给出工作下界。分箱最优时达到工作下界Σ_j d_j/g;两种选择器实现分别以空间换深度或反之,难以兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LCU块编码A=Σ_j α_j U_j在一个寄存器上串行执行U_j,选择深度等于总工作量Σ_j d_j,深度是块编码中唯一"没有调节旋钮"的约束。作者希望通过将项分配到多个并行寄存器(g-branched实现)来为深度提供一个可调参数,并用分箱(binning)策略控制深度与宽度的权衡。

Method: 1) 提出g分支(g-branched)LCU实现:至多g个oracle并行,选择深度上界/下界匹配为Θ(C+log(g·max{n,m})),C为分箱makespan,m为最大分箱。2) 在k-local原子oracle模型中给出紧性。3) 将工作下界归约为经典多处理器调度:任一实现诱导一个g机调度,构造可实现任意分箱,故最优分箱达到下界。4) 加法log项解释为光锥路由代价,与选择器编码无关,并对寄存器复用、测量、重置、隐形传态稳健;宽度至少gn。5) 引入McNaughton规则允许拆分oracle以达工作下界Σ_j d_j/g。6) 比较两种选择器实现:dense(Möttönen)内存紧但控制复用带来log log(m+1)因子;one-hot深度紧但需T个选择比特。

Result: 选择深度在g≥2时达到紧的Θ(C+log(g·max{n,m}));工作下界由-to-经典调度的双向归约确立,最优分箱可达Σ_j d_j/g(允许拆分时);1/α次归一化与成功概率不变;端点情形为顺序LCU(g=1)与全并行(g=T);dense选择器在标准LCU占用下内存紧但makespan多乘log log(m+1),one-hot选择器在T个选择比特下深度紧,二者无法同时最优。

Conclusion: 并行分箱为LCU块编码补充了"深度旋钮":通过g个并行寄存器与调度式分箱,可在保持次归一化与成功概率不变的前提下将选择深度从总工作量降至调度makespan加对数路由项,且当允许拆分oracle时可触及工作下界Σ_j d_j/g;但选择器实现存在空间-深度权衡,同时达到内存与深度最优仍需新构造。

Abstract: An LCU block-encoding of $A=\sum_jα_j U_j$ runs the $U_j$ in series on one register, so its select depth is the total work $\sum_j d_j$. Depth was the one block-encoding constraint with no knob: grouping the $T$ terms into $g$ registers that run in parallel gives it one, and the binning is the knob. For a $g$-branched realisation (up to $g$ oracles in parallel) the select depth is tight at $Θ(C+\log(g\,\max\{n,m\}))$ for $g\ge2$, with $C$ the binning makespan and $m$ the largest bin, in the $k$-local atomic-oracle model. The work floor is a two-way reduction to classical multiprocessor scheduling: every realisation \emph{induces} a $g$-machine schedule, and the construction realises any binning, so an optimal binning attains it. The additive logarithmic routing term is a light-cone cost, independent of the selector encoding and robust to register reuse, measurement, reset, and teleportation; width is at least $gn$. The standard $1/α$ subnormalisation and success probability are unchanged; the endpoints are sequential LCU ($g{=}1$) and the fully parallel realisation ($g{=}T$). Allowing the oracles to be split (McNaughton's rule) reaches the work floor $\sum_j d_j/g$. Two selector realisations trade space for depth: a dense (Möttönen) one is memory-tight at the standard LCU footprint but its multiplexed controls cost a $\log\log(m{+}1)$ factor on the makespan; a one-hot one is depth-tight at $T$ selector qubits. Neither reaches both at once.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-head Quantum Neural Networks: Replacing Transformer Blocks with NISQ Modules inside a 30-Billion-Parameter LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15250)
*Maforikan Amoussou,Borja Aizpurua,Sukhbinder Singh,Román Orús,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出多头量子神经网络（multi-head QNN）：将隐空间分为k个子头、各角度编码进n_q比特寄存器并行处理，经经典mixer耦合，用约14.5万参数替换Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct中连续8个transformer块（占16.33%参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子模块嵌入生产级LLM的瓶颈在于隐层编码：振幅编码电路深度随宽度爆炸，角度编码比特数爆炸，两者在真实宽度下均不可行；目标是构建NISQ兼容、参数高效、可替换transformer块的量子模块。

Method: 将D=2048隐空间划分为k=32个不相交头，每头以角度编码进n_q=8比特寄存器（L=4层电路），并行处理，头间用受约束的经典线性层（mixers）耦合；用精确态矢模拟进行经典训练，并在156比特IBM Heron处理器上运行融合checkpoint的量子头。以消融实验（关闭纠缠、冻结初始/随机角度、参数匹配的经典MLP）作对照。

Result: 单模块145,281可训练参数即可替换Qwen3-30B中连续8个块，精度损失很小；单块替换时量子头（电路+学习读出）而非mixer承担功能恢复；融合跨度下仅mixer即可逼近完整模块1–3%困惑度内。但对照显示：关闭纠缠（2种子）、冻结于初始或均匀随机角度（1种子）在留出困惑度上均优于完整模块，参数匹配经典MLP亦领先数个百分点，故纠缠量子电路的增益未被确证。

Conclusion: 该工作证明NISQ级量子模块可安装进生产规模LLM并在真实硬件（156比特IBM Heron）上执行，但在当前设置下未显示量子纠缠电路相对经典基线的明确优势，量子效益的归因仍待建立。

Abstract: We introduce the multi-head quantum neural network (QNN), a quantum module compatible with noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices that can replace transformer blocks inside pre-trained large language models (LLMs) with little loss in accuracy and with far fewer parameters than the blocks it replaces. Encoding a model's $D$ hidden neurons into a qubit register is the obstacle: amplitude encoding scales poorly in circuit depth and angle encoding in qubit count, so neither is practical at production-scale width. We instead partition the hidden space into $k$ disjoint heads, angle-encode each into an $n_q$-qubit register, process the heads in parallel, and couple them through constrained classical linear layers, the mixers. Performing surgery on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, we show that a single multi-head QNN with $145\,281$ trainable parameters ($D=2048$, $k=32$, $n_q=8$, $L=4$) can replace a contiguous span of eight transformer blocks, displacing $16.33\%$ of the model's parameters. Ablations show that, for a single replaced block, the quantum heads (circuits followed by a learned readout), and not the classical mixers alone, recover the displaced function, whereas for fused spans the mixers alone come within $1$-$3\%$ of the full module in perplexity. Whether this benefit is specific to trained, entangling quantum circuits is not established: controls that switch the entangler off (two seeds), or freeze the circuit at its initial or at uniformly random angles (one seed), beat the full module on held-out perplexity, and a parameter-matched classical multilayer perceptron remains a few percent ahead of the full module. We train classically with exact statevector simulation, then execute the quantum heads of our fusion checkpoints on a $156$-qubit IBM Heron processor, showing that NISQ-scale quantum modules can be installed in a production-scale LLM and run on hardware.

</details>


### [135] [Device-independent witnesses of genuine multipartite entanglement and nonlocality for GHZ states and Bell-pair tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15283)
*Josep Batle,Tomasz Białecki,Adam Bednorz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文推导并比较了GHZ态与贝尔对树结构的真多体纠缠(GME)与真多体非局域性(GMNL)的设备无关见证：给出四方测量上下文的二结果族具有与粒子数无关的紧致双可分界，并区分于Svetlichny-混合界；对GHZ态给出2N上下文GMNL见证并与MABK、Svetlichny检验对比；对贝尔对树用CHSH与链式不等式之和分别认证GME与GMNL；另证明四上下文网络界 I_λ≤2（N≥3, λ≥2）并约束由二分源组装网络的MABK得分；在IBM Quantum上对最多10比特GHZ态完成基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关地认证真多体纠缠与真多体非局域性是量子信息基础与量子网络验证的核心问题。现有见证（如MABK、Svetlichny）的紧致性、对通信模型的假设以及与网络纠缠结构的关系尚不清晰，且缺乏在真实硬件上的系统比较，因此需要统一框架推导并对比不同见证的界，并检验其可行性。

Method: 构造基于四种全局测量上下文的二结果(non-signaling型)见证族，推导其紧致双可分界；将该GME界与允许任意单侧通信的Svetlichny-混合界作区分。对GHZ态进一步构造2N上下文GMNL见证，并与MABK、Svetlichny检验比较。对贝尔对树结构，分别采用CHSH不等式之和（GME）与链式不等式之和（GMNL），明确输入协议与多比特局域输出。解析证明四上下文网络界 I_λ≤2（∀N≥3, λ≥2）并给出由二分源组装网络的MABK得分上界。最后在IBM Quantum上对最多10比特GHZ制备进行实验基准测试。

Result: 四上下文二结果族的双可分界为紧致且与粒子数无关，可清晰区别于Svetlichny-混合界；GHZ态下2N上下文GMNL见证与MABK、Svetlichny检验形成明确对比；贝尔对树中CHSH之和与链式不等式之和分别成功认证GME与GMNL；证明四上下文网络界 I_λ≤2 对所有N≥3、λ≥2成立，并给出二分源网络MABK得分界；在IBM Quantum上至10比特GHZ态上完成MABK、四上下文与Svetlichny见证的基准测试。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一的设备无关GME/GMNL见证框架：四上下文族具有粒子数无关的紧致界，能区分GME与允许单侧通信的混合模型；结合2N上下文GHZ见证、贝尔对树的CHSH/链式不等式方案以及网络界的解析结果，构成对多体纠缠与非局域性认证的系统性工具集，并在真实量子硬件上验证了其可扩展性与实用性。

Abstract: We derive and compare device-independent witnesses of genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) and genuine multipartite nonlocality (GMNL) for GHZ states and trees of Bell pairs. A binary-outcome family using four global measurement contexts has the tight biseparable bound independent of the number of parties. We distinguish these GME bounds from Svetlichny-hybrid bounds, which permit arbitrary communication within either side of a bipartition. For GHZ states, we also give a $2N$-context GMNL witness and compare it with MABK and Svetlichny tests. For Bell-pair trees, sums of CHSH and chained inequalities certify GME and GMNL, respectively, with explicitly specified input protocols and multi-bit local outputs. Separately, we prove the sharp four-context network bound $I_λ\leq2$ for every $N\geq3$ and $λ\geq2$, and bound the MABK scores of quantum networks assembled from bipartite sources. We successfully benchmark MABK, four-context, and Svetlichny witnesses on IBM Quantum GHZ preparations up to $10$ parties, under the local-measurement and sampling assumptions.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum Recurrent Architectures for Financial Time Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15298)
*Danyal Maheshwari,Gerhard Hellstern,Martin Zaefferer,Martin Braun,Tanja Döhler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估量子-经典混合循环架构（QLSTM与带滞后输入的QRC）在金融时间序列预测中的表现，发现在低维设定下量子模型相对经典模型无精度或参数效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"量子循环架构能带来金融时序预测的精度提升或参数效率优势"这一常见主张，需通过严格对照实验加以验证，尤其是将量子贡献与参数量、接口架构等混杂因素区分开来。

Method: 构建QLSTM与QRC两种混合模型：经典滞后向量经振幅编码进入量子态，QLSTM门与QRC储层由参数化量子电路实现，接口层与读出层由经典优化器训练。数据为经高斯过程–隐马尔可夫生成器扩展至1056个月的月度营收序列，采用固定时间顺序70/15/15划分与一步预测。对照组包括全尺寸LSTM、参数量与接口架构匹配的经典控制模型、同预算小型普通LSTM、经典储层计算，共10个随机种子，并与持久性、季节朴素及SARIMA基准比较；另做24个月滚动起点回测。

Result: 参数仅2,105的QLSTM与参数量为其126倍的LSTM统计上不可区分，但同时与匹配的经典控制和同预算普通LSTM也不可区分，故参数效率无法归因于量子电路；量子储层在全部10个种子下均劣于同等特征维度的经典储层，且劣于SARIMA；所有循环模型与两种经典储层相对SARIMA的RMSE约改善2%；滚动回测中经典LSTM的退化慢于QLSTM。

Conclusion: 在该低维设定下未发现量子架构带来精度或参数效率优势的证据，量子收益可能来自接口/架构设计而非量子电路本身。

Abstract: This study evaluates quantum classical hybrid recurrent architectures for financial time-series forecasting: a Quantum Long Short-Term Memory (QLSTM) network and Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) with lag-based inputs. Classical lag vectors are encoded into quantum states via amplitude encoding, which compactly represents normalized observations but requires nontrivial state preparation; the QLSTM gates and the QRC reservoir are implemented with parameterized quantum circuits, while classical optimizers train the interface and readout layers. On a monthly revenue series extended to 1056 months by a Gaussian-process--hidden-Markov generator, with a fixed chronological 70/15/15 split and one-step-ahead forecasts, we compare both models against a full-size LSTM, a classical control matched in parameter budget and interface architecture, a small plain LSTM, and classical reservoir computing, over ten seeds and against persistence, seasonal-naive and SARIMA benchmark forecasts. The QLSTM, with 2,105 parameters, is statistically indistinguishable from an LSTM 126 times larger, but equally indistinguishable from the matched classical control and from a plain LSTM of the same budget, so its parameter efficiency cannot be attributed to the quantum circuits. The quantum reservoir is worse than a classical reservoir of equal feature dimension in all ten seeds and worse than SARIMA, whereas all recurrent models and both classical reservoirs improve on SARIMA by about 2\% in RMSE. In a 24-month rolling-origin backtest the classical LSTMs degrade more slowly than the QLSTM. We find no evidence of a quantum accuracy or parameter-efficiency advantage in this low-dimensional setting.

</details>


### [137] [On the domain of the effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian in quantum markovian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15322)
*Franco Fagnola,Zheng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子马尔可夫半群无界生成元的GKLS表示中，算子 $G=G_0-\mathrm{i}H$ 的定义域是否等于 $\Dom(G_0)\cap\Dom(H)$，证明了在有限模玻色Fock空间中 $G_0,H$ 为湮灭与产生算子的二次自伴多项式且满足附加条件时该等式成立，并给出反例说明一般情形下不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKLS表示依赖一个生成强连续压缩半群的算符 $G$，形式上为负自伴算子 $G_0$ 与 $-\mathrm{i}H$ 之和；当两者均无界时，其定义域关系（特别是 $\Dom(G)=\Dom(G_0)\cap\Dom(H)$ 是否成立）是刻画生成元结构的关键，而这一般并不自动成立，需要明确可判定的充分条件。

Method: 在有限模玻色Fock空间中，取 $G_0$ 与 $H$ 为关于湮灭、产生算子的二次自伴多项式，分别在 $G_0\leq 0$ 或对 commutator $[G_0,H]$ 施加某种 half bound（半有界型估计）的假设下，通过算子定义域分析证明 $\Dom(G)=\Dom(G_0)\cap\Dom(H)$。

Result: 在 $G_0\leq0$ 或 commutator 满足特定半界条件时，$\Dom(G)=\Dom(G_0)\cap\Dom(H)$ 成立；同时构造反例表明该结论在一般情形（条件不满足时）不成立。

Conclusion: 二次多项式型玻色算子在一定附加条件下其和算子的定义域可精确分解，但该性质并非普遍成立，说明GKLS表示中对 $G$ 的定义域需逐案验证，所给充分条件与反例共同界定了结论的适用范围。

Abstract: The GKLS representation of the unbounded generator of a quantum Markov semigroup crucially depends on an unbounded operator $G$, generating a strongly continuous contraction semigroup on the underlying Hilbert space, which is formally the sum of a negative self-adjoint operator $G_0$ and $-\mathrm{i}H$ with $H$ symmetric.
  In many situations it is useful to know the relationships between the domains of the three operators, in particular whether $\Dom(G)=\Dom(G_0)\cap\Dom(H)$ when both $G_0$ and $H$ are unbounded. In this note we show that this happens when $G_0$ and $H$ are self-adjoint quadratic polynomials in annihilation and creation operators in a finite-mode bosonic Fock space and $G_0\leq0$ or assuming a certain half bound on the commutator of $G_0$ and $H$.
  A counterexample shows that the conclusion does not hold in general situations.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum tunneling of a measurement-driven active particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16626)
*Tobias Nadolny,Matteo Brunelli,Christoph Bruder,Michel Fruchart*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出并论证了一种"量子主动粒子"：借助自旋-轨道耦合与阻尼使粒子运动锁定于自旋取向，并通过量子Zeno效应下的连续测量使取向保持持久，从而在单条量子轨迹中呈现run-and-tumble式主动运动，并在势垒处表现出积累与隧穿增强的穿越率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典主动物质的核心特征是"运动方向由自身取向决定且取向具有持久性"，但在量子层面如何实现这一性质尚不明确；同时，量子粒子遇势垒通常表现为反射或隧穿，缺乏类似主动粒子的势垒附近聚集行为。

Method: 以带自旋的量子粒子为模型，利用自旋-轨道耦合与阻尼将平动速度锁定于自旋取向；再通过连续测量（量子Zeno效应）抑制自旋翻转，使取向在时间上保持持久；随后分析单条量子轨迹的统计特征，并研究粒子入射势垒时的输运行为，与自由量子粒子及相应的经典主动粒子作对比。

Result: 单条量子轨迹呈现主动的run-and-tumble运动；粒子撞击势垒时，其持久速度导致在势垒附近的积累，而不同于自由量子粒子的反射行为；量子隧穿使该主动粒子的透射率高于对应的经典粒子。

Conclusion: 该工作将主动物质的关键属性（取向决定运动、取向持久性）成功移植到量子体系，表明连续测量可作为实现取向持久性的有效机制，并揭示量子隧穿对主动输运的增强作用，为量子主动物质的研究提供了可操作范式。

Abstract: Active particles can exhibit persistent motion set by their orientation. We demonstrate this key property for a quantum active particle: The particle's motion is locked to the orientation of its spin due to spin-orbit coupling and damping, and the orientation is made persistent through continuous measurement via the quantum Zeno effect. As a result, individual quantum trajectories feature an active run-and-tumble motion. When the particle impinges on a potential barrier, its persistent velocity leads to accumulation near the barrier, in contrast with the reflection of a free quantum particle. We show that quantum tunneling enhances the active particle's transmission rate compared to that of an analogous classical particle.

</details>


### [139] [Hybrid Classical-Quantum Optimization for CVRPTW: A Systems Framework with Provable Feasibility and Empirical Hardware Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15343)
*Syamasundar Gopasana,Swatantra Singh,Rohan Joydhar,Shubhashis Sengupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出MILP+车辆级QUBO的混合经典-量子架构求解大规模CVRPTW，在伦敦数据基准上SQBM+实现2.80%/0.22%车队距离缩减，QAOA可靠约束满足的硬件边界为4客户（16量子比特）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模带时间窗容量约束车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）因严格运营约束与规模限制，精确方法与启发式方法均受制约；同时单体式CVRPTW的QUBO建模面临变量规模膨胀与约束难以强制的困难，需探索可扩展的混合求解范式。

Method: 采用经典-量子混合分解架构：上层用混合整数线性规划（MILP）处理分配层问题，下层构造车辆级QUBO子问题；分别使用Toshiba SQBM+，以及在Rigetti Ankaa-3与IQM Emerald量子处理器上运行QAOA求解。在5个基于伦敦交通与位置数据的真实基准（10–70客户）上评估，并设计强制可行性与相对经典基线单调改进的验证流水线。

Result: SQBM+取得经验证的车队距离缩减：25客户实例2.80%（7,927 m），55客户实例0.22%（1,186 m）；QAOA实验确定当前硬件可靠满足约束的边界为≤4客户（16量子比特）子问题；诊断表明分配层时间窗紧致度、而非局部路径优化，是主要可扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 混合经典-量子优化在近期具有实用价值，但当前量子硬件存在明确的经验极限；未来改进重点应放在分配层时间窗处理而非局部路由量子化。

Abstract: Solving large-scale Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problems with Time Windows (CVRPTW) remains challenging because strict operational constraints and scale limit both exact and heuristic methods. We present a hybrid classical-quantum architecture combining Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) with vehicle-level Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO), solved using Toshiba SQBM+ and QAOA on Rigetti Ankaa-3 and IQM Emerald quantum processors. The decomposition avoids the variable growth and constraint-enforcement difficulties of monolithic CVRPTW QUBO formulations. We evaluate the framework on five realistic benchmarks derived from London traffic and location data, spanning 10--70 customers, using a validation pipeline enforcing feasibility and monotone improvement over classical baselines. SQBM+ achieves verified fleet-distance reductions of 2.80% (7,927 m) for the 25-customer instance and 0.22% (1,186 m) for the 55-customer instance. QAOA experiments establish a current hardware boundary for reliable constraint satisfaction at subproblems of up to 4 customers (16 qubits). Diagnostic results identify assignment-level time-window tightness, rather than local route optimization, as the dominant scalability bottleneck. The results demonstrate a practical near-term role for hybrid classical-quantum optimization while establishing empirical limits for current quantum hardware.

</details>


### [140] [When Do Quantum Correlations Matter for Quantum Annealing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15351)
*Rongfeng Xie,Evgenii Ievlev,Alex Kamenev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 比较量子退火(QA)与保留哈密顿量与退火调度但不含非局域量子关联的经典"量子启发退火"(QIA)，发现二者的标度差异强烈依赖模型：QA在干净低维系统中占优，维度升高至三维及以上时优势可忽略，无序与阻挫进一步缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联能否为非绝热退火带来标度优势仍是开放问题，且缺乏剥离量子关联的严格经典对照基线，难以界定量子效应的定性作用范围。

Method: 构造与QA共享同一哈密顿量结构与退火调度的经典协议QIA，仅排除非局域量子关联；在维度、无序度与阻挫各异的模型族上系统比较两者的标度行为。

Result: QA在干净低维系统中显示出明确标度优势；随维度增加优势减弱，三維及以上可忽略；无序与阻挫进一步压缩差异，总体呈现强模型依赖性。

Conclusion: 量子关联仅在对维度和无序敏感的特定区间对标度产生定性影响，并为评估量子退火中量子关联的作用提供了经典基线。

Abstract: Whether quantum correlations give non-adiabatic annealing a scaling advantage remains an open question. We address it by comparing quantum annealing (QA) with quantum-inspired annealing (QIA), a classical protocol that retains the Hamiltonian structure and annealing schedule of QA while excluding nonlocal quantum correlations. Across models varying in dimensionality, disorder, and frustration, we find that the difference in scaling between the two protocols is strongly model dependent. QA shows a clear advantage in clean, low-dimensional systems, but the advantage diminishes with increasing dimensionality and becomes negligible in three or more dimensions. Disorder and frustration further narrow the difference. These results identify where quantum correlations have a qualitative effect on annealing scaling and provide a classical baseline for assessing their role.

</details>


### [141] [A Unified Benchmarking Study of Variational Quantum Eigensolver Ansatz for Electronic Structure Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15365)
*Julie Maria Raju,Abhoy Kole,Julen Larrucea,Kamalika Datta,Lennart Weingarten,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 系统对比UCCSD、fermionic ADAPT-VQE、BasicPCA HEA与激发保持HEA在H₂、LiH、N₂上的电路深度、参数量、基态能量误差与NISQ可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子关联增强使经典电子结构计算成本高昂，VQE在NISQ设备上有潜力；但ansatz选择对性能与可扩展性至关重要，现有化学启发式ansatz、自适应方案与HEA缺乏统一比较，各方法在不同分子尺寸下的可扩展性与权衡不明。

Method: 开展系统性基准测试：在H₂、LiH、N₂等不同规模分子上比较UCCSD、fermionic ADAPT-VQE、BasicPCA HEA及激发保持HEA（Excitation Preserving HEA），评估电路深度、参数数量、基态能量误差与NISQ可行性之间的权衡。

Result: ADAPT-VQE以更低资源开销达到与UCCSD相当的精度，但在约10电子规模下因电路复杂度上升而变得不实用；HEA降低硬件成本但参数更多、精度下降且性能强烈依赖分子尺寸；激发保持ansatz在H₂与LiH上未达化学精度，在N₂上失效，凸显表达性与可训练性需平衡。

Conclusion: 结果揭示各VQE ansatz在精度与资源效率之间的关键权衡，指出需改进ADAPT-VQE可扩展性与HEA精度，并解决近期量子硬件的优化难题。

Abstract: Electronic structure calculations become computationally expensive on classical computers as electron correlation increases. Quantum computing offers a promising alternative through hybrid quantum-classical Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) on Noisy-Intermediate Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. The choice of ansatz plays a pivotal role in the performance and scalability of the VQE, yet selecting an optimal ansatz remains challenging. Existing approaches including chemically inspired ansatz, adaptive schemes and Hardware Efficient Ansätze (HEA) lack a unified comparison. This gap makes the scalability and trade-offs of each method unclear for varying molecular sizes.
  This work presents a systematic benchmarking study comparing Unitary Couples Cluster Singles and Doubles Ansatz (UCCSD), fermionic ADAPT-VQE, BasicPCA HEA, and Excitation Preserving HEA across molecules of varying size like $H_2$, $LiH$, $N_2$. We evaluate trade-offs among circuit depth, parameter count, ground-state energy error, and NISQ feasibility. ADAPT-VQE achieves accuracy comparable to UCCSD with lower resource overhead, but becomes impractical for modest system sizes ($\sim$10 electrons) due to increasing circuit complexity. In contrast, HEA reduces hardware costs but requires more parameters and sacrifices accuracy, with performance strongly dependent on molecular size. The Excitation Preserving ansatz remains below chemical accuracy for $H_2$ and $LiH$, and fails for $N_2$, underscoring the need to balance expressibility and trainability. Overall, these results highlight key trade-offs between accuracy and resource efficiency across VQE ansätze, emphasizing the need to improve ADAPT-VQE scalability and HEA accuracy while addressing optimization challenges for near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [142] [Comprehensive quantum electrodynamics of fast electrons interacting with dissipative nanostructures and quantum light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15967)
*Alessandro Ciattoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于修正Langevin噪声形式（MLNF）与精确二阶Magnus展开，给出快速电子与纳米结构相互作用中横向动量分辨能量交换概率的闭式非微扰母公式，揭示真空散射场与热涨落的可分离性导致的Stokes–Anti-Stokes不对称与广义细致平衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宏观量子电动力学（MQED）处理真空中有限物体时，需借助渐近极限手续来引入自由空间辐射模式，缺乏对这些模式的显式、独立处理，因此需要一种从一开始就显式包含渐近散射模式的理论框架。

Method: 采用修正Langevin噪声形式（MLNF），对渐近散射模式与耗散材料源产生的电磁场分别独立量子化；结合精确二阶Magnus展开，推导横向动量分辨能量交换概率的闭式、非微扰主方程，并分别讨论未照明与照明两种情形。

Result: 在未照明情形下，有限温度时真空散射项与热材料涨落项在数学上不再重新组合为并矢格林函数的虚部总项；该分离不违反涨落–耗散定理，因零温散射场作为独立动力学系统使体系处于非全局热平衡。由此得到内禀Stokes–Anti-Stokes不对称：能量损失与热能量增益呈现不同的横向衍射图样，并建立支配纳米尺度电子测温表观温度辐射降低的广义细致平衡关系。在照明情形下，该框架将量子光学态严格纳入宏观耗散电动力学。

Conclusion: MLNF可显式分离真空散射模式与材料热涨落，突破标准MQED对渐近极限手续的依赖，为非平衡电子–纳米结构能量交换、内禀谱不对称及照明条件下的量子光学–耗散电动力学统一描述提供严格基础。

Abstract: Theoretical descriptions of the quantum electrodynamical interaction between fast electrons and nanostructures conventionally rely on standard macroscopic quantum electrodynamics (MQED), which, when applied to bounded objects in vacuum, formally requires an asymptotic limiting procedure to account for free-space radiation modes. To explicitly incorporate these modes from the outset, we employ the modified Langevin noise formalism (MLNF), which independently quantizes both the asymptotic scattering modes and the electromagnetic field generated by dissipative material sources. Utilizing an exact second-order Magnus expansion, we derive a closed-form, nonperturbative master formula for the transverse-momentum-resolved energy exchange probability. In the unilluminated regime, we demonstrate that at finite nanostructure temperatures, the vacuum scattering and thermal material fluctuations do not mathematically recombine into the total imaginary part of the dyadic Green's function. This breakdown does not violate the Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem, because the explicit zero-temperature scattering field acts as an independent dynamical system that maintains the configuration out of global thermodynamic equilibrium. This fundamental separation reveals an intrinsic Stokes--Anti-Stokes asymmetry, yielding distinct transverse diffraction patterns for energy loss and thermal energy gain, and establishing a generalized detailed-balance relation that governs the radiative reduction of the apparent temperature in nanoscale electron thermometry. In the illuminated regime, our framework rigorously incorporates quantum-optical states into macroscopic dissipative electrodynamics, demonstrating that...

</details>


### [143] [Full Mesh Dynamic Four-User Entanglement-based Switched QKD Network Integrated with SD-KMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15374)
*Nikolas Makris,Persefoni Konteli,Zafeirios Georgiadis,Alkinoos Papageorgopoulos,Grigorios Anastasiou,Panagiotis Papanastasiou,Konstantinos Tsimvrakidis,George T. Kanellos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 演示了一个基于偏振纠缠的可重构四用户全互联量子密钥分发网络，并引入软件定义密钥管理系统来编排动态光交换与实时密钥供给，服务于抗量子应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发要走向实际部署，需要从点对点链路扩展到多用户、可重构的网络，并在其上实现密钥的自动管理与面向应用的实时分发。

Method: 采用偏振纠缠光源构建四用户全互联（full-mesh）QKD网络，通过光交换实现路径的动态重构；上层部署软件定义密钥管理系统，负责编排光交换配置并进行实时密钥供给。

Result: 实现了四用户任意两两之间的全互联量子密钥分发，并借助软件定义控制平面完成动态光路切换与实时密钥分发，验证了网络可重构性与密钥供给的联动能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明偏振纠缠QKD可与软件定义的密钥管理/光交换控制有效集成，构成可重构的多用户量子安全网络架构，为抗量子应用提供按需密钥服务。

Abstract: We demonstrate a reconfigurable, four-user, full-mesh quantum key distribution network using polarization entanglement, integrated with a software-defined key management system to orchestrate dynamic optical switching and real-time key provisioning for quantum-safe applications.

</details>


### [144] [The importance of shear strain in narrow Si/SiGe quantum wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16015)
*Hudaiba Soomro,Talise Oh,M. A. Eriksson,Benjamin D. Woods,Mark Friesen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对 Si/SiGe 量子阱异质结构，研究长波长谷耦合项 k₁ 与剪切应变耦合的机制，指出金属顶栅诱导的剪切应变可能足以激活 k₁ 谷耦合，从而无需"超陡"界面即可获得大谷能谷分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有多种 Si/SiGe 量子阱异质结构方案旨在产生控制导带谷耦合的两个波矢 k₀ 与 k₁，以改善量子点自旋量子比特的性能；但大谷分裂通常被认为依赖"超陡"界面，工艺难度大。作者希望放宽这一约束。

Method: 聚焦与剪切应变配对时耦合谷、并主导 Dresselhaus 自旋轨道耦合的长波长 k₁ 项，分析金属顶栅所诱导剪切应变对 k₁ 谷耦合机制的激活作用，并提出基于窄量子阱的异质结构方案。

Result: 金属顶栅诱导的剪切应变可能足以激活 k₁ 谷耦合机制，从而弱化对超陡界面的需求；所提出的窄量子阱方案在配合剪切应变时有望同时给出大的确定性谷分裂、大的自旋轨道耦合以及显著的 g 因子修正。

Conclusion: 剪切应变可作为激活 k₁ 谷耦合的有效手段，使 Si/SiGe 量子阱在不具备超陡界面的条件下仍能实现大谷分裂与强自旋轨道耦合，对实验实现量子点自旋量子比特具有重要价值。

Abstract: Several heterostructure profiles have been proposed for Si/SiGe quantum wells, to improve the operation of quantum-dot spin qubits. These structures share a common goal of generating either of the two wave vectors that govern the conduction-band valley coupling, dubbed $k_0$ and $k_1$. Here, we focus on the long-wavelength $k_1$ term that couples valleys when paired with shear strain, and which governs the Dresselhaus spin-orbit coupling. We show that shear strain induced by metal top gates may be enough to activate the $k_1$ valley-coupling mechanism, mitigating the need for "ultrasharp" interfaces, as commonly assumed for large valley energy splittings. We propose a scheme featuring narrow quantum wells that does not require ultrasharp interfaces, but should still provide large deterministic valley splittings (when paired with shear strain), and large spin-orbit couplings and $g$-factor corrections, which are of great interest for experimental implementations.

</details>


### [145] [The Quantum Euclid Algorithm and its Application to Factoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15387)
*Asma Al-Qasimi,Daniel F. V. James*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出欧几里得算法（最大公约数）的量子实现，其中参与的一个整数是两个奇素数之积，并讨论其用于量子分解的可能性：无需模幂运算，因子可直接由测量获得，但方法对大规模数目的扩展性不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规量子分解（如Shor类算法）依赖模幂运算并需要量子傅里叶变换等重资源步骤；作者希望探索一条更直接的路径，即把GCD计算量子化，从而在分解问题中绕开模幂运算，使因子通过测量直接显现。

Method: 构造欧几里得算法的量子版本：以“一个整数为两个奇素数之积”为输入设定，通过量子线路实现反复的取模/减法与交换等GCD迭代步骤，最后以测量读取结果；并将该量子GCD用作分解原语，替代模幂运算环节。

Result: 在该特定输入情形下，因子可通过测量直接揭示，且不需要模幂运算；但作者明确指出该方法对大规模整数的扩展性不具优势，因此并非可扩展的分解方案。

Conclusion: 量子GCD为量子分解提供了一种概念上简洁的替代思路，绕过了模幂运算并让因子直接由测量给出；然而其资源/复杂度随规模增长不利，实用性有限，更适合作为原理性讨论而非可扩展算法。

Abstract: We present a quantum implementation of the Euclid algorithm for calculating the Greatest Common Divisor when one of the integers involved is a compound of two odd-valued primes. We discuss a possible application to the quantum factoring problem, for which modular exponentiation is no longer required, and the factors are revealed directly by measurements, although the method does not scale for large numbers in a favourable manner.

</details>


### [146] [Large-scale characterisation of silicon T centres with tuning-free broadband cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15412)
*Christian F. Primavera,Matteo Pirro,Elena Volkova,Joan Mascaró Rosselló,Cas C. P. E. Hoppenbrouwers,Bram Zijlstra,Ronald Hanson,Carlos Errando-Herranz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作开发低Q值共聚焦可寻址微腔阵列（400个腔），无需逐个调谐即可将T色心零声子线信号中位数提升6.5倍（测量加速42倍），并借此推断样品中T色心密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中T色心兼具O波段光跃迁与长相干电子/核自旋，且与光子学和CMOS工艺兼容，但其零声子线（ZPL）光子发射弱导致测量缓慢。高Q腔虽可增强ZPL发射，却需逐个与T色心波长对准，难以对大量色心进行统计研究。

Method: 设计并制备低Q值、共聚焦可寻址的微腔阵列：共400个腔，失谐量为0.0±1.1 nm，平均品质因子298±78；高定位精度与中等Q值相结合消除了逐个调谐的需求。并结合蒙特卡洛模拟对腔统计特性进行分析。

Result: 阵列使T色心信号中位数增强6.5倍，对应42倍测量加速；通过与蒙特卡洛模拟对比，推断样品中T色心密度为4.2(＋1.3/－0.8) μm⁻²，且不依赖对装置收集效率的估计。

Conclusion: 低Q共聚焦可寻址腔阵列提供了一种免调谐、可规模化并行增强T色心发射的方案，既提升测量通量，又可通过腔统计特性开展新的定量测量（如色心密度），为T色心的大规模统计表征奠定基础。

Abstract: T centres in silicon combine an optical transition in the telecom O-band with a long-lived electron and nuclear spin in a photonics- and CMOS-compatible host. However, weak emission of photons in the zero-phonon line (ZPL) makes measurements slow. Cavities can increase the ZPL photon emission, but high-Q cavities require individual tuning to the T centre wavelength, hindering statistical understanding of T centre properties. Here, we overcome this limitation by developing low-Q confocally-addressable cavities. We create an array of 400 cavities, detuned $0.0\pm 1.1$ nm from the T centre, with an average quality factor of $298\pm 78$. The combination of high accuracy and moderate quality factors removes the need for individual tuning. The array yields a median T centre signal increase of $6.5\times$, corresponding to a $42\times$ measurement speedup. Studying the cavity statistics enables new measurements: comparing to a Monte Carlo simulation we infer a T centre density in the sample of $4.2^{+1.3}_{-0.8}~\mathrm{μm^{-2}}$, without relying on an estimate for the setup collection efficiency.

</details>


### [147] [Extractable Work from Collective Dark-State Charging of Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15416)
*Jian-Jian Cheng,Ming-Liang Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出由集合充电器与集合电池共腔耦合的量子电池方案，利用集体衰减的破坏性干涉选出可调暗态族，实现受控的激发转移；并证明该方案下电池被动能始终低于单比特激发能 ω₀，而总储能随 N 线性增长、全局遍历功与储能之比趋近于 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池研究关注如何在多体体系中实现高效、可控的充电，并提取尽可能多的有用功（ergotropy）。为此需要一种机制，既能控制从充电单元到储能单元的激发转移，又能在多比特规模下避免被动（不可提取）能量随电池尺寸膨胀。

Method: 将两个超导比特系综（一个作充电器、一个作电池）耦合到同一个有耗谐振腔。利用两者集体衰减振幅之间的破坏性干涉，筛选出一族可调的多体暗态，充当充电器到电池的激发转移通道。分析集体充电动力学对电池约化态的限制：保留对称 Dicke 子空间，使约化态至多含 N+1 个非零本征值，并结合电池第一激发能级的 N 重简并。

Result: 在固定每个电池比特平均储能且非零的条件下，整个电池的被动能始终低于单比特激发能 ω₀，与电池尺寸 N 无关；总储能随 N 线性增长；同时全局 ergotropy 与总储能之比随 N 增大趋近于 1，即储能几乎全部可提取为有用功。

Conclusion: 该共腔双系综暗态方案提供了一种可扩展的量子电池充电机制：被动能受 ω₀ 上界约束而不随尺寸恶化，储能线性增长且几乎完全可提取，显示出多体集体效应在量子电池性能上的优势。

Abstract: We consider two ensembles of superconducting qubits coupled to a common lossy resonator, one serving as the charger and the other the battery. Destructive interference between their collective decay amplitudes selects a tunable family of many-body dark states, allowing controlled excitation transfer from the charger to the battery. The collective charging dynamics preserves the symmetric Dicke subspace of the battery, so its reduced state has at most $N+1$ nonzero eigenvalues. Together with the $N$-fold degeneracy of the battery's first excited energy level, this restriction keeps the passive energy of the entire battery below the single-qubit excitation energy $ω_0$, independently of the battery size. When the average stored energy per battery qubit is fixed and nonzero, the total stored energy grows linearly with $N$, while the ratio of global ergotropy to stored energy approaches unity as $N$ increases.

</details>


### [148] [Gatemon qubits based on in-situ ternary InAsSb/Al nanowire Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16595)
*Zhenhai Sun,David Feldstein-Bofill,Sangeeth Kallatt,Sabbir A. Khan,Morten Kjaergaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于单次生长中原位阴影定义的InAsSb/Al约瑟夫森结，实现了栅极可调的门控量子比特（gatemon），并验证其相干操控与退相干机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元InAsSb/Al纳米线兼具可组分调控的半导体性质与外延近邻超导性，是混合量子电路与介观超导研究的理想平台，但亟需验证其作为相干、可栅控电路元件的可行性。

Method: 在同一生长过程中以阴影法原位制备InAsSb/Al约瑟夫森结构建gatemon；通过栅压依赖的微波谱、Rabi振荡与Ramsey干涉进行表征；对三块芯片上五个器件统计弛豫时间，并与电路自发辐射估算对比。

Result: 栅压可将比特跃迁频率电学调谐数GHz；Rabi与Ramsey显示相干操控，代表性T1=0.79 μs、T2*=0.91 μs；五个器件T1约0.2–1 μs，且随比特频率升高而下降，量级与向电磁环境自发辐射的电路估算相当。

Conclusion: 原位InAsSb/Al结可作为相干、栅极可调的电路元件；cQED架构可作为探测三元弱链动力学的微波探针。

Abstract: Ternary InAsSb/Al nanowires combine compositionally engineerable semiconductor properties with epitaxial proximity superconductivity, making them attractive for hybrid quantum circuits and mesoscopic superconductivity. Here, we realize gatemon qubits based on shadow-defined in-situ InAsSb/Al Josephson junctions in a single growth process. Gate-dependent microwave spectroscopy demonstrates electrostatic tuning of the qubit transition over several gigahertz. Rabi oscillations and Ramsey interference show coherent qubit control, with representative values T1 = 0.79 us and T2* = 0.91 us. Across five gatemons distributed over three chips, the measured relaxation times range from approximately 0.2 us to 1 us and decrease with increasing qubit frequency. Their magnitude is comparable to a circuit estimate of spontaneous emission into the electromagnetic environment. These results establish in-situ InAsSb/Al junctions as coherent, gate-tunable circuit elements and introduce cQED architecture as a microwave probe of the dynamics of the ternary weak link.

</details>


### [149] [Subadditivity and the failure of strong subadditivity in the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15431)
*Lin Zhang,Wenlin Zhang,Haobo Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明Nichols–Wootters熵族（连接von Neumann熵与次熵）在统一环境维数下满足次可加性，且除von Neumann熵外，等号成立当且仅当至少一个边缘态为纯态；从而解决了关于次熵的次可加性猜想，并系统刻画了该熵族强次可加性的失效结构与缺陷界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次熵（subentropy）及Nichols–Wootters插值熵族在量子信息中有应用价值，但其基本的不等式性质（次可加性、强次可加性）此前未被确定，尤其"次熵是否次可加"是一个公开猜想。作者希望建立该熵族的加性/次加性理论，并理解其与von Neumann熵在强次可加性上的本质差别。

Method: 在统一的环境维数下对所有谱进行评估；用压缩矩阵熵的Haar平均给出正表示，由此得到作为核心工具的pinching不等式；借助该表示分析次可加性的等号条件，并研究强次可加性缺陷在最大纠缠态⊗混合量子比特态、量子马尔可夫态以及纯双分态⊗辅助态上的符号与极值，结合秩约束求精确最大值，用边缘纯度给出显式下界，并对两量子比特态确定最优系数（含次熵系数1/3）。

Result: 1) 证明Nichols–Wootters熵族次可加；除von Neumann熵外等号当且仅当某边缘为纯态，从而解决次熵次可加性猜想。2) 除von Neumann熵外所有成员均不满足强次可加性，反例为最大纠缠两量子比特态⊗混合量子比特；其强次可加缺陷在量子马尔可夫态上可取正负号。3) 对纯双分态⊗辅助态，将缺陷刻画为一个分别凹的乘积间隙，并在秩约束下求其精确最大值。4) 对任意双分态给出以边缘纯度表示的显式下界；对两量子比特态在任意公共环境维数下确定乘较小边缘特征值乘积的最优系数，次熵情形为1/3。5) pinching不等式还给出酉量子信道的熵增益界；作为应用，每个熵等于在指定平均输入态下、经特定各向同性部分投影测量后保留的经典通信容量（即使允许纠缠块编码）。

Conclusion: Nichols–Wootters熵族虽保持次可加性，但强次可加性仅对von Neumann熵成立，其缺陷具有丰富的结构（可取双号、可由乘积间隙精确刻画）。压缩矩阵熵Haar平均的正表示与pinching不等式是统一的技术核心，既给出尖锐的缺陷界与两量子比特最优系数，也导出酉信道熵增益界和测量-通信容量的操作解释。

Abstract: We prove subadditivity for the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies interpolating between the von Neumann entropy and the subentropy, with all spectra evaluated in one common ambient dimension. Beyond the von Neumann entropy, equality holds exactly when at least one marginal is pure. This settles the subadditivity conjecture concerning subentropy. Every member except the von Neumann entropy fails strong subadditivity, already on a maximally entangled two-qubit state tensored with a mixed qubit. Its strong-subadditivity defect takes both signs on quantum Markov states. For a pure bipartite state tensored with an ancillary state, we identify the defect with a separately concave product gap and determine its exact maximum under rank constraints. For arbitrary bipartite states, we obtain an explicit lower bound in the marginal purities. For two-qubit states, we determine the optimal coefficient multiplying the product of the smaller marginal eigenvalues in every common ambient dimension, including the subentropy coefficient $1/3$. A positive representation by Haar averages of compressed matrix entropies gives the pinching inequality underlying these results and also yields entropy-gain bounds for unital quantum channels.As an application, each entropy equals the classical communication capacity retained after a specified isotropic partial projective measurement, at a prescribed average input state, even when entangled block encodings are allowed.

</details>


### [150] [The infrared vacuum reduces the laser energy needed to break molecular bonds of simple molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15450)
*Johan F. Triana,Felipe Herrera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究单个分子与红外纳米腔共振模式强耦合下的红外光解离，发现通过向腔场注入光子驱动，所需能量比直接驱动分子低最多三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下红外纳米腔中少分子振动强耦合已被证实，为利用受限电磁真空调控化学反应提供了新途径，但量子化电磁模式如何改变反应性的内在机制尚不清楚。

Method: 将单个分子与共振红外纳米腔模式强耦合，用单色红外激光驱动系统（可直接振动激发分子或向腔场注入光子），对比CO2与H2O分子的腔内动力学。

Result: 腔注光子驱动解离所需能量比直接驱动分子低最多三个数量级；光解离增强源于核非谐性、强光-物质耦合与真空场极化（取决于分子键偶极特性）之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 研究为从腔量子电动力学视角理解化学过程调控、设计少分子级纳米光子学实验提供了概念与数值工具。

Abstract: Vibrational strong coupling within a few molecules has recently been demonstrated in infrared nanocavities at room temperature, opening new alternatives to explore chemical reactions assisted by the confined electromagnetic vacuum. However, the intrinsic mechanisms of how the quantized electromagnetic mode modifies reactivity are not fully understood. We study infrared photodissociation of a single molecule strongly coupled to a resonant infrared nanocavity mode. The coupled system is driven by a monochromatic infrared laser that vibrationally excites the molecule directly or injects photons into the nanocavity field. We compare the intracavity dynamics of CO2 and H2O molecules and demonstrate that the driving energy required for dissociation is up to three orders of magnitude lower inside the cavity by injecting photons into the nanocavity than by driving the molecule directly. We show that photodissociation enhancements are due to the interplay between nuclear anharmonicity, stronglight-mattercoupling, and vacuum field polarisation according to the dipolar nature of the molecular bond. Our results provide the conceptual and numerical tools for understanding how to control chemical processes and to design nanophotonics experiments at the few-molecule level within a cavity quantum electrodynamics perspective.

</details>


### [151] [Restoring Heisenberg Scaling in Hamiltonian Estimation despite Noise Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15487)
*Yan Luo,Lingna Wang,Hongzhen Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究噪声参数未知（仅部分刻画）时量子传感的精度极限，给出任意马尔可夫动力学与任意量子控制下哈密顿参数保持海森堡标度的充要条件，并提出利用噪声参考系统恢复该标度的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子控制抗噪策略通常假设噪声已知，但实际中噪声往往仅被部分刻画，其参数需与待估的哈密顿参数联合估计；这种噪声参数不确定性对量子度量学基本极限的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 针对一般马尔可夫动力学与任意量子控制，推导每个哈密顿参数保留海森堡标度的充要条件，并确定条件成立时的最优渐近精度；当条件失效时，提出引入合适噪声参考系统的协议——该参考系统对哈密顿参数不敏感，但经历传感器噪声的独立副本。

Result: 证明噪声参数的不确定性即使相干性得以保持，也可能破坏原本可达的海森堡标度；所提参考系统协议可恢复在噪声已知情形下可达的最优精度。

Conclusion: 噪声的不完全知识会改变量子度量学的基本极限，对在实际环境中实现量子增强精度具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Noise degrades quantum coherence and limits the precision of quantum sensors. Quantum control can counteract these effects, but existing strategies typically assume that the noise is known. In practice, noise is often only partially characterized, so its parameters must be estimated jointly with the Hamiltonian parameters of interest. Here we show that uncertainty in the noise parameters can destroy otherwise attainable Heisenberg scaling, even when coherence is preserved. For general Markovian dynamics under arbitrary quantum control, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for each Hamiltonian parameter to retain this scaling and determine its optimal asymptotic precision when the condition holds. When the condition fails, we propose a protocol that uses a suitable noisy reference system to restore this scaling, and prove that a reference insensitive to the Hamiltonian parameters but subject to an independent copy of the sensor's noise recovers the optimal precision attainable with known noise. Our results reveal how incomplete knowledge of the noise affects the fundamental limits of quantum metrology, with broad implications for achieving quantum-enhanced precision in realistic environments.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum Metrology and Quantum Battery in a Hyperfine-Coupled Spin System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17310)
*Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究受氢原子超精细结构启发的电子-质子有效自旋系统的多参数量子度量学与能量学性质：一方面计算量子Fisher信息矩阵并建立磁场与超精细耦合强度同时估计的量子Cramér-Rao界；另一方面在幺正充电协议下刻画其功（ergotropy、反ergotropy）、储能容量及能量基相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢原子超精细结构提供了天然的两参数耦合自旋平台，既有望用于多参数量子传感，也可能作为量子储能介质；但该体系中参数估计精度、参数间关联与能量存储性能尚缺乏统一刻画。

Method: 构建电子-质子有效自旋模型，视外磁场B与超精细耦合强度Λ为未知参数，推导量子Fisher信息矩阵与量子Cramér-Rao界，检验弱可交换性条件；独立地在幺正充电协议下计算ergotropy、反ergotropy、储能容量与能量基相干性，并分析其对磁场、超精细耦合、温度与充电时间的依赖。

Result: 尽管对应对称对数导数非对易，系统仍满足弱可交换性条件，从而保证两参数联合估计的Cramér-Rao界有效；能量学量随磁场、超精细耦合、温度与充电时间呈现可辨识的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该超精细自旋量子系统在度量学与能量学两方面均得到较完整的表征，展现出作为量子传感与储能平台的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate multiparameter quantum metrology and the energetic properties of an effective electron--proton spin system inspired by the hyperfine structure of the hydrogen atom. Considering the external magnetic field $\mathcal B$ and the hyperfine coupling strength $Λ$ as unknown parameters, we derive the quantum Fisher information matrix and establish the corresponding quantum Cramér--Rao bounds for their simultaneous estimation. We analyze the estimation precision and the correlations between the two parameters, showing that the weak-commutativity condition is satisfied despite the noncommutativity of the associated symmetric logarithmic derivatives. Independently, we explore the energetic properties of the system under a unitary charging protocol, characterizing its ergotropy, anti-ergotropy, storage capacity, and energy-basis coherence. We investigate the dependence of these quantities on the magnetic field, hyperfine coupling, temperature, and charging time. Our results provide a comprehensive characterization of the metrological properties and energetic performance of a hyperfine spin-based quantum system, highlighting its potential as a platform for quantum sensing and energy storage.

</details>


### [153] [Higher-Group Statistics on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16962)
*Chen Yang,Yu-An Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了以"跳跃算符闭合序列的Berry相位"定义的格点高阶群统计框架，给出高阶群统计过程的分类定理（d维球面上由 $H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$ 分类），并提出两种互补的构造方法，用于探测高阶群SPT相的边界反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同维数缺陷之间的耦合（畴壁置换任意子、弦相交产生粒子）由高阶群刻画，但缺乏统一、可计算的统计理论。现有Smith标准形算法仅适用于普通群，难以处理复杂高阶群结构，且枚举缺陷构型在计算上不可行。

Method: 构造格点框架：将高阶群统计定义为对局域算符修改不变的、跳跃算符闭合序列的Berry相位。核心例子为嵌入三维的闭合Majorana链（一维拓扑超导体），构造带相位 $+i$ 的loop-flip过程。并给出两种互补构造：(1) 将[Phys. Rev. X 16, 011010 (2026)]的Smith标准形算法推广到高阶群，由格点几何与给定局域移动生成统计过程；(2) 直接从同调类构造过程，避免指数级枚举缺陷构型。

Result: 证明有限高阶群 $\mathbb G$ 在 $d$ 维球面上的统计过程由 $H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$ 分类；loop-flip获得 $+i$ 相位，通过链与物理费米子间费米子宇称的中间转移，细化通常的 $\pm1$ 不变量；二维中电荷-畴壁统计可区分两个被 $\mathbb Z_2$ 电荷共轭对称富化的 $\mathbb Z_4$ toric code（任意子统计相同但规范理论不同，分别为 $D_4$ 与 $Q_8$ 的量子双重）；并区分1、2、3维中的2-群反常。

Conclusion: 高阶群统计为刻画量子多体系统中耦合缺陷提供了新不变量，可有效探测高阶群SPT相的边界反常，并显著拓展可计算的统计范围。

Abstract: Crossing a domain wall can permute anyons, and intersecting strings can create a particle. These couplings between defects of various dimensions are specified by higher groups. We develop a lattice framework defining higher-group statistics as Berry phases of closed sequences of hopping operators, invariant under local modifications of the operators. Our central example is a closed Majorana chain, a 1-dimensional topological superconductor, embedded in 3 dimensions. We construct a loop-flip process with phase $+i$, refining the usual $\pm 1$ loop-flip invariant through intermediate transfer of fermion parity between the chain and physical fermions. In two dimensions, charge-wall statistics distinguish two $\mathbb Z_4$ toric codes enriched by $\mathbb Z_2$ charge-conjugation symmetry, with identical anyon statistics but distinct gauged theories: the quantum doubles of the dihedral group $D_4$ and the quaternion group $Q_8$. We provide two complementary constructions of statistical processes. The first extends the Smith normal form algorithm in [Phys. Rev. X $\textbf{16}$, 011010 (2026), arXiv:2412.01886] to higher groups, generating processes from lattice geometry and prescribed local moves. For a finite higher group $\mathbb G$, we prove that statistical processes on a $d$-sphere are classified by $H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$. The second constructs processes directly from homology classes without examining exponentially many defect configurations, enabling us to study statistics for complex higher-group structures where the previous method is computationally prohibitive. These processes detect boundary anomalies of higher-group symmetry-protected topological phases. For example, we distinguish 2-group anomalies in 1, 2, and 3 dimensions. Higher-group statistics provide new invariants for characterizing coupled defects in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [154] [Feedback Control and Neural-Network-Based Prediction of Quantum Entanglement in Fano-mirror cavity optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15516)
*Ya-Feng Jiao,Ze-Yu Zhu,Jie Wang,Qing-Shou Tan,Wen-Qiang Fang,Le-Man Kuang,Hui Jing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于含相干反馈回路的Fano镜腔光力系统的理论方案，可有效产生、调控并预测双体光力纠缠，并利用监督前馈神经网络从可测光学信号预测纠缠大小，避免全量子态层析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观尺度物体质心运动的量子纠缠在基础科学与新兴量子技术中至关重要，但此类宏观非经典态的产生与验证仍极具挑战。

Method: 构建含相干反馈回路的Fano镜腔光力系统，腔模与Fano光学模通过相干与耗散相互作用耦合；分析Fano镜与腔模、机械模形成的闭合三体相互作用环路及直接/间接路径的量子干涉；考察相干反馈与光频失配的联合作用；并训练监督前馈神经网络，由易测光学信号预测纠缠量。

Result: 直接与间接相互作用路径间的量子干涉显著增强腔-机械纠缠，并产生鲁棒的Fano-机械纠缠；相干反馈结合适当的光频失配可进一步增强纠缠；训练后的神经网络能由可测光学信号准确预测纠缠大小。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了全光学、实验可行的宏观光力纠缠产生、调控与表征方法，在量子信息处理中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement involving the centre-of-mass motion of macroscopic-scale objects plays an essential role in both fundamental science and emerging quantum technologies, yet generating and verifying such macroscopic nonclassical states remain formidable challenges. Here, we report a theoretical scheme to effectively generate, manipulate, and predict bipartite optomechanical entanglement based on a Fano-mirror cavity optomechanical system with a coherent feedback loop, where the cavity and Fano optical modes couple through coherent and dissipative interactions. We show that the Fano mirror forms a closed tripartite interaction loop with the cavity and mechanical modes, whereby the quantum interference between direct and indirect interaction pathways substantially enhances the cavity-mechanical entanglement and gives rise to a robust Fano-mechanical entanglement. More importantly, we find that coherent feedback combined with an appropriate optical frequency mismatch can further enhance the entanglement, and a trained supervised feedforward neural network can be used to predict the entanglement magnitude from the readily measurable optical signals, relaxing the requirement of full quantum-state tomography. Our work, establishing an all-optical and experimentally feasible approach to generate, manipulate, and characterize the macroscopic optomechanical entanglement, holds great potential for applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-particle quantum walk search on a two-dimensional lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17061)
*Gonçalo Meneses,D. O. Oriekhov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多粒子量子行走搜索问题的建模与算法效率，采用Grover量子行走演化算子描述粒子离散时间演化，重点分析二维网格上的局域相互作用模型，揭示相互作用强度与最大纠缠度对搜索渐近成功时间与概率的影响，并证明相互作用的多粒子算法较单粒子版本有常数因子改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子量子行走搜索已较为成熟，但多粒子情形下粒子间局域相互作用如何影响搜索效率尚不清楚；作者希望厘清搜索问题的形式化表述，并量化相互作用强度与粒子间纠缠对算法成功时间与成功概率的作用。

Method: 以Grover量子行走演化算子描述每个粒子的离散时间演化；聚焦局域相互作用模型，即仅当两个及以上粒子处于同一网格站点时其状态才被修改；空间主要取二维网格；通过解析与数值分析考察搜索渐近成功时间、成功概率随相互作用强度及所生成最大纠缠度的依赖关系，并对比单粒子、双粒子与三粒子（相互作用与非相互作用）搜索算法；进而提出一种新的多粒子量子行走相互作用广义模型。

Result: 相互作用的双粒子和三粒子搜索算法（以及其非相互作用对应版本）在渐近成功时间上相对单粒子情形获得常数因子改进；成功时间与成功概率显著依赖相互作用强度及粒子间生成的最大纠缠度。

Conclusion: 多粒子量子行走搜索可通过局域相互作用与粒子间纠缠获得相对单粒子的常数级加速，相互作用强度是关键可调参数；所提出的广义相互作用模型为后续多粒子量子行走搜索算法设计提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In the present paper we study the formulation of a search problem and the efficiency of multi-particle quantum walk search algorithms. The discrete-time evolution of each particle in space is treated according to Grover's quantum walk evolution operator. We focus on the local interactions models, where the particles' states are modified only when two or more of them appear on the same grid site. The main focus is given to the two-dimensional grid in space. We study the dependence of the search algorithm asymptotic success time and probability on the interaction strength and the generated maximal entanglement between particles. We show that the interacting and non-interacting two- and three-particle search algorithm attains a constant factor improvement over the single-particle counterpart. In addition, we provide a new generalized model of interactions for multi-particle quantum walks.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum non-Gaussian coherence via dissipation to single qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15547)
*Sirachat Kaewkongtham,Kimin Park,Darren W. Moore,Atirach Ritboon,Radim Filip*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将脉冲高斯控制与未测量基态量子比特的耗散相结合，可确定性产生高阶量子非高斯相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子非高斯相干是玻色系统量子技术的关键资源，且与高斯态提供的相干在操作上不同；但在多数实验平台上确定性产生它极具挑战。

Method: 线性振子的脉冲高斯控制与最小非线性元件（未测量的基态量子比特，经共振Jaynes-Cummings相互作用耦合）引起的耗散交替进行；该耗散过程本身相位不敏感、单独不产生局域相干，但与高斯操作叠加后可实现确定性制备，并用QNG相干层级进行认证。

Result: 确定性产生高质量低阶Fock态二值叠加 (|0⟩+|N⟩)/√2，N 最高至 4，保真度 F>0.98；同时高保真（F>0.98）制备二项码态与逻辑魔术态。

Conclusion: 耗散型非线性（即使源自相位不敏感的未测量量子比特）与高斯操作结合可作为确定性生成高阶QNG相干的通用机制，为玻色量子信息处理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum non-Gaussian (QNG) coherence is a critical resource for advanced quantum technologies with bosonic systems, operationally distinct from the coherence provided by Gaussian states. For many experimental platforms, its deterministic generation remains a formidable experimental challenge.
