Here we propose that pulsed Gaussian control of a linear oscillator alternating with dissipation by the smallest nonlinear element -- an unmeasured ground-state qubit coupled via resonant Jaynes-Cummings (JC) interactions -- deterministically generates high-order QNG coherence. Although such dissipative processes are phase-insensitive and do not generate local coherence alone, when added to Gaussian operations they deterministically produce high-quality binary superpositions of lower Fock states $(|0\rangle + |N\rangle)/\sqrt{2}$ up to $N=4$ with high fidelity ($\mathcal{F} > 0.98$), certified by the QNG coherence hierarchy, as well as binomial code states and logical magic states with high fidelity ($\mathcal{F} > 0.98$).

</details>


### [157] [Exact Mixed-Locality Encodings for Resource-Constrained QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15663)
*Jingcong Liu,Yiding Su,Ruojun Zhang,Hafiz Muhammad Waseem,Bo Chen,Carsten Maple*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一类“逐点精确”的高阶目标哈密顿量混合表示：将部分高阶相互作用替换为共享的 Rosenberg 辅助比特、其余保持原生，并给出单步罚项条件与三次残差闭式，证明选择性约简的精确性；随后在直接配对类内建立静态可行性理论，证明存在仅混合表示可在联合预算、门与深度限制下满足的目标族，并在 12 比特线/环/网格上做了编译与含噪评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶目标的精确哈密顿量编码不唯一，且在量子比特数、罚项、门数与深度上代价不同；在资源受限硬件上运行 QAOA 时，表示选择本身是算法决策，但缺乏刻画“何时必须用混合表示”的严格理论。

Method: 1) 构造共享 Rosenberg 辅助比特的选择性约简表示族，给出精确性的单步罚项条件及三次残差闭式；2) 在直接配对类内建立静态可行性理论，分析原生、全约简与混合端点的联合预算；3) 在固定综合流程下把分离性推广到门与深度限制；4) 在 12 比特线、环、网格目标上编译，覆盖加权最大三可满足性、构造实例与立方自旋玻璃，并做路由实验与无噪声深度一评估。

Result: 编译结果显示：加权最大三可满足性 30 个实例中 23 个、构造实例 30/30、立方自旋玻璃 30 个中 2 个在所述类别与罚项规则下存在经认证的“仅混合”可行区域；路由实验在五个随机种子、固定候选集下保留 52.9% 的仅混合预算点。无噪声深度一评估中，原生编码在测试协议下取得最佳平均目标值，主要预算下未发现仅混合的成功案例。

Conclusion: 资源可行性是选择混合编码的充分理由，但不能保证解质量；表示选择应将“可行性认证”与“质量评估”分开处理。

Abstract: Higher-order objectives possess many exact Hamiltonian encodings, and these encodings differ in the qubits, penalties, gates, and depth that they require. Representation selection is therefore an algorithmic decision for the quantum approximate optimisation algorithm on resource-constrained hardware. This work develops a pointwise-exact family of representations in which selected higher-order interactions are replaced by shared Rosenberg auxiliaries while the remaining interactions stay native. A sharp single-step penalty condition and a closed form for cubic residuals certify exactness at every stage of such a selective reduction. Within the direct-pair class, a static feasibility theory identifies infinite families of objectives for which a mixed representation meets joint budgets that exclude both the native and the fully reduced endpoint. A separate construction extends this separation to gate and depth limits under a fixed synthesis. Compilation on 12-qubit line, ring, and grid targets finds certified mixed-only regions for 23 of 30 weighted maximum three-satisfiability instances, all 30 constructed instances, and 2 of 30 cubic spin-glass instances under the stated class and penalty rule. A separate routing experiment retains 52.9% of mixed-only budget points across five seeds within fixed candidate sets. In noiseless depth-one evaluation, the native encoding attains the best mean objective under the tested protocols, and the joint resource-and-quality test finds no mixed-only success at the primary budget. Resource feasibility is thus a sound reason to select a mixed encoding, whereas solution quality requires a separate assessment.

</details>


### [158] [Critical excited states in quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15683)
*Marek Kuchař,Michal Macek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究强耦合量子比特-玻色子体系中基态与激发态量子相变附近的临界增强传感：计入有限尺寸效应设计驱动方案，利用发散磁化率提升参数估计信噪比；并证明激发态临界性可借单模玻色压缩在规避"绝热性死亡"的区间提供计量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临界量子计量依赖基态临界性，但受"绝热性死亡"（adiabaticity death，即临界点附近能隙闭合导致绝热制备/驱动在有限时间内失效）限制，临界增强在实际参数估计中难以兑现；本文旨在寻找可规避该瓶颈的临界资源。

Method: 在强耦合 qubit-boson（如 Rabi/Dicke 型）模型中考察基态与激发态量子相变（ESQPT）；在计入有限尺寸效应的前提下，针对实验可行的可观测量与驱动方案，构造利用发散磁化率/极化率的驱动协议，并分析单模玻色压缩对参数估计精度（信噪比、Fisher 信息）的贡献，比较基态临界与激发态临界的计量性能。

Result: 激发态临界性可在单模玻色压缩机制下提供计量优势，且其工作区间可避开因能隙闭合导致的绝热性失效区域，从而在有限尺寸、有限时间条件下仍保持增强的参数估计信噪比。

Conclusion: 激发态量子相变是可行的临界计量资源，可在规避"绝热性死亡"的前提下实现超越常规基态临界方案的传感增强；结合有限尺寸修正与实验可行的驱动与生成元设计，该方案具备实际可实施性。

Abstract: We investigate criticality-enhanced sensing near ground- and excited-state quantum phase transitions in a strongly coupled qubit-boson system. Accounting for finite-size effects, we design driving protocols that exploit diverging susceptibilities to enhance signal-to-noise ratios in parameter estimation using experimentally relevant generators. We show that the excited-state criticality provides a metrological advantage through single-mode bosonic squeezing in regimes that circumvent the 'death of adiabaticity', limiting conventional critical quantum metrology with ground-state criticality.

</details>


### [159] [Enhanced Entanglement Gate Fidelity via Correlated and Synchronized Atom Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15819)
*Christopher W. Wächtler,Frederic Hummel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 工程化的集体耗散可使量子系统的运动相互关联甚至同步，本文证明这种运动关联能抑制中性原子镊阵列中基于里德堡阻塞的两比特控制相位门的运动诱导误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子里德堡门在有限温度下受原子运动引起的保真度损失限制，而集体耗散已被证明能关联并同步量子系统的运动，因此作者探究运动关联能否作为一种资源来抑制门误差。

Method: 在Lamb-Dicke参数最低阶展开下，推导门的不保真度中运动贡献的表达式；分别针对关联热态与同步量子van der Pol振子两类情形进行计算，并与独立热原子情形对比。

Result: 在Lamb-Dicke参数领头阶，运动对门不保真度的贡献仅由两原子的运动占据数与关联决定，且与门协议无关；对关联热态与同步van der Pol振子，在有限温度下运动关联均相对独立热原子情形降低了门不保真度。

Conclusion: 原子运动关联可作为实现高保真中性原子门的资源，并可能应用于模拟量子模拟器。

Abstract: Engineered collective dissipation can correlate and even synchronize the motion of quantum systems. We show that such motional correlations can suppress motion-induced errors in two-qubit controlled-phase gates based on the Rydberg blockade in neutral-atom tweezer arrays. To leading order in the Lamb-Dicke parameter, the motional contribution to the gate infidelity is determined solely by the two-atom motional occupations and correlations, and is independent of the gate protocol. We illustrate this result for correlated thermal states and synchronized quantum van der Pol oscillators. In each case, motional correlations reduce the gate infidelity relative to independent thermal atoms at finite temperature. Our results establish atomic-motion correlations as a resource for high-fidelity neutral-atom gates and may also find applications in analog quantum simulators.

</details>


### [160] [k-partite quantum homomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15839)
*Amin Karamlou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入k-分体同态博弈，将k-XOR博弈从线性方程组推广到任意关系结构，并用态依赖分次量子monad的Kleisli态射刻画张量积与交换算子模型下的完美策略；随后证明同步量子优势与k-分体量子优势在CSP实例层面不可比较，并给出有界宽度CSP模板不存在k-分体量子优势的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-XOR博弈与线性BCS博弈源于ℤ₂上的线性方程组，是对同一经典CSP的两种不同量子松弛；线性BCS博弈已被推广为任意关系结构上的同步同态博弈并有量子monad给出的组合语义，但缺乏对应的k-分体（多分体）推广框架，无法系统研究中多体量子CSP与量子同态。作者旨在建立该框架并厘清两类量子优势的关系。

Method: 定义k-分体同态博弈，把k-XOR博弈从线性系统扩展到任意关系结构，从而构建多分体量子CSP与量子同态的研究框架；在张量积模型和交换算子模型中，用k-分体关系结构范畴上合适的态依赖分次量子monad的Kleisli态射刻画完美策略；进而以伪心灵感应意义（存在完美量子策略而无可完美经典策略）研究量子优势，通过构造反例比较同步优势与k-分体优势，并分析有界宽度模板。

Result: 同步量子优势与k-分体量子优势在CSP实例层面不可比较：GHZ实例具有三人量子优势但无同步交换算子优势；魔方（magic-square）实例具有同步量子优势，但对任意玩家数与任意正常着色均无k-分体交换算子优势。此外给出一个显式的六元素模板，其CSP为NP完全且具有两人量子优势；并证明任何有界宽度CSP模板都不具有k-分体量子优势，即使在交换算子模型下亦然。

Conclusion: k-分体同态博弈为多分体量子CSP与量子同态提供了统一范畴语义工具；同步与k-分体两类量子优势互不包含，需分别研究；有界宽度模板在交换算子模型下也无法产生k-分体量子优势，而某些NP完全模板（如六元素模板）已可出现两人量子优势。

Abstract: $k$-partite XOR games and linear binary constraint system (BCS) games arise from systems of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}_2$, providing distinct quantum relaxations of the same classical constraint satisfaction problem (CSP). Linear BCS games have been generalised to synchronous homomorphism games on arbitrary relational structures, with compositional semantics provided by the quantum monad.
  We introduce $k$-partite homomorphism games, extending $k$-XOR games from linear systems to arbitrary relational structures. This yields a framework for studying multipartite quantum CSPs and quantum homomorphisms. We characterise perfect strategies in both the tensor-product and commuting-operator models as Kleisli morphisms of suitable state-dependent graded quantum monads on a category of $k$-partite relational structures.
  We then study quantum advantage in the pseudotelepathic sense: the existence of a perfect quantum strategy without a perfect classical strategy. We show that synchronous and $k$-partite quantum advantage are incomparable at the level of CSP instances. The GHZ instance admits three-player quantum advantage but no synchronous commuting-operator advantage, whereas the magic-square instance admits synchronous quantum advantage but no $k$-partite commuting-operator advantage for any number of players or proper colouring. We also exhibit an explicit six-element template whose CSP is NP-complete and admits two-player quantum advantage. Finally, we prove that no bounded-width CSP template admits $k$-partite quantum advantage, even in the commuting-operator model.

</details>


### [161] [Revealing Bell Nonlocality Does Not Require the Same Physical Measurement to be Performed Twice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15871)
*Paweł Cieśliński,Carlotta Versmold,Jan Wójcik,Eun Mi Kim,Jan Dziewior,Paweł Kurzyński,Tomasz Paterek,Harald Weinfurter,Wiesław Laskowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究测量方向从不重复（不可精确重现）情形下的Bell不等式违背，理论与实验证明Bell非局域性及其操作意义仍然保留，并可扩展至随机测量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Bell非局域性分析均假设一小组预定义测量方向可被精确重现。但受基本原理或技术原因（如测量装置漂移）影响，测量方向可能根本无法重现，此时Bell违背是否仍可能、是否仍具操作意义尚不清楚。

Method: 在测量方向不可重复的设定下进行理论分析，证明Bell不等式违背依然成立且保持操作意义；并以偏振纠缠光子开展实验验证，每个随机选定的分析方向仅探测一对粒子。

Result: 实验确认即使测量方向从不重复，Bell不等式违背仍可实现并保持原有操作意义。

Conclusion: 结果确立了Bell不等式违背对测量不可重复性的鲁棒性，将Bell检验与随机测量框架相联系，并把量子信息协议推广到测量控制受限的场景，如量子器件认证、通信复杂度与量子密码。

Abstract: Bell inequality violations, or Bell nonlocality for short, is not only central to testing the foundations of quantum physics but have also become an essential ingredient of numerous quantum information protocols, from quantum key distribution to the device-independent certification of randomness and secure communication. Existing analyses of Bell nonlocality assume that a small set of predefined measurement directions can be reproduced exactly. But what if these directions cannot be reproduced at all? This may happen for fundamental or technical reasons, such as variations in the measurement apparatus. Here we show that the violation of a Bell inequality remains possible and that it also retains its operational significance, even when the measurement directions never repeat. We experimentally confirm these results using polarization-entangled photons, detecting only one particle pair per randomly chosen analyzer direction. Our results establish the robustness of Bell inequality violations against imperfect measurement repeatability and further connect Bell tests with the broader framework of randomized measurements. Moreover, they extend quantum information protocols to settings with limited measurement control, including quantum device certification, communication complexity, and quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [162] [Superradiant photon bunching in nanolasers: from a polarization-order hierarchy of the master equation to Dicke Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15898)
*Matias Bundgaard-Nielsen,Jesper Mørk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究纳米激光器中多个量子发射体集体耦合单模光场时的超热光子聚束现象，提出按偏振阶数（参与光与发射体间相干的光子数）构建模型层级，从全主方程出发系统消除各阶相干，得到Dicke马尔可夫链（仅消除全部相干）与布居-偏振模型（保留一阶光偏振、消除二阶相干），并发展对称性约化主方程作为数值精确基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米激光器中少数至数百个发射体共享单一光模，集体亮/暗态导致阈值以下的超辐射爆发与强光子聚束，但全主方程随发射体数增长而难以求解。作者希望厘清这种集体行为在多大程度上可被理解为集体Dicke态之间的随机跳跃，并建立可扩展且系统可控的近似层级，以替代对全主方程的依赖。

Method: 从激光主方程出发，按偏振阶数（发射体与光之间相干所涉及的光子数）定义模型层级并逐阶包含相干；消除全部相干时得到Dicke马尔可夫链——在光子数与Dicke态上的随机跳跃过程，集体效应仅通过增强或抑制的跃迁速率进入；保留一阶（光偏振）、消除二阶（双光子相干）得到布居-偏振模型。同时构造对称性约化主方程作为数值精确参考，并详细比较各方法的数值性能。

Result: Dicke马尔可夫链预测超热聚束及对发射体数的显著奇偶依赖性，在最相干的区域内可复现约80%的聚束，而常规速率方程在该区域预测无超热聚束；布居-偏振模型几乎完美复现全主方程的聚束。对称性约化主方程将模拟范围扩展至阈值以下34个、阈值以上24个发射体；Dicke马尔可夫链可直接求解数百个发射体、并行采样数千个发射体。

Conclusion: 该偏振阶层级为累积量展开提供了一种替代方案，且不同于累积量展开，它在最高阶可回到主方程本身。Dicke马尔可夫链与布居-偏振模型分别以不同精度和可扩展性覆盖从数十到数千发射体的范围，所给出的数值性能比较可为方法选择提供指导。

Abstract: In nanolasers, a few to a few hundred quantum emitters share a single mode of light. Coupled identically to the mode, the emitters do not radiate independently but form collective bright and dark states, producing superradiant bursts and strongly bunched photon statistics below the laser threshold. We investigate to what extent this behavior can be understood as random jumps between collective Dicke states. From the laser master equation, we derive a hierarchy of models defined by the polarization order, i.e., the number of photons involved in the coherence between the emitters and the light, and include the coherence to various orders. With all of the coherences eliminated, we obtain a Dicke Markov chain, a stochastic jump process over the photon number and the Dicke states, in which collective effects enter only through enhanced or suppressed transition rates. It predicts superthermal bunching and a strong even--odd dependence on the emitter number. It recovers about 80\% of the bunching in the most coherent regime, where conventional rate equations predict no superthermal bunching. Keeping the first polarization order, i.e., the optical polarization, and eliminating the second order, i.e., the two-photon coherences, yields a population-polarization model, which recovers the bunching of the full master equation almost perfectly. This hierarchy of polarization orders is an alternative to the cumulant expansion and, unlike it, recovers the master equation at the highest order. As a numerically exact reference, a symmetry-reduced master equation extends the simulation range all the way to 34 (24) emitters below (above) threshold. The Dicke Markov chain can be solved directly for hundreds of emitters and sampled for thousands of emitters, further extending the range of numerical methods. We compare the numerical performance of the methods in detail to guide the choice of technique.

</details>


### [163] [Quasi-Cyclic Qudit Lifted Product Codes with Almost Linear Minimum Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15952)
*Shantom K. Borah,Abhinav Vaishya,V. Lalitha,Asit Kumar Pradhan,Bane Vasic,Narayanan Rengaswamy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出循环群代数上的 qudit 版 lifted product（LP）构造，推广超图乘积与 LP 码到任意素数幂维度，得到准循环 qudit CSS 码；给出基于加性码的码率界，以及全堆叠情形下 Θ(log N) 逻辑 qudit、Θ(N/log N) 最小距离的渐近好码族；并研究加性译码器与线性合并伴随式译码器，数值上优于可比 qubit 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔 lift 的 LP 码渐近性能好，但循环 lift 产生的准循环码具有循环矩阵结构，便于硬件译码器利用，这对校验节点操作昂贵的非二进制译码尤为关键；同时 qudit 在信息表示效率、魔法态蒸馏与代数结构上优于 qubit，但缺乏循环群代数框架下的系统构造与性能分析。

Method: 在循环群代数上构造 qudit 版 LP：以 LP 因子（提升 Tanner 码）为输入，扩展超图乘积与 LP，对任意素数幂维度 d 及任意堆叠阶数生成准循环 qudit CSS 码；通过与加性码的联系界定编码率；证明适用于一般 LP 因子的距离界；设计一般 qudit 码的加性译码器与全堆叠码的线性合并伴随式译码器；在符号错误概率相等的去极化信道下做蒙特卡洛仿真。

Result: 全堆叠情形下，由扩展图上提升 Tanner 码构造的 qudit LP 码族具有 Θ(log N) 个逻辑 qudit 与近乎线性的最小距离 Θ(N/log N)。仿真显示，在等符号错误概率的去极化信道下，qudit 码的帧错误率低于可比 qubit 码；对全堆叠码，线性合并伴随式译码器带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 循环群代数上的 qudit LP 构造兼顾渐近好参数与准循环硬件友好结构，为面向非二进制译码的低开销容错量子计算提供可行码族；配套的加性译码与线性合并伴随式译码实用有效，qutrit/qudit 方案在同等符号错误率下优于 qubit 方案。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are a promising route to fault-tolerant quantum computation with low overhead, and qudits offer advantages over qubits including more efficient information representation, improved magic state distillation, and richer algebraic structure for code design. Asymptotically good QLDPC codes arise from the lifted product (LP) construction with non-abelian lifts, but cyclic lifts remain attractive in practice because the resulting quasi-cyclic codes have circulant structure that hardware decoders exploit, which matters especially for non-binary decoders with costly check node operations. We introduce a qudit generalization of the LP construction over cyclic group algebras, which extends hypergraph product and LP codes to arbitrary prime-power dimensions and yields quasi-cyclic qudit CSS codes for every stacking order. We bound encoding rates via a connection to additive codes, and show that in the fully stacked case, qudit LP codes built from lifted Tanner codes on expander graphs form families with $Θ(\log N)$ logical qudits and almost linear minimum distance $Θ(N/\log N)$, via a distance bound that holds for a general class of LP factors. We also study an additive decoder for general qudit codes and a linear merged-syndrome decoder for fully stacked codes. Monte Carlo simulations under a depolarizing channel with equal symbol error probability show that qudit codes achieve lower frame error rates than comparable qubit codes, and that the linear decoder gives further gains for fully stacked codes.

</details>


### [164] [Quantum geometric bounds and uncertainty relations for velocity variance and spread of Wannier function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15977)
*Felipe P. Abreu,Luis F. Cárdenas-Castillo,David Porlles,Wei Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于量子Cramér-Rao界推导与Efimov-Dodonov论证，导出约束多可观测量协方差矩阵对角积、迹及各对角元的混合态不确定性关系；取可观测量为对称对数导数即给出平均Uhlmann曲率对量子Fisher信息矩阵的几何界，纯态情形化为Berry曲率对量子度规的界，并借此揭示速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽的光学求和规则及其受Berry曲率与速度算符对易子限制的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Fisher信息矩阵、量子几何张量（量子度规与Berry曲率）及Uhlmann曲率之间存在结构性关联，但缺乏统一的不确定性关系框架来刻画其相互制约；同时，速度方差、Wannier函数展宽等物理量与Berry曲率之间的定量联系（类似光学求和规则）尚需系统性理论说明。

Method: 从量子Cramér-Rao界的推导出发，结合Efimov与Dodonov提出的论证，构造适用于混合态的不确定性关系，分别约束可观测量协方差矩阵的对角积、迹与单个对角元；将可观测量选取为对称对数导数（SLD），把上述关系转译为平均Uhlmann曲率对量子Fisher信息矩阵的几何界（纯态极限下退化为Berry曲率对量子度规的界）；再将形式体系应用于速度算符，导出速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽的光学求和规则；最后在自旋正则系综、拓扑材料、常见半导体与过渡金属硫族化合物等体系中做数值验证。

Result: 得到一组约束协方差矩阵对角积、迹及对角元的混合态不确定性关系；由SLD选择导出由平均Uhlmann曲率给出的量子Fisher信息矩阵几何界，纯态下即为Berry曲率对量子度规的界；并给出速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽的光学求和规则，表明二者受Berry曲率与速度算符对易子共同上限约束。自旋正则系综、拓扑材料、常见半导体及过渡金属硫族化合物的计算均验证了这些几何界与不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了不确定性关系—量子Fisher信息—量子几何（Uhlmann曲率/Berry曲率）之间的统一框架，说明量子几何量对信息度量与动量空间波函数展宽具有普遍的上界约束，并具有可检验的物理后果。

Abstract: By utilizing the derivation of quantum Cramér-Rao bound and an argument put forward by Efimov and Dodonov, we derive several uncertainty relations for mixed states that constrain the diagonal product, trace, and each diagonal element of the covariance matrix of multiple observables. By choosing the observables to be the symmetric logarithmic derivative, the uncertainty relations imply various geometric bounds on quantum Fisher information matrix caused by mean Uhlmann curvature, which in pure states become geometric bounds on quantum metric caused by Berry curvature. Our formalism reveals optical sum rules for velocity variance and spread of valence band Wannier functions, and how they are bounded by the Berry curvature and commutator of velocity operators. Canonical ensembles of spins, topological materials, common semiconductors, and transition metal dichalcogenides are used to verify these geometric bounds and uncertainty relations.

</details>


### [165] [Communication inequalities: robust quantum advantage, measurement testing and dimension witnessing with minimal setups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15995)
*Albert Rico,Javier Fernández,Mani Zartab,John Calsamiglia,Some Sankar Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了仅依赖输入-输出统计中四个参数（关联与反关联事件）的维度不等式，首次为通信场景提供了区分经典与量子统计的通用方法，并在维度假设下给出SIC-POVM的解析噪声鲁棒性认证及无需测量不相容性的维度见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信场景（CS）可视为观察量子行为的最简单设定，但与Bell场景或含共享随机的制备-测量场景不同，此前尚无已知通用方法能区分经典与量子统计，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从通信场景的输入-输出统计出发，构造仅依赖四个参数（对应关联与反关联事件）的维度不等式，通过不等式饱和与否来判定经典性与量子性，并在维度假设下开展认证分析。

Result: 所构造的维度不等式可区分经典与量子统计；在维度假设下，饱和其中某一不等式可认证SIC-POVM，并给出解析的噪声鲁棒性界限；该不等式还同时为关联与反关联统计提供维度见证。

Conclusion: 通信场景中的经典-量子统计分离可通过维度不等式实现，且该框架无需测量不相容性即可给出维度见证，并可直接用于SIC-POVM的自检测类认证。

Abstract: Communication scenarios (CS) are arguably the simplest setting in which quantum behaviors can be observed. Yet, in contrast to Bell scenarios or prepare-measure scenarios with shared randomness, no general method is known to separate classical and quantum statistics. Here we close this gap by deriving dimensional inequalities to separate classical and quantum statistics in CS. These depend only on four parameters of the input-output statistics, corresponding to correlated and anticorrelated events. Under dimensional assumptions, saturating one of the inequalities certifies SIC-POVMs with analytical noise robustness. The inequalities also yield a dimension witness for both correlated and anti-correlated statistics, without the need of measurement incompatibility.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum Material Generation via Coherent Hamiltonian Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16016)
*Jiang Yuhan,Matthew Otten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QMage——首个由哈密顿量引导的全相干量子生成算法，在原子组分、晶胞结构与原子坐标构成的构型空间内做相干搜索，直接输出计算性质满足目标容差的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学的核心目标是在巨大候选空间中寻找具有目标性质的新材料。数据驱动的生成模型只能学习已知材料分布、外推能力差，且生成结构不保证稳定；而材料的性质本质上由其哈密顿量决定，可由量子计算机直接计算。

Method: 将“生成—筛选—放大”整合为单一相干量子线路：对每个晶体构型进行哈密顿量块编码（block-encoding），隔离目标本征态并评估其性质，再用定点搜索（fixed-point search，即无相位估计的振幅放大）放大满足条件的构型并测量。算法面向成熟容错量子计算时代设计，并以逻辑量子比特数与Toffoli深度估计代价。

Result: 同一框架可覆盖形成能、带隙、弹性与电导率等多类目标；从经典上常规可算的基准出发，一直外推到既有方法不可靠的目标区间，给出资源估计（逻辑量子比特数、Toffoli深度）。

Conclusion: QMage从第一性原理生成候选材料，而非对已知材料做泛化，在目标性质难以经典预测的场景下具有推动材料发现的潜力。

Abstract: Discovering new materials with targeted properties is a central goal of materials science, yet the space of candidates is vast. Data-driven generative models learn the distribution of known materials, but they extrapolate poorly, and a generated structure is not known to be stable. A material's properties follow from its Hamiltonian, and a quantum computer can calculate them from it. We introduce the quantum material generative algorithm (QMage), a coherent search, guided by the Hamiltonian, over crystalline configurations of atomic composition, unit cell structure, and atomic positions, for one whose calculated properties meet the targets to within a given error tolerance. The computation is a single coherent quantum circuit in which each configuration's Hamiltonian is block-encoded, its target eigenstate isolated and properties evaluated, and the satisfying configurations amplified by fixed-point search and measured. One framework covers many targets, from formation energy and band gaps to elasticity and conductivity. We design the algorithm for the mature fault-tolerant era and estimate its cost in logical qubits and Toffoli depth from a classically routine benchmark to targets where established methods are unreliable. The approach generates candidate materials from first principles, rather than generalizing from those known, and could enable materials discovery when the target properties are difficult to predict classically.

</details>


### [167] [Operator uncertainty and cross-domain information in quantum signals: Second-moment bounds for quantum waveform design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16049)
*Mostafizur Rahaman Laskar,Sajad Daei,Gabor Fodor,Mikael Skoglund,Franco Nori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 结合熵不确定关系与连续变量系统的协方差描述，给出量子波形设计的二阶矩信息上界：到达时间与横向波数的互信息不超过时频与位置-波数不确定度乘积中较小者两倍的对数，并证明双线性时空相位高斯波包可达该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子波形设计中缺乏对跨正则共轭对（时间-频率、位置-波数）兼容可观测量之间可获得信息量的定量界，需要判定目标信息量是否可达的充要条件与最优态构造方法。

Method: 将熵不确定关系与连续变量高斯的协方差矩阵描述相结合，对具有有限二阶矩与熵的任意态推导到达时间与横向波数互信息的二阶矩上界；以带双线性时空相位的高斯波包作为候选态验证紧性，并评估局域高斯测量、Rényi-2熵、量子Fisher信息以及在加性探测器噪声下的抖动影响，最后给出基于脉冲前沿倾斜与透镜的光学实现方案。

Result: 任意态下互信息受 log[2·min(ΔTΔΩ, ΔxΔk)] 型界约束；双线性相位高斯波包在任意可行的标准差预算集合下均饱和该界，因而该界成为在正则模型内达到目标信息的充要条件。对该态，到达时间与横向波数的联合测量在局域高斯测量中最优，达到约化态的Rényi-2熵，并在固定输入宽度下达到耦合的量子Fisher信息；加性探测器噪声下，时间抖动将记录信息限制为由抖动与脉冲持续时间之比决定的水平。

Conclusion: 该工作为时空耦合量子信号设计提供了可操作的理论框架：以不确定度乘积给出的信息界兼具必要性与充分性，指导高斯态与测量方案的联合优化，并存在基于脉冲前沿倾斜与透镜的可行光学实现。

Abstract: We derive second-moment bounds for quantum waveform design by combining entropic uncertainty relations with the covariance description of continuous-variable systems. For any state with finite second moments and entropies, the mutual information between the arrival time and the transverse wave number, which are compatible observables from different canonical pairs, is bounded above by the logarithm of twice the smaller of the time-frequency and position-wave-number uncertainty products. We demonstrate that a Gaussian wave packet with a bilinear space-time phase saturates this bound for every admissible set of standard-deviation budgets, and that the bound therefore provides a necessary and sufficient condition for reaching a target information within the canonical model. For this state, the joint measurement of the arrival time and the transverse wave number is optimal among local Gaussian measurements, yields the Rényi-2 entropy of the reduced state, and attains the quantum Fisher information for the coupling at fixed input widths. We also show that, with additive detector noise, timing jitter limits the recorded information to a level set by the ratio of jitter to pulse duration. These results provide a framework for designing spatiotemporally coupled quantum signals, and we propose an optical realization based on pulse-front tilt and a lens.

</details>


### [168] [TGen-SQD: Physics-Informed Transferable Configuration Generator for Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16080)
*Niloy Deb Roy Mishu,Lei Jiang,Mohsen Heidari,Ruizhe Zhang,Fan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出物理信息驱动的生成器TGen-SQD，跨多个哈密顿量联合训练，冻结权重后即可对已见与零样本几何进行确定性空间筛选，在20量子比特H10链上能量误差较PIGen-SQD基线降低约12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQD避免了迭代变分电路优化，但其精度与成本取决于能否选出紧凑且能捕获基态的行列式子空间；现有机器学习选择器依赖任务特定物理线索，且通常需对每个哈密顿量重新训练，泛化性与训练开销受限。

Method: 设计TGen-SQD生成器：利用组态相互作用加权数据学习生成分布，并结合轨道级与体系级描述符引导采样；在多个哈密顿量上联合训练，使模型获得跨体系的可迁移表征。训练完成后冻结权重，直接用于已见几何与零样本几何的推断，并在匹配的shot与proposal预算下评估。

Result: 在20量子比特H10链上，已见与未见几何的平均能量误差分别为40.18±1.52 mHa和43.97±1.20 mHa，较最先进的PIGen-SQD基线分别降低12.7%和11.7%（shot与proposal预算对齐）。

Conclusion: 物理信息联合训练可在不逐哈密顿量重训的前提下提供更强泛化的行列式子空间选择，显著提升SQD精度；零样本几何上的小幅性能衰减表明冻结权重的跨体系迁移具有可行性与稳定性。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) avoids iterative variational circuit optimization, but its accuracy and cost depend on selecting a compact determinant subspace that captures the ground state. Existing machine-learning selectors use task-specific physical cues and typically retrain for each Hamiltonian. We introduce TGen-SQD, a physics-informed generator jointly trained across multiple Hamiltonians. It learns from configuration-interaction-weighted data and uses orbital- and system-level descriptors to guide generation. After training, its weights remain frozen for inference on seen and zero-shot geometries. On a 20-qubit H10 chain, TGen-SQD achieves mean energy errors of 40.18 +/- 1.52 and 43.97 +/- 1.20 mHa on seen and unseen geometries, respectively, 12.7% and 11.7% lower than the state-of-the-art PIGen-SQD baseline under matched shot and proposal budgets.

</details>


### [169] [Testing Quantum Advantage in High-Frequency Trading with Market-Derived Nonlocal Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16113)
*Daiwei Zhu,Brendan Pankovich,Alireza Marefat Khah,Haakam Aujla,Ramis Movassagh,Martin Roetteler,Johannes Borregaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 作者提出"市场衍生非局域博弈"，用真实订单簿数据与联合动作回测决定博弈的输入与收益，检验量子关联能否在高频交易类分布式协调任务中带来可实现的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多在理论上设定博弈及其收益结构，使用理想化玩具市场模型，未检验这些模型是否反映真实市场行为；因此量子优势在真实市场条件下是否成立尚不明确。

Method: 以经典交易策略为出发点，仅用量子关联协调智能体决策；用历史订单簿数据与联合动作回测构造市场衍生非局域博弈的输入和收益；计算精确经典最优值与量子上界；对可能存在量子优势的博弈，提出优化算法求显式纠缠策略并进行端到端回测；在双智能体、三股票的跨交易场所套利场景中验证，并受控改变流动性竞争与跨股票重定价。

Result: 由观测市场数据导出的博弈在所测交互尺度上未显示出可分辨的量子—经典差距；在受控变化下，该方法能够识别支持量子优势的机制/情形、构造显式策略，并检验其优势能否在修改后的历史数据端到端回测中存活。

Conclusion: 将博弈构造直接锚定于市场数据与回测，可在真实约束下评估量子协调优势；真实市场在典型尺度上暂无可分辨优势，但该框架提供了识别优势情形并端到端验证的系统方法。

Abstract: Non-classical correlations enabled by entanglement could be exploited for distributed coordination tasks with communication constraints such as high-frequency trading in fragmented markets. However, previous studies have largely specified the game and its payoff structure theoretically, using toy market models without testing how well these models reflect observed market behavior. Here, we introduce market-derived nonlocal games, in which historical order-book data and backtests of joint actions determine the game's inputs and payoffs, rather than a prespecified idealized model. We start from classical trading strategies based on market considerations, using quantum correlations only to coordinate the agents' decisions. We compute exact classical optima and quantum upper bounds for the payoff. For games with a possible quantum advantage, we introduce an optimization algorithm to find explicit entangled strategies and end-to-end backtest them on historical data. We demonstrate the approach in a two-agent, three-stock cross-venue arbitrage setting. The game derived from the observed market data shows no resolved quantum-classical gap at the measured interaction scales. Controlled variations in liquidity competition and cross-stock repricing then show how the approach identifies regimes supporting quantum advantage, constructs explicit strategies, and tests whether their advantage survives end-to-end backtesting on modified historical market data.

</details>


### [170] [Phase Interference and Transverse Catalysts Govern the Noise Fragility of Long-Range Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16115)
*Longhui Shen,Fangzhou Jin,Xiaoxia Yi,Bin Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明变分线路的单故障脆弱性F由故障相位在目标哈密顿量上的干涉方式决定，而非故障传播范围；并据此给出抑制噪声平台、降低退相干误差的线路设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程纠缠门能缩短变分态制备，但也会加速算符在寄存器上的扩散，人们通常因此把"非局域性"与"噪声脆弱"直接挂钩。作者希望厘清：变分线路对单故障的脆弱性究竟由什么物理机制支配，从而判断能否在保留相干表达力的同时降低噪声敏感性。

Method: 以单故障脆弱性F为核心量，在Heisenberg链上构造并比较具有相同广泛支撑（extensive support）的两类故障，解析计算其能量损伤比例；引入"相位匹配的纵向光晕"（longitudinal halo）与横向"催化剂"（空间非均匀单比特旋转、两比特XX/YY门的pair-flip分量）概念，分析对称性对能量暗态的点火作用；在含全宽长程ZZ门的噪声训练中进行数值实验，分别抑制单一催化剂与同时抑制两者，考察 ignited plateau、梯度力平衡与退相干误差；最后用门数暴露量λ=pK结合单次F测量预测噪声诱导的能量移动。

Result: Heisenberg链上，两个支撑范围相同的故障可分别造成有界或广泛能量损伤，二者比值严格为N/2；相位匹配的纵向光晕在被横向催化剂（非均匀单比特旋转或XX/YY的pair-flip分量）点火前保持能量暗，而均匀旋转与守恒磁化的交换门使其严格保持暗态。全宽长程ZZ门噪声训练中，单独抑制任一催化剂，线路仍被困在由远程XY耦合维持的ignited plateau；同时抑制两者则平台消失，F降至最小（尽管故障算符更大），退相干误差减半。训练动力学是催化剂剪枝与梯度力平衡之间的竞争：只退火横向催化剂可挽救所有轨迹，退火整个纵向部分则不能。结合λ=pK，单次脆弱性测量可跨架构、跨噪声强度准确预测噪声诱导的能量移动。

Conclusion: 变分线路的噪声脆弱性由故障相位在目标哈密顿量上的干涉结构（而非算符扩散距离）决定，因此ansatz设计可归结为"相干表达力 vs 门数"的权衡：剪除横向催化剂是消除噪声平台、降低退相干误差的关键，且λ=pK与单次F测量即可预测噪声-induced能量偏移。

Abstract: Long-range entangling gates shorten variational state preparation, but they also accelerate operator spreading across the register, linking nonlocality to a presumed noise fragility. Here we show that the single-fault fragility $F$ of a variational circuit is governed by how fault phases interfere on the target Hamiltonian, not by how far faults spread. For Heisenberg chains we prove that two faults with identical extensive support can incur either bounded or extensive energy damage, in the exact ratio $N/2$. A phase-matched longitudinal halo remains energetically dark until ignited by transverse catalysts that break a symmetry of the remote register, namely spatially nonuniform single-qubit rotations or the pair-flip component of two-qubit $XX/YY$ gates, whereas uniform rotations and magnetization-conserving exchange gates leave it strictly dark. In noisy training with full-width long-range $ZZ$ gates, suppressing either catalyst alone leaves circuits trapped on an ignited plateau sustained by remote $XY$ couplings, whereas suppressing both eliminates the plateau, achieves minimal fragility despite larger fault operators, and halves the dephasing error. Training is driven by a race between catalyst pruning and gradient force balance, as annealing the transverse catalysts out of trapped circuits rescues every trajectory while annealing the entire longitudinal sector does not. Combined with the gate-count exposure $λ=pK$, a single fragility measurement accurately predicts the noise-induced energy shift across circuit architectures and noise strengths, reducing ansatz design to a trade-off between coherent expressivity and gate count.

</details>


### [171] [Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16117)
*Matthias M. Müller,Tommaso Calarco,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本章为量子控制领域的综述性导论，界定控制问题、梳理误差来源与抑制方法，并介绍绝热方法、量子最优控制与机器学习等前沿求解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统动力学脆弱且易受噪声与误差影响，而精确操控量子态是量子技术应用的基础，同时能量结构与时间动力学之间的关联也是量子力学核心问题之一，故需系统梳理量子控制的理论框架与实用方法。

Method: 采用综述/教程式论述：先形式化定义控制问题，再归纳基本误差来源及其抑制手段，最后分类介绍求解控制问题的主流技术，包括绝热方法、量子最优控制和机器学习方法。

Result: 构建了从问题定义、误差分析到方法求解的完整逻辑链条，给出了量子控制领域的方法谱系与层次化概览。

Conclusion: 量子控制兼具技术应用价值与基础物理意义；多种方法（绝热、最优控制、机器学习）互补，可针对不同误差机制与控制目标选择合适的控制策略。

Abstract: Quantum Control is the art of steering the fragile dynamics of a quantum system in a precise way toward a desired final state or transformation. It is a cornerstone of many quantum technology applications and at the same time allows us to shed light on the fundamental connection between the energy structure and the time dynamics of quantum systems at the heart of some of the most interesting phenomena of quantum mechanics. In this chapter on quantum control, we introduce the control problem, go through basic error sources and methods to suppress these errors and then discuss state-of-the-art techniques to solve the control problem, like adiabatic methods, quantum optimal control and methods of machine learning.

</details>


### [172] [Infinite or indefinite: the query cost of simulating the quantum switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16229)
*Dantong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在电路模型下用量子计算机复现量子开关（quantum switch）所需的黑箱调用次数，证明一般信道上精确模拟需无限次调用、近似模拟在半个钻石范数下需 Ω(d^{1/4}) 次调用，并给出噪声刻画任务中开关相对普通电路的指数级查询优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关以因果顺序的相干叠加作用两个操作，是 indefinitite causal order 的简单实例，任何每个操作只用一次的确定顺序电路都无法描述它。作者希望量化：普通电路模型需要多少次黑箱调用才能复现量子开关的行为，从而判断这种非确定因果结构是否带来可证明的查询复杂度优势。

Method: 将问题形式化为查询复杂度问题：允许电路使用经典前馈（classical feed-forward）与无限辅助量子存储。情形一：若其中一个黑箱已知为幺正，则三次调用足够。情形二：对 d 维系统上的一般信道，分别在半个钻石范数（half diamond norm）下考察精确模拟与固定误差 ε<1/2 的近似模拟下界。情形三：构造具体任务——对实际噪声为固定未知基下退相位（dephasing）、而理论模型为退极化（depolarizing）信道的器件做区分，二者纠缠保真度与平均门保真度相同，比较量子开关与电路模型的调用次数。

Result: 对一般信道，证明三条结论：(1) 最坏情形下不存在有限调用次数可实现精确模拟；(2) 以半个钻石范数度量，任何固定误差 ε<1/2 的近似都需 Ω(d^{1/4}) 次调用，即随量子比特数指数增长；(3) 普通电路可能需要指数多次调用才能提取量子开关用少数几次即可得到的单个比特。在噪声刻画任务中，固定噪声强度下量子开关以与 d 无关的器件使用次数区分两种信道，而电路模型需 Ω(d^{1/16}) 次调用。

Conclusion: 量子开关在噪声刻画这一量子纠错所需任务上具有可证明的查询优势，为开关在实用量子计算场景中的价值提供了证据；作者呼吁后续工作将此类优势推广到更贴近实际的计算任务。

Abstract: The quantum switch applies two operations in a coherent superposition of their causal orders and is a simple example of indefinite causal order. No quantum circuit that uses each operation once in a definite order can even describe it, yet photonic experiments have demonstrated its physical realization. We ask how many calls to the black-box operations a quantum computer in the circuit model needs to reproduce the switch. If one of the black boxes is known to be unitary, three calls suffice. For general channels on a $d$-dimensional system, we prove three results. In all three, circuits may use classical feed-forward and unlimited ancillary quantum memory. First, no finite number of calls suffices for exact simulation in the worst case. Second, approximation to any fixed error below $1/2$, measured in half the diamond norm, requires $Ω(d^{1/4})$ calls, exponential in the number of qubits. Third, an ordinary circuit can require exponentially many calls to obtain a single bit that the switch extracts with a few uses. We consider a device whose actual noise is dephasing in a fixed unknown basis and test it against a theoretical depolarizing model. The two channels have the same entanglement and average gate fidelities, so these figures alone do not distinguish them. At fixed noise strength, the quantum switch tells them apart with a number of device uses independent of $d$, whereas the circuit model requires $Ω(d^{1/16})$ calls. Noise characterization is needed for quantum error correction, and this gives a provable query advantage of the switch in a task relevant to practical quantum computing. We invite future work that extends such advantages to more practical computing tasks.

</details>


### [173] [Hierarchical Decoding for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16271)
*Xulong Tang,Narges Alavisamani,Joan Camps,Matthew Cha,Elise Jennings,Jack O'Connor,Humberto Munoz Bauza,Josh Izaac,Namit Anand,Ron Bewtra,Jeremiah Coleman,Johannes Heinsoo,Michael Ferguson,Ben Lau,P. Aaron Lott,Leonhard Neuhaus,Lee J. O'Riordan,Muhammad Osama,Masoud Mohseni,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述以“解码器级层次”与“码级层次”的统一分类梳理容错量子计算中的经典解码问题，系统比较了单码层次解码器、层次化/级联码的跨层协调方式、比特模态对负载与时延的影响，以及CPU/GPU/FPGA/ASIC/低温平台等实现方案，并指出性能报告与系统集成方面的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求经典解码器跟上综合征数据流，并向量子控制系统提供纠错信息与逻辑测量结果；在规模化条件下既要高吞吐，又要控制从读出到可用纠错的端到端时延。而相关文献在算法、执行策略、码构造与硬件平台上术语各异、评价条件不一，缺乏可横向比较的共享框架。

Method: 综述与分类学归纳：利用共同分类体系组织解码算法、执行策略与码构造，并在所报告的评测条件下比较其性能与权衡；明确区分“解码器级层次”（多级多资源协作解码单一码，含级联预解码、窗口化、流式与分布式实现）与“码级层次”（码构造本身引入多个解码层级，含层次化码与级联码，按硬判决、软可靠性信息、综合征信息协调跨层解码）；同时分析比特模态对解码负载与时间约束的影响，并比较CPU、GPU、FPGA、ASIC与低温平台的适配性。

Result: 建立了跨文献的连接与统一的术语/分类框架，给出各类解码方案在吞吐、时延与资源开销上的权衡分析，并梳理不同解码平台满足时延与吞吐需求的能力边界。

Conclusion: 该综述为QEC设计空间探索提供了共同基础，并识别出性能报告规范性与系统集成方面的空白，为面向实用量子纠错的后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires classical decoders that can keep up with syndrome data streams and supply correction information and logical measurement outcomes to the quantum control system. Meeting these requirements at scale requires high throughput while controlling the end-to-end latency from readout to usable correction. In this paper, we organize decoding algorithms, execution strategies, and code constructions using common classifications and examine their performance and trade-offs under the reported evaluation conditions. We distinguish decoder-level hierarchy, where multiple decoding stages or resources cooperate to decode a single code, from code-level hierarchy, where the code construction itself introduces multiple decoding levels. We first review single-code hierarchical decoders and their cascaded predecoding and windowed, streaming, and distributed implementations. We then study hierarchical and concatenated codes, focusing on how decoding is coordinated across code levels through hard decisions, soft reliability information, or syndrome information. We also examine how qubit modalities shape decoding workloads and timing requirements, and review how different decoding platforms meet these requirements, including central processing units (CPUs), graphics processing units (GPUs), field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), application-specific integrated circuits (ASICs), and cryogenic platforms. By assessing design trade-offs, this paper clarifies connections across the literature and provides a shared foundation for QEC design exploration. It also identifies gaps in performance reporting and system integration to guide future research toward practical quantum error correction.

</details>


### [174] [Scaling Pêlagos: Multi-GPU Orchestration for Relay Belief-Propagation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16285)
*Xulong Tang,Dionysios Diamantopoulos,Tristan Müller,Apo Kayi,Nelson Mimura Gonzalez,Muhammad Osama,Seetharami Seelam,Haralampos Pozidis,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将Pêlagos中继置信传播(Relay-BP)解码器扩展为多GPU并行架构（进程隔离调度、并行主机预处理、共享内存输出），在AMD MI300X/MI355X上实现端到端5.68×–7.01×加速，GPU解码吞吐接近线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要低逻辑错误率且满足实时约束的QEC解码器，启发式解码器的性能必须依赖大规模仿真评估；多GPU扩展是自然选择，但简单按syndrome批次分发会引入主机端预处理与结果收集开销，限制扩展性。

Method: 在Pêlagos Relay-BP解码器基础上引入三项优化：进程隔离的编排(orchestration)、并行主机预处理、共享内存输出；并在单gross [[144,12,12]]与双gross [[288,12,18]]码、R=50电路上进行多GPU基准测试（8×MI300X与8×MI355X）。

Result: 单gross：端到端加速5.68×(MI300X)与6.23×(MI355X)；双gross：6.92×与7.01×；按GPU解码时间计的吞吐双gross达7.58×与7.45×，呈近线性GPU扩展。并在ORNL基于MI355X的Lux超算上部署Pêlagos作为高通量QEC仿真探路应用。

Conclusion: 通过消除主机端开销的高效多GPU解码，为大规模QEC仿真提供了可行加速路径，并展示了量子计算在AI+HPC基础设施上的实际用例。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires quantum error-correction (QEC) decoders that achieve low logical error rates under real-time operating constraints. These constraints motivate heuristic decoding algorithms whose error-correction performance must be evaluated through large-scale simulation. High-throughput decoding accelerates this evaluation and the exploration of decoder parameters. Using multiple graphics processing units (GPUs) is a natural way to scale these simulations, but naively distributing syndrome batches introduces host-side preprocessing and output-collection overheads that limit scaling. To address these limitations, we extend the Pêlagos relay belief-propagation (Relay-BP) decoder with process-isolated orchestration, parallel host preprocessing, and shared-memory output. We evaluate both one-gross ($[[144,12,12]]$) and two-gross ($[[288,12,18]]$) codes in a multi-GPU environment. Specifically, for the one-gross circuit at $R=50$, eight AMD Instinct MI300X GPUs and eight AMD Instinct MI355X GPUs achieve optimized end-to-end speedups of $5.68\times$ and $6.23\times$, respectively. For the two-gross circuit, the corresponding speedups are $6.92\times$ and $7.01\times$. When throughput is calculated using GPU decoding time, the corresponding two-gross speedups are $7.58\times$ and $7.45\times$, indicating near-linear GPU scaling. We deploy Pêlagos on Lux, Oak Ridge National Laboratory's AMD Instinct MI355X-based supercomputer, as a pathfinding application for high-throughput QEC simulation. This deployment provides a concrete quantum-computing use case for the AI + HPC infrastructure supporting the Genesis Mission's scientific-discovery goals. These end-to-end measurements demonstrate a practical path to accelerating large-scale QEC simulation through efficient multi-GPU decoding.

</details>


### [175] [Collective information backflow from a local population record: Identifiability and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16334)
*Yifan Shi,Pengju Chen,Duan Wang,Quanzhen Ding,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究复合量子系统中信息回流（information backflow）诊断与实验仅有稀疏布居记录之间的不匹配问题；对两个相同量子比特集体耦合到指数关联真空库的情形，证明亮态布居轨迹可映射到迹距离回流诊断，可用支持向量学习或物理拟合从稀疏数据预测，但从短时噪声记录做普适推断受测量统计限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息回流需通过成对演化态定义，而实际实验往往只提供有限的布居数记录，两者存在缺口。作者希望厘清：在已知库族的框架下，布居记录是否足以推断全量子信道的非马尔可夫性诊断，以及在有限测量统计下这种推断的极限。

Method: 考虑两个相同量子比特集体耦合到关联函数为指数形式的真空库；用理想亮态布居轨迹确定与固定时间网格、固定态对库上的有限时迹距离回流诊断相关的库参数组合；分析失谐符号不可分辨性的影响；采用支持向量学习与直接物理拟合从稀疏布居记录预测诊断量；并构造双假设界分析有限测量统计下的误差下界。

Result: 未分辨的失谐符号不改变该诊断量，故亮态布居轨迹与回流诊断之间存在确定映射；稀疏布居记录可借支持向量学习或物理拟合较准确预测诊断。有限统计方面，双假设界识别出短时布居记录相似但回流显著不同的库；在总shots为600的六时刻协议下，任一类估计器在其中某个指定库处的期望绝对误差至少约0.13。

Conclusion: 在已知库族内，布居记录对回流诊断是充分的，可从稀疏数据学习预测；但在短时、含噪且测量次数有限的记录下，无法实现一致准确的推断，二者并不矛盾，说明“充分性”依赖已知库族与理想轨迹假设。

Abstract: Information backflow in a composite quantum system is defined through pairs of evolving states, whereas an experiment may provide only a restricted population record. We study this mismatch for two identical qubits coupled collectively to a vacuum reservoir with an exponential correlation function. An ideal bright-state population trajectory determines the bath-parameter combinations relevant to a finite-time trace-distance backflow diagnostic of the full two-qubit channel, evaluated on a fixed time grid and state-pair bank. The unresolved detuning sign leaves this diagnostic unchanged. This relation allows sparse population records to predict the diagnostic using support-vector learning or direct physical fitting. Finite measurement statistics impose a different restriction: a two-hypothesis bound identifies reservoirs with similar short population records but substantially different backflow. For one six-time protocol with 600 total shots, every estimator using those observations has expected absolute error at least approximately 0.13 at one of two specified reservoirs. Thus a population can be sufficient within a known bath family without permitting uniformly accurate inference from a short noisy record.

</details>


### [176] [Training quantum neural networks one job at a time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16365)
*Muhammad Faryad,Rabab Alomairy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将云队列下的量子-经典混合训练视为"执行效率"问题：在相同作业数与每作业采样数下，比较不同优化器"填满每个作业"的方式。提出以参数移位模板作为天然批次的批式参数移位训练，其梯度误差随每作业采样数反比下降（无下限），而SPSA存在无法通过增加采样消除的误差下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过云队列访问量子处理器时，每次往返代价高昂且一次往返可承载多个电路，因此经典优化器决定的不只是"每采样学到多少"，而是"每作业学到多少"。作者希望以开销摊销与加速器资源高效利用的视角重新审视优化器设计，回答如何从每次昂贵的量子交互中获得最大优化进展。

Method: 针对量子神经网络分类器，在作业数与每作业采样数固定、仅"作业填充方式"不同的条件下比较优化器。直接移植自QAOA的批式模式搜索在模型参数达数十个时失效，但其核心原则可迁移：每个作业内装入一批在发送前即固定的参数向量。对由Pauli旋转构成的电路，自然批次为参数移位模板（stencil）；另提出结构感知的模板搜索（stencil search），将其读作正弦结构。并与SPSA对比误差标度（仿真含两个噪声水平；硬件在IBM ibm_fez上6与8比特、三次交错运行）。

Result: 理论：批式参数移位的梯度误差随每作业采样数呈反比下降，而SPSA误差存在采样无法消除的下限。仿真：24–48个参数下，批式参数移位训练所需作业数为SPSA的1/5至1/2，且该排序在两个噪声水平下成立；结构感知模板搜索同样优于SPSA。硬件（ibm_fez，6/8比特，三次交错运行）：批式优化器在SPSA的50个作业中的第4至第20个即达到其最终训练损失；8比特测试准确率明显更高，6比特差异不显著。所有硬件数据可由原始采样重新推导。

Conclusion: 优化器设计应被理解为开销摊销问题：以"作业内固定批次"为原则、以参数移位模板为具体实现，能在有噪声的中等规模参数下显著减少昂贵量子交互次数，且梯度误差不因采样瓶颈受限。6比特下准确率差异不显著提示收益随规模/噪声条件而异，硬件结论可由原始采样复现以保证可验证性。

Abstract: When a quantum processor is reached through a cloud queue, a variational algorithm pays for every round trip, and a single round trip can carry many circuits. The classical optimizer therefore decides how much is learned per job, not only per shot. We study this for a quantum neural network classifier. We compare optimizers that are given the same number of jobs and the same shots per job, and that differ only in how they fill each job. This comparison treats hybrid quantum-classical training as an execution-efficiency problem: how to obtain useful optimization progress from each costly interaction with a quantum processor. The resulting perspective connects optimizer design to overhead amortization and efficient use of accelerator resources. A direct port of a batched pattern search that worked for the quantum approximate optimization algorithm stalls once the model has a few dozen parameters. What does transfer is the principle behind it: fill each job with a batch of parameter vectors that is fixed before the job is sent. For circuits built from Pauli rotations the natural batch is the parameter-shift stencil. We show that its gradient error falls as the inverse of the shots per job, while the error of simultaneous-perturbation methods (SPSA) has a floor that no number of shots removes. In simulation, batched parameter-shift training needs between a fifth and a half of the jobs SPSA needs, for 24 to 48 parameters, and the ordering holds at two noise levels. A new structure-aware stencil search reads the same kind of rows as sinusoids and also beats SPSA. On IBM's ibm_fez processor, in three interleaved runs at six and eight qubits, the batched optimizer reached SPSA's final training loss within 4 to 20 of its 50 jobs. Its test accuracy was clearly higher at eight qubits; at six qubits the difference was not significant. All hardware numbers can be re-derived from the raw shots.

</details>


### [177] [Generation and conversion of vortex light in coherently prepared hydrogen atoms and hydrogen-like ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16367)
*Fan Meng,Guo-Feng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出在相干制备的三能级Λ型原子介质中产生与转换光学涡旋的理论方案，利用下能级间方位角变化的相对相位给场附加相位缠绕，理想模型下满足l2=l1+m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索借助空间原子相干性调控涡旋光，实现高斯光生成涡旋以及入射涡旋OAM模式的转换。

Method: 理论解析建模：三能级Λ型原子介质，两下能级间设方位角依赖的相对相位（相干缠绕数m），推导生成场拓扑荷关系l2=l1+m，并用氢原子跃迁参数做数值示例；共振条件下分析传播中的吸收抑制与EIT效应。

Result: 解析与数值结果表明：高斯输入可产生涡旋，入射涡旋可转换为不同OAM模式；共振时亮态分量被衰减、出现EIT，后续传播吸收被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 该方案为通过空间原子相干性操控涡旋光提供了潜在框架；但空间相干作为初始条件被假定，其具体实验制备仍待解决。

Abstract: We propose a theoretical scheme for generating and converting optical vortices in a coherently prepared three-level Λ-type atomic medium. An azimuthally varying relative phase between the two lower states imparts additional phase winding to the generated field, yielding l2=l1+m in the ideal model, where l1 is the input topological charge and m the coherence winding number. Thus, a Gaussian input can generate a vortex, while an incident vortex can be converted to a different orbital angular momentum (OAM) mode. Analytical results and numerical examples using hydrogen transition parameters illustrate both processes. Under resonant conditions, the model predicts strongly suppressed absorption during subsequent propagation as the bright-state component is attenuated and electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) emerges. Spatial coherence is assumed as an initial condition; its detailed experimental preparation remains open. This scheme offers a potential framework for controlling vortex light through spatial atomic coherence.

</details>


### [178] [No Convergence in Learned Eavesdropping-Detection Dynamics for BB84 Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16372)
*Ramon Jose C. Bagunu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将BB84窃听者与阈值自适应检测器建模为交替最优响应对抗博弈，发现在多种学习规则与噪声水平下博弈不收敛，低噪声处窃听窗口仍开放；学习的检测器无法从任何单轮结果得到认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作证明强化学习窃听者可在保持误码率低于中止阈值并留有可用可蒸馏密钥的前提下截获BB84，本文追问同样具备自适应学习能力的检测器能否封闭该"隐蔽窗口"。

Method: 把交互设为表格型窃听者与表格型阈值自适应检测器之间的交替最优响应博弈；用"可利用性"（新攻击者对最终检测器做最优响应所达到的检测率与可蒸馏密钥比例）衡量；覆盖Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning三种学习规则、三种信道噪声水平与统一虚警纪律，并设两组冻结对手的对照实验，另检验末位行动者信息不对称机制。

Result: 博弈在20轮内未收敛，可利用性不趋近不动点，轮间波动不衰减，且波动幅度与最优响应过程自身方差相当；行为依赖噪声：低噪声（实用QKD工况）下隐蔽窗口仍开放，中噪声下检测器平均意义上压制攻击但竞争不收敛，高噪声下由信道而非学习策略抑制攻击；中噪声呈显著反持续性，支持末位行动者信息不对称机制。

Conclusion: 学习型检测器无法由单轮结果认证；封闭隐蔽窗口需打破信息结构（如随机化时序或消除末位行动优势），而非单纯让检测器"更会适应"。

Abstract: In prior work we showed that a reinforcement-learning eavesdropper can intercept BB84 quantum key distribution while holding the quantum bit error rate below the abort threshold and keeping a usable distillable key. In this paper we ask whether an equally adaptive learning detector can close this stealth window. We cast the interaction as an alternating best-response game between a tabular eavesdropper and a tabular threshold-adapting detector. We measure exploitability, which is the detection rate and distillable key fraction achieved by a fresh attacker best-responding to the final detector. Across three learning rules (Q-Learning, SARSA, Double Q-Learning), three channel-noise levels, and a uniform false-alarm discipline, the dynamics do not settle out to twenty rounds. The exploitability does not approach a fixed point, and its round-to-round swings do not decay. Two controls, one retraining the attacker against a frozen defender and one retraining the defender against a frozen attacker, show that the swings on each side match the variance of the best-response procedure itself. The dynamics fluctuate at the scale of best-response variance rather than converging, with neither player settling to a fixed response. The behavior is noise-dependent, and the stealth window stays open at the low noise where practical QKD operates. The defender contains the attacker on average at intermediate noise without the contest settling, and at high noise the channel rather than the learned policy suppresses the attack. As a candidate mechanism we examine a last-mover information asymmetry, supported by significant anti-persistence at intermediate noise and consistent with best-response noise at low noise. The results suggest that a learned detector cannot be certified from any single round, and that closing the window calls for breaking the information structure rather than out-adapting the attacker.

</details>


### [179] [Loss of the quadratic speedup in Grover search under local and coherent noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16398)
*Imama Tul Birrah Khan,Muhammad Faryad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析研究Grover算法在噪声下的性能退化：去极化信道与门对易，使噪声态等价于无噪声态乘以随深度几何衰减的相干权重；独立量子比特误差下，理想Grover算符会将逸出二维搜索平面的振幅"冻结"，由此可精确刻画单次误差效应，并将大数据库情形约化为Bloch型方程求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法仅有√N量级的查询复杂度优势，但噪声会侵蚀该优势。作者希望定量、解析地回答"噪声使优势退化得多快"，并统一解释不同噪声模型（比特翻转、相位翻转、去极化、振幅阻尼）的危害差异，此前仅有针对特定噪声模型的分散标度结果。

Method: (1) 全局去极化噪声：每层门后作用于整个寄存器，与门对易，故噪声态=无噪声态×几何衰减的相干权重，Grover旋转不被减速，仅权重衰减、最佳停止点前移。(2) 独立单量子比特误差：证明理想Grover算符使被推出二维搜索平面的振幅"冻结"，从而给出单次误差的精确作用。(3) 大数据库极限：将噪声搜索约化为平面内分量满足的Bloch型方程，算法为旋转、噪声表现为泄漏与退相干，推导各噪声模型的速率并解析求解。(4) 相干旋转角误差：用随深度线性展宽的带状界约束成功概率。

Result: 相位翻转在搜索早期危害最大、比特翻转在后期危害最大、组合误差在任何时刻都有害；局部噪声在主导阶上等价于被量子比特数增强的全局信道，因此每个量子比特可容忍误差随√N下降之外还额外快一个寄存器尺寸的幂次；相干误差下成功概率被宽度随深度线性增长的带状区域界定；所得闭式结果复现了此前针对特定噪声模型的标度律。

Conclusion: 噪声下Grover算法的√N标度仅在电路累积噪声较小时保持，最佳停止点须提前；二维平面外的振幅冻结机制是理解单次误差与局部噪声放大的核心，据此可由Bloch型方程统一推导各类噪声的速率与危害排序，并解释了为何某些误差更具破坏性。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm searches an unstructured database with a number of oracle queries that grows only as the square root of its size. Noise erodes this advantage, and we ask, analytically, how quickly. When a depolarizing channel acts on the whole register after every gate layer, it commutes with the gates, and the noisy state is the noiseless one with a coherent weight that decays geometrically with depth. The Grover rotation is not slowed. Its weight decays, the best stopping point moves earlier, and the expected query cost keeps its square-root scaling only while the accumulated noise over the circuit stays small. For independent errors on the qubits we show that the ideal Grover operator freezes any amplitude pushed out of the two-dimensional search plane. This gives the exact effect of a single error: phase flips do most damage early in the search, bit flips late, and combined errors at any time. For large databases it also reduces the noisy search to Bloch-type equations for the part of the state that stays in the plane, in which the algorithm is a rotation and the noise appears as leakage and dephasing. We derive the rates for bit-flip, phase-flip, depolarizing, and amplitude-damping noise and solve the equations. Local noise acts, to leading order, like a global channel strengthened by the number of qubits, so the tolerable error per qubit falls faster than the inverse square root of the database size by one power of the register size. For coherent errors in the rotation angle we bound the success probability by a band that widens linearly with depth. Our results recover, in closed form, scalings found earlier for particular noise models, and they explain why some errors are more harmful than others.

</details>


### [180] [Probing the extreme quantum violation of the Leggett-Garg inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16401)
*Akarshit Baranwal,Vishnu Purushothaman,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文反驳了近期关于"时间Tsirelson界(TTB)可被量子理论超越"的主张，指出这些证明存在概念性缺陷，在正确的标准Leggett-Garg框架下TTB不可被违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leggett-Garg不等式用于检验宏观实在论与量子理论的相容性，其量子最大违反通常称为时间Tsirelson界(TTB)。但有研究声称TTB不同于Bell不等式的Tsirelson界，可在量子理论内被超越，这动摇了该界的物理地位，需加以澄清。

Method: 批判性审视两篇代表性工作中TTB违反论证的推理逻辑：Phys. Rev. Lett. 113, 050401 (2014) 采用破缺简并的测量，Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 220202 (2025) 采用酉算符叠加。分析这些方案是否仍属于标准LG框架。

Result: 所考察的TTB违反论证存在概念性缺陷：其构造超出了标准LG框架（如引入非标准测量或非标准时间演化结构）。在恰当处理这些问题后，LG不等式的宏观实在论界可达代数最大值，故不存在对TTB的违反。

Conclusion: 所谓TTB违反依赖超出标准LG框架的方案，与检验宏观实在论和量子理论不相容性的目的无关；TTB作为时间域对应Tsirelson界的概念仍然成立。

Abstract: The Leggett-Garg (LG) inequalities were formulated for testing the incompatibility between the classical worldview of macrorealism and quantum theory. By analogy with the Tsirelson bound for Bell inequalities, the maximal quantum violation of LG inequalities is commonly referred to as the temporal Tsirelson bound (TTB). Nevertheless, several studies have claimed that, unlike the Tsirelson bound, the TTB can be exceeded within quantum theory. In particular, by employing degeneracy-breaking measurement in [\href{ https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.113.050401}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{113}, 050401 (2014)}] and by using the superposition of unitaries in [\href{https://doi.org/10.1103/vydp-9qqq}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{135}, 220202 (2025)}], it is argued that the TTB can be violated. In this work, we critically examine the reasoning underlying these purported TTB violations and show that the corresponding arguments suffer from conceptual shortcomings. We further show that once these conceptual issues are addressed appropriately, the macrorealist bound of LG inequality reaches the algebraic maximum, and hence no violation of the TTB is obtained. In particular, we demonstrate that the proposed TTB violations rely on frameworks that go beyond the standard LG framework, and therefore are of no relevance for testing the incompatibility between macrorealism and quantum theory.

</details>


### [181] [Engineering Bosonic Steady States through Fock-Space Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16431)
*Yan Liu,Jiahua Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出统一的Fock空间电流工程框架，用稳态电流平衡定律实现可选择的Fock态自主稳定，并可由真空自发建立目标态布居。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fock态是量子技术的重要资源，但本征损耗使其难以稳定；已有稳定方案通常针对特定微观过程分别构建，缺乏统一且可迁移的设计原理。

Method: 建立统一的Fock空间电流工程框架：推导稳态电流平衡定律，预测布居背景随Fock数反比标度，并量化局域电流如何选择性增强目标Fock态布居；通过全量子模拟检验理论预测。

Result: 电流平衡定律给出布居背景随Fock数反比标度的规律；揭示不同微观实现服从同一稳态设计规则，仅通过移动局域跃迁即可选择不同目标态而无需改变整体稳定架构；全量子模拟验证了从真空自主累积目标态布居。

Conclusion: 该工作为Fock态稳定提供统一物理图像，并确立了可迁移的可选择玻色稳态工程化设计原则，使稳定机制与具体微观实现解耦。

Abstract: Fock states are valuable resources for quantum technologies, but intrinsic loss makes their stabilization challenging. Whereas existing stabilization schemes are typically formulated for specific microscopic processes, here we develop a unified Fock-space current-engineering framework for the autonomous stabilization of selectable Fock states. We derive a stationary current-balance law that predicts a population background scaling inversely with Fock number and quantifies how a local current selectively enhances the target Fock-state population. This law reveals how distinct microscopic implementations obey the same steady-state design rule, allowing different target states to be selected by moving a local transition without changing the overall stabilization architecture. Full quantum simulations further support these predictions and demonstrate the autonomous buildup of target-state populations from the vacuum. Our results provide a common physical picture of Fock-state stabilization and establish a transferable design principle for engineering selectable bosonic steady states.

</details>


### [182] [Circuit-Level Analysis of Quantum Error Correction in Second-Generation Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16435)
*Suraj Vishwanath,Jonas Hawellek,Roberto Ferrara,Christian Deppe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在电路层面仿真了基于稳定子量子纠错的第二代量子中继器，比较Steane码与五量子比特码在不同噪声下的性能，指出码的选择必须依主导噪声机制而定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二代量子中继器需借助量子纠错抑制链路中的各类噪声，但不同稳定子码在不同噪声类型（非相干/相干、单一/混合）下的端到端表现缺乏电路级系统比较，码选择缺乏设计依据。

Method: 构建包含编码、伴随式提取、解码与纠缠交换的两段式中继链路电路级模型，在电路层面直接注入非相干噪声（去极化、退相位）与相干噪声（系统性过旋转），对比单错纠正的Steane码与五量子比特稳定子码，并考察无编码方案作为基准及混合噪声下的交叉效应。

Result: 相干噪声下Steane码优于五量子比特码；非相干噪声低噪声区、且大量物理比特受影响时，五量子比特码端到端保真度更高；高噪声区不编码反而最优；混合噪声下两类噪声对纠错性能存在中等程度的交叉影响。

Conclusion: 第二代量子中继器的码选择须随主导物理噪声机制自适应调整，电路复杂度与面向硬件的实现方式会影响相干噪声主导区的性能，为中继器纠错设计提供指导。

Abstract: We present a circuit-level simulation of second-generation quantum repeaters with stabilizer-based quantum error correction. We model a two-segment repeater link including encoding, syndrome extraction, decoding, and entanglement swapping, and compare the performance of the single-error-correcting Steane and five-qubit stabilizer codes under different noise scenarios. To that extent, we consider noise sources with incoherent (depolarizing and dephasing) and coherent (systematic over-rotation) errors and incorporate them directly at the circuit level. While the Steane code outperforms the five-qubit code at coherent errors, the five-qubit code achieves higher end-to-end fidelities under incoherent noise in the low-noise regime when a large number of physical qubits is affected by it. At higher noise regimes, employing no code turns out to be the best option. In mixed noise scenarios, we observe moderate cross-effects between the noise classes regarding the error correction performance. We conclude that code selection in second-generation quantum repeaters must be adapted to the dominant physical noise mechanism and that circuit complexity and hardware-aware implementation options influence performance in coherent-error-dominated regimes, providing design insights for error correction in quantum repeaters.

</details>


### [183] [Learning Sparse Graph States with Local Pauli Measurements: Tight Nonadaptive Bounds and an Adaptive Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16456)
*Matteo De Francesco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究测量 $X/Y/Z$ 基下识别图态所对应图所需的副本数：证明非自适应学习者的 $O(d^2\log n)$ 二次下界是本质的，但自适应（广度优先）策略可突破该障碍达到 $O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ouyang 与 Tomamichel 证明 $d$-正则图态用 $O(d^2\log n)$ 个副本可识别其图，而任意测量需 $\Omega(d\log n)$，留下 $d$ 的二次依赖是否必要的公开问题；本文旨在回答该问题并厘清自适应性与隐藏二分结构的作用。

Method: 核心观察：单个副本至多揭示邻接矩阵某主子矩阵的二元零化度（binary nullity）。非自适应情形下，对随机完美匹配并（random union of perfect matchings）利用傅里叶估计控制该零化度，导出下界；二分图情形采用类型方法（method-of-types）论证。上界侧构造广度优先自适应学习算法，分析对数直径连通二分 $d$-正则图。

Result: 若 $d^3\ln n\le n$，任何非自适应策略即使配备无限制经典解码器、且允许常数比例边报错，仍需 $\Omega(d^2\log n)$ 个副本；故二次依赖不可避免。对隐藏二分、对数直径的连通二分 $d$-正则图，非自适应仍需 $\Theta(d^2\log n)$，而广度优先自适应学习者仅需 $O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$；若公开二分结构，非自适应代价降至 $\Theta(d\log n)$。

Conclusion: $d$ 的二次依赖是非自适应学习的固有代价而非问题本质；副本间的自适应性可严格超越该屏障，其关键在于利用隐藏二分结构进行广度优先探索。零化度刻画为上下界提供了统一而简洁的分析框架。

Abstract: We study how many copies of an unknown $n$-qubit graph state are needed to identify its graph when every qubit is measured in the $X$, $Y$ or $Z$ basis. Ouyang and Tomamichel showed that $O(d^2\log n)$ copies suffice for $d$-regular graphs, while any measurement needs $Ω(d\log n)$, and asked whether the quadratic dependence on $d$ is necessary. We show that it is for nonadaptive learners: if $d^3\ln n\le n$, every nonadaptive strategy needs $Ω(d^2\log n)$ copies, even with an unrestricted classical decoder and even if a small constant fraction of the edges may be reported wrongly. We then show that adaptivity between copies can beat this barrier. On connected bipartite $d$-regular graphs of logarithmic diameter whose bipartition is hidden, nonadaptive learners still need $Θ(d^2\log n)$ copies, but a breadth-first adaptive learner needs only $O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$; revealing the bipartition brings the nonadaptive cost down to $Θ(d\log n)$. The lower bounds rest on a simple fact: one copy reveals at most the binary nullity of a principal submatrix of the adjacency matrix. We control this nullity for random unions of perfect matchings through Fourier estimates and, in the bipartite case, a method-of-types argument.

</details>


### [184] [Symmetry and quasi-gauge invariance of Krylov complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16474)
*Edoardo Ferretti,Matteo G. A. Paris,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出两类保持Krylov传播复杂度的幺正变换，并将其归因于初态能量分布与哈密顿量矩的不变性；在手性连续时间量子行走中，手性相位会解除谱简并并导致长时平均复杂度发生突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov传播复杂度刻画量子系统在时间演化中探索Hilbert空间的程度，但其在何种变换下保持不变、以及该不变性与谱结构和手性的关系尚不清晰，需要系统厘清。

Method: 通过分析保持初态能量分布及哈密顿量矩不变的幺正变换，确定两类复杂度保持变换：哈密顿量的对称算符，以及初态为其本征态的局域U(1)变换；并将后者作为手性连续时间量子行走中的准规范变换加以应用，结合循环（circulant）哈密顿量的谱分析与非循环量子路由模型进行验证。

Result: 循环哈密顿量中的手性相位一般会解除谱简并，使长时平均复杂度出现不连续变化；对于局域初态与非简并谱，复杂度为 C̄=(D−1)/2（D为Hilbert空间维数），且与连通度无关；非循环量子路由展示了相位依赖的谱权重所起的额外作用。

Conclusion: 即使概率时间反演对称性保持，Krylov复杂度仍可改变；其对手性的响应源于谱结构变化，而非直接源于输运不对称性。

Abstract: Krylov spread complexity quantifies how extensively a quantum system explores its Hilbert space during time evolution. We identify two classes of unitary transformations that preserve the complexity of the associated dynamics, and relate this property to the invariance of the energy distribution of the initial state and of its associated Hamiltonian moments. These transformations are either symmetry operator of the Hamiltonian, or local $U(1)$ transformations of which the initial state is an eigenstate. The latter naturally arise as quasi-gauge transformations in chiral continuous-time quantum walks, to which the invariance properties are applied. We show that chiral phases in circulant Hamiltonians generically lift spectral degeneracies, leading to a discontinuous change in the long-time-averaged complexity. For a localized initial states and a nondegenerate spectrum, complexity is $\bar{C}=(D-1)/2$, where $D$ is the Hilbert-space dimension, independently of the degree of connectivity. A non-circulant quantum router illustrates the additional role of phase-dependent spectral weights. These examples show that Krylov complexity can change even when probability time-reversal symmetry is preserved, linking its response to chirality to spectral changes rather than directly to transport asymmetry.

</details>


### [185] [Quantum Mpemba effect from logical-information exposure in monitored circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16479)
*Bochen Huang,D. L. Zhou,Chenfeng Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在受监测Clifford链中揭示了一种熵"Mpemba效应"反转：未编码Bell对态的纠缠熵在一轮循环后低于由更少Bell对制备的加扰参考态，且差距随链长线性增长，其机制是逻辑信息暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子Mpemba效应的核心疑问是：为何初始更远离稳态的态反而能更快接近稳态。作者希望厘清在受监测量子多体动力学（Clifford链）中这种"超越"是否真实存在、由何驱动，以及在何种条件下可在有限尺寸上被确证。

Method: 考虑监测下的Clifford链单周期演化；比较未编码Bell对态与由更少Bell对经加扰制备的参考态的平均纠缠熵。提出"逻辑信息暴露"机制：每个被记录解析出的独立逻辑奇偶性使稳定子态损失一个ebit；半链内部加扰可在保持所有Schmidt系数不变的前提下将逻辑信息散布到多个格点。并借助独立精确unitary two-design编码器给出一般理论界，再用稳态熵界在128与256量子比特上做数值认证。

Result: 未编码Bell对态的平均纠缠熵低于加扰参考态，且熵差随链长线性增长。理论上证明：在使用独立精确unitary two-design编码器时，任何初态平均熵不更大的纯态参考系综，在任意时刻的平均熵领先至多2 ebits。当稳态熵为次广延时，大尺寸下未编码态的反转构成真正的量子Mpemba效应；并在128与256量子比特规模上通过稳态熵界得到认证。

Conclusion: 监测Clifford链中的熵反转源于逻辑信息暴露而非Schmidt谱差异，是一种可被严格界定的量子Mpemba效应，且随系统尺寸增强；该结果为理解受监测量子动力学中的非马尔可夫式弛豫与初态结构作用提供了定量框架。

Abstract: In the Mpemba effect, a state initially farther from stationarity overtakes one that starts closer. After one cycle in a monitored Clifford chain, an unencoded Bell-pair state has lower mean entanglement entropy than a scrambled reference prepared from fewer pairs. The gap grows linearly with chain length. This reversal is driven by logical-information exposure: a stabilizer state loses one ebit per independent logical parity resolved by the record. Scrambling within each half can spread this information over many sites while preserving every Schmidt coefficient. We prove that with independent exact unitary two-design encoders, any pure reference ensemble with no greater mean initial entropy can lead in mean entropy by at most two ebits at any time. If the stationary entropy is subextensive, the unencoded reversal is a quantum Mpemba effect at large sizes. We further certify this effect at 128 and 256 qubits using stationary-entropy bounds.

</details>


### [186] [Effect of atomic interactions on quantum communication protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16519)
*Zixian Hong,John C. Schotland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了原子介质中光子量子通信的第一性原理分析框架，从实空间量子化出发推导偏振编码单光子态的有效模型，并将其应用于超密编码与BB84协议，表明两种协议性能均随原子-场耦合强度单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子通信协议分析多假设理想信道或唯象噪声模型，缺乏从光与原子相互作用微观机理出发的严格刻画，难以揭示介质中固有损耗与退相干对协议容量的真实影响。

Method: 对耦合到二能级原子系综的电磁场进行实空间量子化，导出偏振编码单光子态的有效模型；在此模型下分析固定Bell态字母表在擦除、退相位与退极化信道下的超密编码容量，并构建丢弃擦除结果、以微观模型估计本征损耗率的改进型BB84协议，推导渐近密钥率。

Result: 超密编码协议容量随原子-场耦合增强而单调下降；BB84渐近密钥率同样随耦合强度单调下降，并在有限临界耦合处降为零（密钥率为零）。

Conclusion: 原子介质中的光-物质耦合是限制量子通信性能的关键内禀因素，存在有限临界耦合使安全通信不可行；该第一性原理框架可将协议层指标与微观物理参数直接关联，为介质辅助量子通信的可行性评估提供定量依据。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework for analyzing photonic quantum communication in an atomic medium. Starting from a real-space quantization of the electromagnetic field coupled to an ensemble of two-level atoms, we derive an effective model for polarization-encoded single-photon states. We apply this framework to superdense coding to study communication efficiency and to the BB84 quantum key distribution protocol for communication security. For superdense coding, we evaluate the protocol capacity of the fixed Bell-state alphabet under erasure, dephasing, and depolarizing channels and show that it decreases monotonically with increasing atom-field coupling. For BB84, we formulate a modified protocol that discards erasure outcomes and uses the microscopic model to estimate the intrinsic loss rate. We derive the corresponding asymptotic key rate and show that it decreases monotonically with coupling strength and vanishes beyond a finite critical coupling.

</details>


### [187] [Asymptotic Attainability of the Channel RLD Fisher-Information Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16578)
*Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出信道RLD Fisher信息等于最优渐近SLD Fisher信息的精确充要条件（仅涉及Choi矩阵及其一阶导数的半定可行性条件），并在广义退相位信道上将其化为一组线性方程，构造出比值无界的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道RLD Fisher信息是可计算的、对最优渐近SLD Fisher信息的上界，但该上界一般不可达，即使Choi矩阵正定；因此需要刻画等式成立的精确条件，并量化二者差距可能有多大。

Method: 从Zhou和Jiang的渐近优化框架出发，建立由Choi矩阵及其一阶导数构成的半定可行性判据；允许RLD矩阵最大本征值退化及Choi矩阵秩亏但导数支撑在其像空间上。对广义退相位信道将判据化为和为1的非负证书权重的线性方程组；在完全退相位点对任意有限次使用计算最优SLD信息，并证明重复无辅助探针即可达到。

Result: 得到等号成立的精确半定可行性条件及其推论；在广义退相位信道（正定关联矩阵）下判据退化为线性方程组；在完全退相位点，单拷贝重复的无辅助策略达到最优SLD信息，且对自适应策略仍最优；构造出两种情形下信道RLD信息与最优渐近SLD信息之比值无界：维度增大时，以及固定qubit维度、最小Choi本征值趋于零时。

Conclusion: 信道RLD Fisher信息上界可达性由半定可行性条件完全刻画，该条件覆盖退化与秩亏等非平凡情形；即便在低维或接近奇异时上界也可能任意松弛，说明RLD上界作为渐近SLD信息的代理在一般情况下不可靠。

Abstract: The channel right-logarithmic-derivative (RLD) Fisher information is a computable upper bound on the optimal asymptotic symmetric-logarithmic-derivative (SLD) Fisher information per channel use over causally ordered, parameter-independent strategies. The bound is not always attained, even if the Choi matrix is positive definite. We determine exactly when equality holds using a semidefinite feasibility condition involving only the Choi matrix and its first derivative. The condition allows a degenerate largest eigenvalue of the RLD matrix and rank-deficient Choi matrices with derivatives supported on their range. The original channel family may change rank nearby. For generalized dephasing channels with a positive-definite correlation matrix at the evaluation point, the condition becomes a system of linear equations for nonnegative certificate weights that sum to one. At a completely dephasing point we also calculate the optimal SLD information for every finite number of uses and show that a repeated, ancilla-free probe attains it, even against adaptive strategies. Explicit examples give an unbounded ratio of the channel RLD information to the optimal asymptotic SLD information per use, either as the dimension grows or at fixed qubit dimension as the smallest Choi eigenvalue tends to zero. We derive the equality criterion and its consequences from the asymptotic optimization of Zhou and Jiang.

</details>


### [188] [Beauty and charm jet identification with end-to-end training on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16607)
*Lucio Anderlini,Pietro Asinari,Gianluca Bertaina,Davide Calonico,Marco Coïsson,Alessio Gianelle,Donatella Lucchesi,Giovanni Passaleva,Matteo Maria Piredda,Lorenzo Sestini,Michele Veltri,Davide Zuliani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IQM 5量子比特超导量子计算机上完整实现了用于LHCb实验b-jet与c-jet分类的变分量子线路，训练与测试全部在真实量子硬件上完成，首次证明硬件噪声不会显著降低性能，并提出量子硬件训练时间评估与线路优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味喷注（b、c喷注）识别是LHC实验中最具挑战的分类任务之一，传统机器学习虽有成效但观测维度高达数百；量子机器学习被视为潜在替代方案，但当前量子计算机比特数有限，能否处理此类高维任务尚属未解问题。

Method: 构建面向LHCb b/c喷注分类的变分量子线路（VQC），部署于都灵理工大学安装的IQM 5比特超导量子计算机，使用LHC实验模拟数据，将机器学习流程的训练与测试阶段全部在真实量子硬件上执行；同时提出量子硬件训练时间评估与线路优化的框架。

Result: 实现了首个在真实量子硬件上完成全流程（含训练与测试）的LHC喷注识别量子算法演示；结果表明硬件引入的量子噪声未显著恶化分类性能；并给出了训练时间与线路优化的评估方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为在更大规模超导量子设备上开展应用奠定基础：更多比特可编码超出经典模拟能力的信息，从而为探究高能物理分类任务中能否实现量子优势迈出一步。

Abstract: The identification of heavy-flavor jets, i.e. collimated streams of particles originating from the fragmentation of beauty and charm quarks produced in proton-proton collisions, represents one of the most challenging classification tasks in measurements at Large Hadron Collider experiments. In recent years, advanced machine learning techniques have been successfully employed to address this problem, while quantum machine learning has emerged as a promising alternative approach. However, the high dimensionality of the problem, typically involving hundreds of observables, raises an open question: whether the current generation of quantum computers, characterized by limited qubit count, can effectively tackle such complex tasks. In this work, a variational quantum circuit for the classification of b- and c-jets at the LHCb experiment is implemented on a 5-qubit superconducting quantum computer manufactured by IQM and installed at the Politecnico di Torino. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of a quantum algorithm for jet identification using simulated data from an LHC experiment in which all stages of the machine-learning workflow, including training and testing, are performed on real quantum hardware. The results demonstrate that the impact of hardware-induced quantum noise does not significantly degrade the performance. A framework for the evaluation of the training time on the quantum hardware and for the circuit optimization is also proposed. These findings pave the way for future applications on larger quantum devices based on superconducting qubits, featuring a larger number of qubits enabling the encoding of information beyond the reach of classical simulation and providing a step toward investigating whether quantum advantage may eventually be achievable in high-energy physics classification tasks.

</details>


### [189] [A Quantum Algorithm for Computing All Eigenvalues and Eigenstates of Sparse Hermitian Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16610)
*Krishnakanta Barik,Goutam Paul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子算法，用于计算稀疏厄米矩阵的谱（互异特征值集合），并为每个特征值在其本征子空间中制备对应的特征向量量子态，运行时间为 Õ(n s² log²n / Δ_min)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏厄米矩阵特征值与特征向量的计算是代数及大量科学计算应用中的基础问题与关键子程序。经典方法中，基于 Householder 三对角化与 QR 迭代的稠密方法需 O(n³) 时间；利用稀疏性的 Lanczos 方法虽可将特征值计算降至 O(n²s)，但恢复全部特征向量仍需 O(n³)。因此需要一种在保持量子态表示特征向量的同时降低整体复杂度的方法。

Method: 针对 n×n、s-稀疏的厄米矩阵 H，设计量子算法：特征值以给定精度的二进制串表示，特征向量保持为量子态而无需重构其经典形式；算法输出可用于估计期望值 ⟨x|M|x⟩（|x⟩ 为制备的本征态，M 为可观测量）。复杂度分析基于标准稀疏访问假设，并以归一化矩阵的最小谱隙 Δ_min 作为参数。

Result: 算法运行时间为 Õ(n s² log²n / Δ_min)。当逆谱隙可达 Δ_min^{-1}=O(n)（即 n 个互异特征值下最优的缩放，由近均匀间隔谱实现，如完美态传输自旋链与 Sylvester-Kac 矩阵），且稀疏度 s=o(√n / log n) 时，该算法在计算全部特征值与特征向量上相比经典 O(n³) 方法取得渐近运行时间改进。

Conclusion: 该量子算法在稀疏访问假设下，对近均匀间隔谱与适当稀疏度条件的稀疏厄米矩阵，实现了超越经典 O(n³) 全谱与全特征向量计算的渐近加速，并避免经典特征向量的重构开销。

Abstract: Computation of eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a sparse Hermitian matrix is a fundamental problem in algebra, arising both as a problem of independent interest and as a key subroutine in a wide range of computational and scientific applications. In this work, we develop a quantum algorithm that determines the spectrum of a sparse Hermitian matrix, defined as the set of its distinct eigenvalues, and for each eigenvalue, prepares a corresponding eigenvector as a quantum state in its eigenspace. The eigenvalues are represented as binary strings with a prescribed precision, while the corresponding eigenvectors are kept as quantum states, eliminating the need to reconstruct their classical counterparts. The algorithm instead enables estimation of expectation values $\braket{x|M|x}$, where $\ket{x}$ is a prepared eigenstate and $M$ is an observable. For an $n \times n$ $s$-sparse Hermitian matrix $H$, dense methods based on Householder tridiagonalization and QR iteration compute all eigenvalues and eigenvectors in $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. Exploiting sparsity, the Lanczos method computes all eigenvalues in $\mathcal{O}(n^2 s)$ time in exact arithmetic, but recovering all eigenvectors still requires $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. In contrast, our algorithm runs in time $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n s^2 \log^2 n / Δ_{\min})$, where $Δ_{\min}$ denotes the minimum spectral gap of the normalized matrix. For inverse gaps up to $Δ_{\min}^{-1} = \mathcal{O}(n)$, the most favorable scaling for $n$ distinct eigenvalues, attained by nearly uniformly spaced spectra such as those of perfect-state-transfer spin chains and the Sylvester-Kac matrix, and for sparsity up to $s = o(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$, the proposed algorithm achieves an improved asymptotic runtime over the classical $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ approach for computing all eigenvalues and eigenvectors, under standard sparse-access assumptions.

</details>


### [190] [Persistent-mediator architecture for universal fault-tolerant quantum computation with color codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16691)
*Zhi-Cheng He,Jingbo Wang,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出跨维度"持久中介"架构：2D色码数据寄存器与3D中介模块在每个周期边界均保持独立编码，仅通过瞬态保护子系统耦合，从而可重复地中介逻辑T门而无需反复制备/破坏性读出3D辅助态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D色码天然支持横向Clifford门，横向T门需借助3D码；已有跨维度方案要么切换计算编码、要么消耗3D编码态，导致辅助态重复制备与末端读出开销。核心问题是：3D模块能否在保持编码与保护的同时，为独立的2D寄存器连续中介T门。

Method: 构造膜状联合逻辑测量，配合匹配的跨维度CZ门、互补的弦状手术（string-like surgery）与前馈，闭合一个确定性逻辑T门周期；无后选择、无破坏性中介读出，并使3D块恢复到初始逻辑态。通过稳定子分析证明逻辑作用与空间距离保持；用Clifford替代模型做唯象与电路级模拟。

Result: 稳定子分析确认逻辑作用正确且空间距离不变；有限距离缩放接近匹配的3D存储基准；顺序测试显示低误差区逻辑错误近似线性累积。

Conclusion: 持久中介使计算寄存器保持2D编码的同时可反复调用高维非Clifford能力，避免辅助态重复制备与终止读出，为跨维度容错T门提供可复用方案。

Abstract: Two-dimensional color codes provide transversal Clifford gates, whereas suitable three-dimensional codes provide a transversal $T$ gate. Representative cross-dimensional schemes either switch the computational encoding or consume a 3D encoded state. Reusing the same protected 3D block would remove repeated auxiliary-state preparation and terminal readout, raising a central question: can the 3D module remain encoded and protected while mediating successive $T$ gates on a separate 2D register? Here we introduce a cross-dimensional persistent-mediator architecture that retains the 2D data register and 3D mediator as distinct encoded modules at each cycle boundary, coupled only through a transient protected subsystem. We construct a membrane-like joint-logical measurement and combine it with a matched cross-dimensional $CZ$, complementary string-like surgery, and feed-forward to close a deterministic logical $T$-gate cycle. The cycle avoids post-selection and destructive mediator readout and restores the 3D block to its initial logical state. Stabilizer analysis establishes the logical action and preserved spatial distance. Phenomenological and circuit-level simulations of a Clifford surrogate show finite-distance scaling close to the matched 3D memory benchmark, while sequential tests show approximately linear logical-error accumulation in the low-error regime. Persistent mediation enables repeated access to higher-dimensional non-Clifford capability while keeping the computational register in its 2D encoding.

</details>


### [191] [Thermodynamically Consistent Quantum-Classical Hybrid Dynamics: A Metriplectic Approach to Generalized Lindblad and Bloch-Torrey Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16708)
*Azeddine Zaidni,Ayoub Arraji,Philip J. Morrison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子—经典混合动力学的metriplectic（度量辛）框架，通过能量守恒与熵产生非负保证热力学一致性；耦合仅经由耗散过程产生，并给出Lindblad方程与Bloch–Torrey方程的混合推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子—经典混合动力学缺乏同时满足能量守恒与熵增（热力学第二定律）的一致性表述，现有混合描述中量子与经典子系统耦合方式及熵产生含义不清。

Method: 将混合Poisson括号与metriplectic $4$-括号结合，构造统一的4-括号结构；令量子—经典耦合项仅来自耗散部分（而非可逆的Hamilton耦合），从而把可逆流与不可逆流分离。

Result: 在该框架下导出热力学自洽的广义Lindblad主方程与Bloch–Torrey方程，实现量子弛豫与经典扩散的统一描述，并给出熵产生的明确定义与解释。

Conclusion: metriplectic 4-括号为量子—经典混合开放系统提供了兼具结构性与热力学一致性的统一表述，可作为构造混合耗散方程的生成性框架。

Abstract: We develop a metriplectic framework for quantum--classical hybrid dynamics that ensures thermodynamic consistency through conservation of energy and non-negative entropy production.
