The approach combines a hybrid Poisson bracket with a metriplectic $4$-bracket, with coupling between quantum and classical subsystems arising solely through dissipative processes. Within this hybrid formulation, we produce thermodynamically consistent generalizations of the Lindblad master equations and the Bloch--Torrey equations for open quantum systems. This provides a unified description of quantum relaxation and classical diffusion, together with a clear interpretation of entropy production.

</details>


### [192] [Reply to the Comment by Hradil and Řeháček](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16716)
*Jens Arnbak Holbøll Nielsen,Jonas S. Neergaard-Nielsen,Tobias Gehring,Ulrik L. Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对Hradil与Řeháček关于"压缩真空相位传感实验的量子计量优势在正确计入测量重复次数与总光子资源后消失"的质疑，作者指出该结论在数学上不成立；无论资源核算方式还是有限样本统计处理，都不足以否定所展示的量子增强局域相位灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hradil与Řeháček对先前的压缩真空相位传感实验提出批评，认为在严格计入测量重复次数和总光子资源后，所声称的量子计量优势将不复存在。作者需要回应这一质疑，澄清资源核算与统计推断中的概念与数学问题，以维护实验结论的有效性。

Method: 以Comment–Reply（评述—答复）形式展开理论辨析：重新审视总光子资源与重复次数的核算框架，检验对方论证中的数学推导与统计假设，并区分局域相位灵敏度（local phase sensitivity）与全局资源缩放两类判据。摘要未披露具体推导细节。

Result: 作者论证Hradil与Řeháček的否定性结论在数学上缺乏依据；资源核算的正确处理以及有限样本统计（finite-sample）考量均不能使已观测的量子增强局域相位灵敏度失效。

Conclusion: 压缩真空相位传感实验中展示的量子计量优势依然成立；对实验结论的质疑源于数学论证的不成立，而非实验本身的缺陷。局域相位灵敏度的量子增强不受资源重新核算与有限样本统计修正的影响。

Abstract: Hradil and Řeháček argue that the quantum metrological advantage demonstrated in our squeezed-vacuum phase-sensing experiment disappears, when measurement repetitions and total photon resources are properly accounted for. We show that this conclusion is mathematically unfounded and that neither resource accounting nor finite-sample statistical considerations invalidate the demonstrated quantum-enhanced local phase sensitivity.

</details>


### [193] [Probabilistic distillation of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16719)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在"叠加自由操作"框架下求出了将一个非正交态叠加（如光学猫态）转换为另一个的最优概率，该概率由"不能在确定性地保留全部分量的同时使干涉更具破坏性"这一原则完全确定（对所有分量置换对称的源态成立）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正交态叠加（光学猫态等）作为量子资源的效用取决于其分量之间的干涉方式，而现有相干性理论主要针对正交基下的叠加；需要建立以非正交参考态为基的"叠加"资源理论并给出态转换的最优定量刻画。

Method: 定义叠加自由操作（不能确定性地使各分量干涉更具破坏性而同时保留所有分量），据此推导置换对称源态的最优转换概率；以相干态为参考态（此时光子损耗、光子减除等把相干态映为相干态的操作为叠加自由）；对两个参考态情形给出完全解；并进一步分析等模叠加、集体（多拷贝）操作，以及对三个及以上等正重叠参考态的情形。

Result: 两个参考态情形完全可解：奇猫态经归一化光子减除可确定性变为偶猫态，而偶猫态变奇猫态仅以概率成功且该概率在小振幅下趋于零；偶猫态与Yurke–Stoler猫态互不可确定性转换。故等模叠加（相干理论中最大相干态的对应物）构成不等价的资源。多拷贝集体操作一般优于逐份转换，但对相干性蒸馏无增益。对三个及以上等正重叠参考态，没有态能以确定性到达所有其他态；干涉最具破坏性的等模叠加可确定性产生最大相干态，且对全参考态叠加的转换至少不劣于该态，但最坏情况下并不更好。

Conclusion: "确定性下不得加剧破坏性干涉"的原则唯一地确定了对称源态的最优转换概率；猫态类等模叠加是非等价资源，确定性可逆转换一般不可能，集体操作仅在部分任务（非相干性蒸馏）外带来增益。该结果为非正交叠加建立了类似相干性理论的定量资源框架。

Abstract: How well a superposition of nonorthogonal states, such as an optical cat state, serves as a resource depends on how its components interfere. Here we determine the optimal probability of converting one such superposition into another by superposition-free operations. These operations cannot make the components of a superposition interfere more destructively with certainty while keeping all of them, and this principle fixes the optimal probability for every source that is symmetric under permutations of its components. When the reference states are coherent states, as in a cat state, every operation that maps each of them to a coherent state, such as photon loss or photon subtraction, is superposition-free. For two reference states we solve the problem completely. Superpositions with coefficients of equal modulus, the counterparts of the maximally coherent states of coherence theory, are therefore inequivalent resources: an odd cat state becomes even with certainty by a normalized photon subtraction, whereas an even one becomes odd only with a probability that vanishes for small amplitudes, and the even and the Yurke$-$Stoler cat states cannot be converted into each other with certainty in either direction. Collective operations on several copies can do better than converting them one at a time, except in the distillation of coherence, which gains nothing from them. For three or more reference states with equal positive overlaps, no state reaches every other state with certainty; the equal-modulus superpositions that interfere most destructively still yield a maximally coherent state with certainty and reach every superposition of all reference states at least as well as such a state does, but in the worst case no better.

</details>


### [194] [Ground-State Spin Squeezing and Collective Excitations in $\mathbb{Z}_2$-Symmetric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16738)
*Arkadiusz Kosior,George Mihailescu,Karol Gietka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文厘清了基态自旋压缩与集体激发之间的关系，指出压缩可由两种横向涨落代价之比决定，而集体能隙由二者几何平均决定，从而证明存在"压缩增强同时能隙增大"的开隙分支，并具备更好的热鲁棒性与更快的绝热制备潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态自旋压缩是量子增强测量的重要资源，但通常与量子临界点附近激发能隙闭合相联系；能隙闭合会使态制备复杂化并降低热鲁棒性。作者希望找出压缩与集体激发之间的物理机制，并判定在何种条件下更强的压缩可以伴随更大的多体能隙。

Method: 以各向异性 Lipkin–Meshkov–Glick 模型为出发点，对二次型集体模式解析给出：压缩由两种横向涨落代价之比决定，集体能隙由其几何平均决定，并分析导致涨落代价不等的配对项与 Z2 宇称的关系；进一步推导压缩、极化率与按集体自旋跃迁强度加权的激发能之间的精确关系，推广到多模情形；结合 XXZ 链的弱耦合与自旋波分析，以及对幂律相互作用（含最近邻极限）的精确有限尺寸计算加以验证。

Result: 在二次型集体模式下，涨落代价比决定压缩强度、几何平均决定集体能隙；配对项受 Z2 宇称允许但非必然存在。对 XXZ 链，弱耦合与自旋波分析表明压缩可在集体能隙与多体能隙同时增大时发展；精确有限尺寸计算确认存在负耦合分支，两能隙重合且随压缩改善而增大；最近邻情形下该分支在较高温度下仍保留压缩，优于基态压缩相当但能隙闭合的分支。相反，在 Ising 链中交错激发可降低多体能隙而不直接贡献集体涨落。

Conclusion: 基态压缩既可伴随集体能隙闭合，也可伴随能隙打开；论文识别出具有增强热鲁棒性、并有望实现更快绝热制备的开隙分支，为在保持大能隙的同时利用压缩资源提供了理论依据与判据。

Abstract: Ground-state spin squeezing is a resource for quantum-enhanced measurements, but is commonly associated with a closing excitation gap near a quantum critical point. A closing gap can complicate state preparation and reduce thermal robustness. Here we identify the physical mechanism linking squeezing to collective excitations and establish when stronger squeezing accompanies a larger many-body gap. Using the anisotropic Lipkin--Meshkov--Glick model, we show that, for a quadratic collective mode, the ratio of two transverse fluctuation costs determines squeezing, whereas their geometric mean sets the collective gap. The pair terms responsible for unequal costs are allowed, but not guaranteed, by $\mathbb Z_2$ parity. To extend this picture beyond a single mode, we derive an exact relation between squeezing, polarization, and excitation energies weighted by their collective-spin transition strengths. For XXZ chains, weak-coupling and spin-wave analyses explain how squeezing can develop while both the collective and many-body gaps increase. Exact finite-size calculations for power-law interactions, including the nearest-neighbor limit, confirm a negative-coupling branch on which the two gaps coincide and increase as squeezing improves. For nearest-neighbor interactions, this branch also retains squeezing at higher temperatures than a gap-closing branch with comparable ground-state squeezing. In the Ising chain, by contrast, a staggered excitation can lower the many-body gap without directly contributing to collective fluctuations. Our results explain why ground-state squeezing can accompany either a closing or an opening collective gap, and identify a gap-opening branch with enhanced thermal robustness and the potential for faster adiabatic preparation.

</details>


### [195] [Reduced filtering using classical control hierarchy with linear optimality for continuous quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16751)
*Manuel Guatto,F. A. Cárdenas-López,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将连续量子纠错重构为经典控制论状态层级（精确双线性约化模型、精确条件滤波器、标准估计与反馈设计），证明滤波器维数由 Syndrome 空间决定并可降至 3·2^(n-k-1)，据此纠正两种反馈失效机制，提出只需 2^(n-k) 个类别概率的 committed-recovery 控制器，纠缠保真度显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子纠错相较数字范式有望降低阈值与硬件开销，但其复杂的模型估计严重削弱了实用性，需要一套可落地、低维、可被标准控制理论处理的建模与估计框架。

Method: 针对所有稳定子生成元均被连续监测、并由单一 Pauli 恢复信道驱动的任意 [[n,k,d]] 稳定子码：构造由精确双线性约化模型、精确条件滤波器以及标准估计/反馈设计组成的状态层级；证明不变子空间滤波下精确滤波器在维数 3·2^(n-k-1) 处闭合且为该类滤波中的最小维数；利用约化模型分析并修正强监测导致旋转伙伴阻尼与反作用扩散注入、以及在码空间线性化造成 syndrome 不可控而控制器停滞两种失效机制；在 correction 期零测量的极限下证明仅依赖 syndrome 的滤波器在每个恢复边界处精确。

Result: 滤波器维数由 syndrome 空间而非 Hilbert 空间决定，在不变子空间滤波内最小；降低 correction 期测量强度使 partner-steering LQR 控制的三比特码纠缠保真度提升 0.096；在偏移工作点设计增益可消除不可控退化；committed-recovery 控制器仅需 2^(n-k) 个类别概率，三比特 bit-flip 码达 0.947（对比基于测量的估计协议 0.920），五比特 perfect 码达 0.726（连续控制器至多 0.407），为所测文献方法中最高。

Conclusion: 连续量子纠错的模型估计可被约化为低维经典控制问题，精确滤波器维数仅随 syndrome 空间增长，配合修正后的反馈设计与 committed-recovery 控制策略，可在极低计算与硬件开销下取得优于现有连续控制器和测量型估计器的纠缠保真度。

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction promises reduced thresholds and hardware overhead compared to the digital paradigm, but its complex model estimation has greatly reduced its practicality. We recast this problem as a classical control-theoretic state hierarchy made of an exact bilinear reduced model, an exact conditional filter, and standard estimation and feedback design. For any $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code whose generators are all continuously monitored and which is driven by a single Pauli recovery channel, we prove that the exact filter closes at dimension $3\cdot2^{\,n-k-1}$. This dimension is set by the syndrome space instead of the Hilbert space and is minimal within invariant-subspace filtering. The reduced model exposes and corrects two failure mechanisms of the feedback loop. Strong monitoring damps the rotational partner through which the controller steers the stabilizer and injects back-action diffusion along the recovery path; lowering the measurement strength during correction raises the entanglement fidelity of partner-steering LQR control by $0.096$ in the three-qubit code with three monitored parities. Linearizing at the codespace leaves the syndrome uncontrollable, so the controller can stall; a gain designed at a shifted operating point removes this degeneracy. In the limit of zero measurement during correction, we prove that a syndrome-only filter is exact at every recovery boundary. The resulting committed-recovery controller needs only $2^{\,n-k}$ class probabilities and gives the highest entanglement fidelity compared among the methods tested in the literature, $0.947$ against $0.920$ for a measurement-based estimator protocol in the three-qubit bit-flip code, and $0.726$ vs at most $0.407$ for continuous controllers in the five-qubit perfect code.

</details>


### [196] [Inter-city 18.9 km free-space QKD over a dense urban corridor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16774)
*Ignacio H. López Grande,Nicolas Linale,Giacomo Paganini,Achraf El Bouazzaoui,Adrián S. Blanca,Ilektra Karakosta-Amarantidou,Manu Canals i Codina,Adam Vallés,Andrea Gorni,Jorge Fuenzalida,Evelyn A. Ortega,Valerio Pruneri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在巴塞罗那都市区约18.9 km自由空间链路上，用航空合规光学架构实验演示了785 nm诱骗态BB84 QKD，无需自适应光学，40分钟外场测试平均信道衰减约19 dB、平均QBER 1.31%，估算有限码长密钥率超过11 kbit/s，验证了简化架构下城际自由空间QKD的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面自由空间QKD可灵活补充光纤网络，但在稠密城市环境的长距离水平链路上，大气湍流、严苛的指向跟踪要求与运行约束会显著提高系统复杂度，实际部署仍具挑战。

Method: 采用面向部署的模块化QKD发射机，接口通用自由空间光终端，并配备独立GPS驯服同步架构；工作波长785 nm，室温单光子探测器，接收端不使用自适应光学；在巴塞罗那都市稠密城区开展18.9 km自由空间诱骗态BB84外场实验。

Result: 给出40分钟外场测试结果：平均信道衰减约19 dB，平均QBER为1.31%；依据实测信道参数估算平均有限码长安全密钥率超过11 kbit/s。同时报告发射机性能与光链路特性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，采用相对简单的航空合规光学架构即可在稠密城市区域实现城际自由空间QKD，系统便于集成到异构光基础设施中。

Abstract: Terrestrial free-space quantum key distribution (QKD) provides a flexible complement to fiber-based networks, but its deployment over long horizontal paths in dense metropolitan environments remains challenging, as atmospheric turbulence, demanding pointing and tracking requirements, and operational constraints can substantially increase system complexity. We experimentally demonstrate the operation of a decoy-state BB84 system over an 18.9~km free-space link across a densely populated urban area in the metropolitan region of Barcelona using an aviation-compliant optical architecture. The system operates at 785~nm using room-temperature single-photon detectors and requires no adaptive optics at the receiver. The modular QKD transmitter, based on a deployment-oriented design, is interfaced with a versatile free-space optical terminal and an independent GPS-disciplined synchronization architecture, facilitating its integration into heterogeneous optical infrastructures. We present the transmitter performance and optical-link characteristics, together with a 40-minute field test characterized by an average channel attenuation of approximately 19~dB and a mean QBER of 1.31\%. From the measured channel parameters, we estimate an average finite-size secret key rate exceeding 11~kbit/s. These results demonstrate the feasibility of inter-city free-space QKD over dense urban areas using a comparatively simple architecture.

</details>


### [197] [Non-Convex Optimization via Quantum Tunneling Search on a Photonic Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16791)
*Xi-Jun Yuan,Shuo Zhou,Yizhou Liu,Xuan Chen,Hong-Jing Du,Xiao-Wen Shang,Wen-Hao Zhou,Yu-Xuan Fu,Bao-Jing Liu,Ze-Feng Lan,Hang Song,Fang-Song Nie,Yu-Lin Yu,Xiao-Yun Xu,Jian-Peng Dou,Tongyang Li,Hao Tang,Xian-Min Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子隧穿搜索（QTS）算法，基于量子隧穿行走与局部退火，利用飞秒激光直写波导阵列实现非凸目标函数到哈密顿量势场的映射，实验上成功探索1D/2D非凸景观的局部极小，并通过变哈密顿量方法实现更高效搜索乃至全局最小识别，展示了相对经典SGD的量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中大量问题具有非凸目标或约束，其景观存在众多局部极小与鞍点，使基于梯度下降的方法难以探索全局最优，亟需新的非凸优化求解思路。

Method: 提出QTS算法，将量子隧穿行走与局部退火结合，借助量子隧穿效应穿越势垒；实验上用飞秒直写技术调控折射率，把目标函数映射为波导哈密顿量的势项，在间距10微米的29×1与21×9波导阵列上以特定输入态测量平均概率分布；进一步引入变哈密顿量方法。

Result: 平均概率分布在29×1和21×9波导阵列上分别凸显了多种1D与2D非凸景观的局部极小；变哈密顿量方法实现更高效搜索并识别全局最小；实验验证QTS相对经典随机梯度下降（SGD）具有量子加速。

Conclusion: QTS是一种成功且高效的非凸优化求解器，可作为近期模拟量子模拟器在机器学习领域的实际应用。

Abstract: Non-convex optimization plays an important role in modern machine learning since plenty of real-world problems have objective functions or constraints with non-convex properties. The non-convex nature of landscapes gives rise to numerous local minima and saddle points, dramatically hindering the ability of gradient descent-based methods to explore the landscape and obtain global optima. Here we present quantum tunneling search (QTS), an algorithm based on quantum tunneling walk and local annealing, which effectively explores the non-convex landscape by leveraging the quantum tunneling effect. In experiments, we map an objective function to the potential term of the waveguide Hamiltonian by controlling the refractive index with the femtosecond direct laser writing technique. With a proper input state, the average probability distributions on 10-micrometer-spaced 29 x 1 and 21 x 9 waveguide arrays highlight the local minima of various 1D and 2D non-convex landscapes, respectively. By further introducing the varying Hamiltonian approach, more efficient search and even the identification of the global minimum are achieved. Our work experimentally verifies that QTS is a successful and efficient non-convex optimization solver, demonstrating quantum speedups over classical stochastic gradient descent (SGD), which serves as a practical application of near-term analog quantum simulators in the field of machine learning.

</details>


### [198] [Hong$-$Ou$-$Mandel-like interference between two independent narrowband thermal states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16794)
*Sheng-Hsuan Huang,Thomas Dirmeier,Andrea Aiello,Kaisa Laiho,Dmitry V. Strekalov,Gerd Leuchs,Christoph Marquardt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用两个热态之间的HOM类干涉，在单光子功率量级下测量两个窄带光源的频率差；时间符合计数可提取频率差绝对值，且与理论模型吻合，并可确定预报单光子源的零失谐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子网络需要大量不可区分窄带光源与原子量子存储直接接口，而独立窄带光源之间的频率匹配（频率锁定/零失谐）通常十分困难。

Method: 研究两个热态之间的Hong-Ou-Mandel类（HOM-like）干涉，在单光子功率量级下，通过时间符合计数测量两光源频率差；并建立理论模型，将热态HOM类干涉的拍频信号与预报单光子源HOM干涉的振荡轮廓进行对比。

Result: 频率差绝对值可由时间符合计数提取，实验结果与理论模型吻合良好；热态间HOM类干涉的拍频信号振荡轮廓与预报单光子源HOM干涉相似。

Conclusion: 该方法可在极低光功率下测量窄带光源频率差，并可用于寻找两个预报单光子源的零失谐，为大规模量子网络中不可区分光源的频率匹配提供可行途径。

Abstract: Narrowband photonic sources are promising candidates in quantum networks for a direct interface with atomic quantum memories. However, building a large-scale quantum network requires many indistinguishable sources, and matching the frequencies of independent narrowband sources is generally challenging. Here, we propose a method to measure the frequency difference between two narrowband light sources in the single-photon power regime by studying Hong$-$Ou$-$Mandel-like (HOM) interference between thermal states. Our results show that the absolute value of the frequency difference can be extracted from the temporal coincidence counts, and these results agree well with our theoretical model. We also demonstrate that the beat signal of the HOM-like interference between two thermal states has a similar oscillation profile to the HOM interference between two heralded single-photon sources, confirming that this method can be used to find the zero detuning of two heralded single-photon sources.

</details>


### [199] [Quantum Keynesian Beauty Contest with Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16829)
*Hiu Ming Lau,Kareem Raslan,Kieran Hymas,Qiang Sun,Azhar Iqbal,Derek Abbott,James Q. Quach,Andrew D. Greentree*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在量子博弈论框架下研究凯恩斯选美竞赛（$p$-Beauty Contest），提出利用多模压缩产生的全局变换对玩家出价进行后处理，从而抑制$p>1$时的暴涨（boom）与$p<1$时的暴跌（bust）理性行为，并证明类似的抑制机制可用经典全局中介实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典$p$-BC中，理性参与者的最优反应不断迭代会不可避免地导致策略性暴涨或暴跌（$p>1$为boom，$p<1$为bust），使博弈结果极端化。作者希望借助量子连续变量处理手段，探索能否通过量子资源"淬灭"这种极端理性行为，并厘清其背后的博弈论机制。

Method: 构建$p$-BC的量子连续变量实现：玩家出价经量子态（多模压缩态）编码，经测量读出后由增益递增的多模压缩算符产生全局变换作用于全体出价；分别分析两玩家、三玩家及平均场型多玩家情形，推导单次测量（single-value measurement）下收益概率分布的解析形式，并与期望值测量（expected-value measurement）情形下的理性行为作对比；同时构造对应的经典信道+全局中介版本以识别机制来源。

Result: 随多模压缩增益增大，暴涨/暴跌的理性偏离被平滑抑制；作者给出单次测量下收益概率分布的解析表达式，并显示单次测量与期望值测量设定的理性行为存在差异；此外证明一个完全类似的博弈可由经典信道下的全局中介实现，取得与量子方案同等的boom/bust抑制效果。

Conclusion: 抑制$p$-BC中暴涨暴跌现象的根本博弈论机制是"全局中介"（global mediation），量子压缩变换只是实现该机制的一种物理途径；量子连续变量平台为改造经典博弈模型提供了可操作且可解析分析的工具。

Abstract: The development of quantum computing has opened up opportunities to re-explore conventional game models by the integration of quantum processes. Through the established framework of quantum game theory, we examine the problem of speculative pricing in the Keynesian Beauty Contest model -- the $p$-Beauty Contest ($p$-BC). In the $p$-BC, players are rewarded with the optimal payoff for successfully bidding at some fraction $p$ of the average of all of the players' bids. Consequently, \textit{rational} plays in the $p$-BC inexorably lead to boom or bust strategies for $p>1$ or $p<1$ respectively. We demonstrate a quantum continuous-variable implementation of $p$-BC where the boom-and-bust rational behavior is mitigated through a global transformation of the players' bids generated by increasing multimode squeezing. While we focus on a quantum mechanical implementation of the $p$-BC, we show that an entirely analogous game can be formulated via classical channels by a global mediator. Thus we identify global mediation as the underlying game-theoretic mechanism for quenching boom and bust phenomena in our model. We analyze the quantum $p$-BC for two, three, and many players in a mean-field type implementation. We derive an analytical form for the probability distribution of the payoff under single-value measurements of the quantum processing circuit, and explore how the rational behavior differs from the expected-value measurement setting.

</details>


### [200] [Uncertainty-aware quantum machine learning based on Dempster-Shafer theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16903)
*Marie Kempkes,Jakob Spiegelberg,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Vedran Dunjko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Dempster-Shafer证据理论的量子分类框架，使参数化量子电路能直接从数据学习"集合级"质量函数分配，从而在预测中显式表达不确定性，并支持多项式资源的量子DS融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习工作主要关注模型的表达能力与平均性能指标（如准确率），而对预测可靠性/不确定性的刻画关注不足；传统量子分类器难以表达"不知道"或标签集合层面的不确定性。

Method: 构建不确定性感知的量子分类框架：将Dempster-Shafer理论的信任分配（质量函数）赋予单标签及标签子集，并提出训练策略使参数化量子电路从数据中直接学习这些分配；据此高效制备相应量子态，并支持多种后处理策略，包括在质量函数定义于指数级子集时仍具多项式资源需求的量子DS融合流程。

Result: 所提训练策略可高效制备对应量子态；量子Dempster-Shafer融合在质量函数覆盖指数多子集的情形下仍只需多项式资源，为不确定性感知的量子分类提供了可行的实现路径。

Conclusion: 该工作使量子模型能够表达对自身预测的不确定性，将量子模型的评估从单一预测性能拓展至可靠性维度，是迈向不确定性感知量子机器学习的一步。

Abstract: A large body of work in quantum machine learning has centered on characterizing the expressivity and performance of quantum models. In contrast, little attention has been paid to the reliability of predictions beyond average performance measures such as accuracy. In this work, we introduce an uncertainty-aware quantum classification framework based on Dempster-Shafer theory. This theory allows to express uncertainty through weights assigned not only to individual classification labels but to sets of labels. We introduce a training strategy that enables parameterized quantum circuits to learn such assignments directly from data. This provides an efficient way to prepare the corresponding quantum states and enables the application of different post-processing strategies. In particular, it offers a practical setting for quantum Dempster-Shafer fusion procedures with polynomial resource requirements, even when the corresponding mass functions are defined over exponentially many subsets. Overall, our work represents a step toward enabling quantum models to express uncertainty about their predictions, thereby extending the evaluation of quantum models beyond predictive performance alone.

</details>


### [201] [When can local unitaries generate a complete orthogonal basis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16921)
*Julian Eisfeld,Hermann Kampermann,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于两qutrit系统(3×3)是否具有通用局部可编码性(ULE)的公开猜想——证明其成立；同时给出了d×2、d×3、含无限维子系统的二分系统等新情形下的通用局部编码构造，将启发式参数计数提升为排除UFE的必要条件，并给出非ULE的 operational 解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "局部可编码性"问的是给定的多体纯态能否仅用局部酉旋转补全为一组标准正交基；若某系统中任一态都具备该性质则称该系统为通用局部可编码(ULE)，这在量子信息论、量子工程与数学中均有应用。该问题虽表述简单，却依赖系统局域维度：已知2×2、4×4、8×8成立，3×3仅为猜想，而Pauwels与Gühne近期证明四比特及以上非ULE。作者旨在解决3×3猜想并系统刻画ULE的维度边界与判据。

Method: 证明两qutrit(3×3)系统的通用性，从而解决该公开猜想；显式构造多类通用局部编码，包括d×2(d≥2)、d×3(d=3,4及d≥6)，以及含一个或两个无限维子系统的任意二分系统；对有限维多体系统，把启发式参数计数严格化为ULE的必要条件；并构造判别任务以刻画非ULE的 operational 意义。

Result: 3×3猜想被证明为真；得到d×2、d×3(d=3,4,d≥6)以及含无限维子系统的二分系统中的通用局部编码构造；所导出的必要条件在无穷多情形下排除了ULE；并证明非ULE等价于在"需检测未知随机局部酉旋转"的判别任务中一般测量优于可投影模拟的测量。

Conclusion: ULE是否成立强烈依赖局域维度，且呈现非平凡的模式；本文以3×3的证明、系列显式构造、严格必要条件及操作解释，基本厘清了有限维与无限维二分/多体系统中局部可编码性的边界。

Abstract: The question of local encodability asks: can a given, multipartite pure quantum state be completed to an orthonormal basis using only local unitary rotations? If any such state in a given system has this property, it is called universally locally encodable (ULE), a feature, which has applications in quantum information theory, engineering and math. Despite its easy formulation, the answer to this question depends nontrivially on the local dimensions of the system: While universal local encodability is known for $2\times 2$, $4\times 4$ and $8\times 8$-dimensional systems, it was so-far only conjectured to hold in $3\times 3$, and non-ULE was recently proved for systems of four and more qubits by Pauwels and Gühne.
  We resolve the open conjecture by proving universality for two qutrits. Furthermore, we construct (among others) universal local encodings in dimensions $d\times2$ for $d\ge2$, in dimensions $d\times3$ for $d=3,4$ and $d\ge6$, and in every bipartite system with one or two infinite-dimensional subsystems. For finite-dimensional multipartite systems, we turn a heuristic parameter count into a necessary condition for universality, which excludes universal local encodability in infinitely many cases. Finally, we give universal local encodability an operational interpretation by showing that non-ULE is equivalent to whether general measurements yield an advantage over projectively simulable ones in discrimination tasks where unknown, random local unitary rotations have to be detected.

</details>


### [202] [Communication Supremacy of a Qubit in Holevo Frenkel Weiner Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16940)
*Partha Patra,Sumit Mukherjee,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在HFW框架下，若注入纠缠或测量非兼容性等量子资源、且共享随机性（SR）作为自由资源，则qubit通信相对c-bit通信无优势；本文证明即使在这些条件下，借助发送方制备的密码学约束，qubit通信仍可超越c-bit，并可证书化量子优势与无种子随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为：在Holevo–Frenkel–Weiner（HFW）通信任务框架中，除非额外提供量子资源或限制经典共享随机性（SR），否则qubit通信不比c-bit通信有优势。作者希望检验在不注入纠缠、测量非兼容性等量子资源且SR自由可用的条件下，qubit是否仍然存在本质优势，并寻找能刻画该优势的可实验检验手段。

Method: 构造一个prepare-measure场景：发送方Alice在特定密码学约束下制备六个量子态，接收方Bob执行三结果测量。该制备约束对qubit通信与c-bit通信的能力影响不同，从而产生量子优势。作者引入一个witness（判据/见证量）以区分超出经典策略集合的量子策略，并求取其最优量子优势值；随后分析witness在噪声条件下的鲁棒性，并讨论Bob三结果qubit POVM对coherence的探测能力。

Result: 所提witness能够区分经典策略集合之外的量子策略，并给出最优量子优势；该优势无需种子随机性即可实现认证随机性生成（因Bob无输入）；在噪声情形下优势仍然存在，具备实验可行性；Bob的三结果qubit POVM表现出独特的coherence探测能力。

Conclusion: 在HFW框架中，仅靠制备端的密码学约束即可使qubit通信在SR自由、无额外量子资源的条件下获得严格量子优势；该优势可由witness证书化，兼具鲁棒性与可实验性，并可用于无种子认证随机性生成。其非经典根源在于三结果qubit POVM对coherence的探测能力。

Abstract: The prevailing wisdom asserts that in the Holevo Frenkel Weiner (HFW) framework of communication tasks, a qubit offers no advantage over a c bit unless additional quantum resources are supplied or the classical shared randomness (SR) is restricted. In contrast, we establish the supremacy of qubit communication in the HFW framework when additional quantum resources, such as entanglement or measurement incompatibility are not injected and SR is a free resource. We consider a prepare-measure scenario where the sender, Alice, prepares six states subject to a specific cryptographic constraint, and the receiver, Bob, performs a three-outcome measurement. Such a constraint on preparation impacts the power of qubit communication quite differently than that of c-bit communication, ultimately giving rise to the quantum advantage. We introduce a witness capable of distinguishing quantum strategies that lie beyond the set of possible classical strategies and demonstrate the optimal quantum advantage. {As an application, we demonstrate the generation of certified randomness without requiring any seed randomness, since Bob has no input.} We further analyse the robustness of the witness and show that the advantage persists in noisy situations, making it suitable for experimental realisation. Finally, we demonstrate that Bob's three outcome qubit positive operator valued-measure (POVMs) has a distinct capability of detecting coherence, which can be regarded as the non-classical feature responsible for showcasing the quantum advantage.

</details>


### [203] [Lorentz Boosts and Governing Equations of the Quantum Geometric Tensor in 4D Energy-Momentum Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16967)
*Yiming Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子几何张量（QGT）从传统的（动量）参数空间推广到四维能量-动量空间（ω/c, k），把频率作为独立坐标，从而把量子度规与Berry曲率统一为"类引力度规 + 类电磁规范场"的量子几何框架，并给出场方程、守恒律与实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QGT的虚部（Berry曲率）支配反常速度与量子化霍尔输运，实部（量子度规）决定量子权重及平带超流刚度、介电响应等几何贡献。然而现有理论通常只在空间动量空间定义QGT，把频率视为外参，无法统一描述含时/时变介质中的几何效应，也无法在同一框架下处理"度规型"几何与"规范场型"几何。作者希望借助洛伦兹协变把二者纳入同一四维结构。

Method: 1）将QGT定义为四维能量-动量空间张量 Q_{μν}=g_{μν}-(i/2)Ω_{μν}，频率与动量作为独立坐标；2）利用实空间平面波相位的洛伦兹不变性，确定能量-动量空间中的洛伦兹变换规则；3）对静态Dirac/Weyl系统作洛伦兹boost，生成频率度规、四极型频率-动量剪切度规及电型Berry-Maxwell场；4）识别五个洛伦兹不变收缩量；5）由静态Weyl几何与洛伦兹协变性推导量子度规与Berry曲率的场方程与流守恒律；6）建立QGT高斯谱振幅与4D时空局域高斯事件的对应关系。

Result: 得到四维QGT的协变表述：boost后出现频率度规、频率-动量剪切度规和类似电场的Berry-Maxwell场；给出五个洛伦兹不变收缩量；导出量子度规与Berry曲率各自的场方程与守恒流；并建立高斯谱振幅与4D局域高斯事件的映射。

Conclusion: 四维QGT提供了一个统一量子几何框架，将类引力度规与类电磁规范场自洽地结合，并把几何自洽性与物理事件动力学联系起来；可在时空晶体或时变介质中实验检验。

Abstract: In solids, the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), $Q_{μν}=g_{μν}-\frac{i}{2} Ω_{μν}$, combines the quantum metric and Berry curvature. Its imaginary part governs anomalous velocity and quantized Hall transport, while its real part determines the quantum weight and geometric contributions to flat-band superfluid stiffness and dielectric response. Here, we extend the QGT to four-dimensional (4D) energy-momentum space ($ω/c,k$), treating frequency and momentum as independent coordinates. Invariance of the real-spacetime plane-wave phase fixes their Lorentz transformation in energy-momentum space. Lorentz boosts of static Dirac and Weyl systems then generate frequency metrics, quadrupolar frequency-momentum shear metrics, and electric-like Berry-Maxwell fields. We identify five Lorentz-invariant contractions and derive the governing field equations and flow conservation laws for both the quantum metric and Berry curvature from static Weyl geometry and Lorentz covariance. We further connect Gaussian spectral amplitudes of the QGT to localized Gaussian events in 4D spacetime. Finally, we discuss experimental realizations in spacetime crystals or time-varying media and clarify how this unified quantum geometry of a gravitational-like metric and electromagnetic-like gauge fields connects geometric self-consistency with physical event dynamics.

</details>


### [204] [Improved Low-Cost Quantum Error Mitigation with Benchmarked-Noise Zero-Noise Extrapolation (bnZNE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16971)
*Joseph Harris,Kevin Lively,Peter Ken Schuhmacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了"基准噪声零噪声外推"(bnZNE)：用经典可模拟、结构镜像的基准电路在每个缩放噪声水平上灵敏地测量有效错误率，改进ZNE的经典外推步骤，在同等总采样成本下取得比标准ZNE更准确的结果，并在IBM与IQM硬件上对多类大规模应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准零噪声外推(ZNE)在总采样预算有限时精度受限，其外推环节依赖整数噪声水平的简单拟合；作者希望在不增加成本的前提下提升外推的准确性与灵活性，并使方法兼容Pauli twirling、读出误差缓解等噪声整形技术。

Method: 提出randomized bnZNE以支持每个噪声水平运行多个随机电路；从标准应用电路生成经典可模拟、结构镜像的基准电路，在各缩放噪声水平上灵敏测量有效错误率（突破整数取值限制）；为IQM超导器件定制基准电路生成方法；在IBM与IQM硬件上对大量大规模应用进行实验测试与对比。

Result: 在IBM和IQM硬件上的众多大规模应用中，bnZNE一致地给出比标准ZNE更准确的结果；理论上前期工作已证明在门级去极化噪声下bnZNE可达与标准ZNE同阶的噪声缓解效果。

Conclusion: bnZNE是ZNE的一种通用替代方案，可结合噪声整形技术，在等效成本下于实用规模(utility scale)实现更高精度的误差缓解。

Abstract: We theoretically extend and experimentally demonstrate benchmarked-noise zero-noise extrapolation (bnZNE), a simple and low-cost method for quantum error mitigation which aims to outperform the highly popular ZNE method for an equal total shot cost by improving the classical extrapolation step. We recently proposed bnZNE as a new error mitigation method (Harris et al., arXiv:2603.10224 (2026)) and showed that it can mitigate noise to the same order as standard ZNE under gate-wise depolarizing noise. Here, we introduce randomized bnZNE which addresses the case of running multiple random circuits per noise level, allowing for the incorporation of noise-tailoring techniques like Pauli twirling and readout error mitigation, and test the method on the IBM and IQM hardware for a plethora of large-scale applications, observing bnZNE to consistently produce more accurate results than standard ZNE. Our approach enhances the extrapolation process to the zero-noise limit by using classically-simulable and structure-mirroring benchmark circuits, generated from the standard application circuit, to sensitively measure the effective error rate at each scaled noise level beyond simple integer values. We give a new method for generating tailored benchmark circuits for the IQM superconducting devices and test them experimentally. Our results demonstrate that bnZNE presents a versatile alternative to ZNE which can enable more accurate results at utility scales with an equivalent cost.

</details>


### [205] [Entanglement detection from moments of the realigned state: application to continuous-variable quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17021)
*Serge Deside,Vojtěch Kala,Nicolas J. Cerf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于密度算符经实排列映射（realignment map）后所得算符的奇异矩与弱矩的一族可分性判据，并给出用线性干涉仪耦合多个相同副本态即可测量这些实排列矩的光学方案，二阶实排列矩判据可证认高斯与非高斯态（含损耗NOON态）的纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子系统中全态层析实验代价高，亟需可实验高效实现且适用范围广的纠缠判据。已有部分转置矩的多副本方法提供了启发，作者希望构造基于其他映射（realignment）矩的新判据家族。

Method: 对密度算符施加实排列映射得到新算符，利用其奇异矩与弱矩构造可分性判据；由于可分态的实排列算符满足相应不等式，违反即证明纠缠。实验上借由耦合若干相同副本态的线性干涉仪实现这些矩的测量，类比测量部分转置矩的多副本技术。

Result: 二阶实排列矩判据已足以证认一大类量子光学态的纠缠；在高斯态与非高斯态（包括有损耗的NOON态）上验证了检测能力，并给出对应光学测量方案。

Conclusion: 实排列矩提供了一族实用、多副本可实现且超越部分转置范式的连续变量纠缠判据，二阶矩层面即具强检测力，适合光量子实验中的纠缠证认。

Abstract: Certifying entanglement is a central problem in quantum information sciences, especially for continuous-variable quantum systems where full state tomography is experimentally demanding. We introduce a family of separability criteria based on the singular moments and weak moments of the operator obtained by applying the realignment map to the density operator. In practice, we find that the criterion based on the second-order moments of the realigned operator is already sufficient to witness the entanglement of a wide range of quantum optical states. The realigned moments can be readily accessed using an optical scheme consisting of a linear interferometer that couples several identical copies of the state, similarly to the multicopy technique used to measure the partial-transpose moments of a state [Phys. Rev. Research 8, 033064 (2026)]. We illustrate the detection capability of the second-order realigned-moment method for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states, including lossy NOON states.

</details>


### [206] [Physical Observables and Superselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17051)
*Martin Paul Vaughan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述超选择规则（superselection rule）如何限制可观测量与态空间，从而重塑"物理"的判定标准，并讨论电荷/能量守恒导致的本征超选择规则以及退相干、缺乏共享参考系导致的"有效"超选择规则的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子力学中态由Hilbert空间单位向量表示、可观测量由自伴算子表示，二者可自由对应；但超选择规则的引入会使部分自伴算子失去物理可观测量地位，并连带限制态与态变换，因此有必要从"何者为物理"的角度系统梳理该概念的后果与依据。

Method: 概念性综述：以Hilbert空间上的超选择规则为出发点，分析其对可观测量集合、态空间的分叶结构（相干叠加受限、相对相位不可观测）及物理过程（对称变换/超选择荷）的约束；进而分别论证由电荷守恒（及可能的能量守恒）产生的超选择规则，并讨论退相干与无共享参考系所诱发的有效超选择规则。

Result: 综述表明：超选择规则使Hilbert空间分解为超选择分块（superselection sectors），跨分块的相干项不可观测；电荷等守恒量可作为超选择荷给出严格（本征）超选择规则；而退相干和参考系缺失则可在实践中产生等效的超选择规则，使某些"本应存在"的叠加与算子失去物理意义。

Conclusion: 超选择规则并非单纯的数学约束，而是界定物理态、物理可观测量与允许物理过程的判据；本征超选择规则源于守恒律，有效超选择规则源于环境退相干与参考系资源的缺失，二者共同构成理解量子理论中"物理性"边界的统一视角。

Abstract: It is well-known that a physical state may be represented by a unit vector in a Hilbert space, whilst a physical observable may be represented by a self-adjoint operator on that same space. However, the introduction of a superselection rule on a Hilbert space imposes constraints such that not all self-adjoint operators can represent observables, which has further consequences for the states themselves as well as the physical processes that transform them. In this article, we review the concept of superselection from the view of how such rules would affect what we consider to be physical. Arguments for the existence of a superselection rule due to charge or possibly energy conservation are given and the emergence of effective superselection rules due to decoherence or the lack of a shared reference frame are also discussed.

</details>


### [207] [Noise robust super-Heisenberg scaling in time-dependent quantum sensing by subspace-twirled dynamical decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17056)
*Bakmou Lahcen,Ke Zeng,Tianyu Yang,Yu Jiang,Kok Chuan Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于子空间twirling的动力解耦方案，在保留编码信号的同时抑制环境噪声，并在无需自适应控制的情形下实现超越Heisenberg极限的标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时哈密顿量可实现超越标准Heisenberg极限（super-Heisenberg limit）的超高精度参数估计，但最优精度标度通常依赖资源消耗大、难以实现的自适应量子控制方案，且该优势常被环境噪声严重削弱。

Method: 采用基于子空间twirling的动力解耦方案，即在子空间（而非整个Hilbert空间）上对随机酉算子做平均；首先证明子空间twirling既能保留编码信号又能去除环境噪声的充要条件，随后给出含时哈密顿量经子空间twirling后无需自适应控制即可达到super-Heisenberg标度的条件及性能保证。

Result: 理论上建立了信号保持与噪声消除的充要条件，并给出无需自适应控制实现super-Heisenberg标度的条件与性能保证；并以一个量子比特传感器为例，验证其在子空间twirl下可同时抑制噪声并获得super-Heisenberg标度。

Conclusion: 子空间twirling型动力解耦为含时、含噪条件下超高精度量子传感器的设计提供了指导，可在避免自适应控制开销的同时兼顾噪声鲁棒性与超Heisenberg精度标度。

Abstract: In quantum parameter estimation, time-dependent Hamiltonians can enable ultraprecise estimates of a physical parameter beyond the standard Heisenberg limit, also called the super-Heisenberg limit. However, achieving optimal scaling of the measurement precision typically requires an adaptive quantum control scheme which is resource-intensive and hard to implement in general. Furthermore, this advantage is often severely eroded by environmental noise. We propose the use of a dynamical decoupling scheme based on subspace twirling -- the averaging of random unitaries over a subspace rather than the entire Hilbert space -- to overcome these two limitations. We first prove necessary and sufficient conditions for subspace twirling to preserve the encoding signal while simultaneously removing environmental noise. We then establish conditions and provide performance guarantees for when time dependent Hamiltonians undergoing subspace twirling can achieve super-Heisenberg scaling without adaptive controls. Finally, we illustrate our results using an explicit example where a qubit sensor undergoing a subspace twirl simultaneously suppresses noise and achieves super-Heisenberg scaling. These results offer guidance for the design of ultraprecise quantum sensors under time-dependent and noisy conditions.

</details>


### [208] [Gaussian ergotropy and spatial correlations: accelerated vacuum versus a thermal field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17057)
*Siwei Li,Xiaofen Huang,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究能否通过功提取来揭示量子环境的空间关联，建立环境空间关联与平衡态高斯能质（Gaussian ergotropy）之间的定量联系，并提出可消除盲点的双构型方案以重构空间关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境空间关联通常难以直接测量，而两个局部谱完全相同但空间关联不同的环境（加速真空 vs 匹配惯性热场）为检验"功提取能否作为探测手段"提供了理想对照场景，作者希望将高斯能质确立为玻色环境中空间关联的可操作探针。

Method: 采用两个谐振子探测器分别耦合到加速真空或匹配的惯性热场；推导平衡态高斯能质与环境空间关联的定量关系，指出主导可提取功由"响应加权空间关联"决定；通过反转探测器间直接耦合构造双测量构型以消除有限距离盲点；并分析可分离探测器对下的全局功优势及高斯能质的瞬态演化。

Result: 两环境局域谱相同但空间关联不同，导致可提取功响应明显区分；发现存在有限距离盲点使单一测量构型无法分辨两环境；反转耦合的双构型方案可消除该模糊性并从功测量中重构空间关联，且在弱耦合区外仍有效；即使探测器对可分离也能获得额外全局功，说明纠缠并非该优势的必要资源；高斯能质呈振荡衰减并依赖加速度与探测器参数。

Conclusion: 高斯能质可作为探测玻色环境空间关联的操作性探针；空间关联带来的功优势不依赖纠缠，双构型功测量可鲁棒重构环境空间关联。

Abstract: Can the spatial correlations of a quantum environment be revealed through work extraction? We address this question using two harmonic-oscillator detectors coupled to either an accelerated vacuum or a matched inertial thermal field. We establish a quantitative connection between the spatial correlations of the environment and the equilibrium Gaussian ergotropy, showing that the leading extractable work is governed by a response-weighted spatial correlation. Although the two environments have identical local spectra, their different spatial correlations give rise to distinct work responses. We further identify a finite-distance blind point at which a single measurement configuration fails to distinguish the two environments. By reversing the direct coupling between the detectors, we develop a two-setting scheme that removes this ambiguity and reconstructs the underlying spatial correlations from work measurements. This reconstruction remains effective beyond the weak-coupling regime. We also show that spatial correlations can provide additional global work even when the detector pair is separable, demonstrating that entanglement is not a necessary resource for this advantage. Finally, we investigate the transient evolution of Gaussian ergotropy and reveal its oscillatory decay and dependence on acceleration and detector parameters. Our results establish Gaussian ergotropy as an operational probe of spatial correlations in bosonic environments.

</details>


### [209] [Efficient computation of PEPS gradients for optimized quantum state preparation on 2D topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17158)
*Illia Lukin,Vladyslav Bohun,Maciej Koch-Janusz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于二维投影纠缠对态（PEPS）梯度优化的硬件拓扑感知量子态制备方法，结合局域解纠缠与全局精调，并给出高效的PEPS梯度计算流程，通过数值模拟验证收敛性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂纠缠初态的浅层量子电路制备难以同时兼顾深度与精度；已有张量网络合成方法多采用一维矩阵乘积态（MPS），在高维纠缠态表达上精度不足。

Method: 以2D PEPS作为态表示，结合硬件拓扑结构设计电路；采用"局域解纠缠 + 基于PEPS梯度优化的全局精调"两阶段策略，并提出高效的PEPS梯度计算方法。

Result: 数值模拟实验考察了方法的收敛行为与制备性能（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 2D PEPS梯度优化为纠缠态的高精度、拓扑匹配的量子态制备提供了可行途径，优于传统1D MPS思路，并具备实际硬件适配潜力。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is a fundamental subroutine for quantum computation. For complex, entangled initial states it is, however, difficult to prepare shallow quantum circuits approximating the state with sufficient accuracy. Tensor networks provide a promising avenue of synthesis of such circuits, but most approaches employ 1D Matrix Products States, which struggle to accurately express entangled states in higher dimensions. Here we present a hardware topology-aware quantum state preparation method based on gradient optimization of 2D projected entangled pair states (PEPS). Our approach combines local disentanglement, with global fine tuning based on PEPS gradient optimization. Towards this end we describe an efficient procedure for PEPS gradient computation. We test the convergence and performance of our method in numerical simulations.

</details>


### [210] [Explicit Absolutely Maximally Entangled states in dimensions twice a prime power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17216)
*Palash Pandya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过量子比特与q能级系统的耦合，构造了局域维数为2q的AME态显式族，并由此完成九体AME态分类：AME(9,d)存在当且仅当d≥3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AME态是完全多体纠缠的核心对象，但除少数小参数外，其存在性判定（如给定局域维数d和粒子数N）长期缺乏完整刻画，尤其九体情形未定。

Method: 利用量子比特与q能级系统（q为奇素数幂）已有的耦合结构：q能级部分消除分支间干涉，使量子比特部分只需平均意义下的最大混合约化。由此构造局域维数2q的显式AME族，并采用分支配对选取，获得在粒子循环置换下由局域酉补偿的AME态。

Result: 得到AME(9,2q)、AME(11,2q)（q≥5）、AME(13,2q)（q≥17）、AME(15,2q)（q≥9）、AME(17,2q)（q≥67）；任意奇N≥3在充分条件q≥max{N,2^{N-2}}下得到AME(N,2q)，并证明该耦合内最小q必须随N指数增长。结合已知构造与乘积，完成九体分类：d≥2时AME(9,d)存在当且仅当d≥3。此外对p≡1 (mod 15)，AME(15,2p)具有循环移位局域酉对称性。

Conclusion: 该耦合框架系统地给出了一大批高粒子数AME态，并解决了九体存在性问题；同时表明该方法的指数下界限制，提示更一般的AME构造仍需新思路。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states are pure multipartite states with maximally mixed reductions on every set of at most half the parties. We construct explicit families in local dimension $2q$, twice an odd prime power $q$, using an established coupling of qubits and $q$-level systems. The $q$-level states remove interference between branches, so the qubit states need maximally mixed reductions only on average. This gives AME$(9,2q)$ and AME$(11,2q)$ for every odd prime power $q\geq5$, AME$(13,2q)$ for $q\geq17$, AME$(15,2q)$ for $q\geq9$, and AME$(17,2q)$ for $q\geq67$. For every odd $N\geq3$, the same coupling gives AME$(N,2q)$ under the sufficient condition $q\geq\max\{N,2^{N-2}\}$ on the odd prime power $q$. Within this coupling, the smallest such $q$ must grow exponentially with $N$. Combined with known inputs and products, these families complete the nine-party classification: for integer $d\geq2$, an AME$(9,d)$ state exists if and only if $d\geq3$. Choosing the branch pairing also gives AME$(15,2p)$ states with a cyclic shift of the parties compensated by local unitaries, for every prime $p\equiv1\pmod{15}$.

</details>


### [211] [Programming Quantum Circuit Parameters with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17246)
*Tiago de Souza Farias,Caio Moraes Porto,Matheus da Silva Fonseca,Nicolas Armando Cabrera Carpi,Anderson de Araujo Oliveira,Celso Jorge Villas-Boas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究冻结的通用大语言模型（LLM）能否直接从符号化的电路与哈密顿量描述生成变分量子电路参数，并系统考察其在随机、结构化（parent）哈密顿量及分子体系上的表现，同时评估迭代能量反馈、能谱下界合规性与"盲"电路解读能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示LLM可在无常规优化的情况下生成变分量子电路参数，但其有效性成立的条件并不清楚。作者希望厘清：问题结构（是否存在可识别的物理/数学结构）如何决定LLM参数推断的成败，迭代反馈是否有效，以及LLM直接预测能量时是否满足物理约束。

Method: 使用一个冻结的通用LLM，输入为符号化电路描述与哈密顿量描述，输出完整的电路参数向量，可选地加入迭代能量反馈；在通用随机哈密顿量、结构化parent哈密顿量与分子体系三类基准上与常规Adam优化对比，检验能量误差；额外考察LLM直接预测最小能量是否违反哈密顿量能谱下界，并开展"盲电路解读"实验（测试LLM在无法识别量子算法时能否预测测量结果）。

Result: 对通用随机哈密顿量，LLM生成参数的能量误差大于Adam优化，额外反馈带来的提升通常有限；对结构化哈密顿量，LLM能生成较有希望的低能参数构型。直接由LLM预测最低能量可能违反能谱下界，而生成的电路参数则可被独立评估，从而给出物理上可实现的能量期望值。在盲电路解读中，LLM有时虽无法识别底层量子算法，却能正确预测测量结果。

Conclusion: LLM量子参数编程的效果高度依赖问题结构：可识别的物理与数学结构有利于参数推断，而迭代LLM优化存在明显局限。直接预测能量不可靠且可能违反物理约束，因此应坚持用生成的参数进行独立、可验证的能量评估。这凸显了在量子参数编程中"可独立验证的预测"的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated the ability to generate parameters for variational quantum circuits without conventional optimization. However, the conditions under which this approach is effective remain unclear. Here, we investigate a frozen, general-purpose LLM that generates complete circuit parameter vectors from symbolic circuit and Hamiltonian descriptions, with optional iterative energy feedback. We evaluate the approach on general random Hamiltonians, structured parent Hamiltonians, and molecular systems. For general Hamiltonians, LLM-generated parameters exhibit larger energy errors than conventional Adam optimization, while additional feedback generally provides limited improvement. In contrast, for structured Hamiltonians, the LLM can generate promising low-energy parameter configurations, suggesting that identifiable physical and mathematical structure facilitates parameter inference. We also show that direct LLM predictions of minimum energies can violate the Hamiltonian spectral lower bound, whereas generated circuit parameters allow independent evaluation of physically realizable energy expectation values. Finally, we investigate blind circuit interpretation and find that the LLM can predict measurement outcomes even when it fails to identify the underlying quantum algorithm. These findings highlight the role of problem structure, the limitations of iterative LLM optimization, and the importance of independently verifiable predictions in quantum parameter programming.

</details>


### [212] [Implementation of a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD under finite-size parameter estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17250)
*Christiano M. S. Nascimento,Maron F. Anka,Gabriel A. V. Menezes,Davi J. G. de Sousa,Nelson A. Neto,Artur A. Matoso,Gustavo C. Amaral,Valéria L. Silva,Guilherme P. Temporão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在12 km标准单模光纤上，采用独立激光器LLO架构实现了基于一维高斯调制的CV-QKD系统实验，并搭建了完整的离线经典后处理链，获得渐近条件下1.421 Mbit/s、有限码长条件下0.364 Mbit/s的保密密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯调制CV-QKD需在两个光场正交分量上编码信息，增加了态制备、调制与标定要求，并可能引入额外制备缺陷；一维高斯调制虽简化了发送端，但缺乏集成化、结合独立激光器相干接收、实用协调、有限码长参数估计与隐私放大的完整实验验证。

Method: 构建一维高斯调制CV-QKD实验系统：采用本地本振（LLO）架构与独立激光器，经12 km标准单模光纤传输；实现相干探测、数字信号处理、信息协调、参数估计、基于物理性的安全性分析及隐私放大，形成集成离线经典后处理链。

Result: 在一维高斯调制下，渐近安全性处理时隐私放大速率为1.421 Mbit/s；有限码长场景下为0.364 Mbit/s。

Conclusion: 实验结果为评估一维高斯调制在性能—复杂度权衡方面提供了实验基线，并为开发低复杂度CV-QKD发射机奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) is a promising technology for quantum-secure communications, offering high secret-key rates and straightforward integration with coherent optical communication systems. Conventional Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD encodes information in both optical-field quadratures, increasing requirements for state preparation, modulation, and calibration, while potentially introducing additional preparation imperfections. Unidimensional (1D) Gaussian modulation offers a simpler alternative by encoding information in only one quadrature, while the conjugate quadrature constrains the protocol's security. However, an integrated experimental 1D implementation combining independent-laser coherent reception with practical reconciliation, finite-size parameter estimation, and privacy amplification remains challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD system over 12~km of standard single-mode fiber using a local local oscillator (LLO) architecture with independent lasers and an integrated offline classical post-processing chain. The implementation includes coherent detection, digital signal processing, information reconciliation, parameter estimation, physicality-constrained security analysis, and privacy amplification. The 1D system achieves a privacy-amplified rate of $1.421$~Mbit/s under the asymptotic security treatment and $0.364$~Mbit/s under the finite-size scenario. These results establish an experimental baseline for the performance--complexity trade-off associated with unidimensional Gaussian modulation and provide an experimental basis for developing lower-complexity CV-QKD transmitters.

</details>


### [213] [Non-interferometric tests of gravitational decoherence models with mechanical oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17260)
*Sandro Donadi,Iarley P. Lobo,Matteo Fadel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三种非干涉手段可探测的量子引力诱导退相干模型（形变相对论对称性、随机度规涨落、随机广义不确定原理），分析其在自由粒子与谐振子中的效应，并给出基于加热和连续测量的实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往量子引力退相干多依赖干涉实验探测；作者希望说明即使不依赖干涉测量，这类退相干仍可留下可观测效应，从而拓宽探测途径。

Method: 针对自由粒子考察动量分布、平均能量、位置扩散与动量空间退相干；针对谐振子分析坍缩算符与哈密顿量不对易导致的异常加热，并推导稳态位置功率谱密度的过量线型；最后结合机械平台、声子模式与低温实验数据做约束估计。

Result: 自由粒子情形下三种模型均保持动量分布与平均能量，但产生位置扩散及依赖模型的动量退相干。谐振子加热速率分别呈常数（模型一）、正比于动能并可指数增长（模型二）、随激发数立方增长（模型三）。弱噪声下三者给出相同的领头阶过量线型，且不同于力噪声。位移噪声测量可将模型一的涨落时间约束到普朗克时间以下；占据数测量对模型二的约束较悬浮粒子基态加热提升最多十四个数量级；综合固体全部声子模的残余热释放，可将模型一约束至远低于普朗克时间、模型二约束至普朗克时间量级。

Conclusion: 加热与连续测量构成与干涉实验互补的非干涉探针，可用于检验与引力相关的退相干。

Abstract: Phenomenological models of quantum-gravity-induced decoherence can leave observable effects even in experiments that do not rely on interferometric measurements. We study three such models, based respectively on deformed relativistic symmetries, stochastic metric fluctuations, and a stochastic generalized uncertainty principle, all of which induce decoherence in the momentum basis. For a free particle, the models preserve momentum distribution and mean energy but produce position diffusion and momentum-space decoherence with model-specific momentum dependences. For a harmonic oscillator, the noncommutativity of the collapse operators with the oscillator Hamiltonian results in anomalous heating, with the three models leading to qualitatively different energy dynamics. The first gives heating at a constant rate, the second at a rate proportional to the kinetic energy, which can lead to exponential growth, and the third at a rate growing cubically with excitation. We further derive the stationary position power spectral density and show that all three models produce the same leading-order excess lineshape in the weak-noise regime, distinct from that of force noise. Comparing with existing mechanical platforms, we find that displacement-noise measurements can already constrain the fluctuation time of the first model below the Planck time, while occupation measurements could improve the bounds on the second model by up to fourteen orders of magnitude over those from ground-state heating of levitated particles. Summing the heating over all phonon modes of a solid, the residual heat release of low-temperature experiments constrains the first model far below the Planck time and the second at the Planck time. Our results establish heating and continuous measurements as complementary non-interferometric probes of gravity-related decoherence.

</details>


### [214] [Scalable Pauli propagation through $\mathbb{F}_2$-linear partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17262)
*Alev Orfi,Andrew Kille,J. Clayton Peacock,Jack Dunham,Lukas Devos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于F2线性哈希划分Pauli串的方法，消除Pauli传播中去重所需的全局同步，实现跨节点高效并行，支撑超10^11量级Pauli串的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pauli传播以Heisenberg图像将可观测量演化为稀疏Pauli串之和，其操作本质是比特运算，性能瓶颈在内存带宽与容量而非算力；但每步门操作后的去重需要全局同步，阻碍了多核多节点内存的聚合利用。

Method: 用F2线性哈希函数对Pauli串进行分区：该性质保证k比特量子通道作用下，每个分区最多耦合到4^k个由该门固定的分区，从而把门的施加分解为通信量有界的独立任务，避免全局同步式去重。

Result: 开源实现的基准测试显示在单节点内与跨节点均具备良好并行扩展性，可模拟超过10^11个Pauli串，并扩展了量子多体动力学的可及时间尺度。

Conclusion: 以F2线性哈希分区替代全局去重同步是突破Pauli传播内存瓶颈的有效途径，显著提升并行可扩展性与可模拟规模。

Abstract: Pauli propagation simulates quantum circuits and many-body dynamics by evolving observables in the Heisenberg picture as sparse sums of Pauli strings. As Pauli-string operations reduce to bit manipulations, its performance is limited by memory bandwidth and capacity rather than computational throughput. Pooling the memory of many cores and nodes is hindered by the deduplication step after every gate, which can require global synchronization. We avoid this bottleneck by partitioning the strings with an $\mathbb{F}_2$-linear hash function, which guarantees that a $k$-qubit channel couples each partition to at most $4^k$ partitions fixed by the gate, decomposing gate application into independent tasks with bounded communication. Benchmarks of our open-source implementation demonstrate efficient parallel scaling within and across compute nodes, enabling simulations with over $10^{11}$ Pauli strings and extending the accessible timescales of quantum many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [215] [A deployed four-city entanglement-based QKD network with real-time key delivery, benchmarked against finite-key security bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17265)
*Gilberto Borges,Saeid Salari,Ekta Panwar,Samgeeth Puliyil,Kartik Kakade,Peter Rapčan,Mario Ziman,Djeylan Aktas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 报道斯洛伐克国家量子通信基础设施中基于纠缠的城际QKD段：四条链路同时运行、中心节点纠缠源经波长被动路由实现无可信中继全网状拓扑；提出被动波长路由矩阵形式化方法；所有密钥率均在完整后处理链后实时经ETSI GS QKD 014交付，109 km链路达13.5 bit/s，无直连光纤的两城间在32.1 dB累积损耗下达4.3 bit/s；有限密钥基准表明启发式隐私放大可越界，改用纠错可将有限密钥率提升4.1–6.6倍并使142.9 km链路由无密钥变为正密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有QKD组网演示多依赖可信中继，且密钥率常由原始符合计数估算而非完整后处理结果，难以反映实际可用性与安全性；需要在实际部署光纤上验证无可信中继、多链路并发、可算法化设计的城际纠缠QKD网络。

Method: 在斯洛伐克西部四城市部署光纤上，用中心节点产生的偏振纠缠光子对，经波长被动路由构建三城市无可信中继全网状子网，第四城经中心节点接入，全链路同时运行；提出被动波长路由的矩阵形式化框架以算法化构造唯一全连接拓扑；采用部分非局域色散补偿，密钥经完整后处理链并依据ETSI GS QKD 014实时交付；并以有限密钥安全界为基准对比启发式隐私放大与"检测-丢弃"策略。

Result: 109 km链路获得13.5 bit/s；无直连光纤的两城市间在32.1 dB累积损耗下获得4.3 bit/s。基准分析显示已实现的启发式隐私放大可超出有限密钥界；以纠错替代检测-丢弃可使有限密钥率提高4.1–6.6倍，并在142.9 km链路上由零密钥率转为正密钥率。

Conclusion: 实际部署的纠缠QKD网络可无可信中继地实现多城并发全连接分发，且报告的是端到端后处理后的真实密钥率；矩阵化波长路由方法支持拓扑的可编程设计。现有启发式隐私放大与检测-丢弃策略是性能瓶颈，采用标准纠错后可显著提升有限密钥率并扩展可达距离。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) generates shared secret keys whose security rests on the laws of physics rather than on computational assumptions. We report the entanglement-based segment of the Slovak national quantum communication infrastructure, connecting four cities in western Slovakia over deployed telecommunication fiber. Polarization-entangled photons generated at a central node are passively routed by wavelength to form a trusted-node-free, fully meshed sub-network of three cities, with the fourth connected through the central node and all links operated simultaneously. We also introduce a matrix formalism for passive wavelength routing that enables the algorithmic construction of uniquely and fully connected topologies. Unlike most previous demonstrations, which estimate key rates from raw coincidences, all rates reported here are obtained after the complete post-processing chain, with keys delivered in real time via ETSI GS QKD 014. With partial non-local chromatic dispersion compensation, we obtain 13.5 bit/s over 109 km, and 4.3 bit/s over 32.1 dB of accumulated loss between two cities without a direct fiber link. Benchmarking against finite-key security bounds shows that the implemented heuristic privacy amplification can exceed the finite-key bound, and that replacing detect-and-discard with error correction would increase the finite-key rate by a factor of 4.1-6.6, and yield a positive finite-key rate on the 142.9 km link where the implemented scheme yields none.

</details>


### [216] [Observation of Genuine Multi-Time Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17268)
*Konstantin Beyer,Valentin Link,Surihan Sean Borjigin,Qian Cao,Orion Lee,Kater Murch,Igor Pikovski,Philip Taranto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出非马尔可夫记忆效应不等于量子记忆，因为所有两时刻（pairwise）判据都可被经典记忆模型复现；作者构造三时刻联合见证量，证明量子记忆存在严格的层次结构，并在超导量子比特平台上实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中的记忆效应本身并不蕴含量子记忆：非马尔可夫动力学通常可以用经典记忆模型重现。现有判据均基于两个时刻的演化比较，因此可能无法识别真正的量子记忆，需要厘清“量子记忆”与“非马尔可夫性”的本质区别。

Method: 构造一个最小量子比特模型，使得任意两时刻的演化都存在经典记忆解释；在此基础上推导解析的三时刻见证量（three-time witness），联合考察三个及以上时刻的统计行为以区分量子记忆与经典记忆；并在具有时间无关耦合的超导量子比特平台上，以时间连续演化方式（不依赖精细调节的含时哈密顿量或门序列）实现该方案。

Result: 证明量子记忆可以隐藏于所有两时刻（pairwise）检验之下，仅在三个及以上时刻联合考察时才可被探测；三时刻见证量在该最小示例中成功认证量子记忆，从而建立了量子记忆效应的严格层次结构（hierarchy）。

Conclusion: 真正的多时刻量子记忆不依赖精细调节的含时哈密顿量或门基演化，可在时间连续、耦合时间无关的物理系统中实现；实验结果确立了量子记忆的层次结构，表明两时刻判据对量子记忆的认证是不完备的。

Abstract: Memory effects in open quantum systems do not by themselves imply quantum memory, since non-Markovian dynamics can often be reproduced by classical-memory models. Known criteria certify the presence of quantum memory in the environment by comparing the evolution of the system at two different times. We show that quantum memory can remain hidden from all such pairwise tests and become detectable only when three or more times are considered jointly. We derive an analytical three-time witness and use it to certify quantum memory in a minimal qubit example in which every pair of times has a classical-memory explanation, thus establishing a strict hierarchy of quantum memory effects. Genuine multi-time quantum memory does not rely on fine-tuned time-dependent Hamiltonians or gate-based evolution. We experimentally demonstrate the hierarchy of quantum memory in a time-continuous realization on a superconducting-qubit platform with time-independent couplings.

</details>


### [217] [Stiffness in Confined Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17272)
*Pablo G. M. Dantas,Rafael Vieira,Edgard P. M. Amorim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究有限格点上带幺正反射边界条件的离散时间量子行走中的"边界诱导刚度"，定义无量纲扩展刚度指标，比较三种初态，发现长时响应强烈依赖初始空间轮廓，且动力学刚性与空间均匀化是两个不同性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对量子行走扩展行为的研究多集中于无界或开放边界情形，而有限尺寸与反射边界如何影响扩散动力学（尤其是"刚度"这一响应性质）尚缺乏定量刻画；同时需要厘清初态空间结构在受限体系长时行为中的作用。

Method: 在有限格点上通过硬币翻转操作实现幺正反射边界，构造离散时间量子行走；以空间宽度瞬时变化相对无界Hadamard行走渐近扩展速率的比值定义无量纲扩展刚度；对局域态、高斯态与截断均匀态三类初态进行长时间数值比较，并考察刚度的一阶、二阶矩渐近一致性关系以及归一化Kullback–Leibler散度。

Result: 长时响应显著依赖初态空间轮廓：局域态与截断均匀态的刚度随限制尺度增大整体上升，而高斯态呈非单调依赖并在中等尺寸处出现宽峰；在高刚度区间，一阶与二阶刚度矩满足预期的渐近一致性关系；归一化KL散度显示空间重组变慢且有限尺寸趋势定性不同。

Conclusion: 幺正受限产生本质上的非定常（nonstationary）区域，其长时性质源于边界反射、系统尺寸与初态空间结构三者的相互作用；动力学刚性与空间均匀化是彼此独立的两类性质，不能相互替代作为受限程度的度量。

Abstract: We investigate boundary-induced stiffness in discrete-time quantum walks confined to a finite lattice by unitary reflecting boundaries implemented through coin-flip operations. We define a dimensionless spreading stiffness from the instantaneous variation of the spatial width relative to the asymptotic spreading rate of the corresponding unconfined Hadamard walk and compare localized, Gaussian, and truncated-uniform initial states. The long-time response retains a strong dependence on the initial spatial profile: the localized and truncated-uniform states show an overall increase in stiffness with increasing confinement size, whereas the Gaussian state exhibits a nonmonotonic dependence with a broad maximum at intermediate sizes. The first and second stiffness moments satisfy the expected asymptotic consistency relation in the high-stiffness regime. The normalized Kullback--Leibler divergence reveals a slower spatial reorganization and qualitatively different finite-size trends, showing that dynamical stiffness and spatial homogenization are distinct properties. These results indicate that unitary confinement produces an intrinsically nonstationary regime whose long-time properties emerge from the interplay between boundary reflections, system size, and the spatial structure of the initial state.

</details>


### [218] [Near-Optimal Multiparameter Pure-State Metrology with a Constant Number of Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17281)
*Bowen Xiao,Zhiyao Yang,Huangjun Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用"Fisher floor"（在所有纯态与所有参数方向上保留的QFI最小比例）刻画多参数纯态估计的均匀灵敏度，证明常数个固定正交基即可实现与维度d无关的近优floor：5个独立Haar基以高概率给出正的、与维度无关的floor，更大的常数族可达约1/2的QFI保留率（测量不相容性允许的上限），且基的数量在精度倒数对数因子内最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数量子计量中，针对不同参数最优的测量彼此不相容，平均意义下的良好平衡（如平均Fisher各向异性最小）并不能保证在每个参数方向上都有效；因此需要一个刻画"最坏情形"均匀灵敏度的指标，并弄清需要多少个固定测量基才能逼近其理论上限。

Method: 以Fisher floor（对全部纯态与参数方向取最小的QFI保留比例）为均匀灵敏度度量；构造由常数个固定正交归一基组成的测量族（如5个独立Haar随机基），分析其floor的概率下界；并对比d+1个互无偏基（MUB）族的平均Fisher各向异性与floor；给出关于基数量的下界（反精度对数因子内最优）。

Result: 5个独立Haar基以高概率给出正且与d无关的Fisher floor；更大的常数族实现近似"普适Fisher对称"，在任意方向保留近1/2的QFI——此为测量不相容性所允许的最大均匀比例；所需基数量在1/精度对数因子内最优。反例：d+1个MUB虽能使d+1基族的平均Fisher各向异性最小，但其Fisher floor至多1/(d+1)。

Conclusion: 实现近优均匀多参数灵敏度只需常数个固定正交基，与Hilbert空间维度无关；平均平衡（如MUB的最小平均各向异性）不等价于均匀灵敏度，Fisher floor是必要且更严格的准则。

Abstract: Multiparameter pure-state metrology is limited by the incompatibility of measurements optimized for different parameters. We characterize uniform sensitivity by the Fisher floor, the smallest fraction of the quantum Fisher information (QFI) retained over all pure states and parameter directions. We prove that a constant number of fixed orthonormal bases suffices for a nearly optimal Fisher floor, independently of the Hilbert-space dimension $d$. With high probability, five independent Haar bases already achieve a positive, dimension-independent floor, and larger constant families achieve approximate universal Fisher symmetry: they retain nearly one half of the QFI in every direction, the largest uniform fraction permitted by measurement incompatibility. The required number of bases is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in inverse accuracy. In contrast, $d+1$ mutually unbiased bases, when they exist, can minimize mean Fisher anisotropy among families of $d+1$ bases yet have a Fisher floor of at most $1/(d+1)$, showing that optimal average balance does not ensure uniform sensitivity.

</details>


### [219] [Sharp Uniform Fisher Information Bounds from Projective Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17288)
*Zhiyao Yang,Bowen Xiao,Huangjun Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究多参数纯态计量中用固定测量（不依赖输入态）时，测量能保留多少量子Fisher信息，并证明仅凭射影设计的阶数就能给出Fisher下限（Fisher floor的保证），且该保证在t=2及奇数阶紧、偶数阶近紧，并构造出线性输出数、分离所有纯态的最优测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定测量的多参数纯态计量面临困境：测量必须在不了解输入态的情况下仍然"有信息量"。单次拷贝下测量至多保留一半量子Fisher信息（单拷贝量子极限），但此前缺少对"固定设计型测量"保留信息量的通用、仅依赖设计阶数的定量刻画。

Method: 将需求量化为Fisher floor——在所有纯态与切方向上，测量保留量子Fisher信息的最小比例；把Fisher floor的下界与射影设计的阶数t关联，推导各维度的Fisher保证；并用有限加权设计、3-设计的采样与谱补全构造具体测量。

Result: 对每个维度d≥2给出仅由射影设计阶数决定的Fisher保证；在t=2与所有奇数阶t≥3时界是紧的，且由有限加权设计达到；偶数阶时上界近似紧。随阶数增大保证趋近1/2（单拷贝量子极限）。对任意3-设计采样并谱补全，以1-O(1/d)概率得到输出数为线性规模、Fisher floor与维度无关的测量。该测量按Hellinger距离分离所有纯态，故N拷贝最大似然估计在全体纯态上一致达到平均不保真度O(d/N)，在通用常数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 设计阶数是刻画固定测量信息保留能力的关键参数：高阶设计可在输出数线性、与维度无关的Fisher floor下实现近最优的多参数纯态估计，达到单拷贝量子极限附近的性能。

Abstract: Fixed measurements for multiparameter pure-state metrology must remain informative without knowing the input state. We quantify this requirement by the Fisher floor, the smallest fraction of the quantum Fisher information that a measurement retains over all pure states and tangent directions. We determine the Fisher guarantees, namely lower bounds on Fisher floors, implied by projective design order alone in every dimension $d\ge 2$. These guarantees are sharp at order $t=2$ and every odd order $t\ge 3$, and finite weighted designs attain them; at even orders, our bounds are nearly tight. As the order increases, the guarantees approach one half, the single-copy quantum limit. Sampling and spectral completion of any 3-design yield, with probability $1-O(1/d)$, a measurement with linearly many outcomes and a dimension-independent Fisher floor. Such a measurement separates all pure states in Hellinger distance, so pure-state maximum-likelihood estimation from $N$ copies attains mean infidelity $O(d/N)$ uniformly over pure states, optimal up to a universal constant factor.

</details>


### [220] [Traces of Vacuum Squeezing: A Superconducting Transmission-Line Analogue of Cosmological Particle Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17295)
*Paula I. Villar,Federico Guglielmucci Nazar,Elián Urtubey,Fernando C. Lombardo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 超导传输线（dc-SQUID阵列）可精确模拟辐射主导FRW宇宙中标量场的模式方程（Weber方程），并用反常磁矩作为Bogoliubov混合的耗散鲁棒探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙中时变量子场的粒子产生是核心现象，但无法在原始宇宙学背景下实验验证，因此需要可控的量子模拟平台。

Method: 理论证明共形时间下、载有dc-SQUID的波导型传输线的模式方程化为Weber方程，与辐射主导背景的宇宙学粒子产生方程相同；用抛物柱函数给出精确解及指数压低的粒子谱；分析类比成立条件（脉冲时长、调制均匀性、SQUID对称性之间的权衡）及耗散影响；提出以各模式的反常磁矩作为Bogoliubov混合的见证量。

Result: 模式方程精确可解并给出指数抑制的粒子谱；耗散不会抹除宇宙学信号；反常磁矩对任意热态为零且在耗散下仍存活，与光子数联合可认证压缩，与态纯度联合可判定真空产生粒子对是否占主导。

Conclusion: 该超导电路方案为辐射主导FRW宇宙的宇宙学粒子产生提供了可直接实验实施的类比模拟，并给出鲁棒、可判据化的观测方案。

Abstract: Particle creation in time-dependent quantum fields is a central phenomenon in early-universe cosmology, yet it remains experimentally inaccessible in its original context. We show theoretically that superconducting circuits based on a waveguide-like transmission line loaded with dc-SQUIDs can provide a direct analogue of a scalar field in a radiation-dominated Friedmann--Robertson--Walker universe in conformal time. The mode equation of the circuit reduces to the Weber equation, the same equation governing cosmological particle creation in a radiation-dominated background, admitting an exact solution in terms of parabolic cylinder functions and an exponentially suppressed particle spectrum. We analyse the conditions under which the analogy holds, which require a compromise between the duration of the pulse, the uniformity of the modulation and the symmetry of the SQUIDs, and show that dissipation does not erase the cosmological signal. We use the anomalous moment of each mode as a witness of the Bogoliubov mixing: it vanishes for any thermal state and survives dissipation, and, together with the photon number, it certifies squeezing, and with the purity of the state, it identifies whether the pairs created from the vacuum dominate.

</details>
